सीगणेशायनधः

## SARAVALI

OF KALYANA VARMA

सारावली

(श्रीमत्कल्यार्वर्मवि रचिता)

## VOL I

English translation, commentary, annotation and editing by

R. SANTHANAM

RANJAN PUBLICATIONS

16, Ansari Road, Darya Ganj, NEW DELHI-110002 (INDIA)

## PROLEGOMENA

The Omnipotent and all-pervading Gayatri, Ganesa and the Grahas have all been exceedingly merciful in enabling us to place before the reading public this monumental treatise, SARAVALI, with my English rendering and commentary.

Kalyana Varma, the famous author of this famous work was a king himself ruling a place called Vyaghrapada, which place some of us trace in modern Madhya Pradesh and yet some in modern Gujarat. He is also identified as Karna Deva. He must have flourished before the 10th century as Bhattotpala (966 AD) reverentially referred to our illustrious author in his various commentaries on works like Brihat Jataka, Brihat Samhita etc. A statement akin to our author’s date as mid-13th century is, however, incredible. Seeing Bhattotpala's reference and statements by various writers, our author seemed to belong to the 6th century, that is immediately after the Great Master, Varaha Mihira.

During the time of Kalyana Varma, there were only a few literary gems on astrology, viz. Prasara Hora, Brihat Jataka, Hora Sara etc. Our author feels the limitation of Brihat Jataka (vide sloka 2, ch 1 infra) and has taken the quintessence of Maharshi Parasara’s Hora Sastra, apart from other great works like Brihat Jataka, as could be seen in chapters like 'Nabhasa Yogas'. The Greek pundits, Yavanacharyas, were more often acknowledged by him, his bold assertion of his rejecting their superfluous doctrines, being significant (vide sloka 4, ch 1). He further remains in gratitude to many a progenitor like Satya, Badarayana (simultaneously the author of Brahma Sutras), Chanakya, Chudamani, Hari, Maniththa, Brahma Saunda etc.

As the reader progresses through the work, he is sure to feel astrologically enlightened and find an invisible preceptor—a new source of guidance in Kalyana Varma's words

which bequeath to us the unparallel and unsurpassed wisdom of the sages.

The author's vast study and scientific treatment of the subject, able exposition and inimitable style will be evident from the provided guidelines. Apart from being an astrologer of high latitude, the king exhibits his literary aptitude in the language of the Gods, i.e. Sanskrit, through many slokas. He was adept in traditional medicine also, vide ch. 11, dealing with combinations to counteract lunar evils. Not only this, he had reasonable knowledge of Yoga for Body Control, as he refers to Basti kriya (in Ch. 11, sloka 4). (Basti Kriya is a process of yogic cleaning of the lower intestines to strenghten abdominal muscles and cure digestive and urinary disorders and chronic constipation.)

Most later authors summarized or verbatim borrowed the slokas of our author. Mentionable among these are: Balabhadra of Hora Ratna, Vaidyanatha of Jataka Parijata and Mantreswara of Phala deepika apart from the author of Jataka Saradeepa. The two works, Hora Ratna, and Jataka Saradeepa (the latter published from Tanjore in Sanskrit), owe a lot to Saravali.

Many important aspects of predictive astrology have been well-explained by the author in the following pages. Apart from ordinary preliminary information, the chapters on Arishtas (evils) and their cancellation, combinations for Renunciation, Nabhasa Yogas, planctary conjunction etc., may prove usefui in delineation of horoscopes scientifically and purposefully. Special mention need be made of chapters 22 through 26 which deal with effects of planets posited in various signs. These positions stand to give modified effects if aspected by other planets, clues to which effect have been given by the author simultaneously. In these chapters, one finds aspects between the Sun-Venus-Mercury mutually, in contradiction to known principles. The author himself mentions about the usual aspects in ch. 4, slokas 32-33. Apparently the said aspects among Sun- Venus-Mercury are only meant to be partial. Simllar as-

pects continue in chapter 27-29. These discussions could be found in Hora Ratna and Jataka Saradeepa also. As partial aspects are possible between the Sun, Mercury and Venus mutually, one can infer that Kalyana Varma refers to limited orbs only, in the relevant context. Full aspects cannot be extended between these planets.

Extensive and useful commentary with notes and quotations from a large number of texts in Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Hindi and English have been added by me in appropriate contexts. Even the most difficult aspects have been lucidly and minutely explained for the benefit of all concerned, according to my mite. Though maximum efforts have been made to keep this long-awaited treatise rid of longueurs, possibly errors could have found their way into the text. We can only seek the broadminded readers to view our short-comings leniently.

The Publishers, Ranjan Publications, have fulfilled the task of this treatise seeirig its print in English for the first time in a commendable way and with their usual missionary enthusiasm. By this act they earn the gratitude of many anxious readers of this divine science.

We now go to the text praying Goddess Saraswati to bestow us the required 'digestive power’.

— R. SANTHANAM

## SARAVALI सारावली 0,0

प्रथमोऽष्यायः

Chapter 1

## Birth of Horasasthra

यस्योदये जगदिदं प्रतिबोधमेति

मध्यस्थिते प्रसरति प्रकृतिक्यायु ।

स्रस्तं गते स्वपिति चोच्छ्वसितंकमात्रं

भानुः स एष जयति प्रकटप्रभावः ॥१॥

1. Glory to the Sun God, whose very breathing causes Creation in this world, with whose rising all the animals (living beings) wake up (from the sleep of their ignorance), whose state at the head (i.e. zenith) accentuates all activities (throws the brightest light on one's wisdom) and whosc setting (absence or weakness) causes all to sleep. Such Sun God has his efficacy spread (all over).

Notes: Most of the astrological savants in recognition of the importance of the Sun, praise him in the beginning of their works. Kalyana Varma, the king-astrologer, throws light on the Sun’s importance in all dally walks of life. The Sun's rise releases one of his night sleep and imparts "creation” in his daily activities. Similarly, if the Sun, at birth, is on the east, he bestows creative power on the native. When the Sun reaches the head at noon, human activities are at height. Likewise, when he is in the Zenith in the horoscope of birth (i.e. on the 10th cusp), one is likely

to reach enviable heights in life. When the Sun sets, the day ends, breaking the vigour of the day while in the horoscope, the Sun in the setting house (7th house) or in a weak state, reduces the force of other heavenly bodies. That is why Prithuyasas etc., state that the strength of the Sun is very much essential in order that other Yogas become efficacious for the native.

We can also see that most authors, e.g. Varaha Mihira, Prithuyasas etc., praised the Sun God in the opening slokas, in their famous astrological works.

विस्तरङतानि मुनिभिः परियुद्ध पुरातनान सात्राशि । होरात्त्रं रबितं बराह्निहिरेख संक्षेपात् ॥२। राशिदशवर्गमूर्तियोमा पुर्दायतो बञ्चादीनान् । विषयविभन्नां स्पष्टं कुतु\* न तु पन्यते यतस्तेन ॥३।। चत एवं विस्तरेष्यो बबननरेन्दादिरचितञ्चार्त्रेम्यः । सकलम्सारं त्यक्त्वा अइ्ये सारं सम्मुद्धृत्य ।४।।

2-4. Varaha Mihira in his Hora Sasthra i.e. Brihat Jataka stated briefly the essence of the teachings of the learned sages, viz ten divisions of the Zodical Sign (a i ), Raja Yogas, longevity, effects of planetary periods etc. This could not be done elaborately by him and hence I (Kalyana Varma) am conveying in this work such useful essence as called out from Yavanas etc., rejecting other unuseful portions, which may be appended to the said Brthat Jataka.

Notes: The author, Kalyana Varma, has studied Brihat Jataka etc., and gives in this work various principles for the sake of the students of astrology.

बेवप्रामपुरप्रपोवखाबलाङ्गब्रह्माण्डसत्पञ्जरे

कोर्तिहसविलासिनोव सहसा वस्येह भात्यातa1 । भीमङ्ग्याप्रपवीद्वरो रचयति स्पट्टां स सारावलीं होराधास्त्रविनिर्मलोहृतमनाः कल्यार्वर्मा हुक्ती 1211

5. I, Kalyana Varma, whose fame, on account of patronizing Gods and administering villages and cities, is brilliant like that of a female swan in the cage of the universe, who is the king of Vyagrapada region, present this Saravali, an astrological treatise, with a chaste heart.

होरावृष्णार्तानां विष्याखां स्कुर्तराबंधिशिरजला । कल्यारात्र्मशैलान्नदीव सारावली प्रसृता ॥६।।

6. Saravali is a river with chilly water which has taken its birth in the mountain called Kalyana Varma, to ward off the thirst for astrology of those interested.

Notes: The author is compared to a mountain as he has given birth to the river called Saravalt, i.e., the present work, which removes the students' thirst for Jyotisha.

वति कस्पाखावर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां शास्त्रावतारो बाम प्रथमोऽध्यायः ॥१।

Thus ends the 1st Chapter "Birth of Horasasthra" in Kalyana Varma’s Saravali.

## Chapter 2 होराशब्द निरूपखाऽध्यायः ।।२।

## Meaning of Hora

विषात्रा लिखिता याऽसौ ललाटेऽक्षरमालिका । बेवज्षस्तां पठे्रव्यक्तं होरानिमलचक्षुषा ।१। श्राद्यन्तवर्णलोपादोरा सिद्धचत्यहोरात्रात् । तत्त्रतिबद्धञ्चायं प्रह्भगर्षिचन्त्यते यस्मात् ॥२। कर्मफललाभहेतुं चतुराः संवर्णयन्यन्ये। होराशास्त्रं संत्ञा लग्नस्य तथार्धरावेद्च ।३। जातंकमितिं प्रसिद्धं बल्लोके तबिह कीर्वते होरा । श्रेंचवा देवविमर्शनपर्यायः खल्वप्रे शब्दः ॥४।। श्रंचार्जेने सहायः पुरुवातामापदर्भंवे पोतः । यांत्रासमयै मन्त्री जातकमपहाय नास्त्यपरः ॥५I

1-5. The Creator, Brahma, has written on the foreheads of all living beings their fates which are deciphered by the astrologers through their pure insight. The first and last letters in the word (i.e. day and night) are removed and the word "" is thus born and has come to’ exist. The zodiac and the planets are therein as discussed (in these pages). Some scholars say Hora Sasthra is indicative of effects of one’s Karma i.e. fate. Some call Lagna or half of a Rasi as Hora. In practice, the science relating to horoscope is called Hora Sasthra. Hora is capable of analysing the destiny. Barring this Hora Sasthra (ie. Astrology) there is no device to help one earn money, to help as a boat to cross the ocean of unexpected situations and to serve as an advisor in journey.

Notes: The purpose of astrology is to assess one’s livelihood, bad and good incidents in life, journey etc., beforehand and plan life suitably.

See Brihat Jataka, Chapter I, Sloka 3, which states that whatever has been acquired by a person through his past deeds—good or bad—now attain fruition. See what नारद (Narada)states: भज्ञातजन्मानां नृखां फलाप्तर्चुखवखंवत्, meaning that without the knowledge of birth time, i.e. sans a horoscope, it is akin to describing an inciston in wood or a book. These show the importance of Hora Sasthra or the Sclence of Astrology.

इति कल्याख्वर्मबिरचितायां सारावल्यां होरावन्दार्थचिन्ता नाम

## द्वितीयोऽध्यायः ।।२।।

Thus ends the 2nd Chapter "Meantng of Hora" „in Kalyana Varma’s Saravall.

## - Chapter 3

## ऋथ राशिमेदाऽप्यायः ।३।।

## Rasi Description

तमसावृते समन्ताञ्जलमूते भूतले ततोऽ्कस्मात् । उवितो भगवान भानुः प्रकाशायन् स्वप्रकाशेन ।१।। व्यसृज्जगत्समस्तं प्रहर्षसंघातकल्पितावयवम् । ह्वादशमेव विचत्रः कालः \_\_\_ संत्रस्तुतस्तस्मात् ।२।।

\* 1-2. At the time of Pralaya (q, i.e. Destruction), the Universe plunged into darkness and was flooded with water, when, all of a sudden, the Sun God, with his brilllant rays appeared in the Universe. He created all the planets and the 12 months.

Notes: Each solar month is the result of the Sun’s movement in a particular Rasi and 12 solar months are caused during his sojourn in the 12 Rasis of the zodiac.

मेववृधमिथुनकर्कटिसिहाः कन्या तुजाऽ्य वृदिचककः । धम्बी मकर: कुम्भो मीनस्त्विति राशिनामानि ।३। कुम्भः कुम्भवरो नरोऽ्य मियुनं बीखागदामुन्तरों मोनो मोनयुतं धनुइव सघनुः पञ्चाच्छरीरो हयः । एर्तास्यो मकरः प्रदीपसहिता कन्या च नौरसंस्यता शेवो राक्षिणतः स्वनामसह्वशो पत्ते ठुलामृतुलाम् ।।

## DESCRIPTION OF THE 12 RASIS

3-4. The names of the 12 Rasis are : Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius and Pisces. The sign Aquarius resembles a man holding a pot while Gemini is compared to a couple holding Veena (ी! ) and mace ( t ). A pair of fish is the imagination about Pisces. Sagittarius is compared to a man, having a hip like that of a horse and

holding an arch in his hands. Deer-faced sign is Capricorn. Virgo resembles a virgin sitting in a boat with a lamp in her hands. A male holding Scales in hand represents Libra. The other signs are identical with their names.

शीर्वास्यबाहुहृदयं जठरं कटिबस्तिमेहनोव्युगम् । जान् घज्चे चरखौ कालस्याङ्गानि राशयोऽजाद्याः ।५। कालनरस्यावयवान् पुरुषारां कल्पयेत्सवकाले । सदसङ्ग्रहसंयोगात् पुष्टान् सोपद्रवांश्चापि ।६।।

5-6. THE LIMBS OF KALA PURUSHA AND THEIR USE: The signs Aries etc., represent the limbs of Kalapurusha as under:

Aries : head; Taurus : face; Gemini : arms; Cancer : heart; Leo : stomach; Virgo : hip; Libra : space below navel and private parts; Scorplo: private parts; Sagittarius : thighs; Capricorn : knees; Aquarius : ankles; Pisces : feet. If at birth, one has benefic planet in a particular sign, the particular limb will be strong. A malefic makes such a limb weak.

Notes: While for Kalapurusha, the respective limbs are identified from Aries onwards, for an individual, apart from considering the Rasis as above, one should count from the natal lagna also. For example, suppose one is born in Leo. This sign indicates his head. Hence, when assessing the head of a Leo native, look at Leo and Aries as well.

मेवादीनां क्रियतावुरुजुतुमकुलीरलेयपायोनाः । सञ्ज्ञास्तु जूककौपिकतौक्षाकोकेरह्दयरोगान्त्याः ।।।

7. SYNONYMS OF RASIS : Krtya, Tavuru, Jutuma, Kulira, Leya, Pathona, Jook, Kaurpika, Tauksha, Akokera, Hridaya Roga, and Anthya are the other names given respectively to Rasis from Aries onwards.

Notes: Such other synonyms can be found in Brihat Jataka, Hora Sara, Hora Ratna, Phala Deepika etc..

ऋलं मवननामानि राशिः लेर्च बमेव वा १ उक्तानि पूर्वमुनिभिस्तुल्यार्चप्रतिपत्तये ।८।।

8. SYNONYMS FOR HOUSES: Riksha. (N), Rasi, Kshetra, Bha (w) and Bhavana are ulentical names given to a house by the sages.

हावस चक्रं भगरस्तस्यार्थे सिहतो रविर्गाधः । कर्कटकात् प्रतिनोमं शवी तचाम्येऽपि तत्स्थामात् ।।। भानोरचे विहर्गः शूरास्तेजस्विनञ्च साहसिकाः । सक्षिनो मृदवः सौम्याः सौभाग्ययुताः प्रजायन्ते ॥१०।

9-10. ZODIACAL HALVES, THEIR LORDS, AND EFFECTS: The Rasi chakra with 12 Rasis is called Bhagana ( ). Six signs counted from Leo to Capricorn consist of Solar Half and the Sun rules this \Psi #(Half Zodiac). Six signs in the reverse order from Cancer to Aquarlus is Lunar Half and ruled by the Moon. Barring Cancer and Leo, planets from Mars to Saturn get one Rasi each in each Half, as above. If at birth more planets occupy the Solar Half, the native is brilliant, and if more planets are in Lunar Half, he is soft, good and lucky.

Notes: If a majority of planets are placed from Leo to Capricorn, the native acquires the qualities of the Sun. Should a majority be disposed from Aquarius to Cancer (via Pisces), the native begets Moon’s disposition. The former makes one splendorous and valorous while the latter makes one soft-spoken and fortunate.

कजभुगुबुषेन्दुरविश्विसुतसितवविरार्यमन्दसनिजीवाः ।

## गृहपा नवभागानामजमूगतुलकर्कटाद्याक् ।११।

11. THE RULERS OF RASIS AND NAVAMASAS: Mars, Venus, Mercury, Moon, Sun, Mercury, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Saturn and Jupiter are the lords respectively of Aries to Pisces. The Navamsas for these Rasis

are calculated from Aries, Capricorn, Libra and Cancer in their order.

Notes: How to calculate the Navamsa positions is explained below:

Each Rasi has nine Navamsas of 3° 20' each. The counting for Aries, Leo and Sagittarlus is done from Aries itself. It is done from Capricorn for the signs Taurus, Virgo and Capricorn. For Gemini, Libra and Aquarius, the counting is commenced from Libra. The Navamsas of Cancer, Scorpio and Pisces range from Cancer.

Another method is to count from stellar quarters. Take the first 3 stars or 12 quarters and count from Arics and conclude with Pisces. Proceed in the same manner for each group of 3 stars again and again.

That is, Aswini-Bharani-Krittika form one group the Navamsas of which range from Aries to Pisces. Then again proceed in the same way for Rohini-Mrigastra-Aridra group and for other groups.

भक्षनादिर्षः समस्तं जातकविहितं विचिन्तयेन्मतिमान् । एभिविना न वाक्यं पदमपि गन्तुं महाशास्त्रे ।१२।।

12. SUB-DIVISIONS ESSENTIAL: The effects of a horoscope should be predicted according to the divisions of houses. Without knowing the strength of such lords, one cannot lay even a step forward in the direction of astrology.

बर्गोत्तमा नवांशास्तथा दिमष्यान्तगाद्चराद्येषु । . सुती कलमुख्यकरा द्वादश भागाः स्वराद्याद्याः ।।१३।।

13. VARGOTHAMAMSA: The first Navamsa of a Movable Rasi, the 5th one in a Fixed Rasi and the 9th one in a Common Rasi (dual sign) are called Vargothamamsas. Should the natal Lagna be in such Vargothama Navamsa, the native becomes an important person in his circle. The rulers of Dwadasamasas start from that Rasi itself.

Notes: The following are 12 Vargothama Navamsas:

Dwadasamsas: Each Rasi is divided into 12 Dwadasamsas of 2° 30' each. The first one falls in that Rasi. itself, the second one in the second from there, the third one in the third from there and so on and so forth. These are tabulated on page No. 19.

स्वर्वसुतनवममेशा प्रेक्काखानां क्रमाच्च होरारणाम् ।

रविच्त्राविन्दुरवी विषमसमेष्वर्ष राशौनाम् ॥।१४।

14. LORDS OF DECANATES AND HORAS: The lords of decanates are: First decanate—lord of the same Rasi, second decanate—lord of the 5th Rasi therefrom and third decanate—lord of the 9th from the Rasi in question. The first Hora of an odd Rasi belongs to the Sun while the second one is ruled by the Moon. In the 'case of an even Rasi, the first Hora is ruled by the Moon and the second Hora by the Sun.

Notes: Decanates: Each Rasi is made in three equal parts of 10° each. The three are respectively lorded by the Ist, 5th and 9th lords therefrom, See Table on page No. 20.

HORAS: Each Rasi is divided in two equal parts of 15°. The first Hora of an odd Rasi is ruled by the Sun and the second by the Moon. In the case of even Rasis, the first and second Horas are respectively ruled by the Moon and the Sun. See the Table below:

| Libra       |       | Sun         | Moon         |

| Sagittarius |       | Sun         | Moon         |

| Aquarius    |       | Sun         | Moon         |

Assume a planet is in 17° of Virgo, which is an even Rast. It is the second Hora ruled by the Sun.

शरपञ्चाष्टमुनोन्तरियभागास्त्र्यांशकास्तु विषमेषु । युर्मेवृत्करमयच्याः कुजाकिजीवतधुकाराम् ॥१५।।

15. LORDS OF TRIMSAMSAS:

In odd signs, 5, 5, 8, 7 and 5 degrees are Trimsamsas of Mars, Saturn, Jupiter, Mercury and Venus respectively while the arrangement is reverse in even signs.

Notes: The Sun and the Moon have no lordship over Trimsamsa division. Now see the next table:

## TABLE OF TRIMSAMSAS

|               | Odd Signs |         |  |               | Even Signs |
|---------------|-----------|---------|--|---------------|------------|
| upto 5º       |           | Mars    |  | upto 5º       | Venus      |
| 5° 1' to 10°  |           | Saturn  |  | 5° 1' 12°     | Mercury    |
| 10°,1' to 18° |           | Jupiter |  | 12° 1' to 20° | Jupiter    |
| 18° 1' to 25° |           | Mercury |  | 20° 1' to 25° | Saturn     |
| 25° 1' to 30° |           | Venus   |  | 25° 1' to 30° | Mars       |

Suppose a planet is at 28° of an odd sign, then it falls in Venusian Trimsamsa. If it is in 8° of an even sign, then the Trimsamsa belongs to Mercury.

In the context of Female Horoscopy, these positions are very useful. See chapter 46 for details.

मेवालिमियुनमृगहरिमीनतुलावृषभचापधरकर्षो ।

घटधरकम्यापूर्वाः सप्तांशानां भवन्तीशाः ॥१६।।

16. LORDS OF SAPTAMSAS: The Saptamsas for the 12 Rasis from Aries onwards are respectively counted from Aries, Scorpio, Geminl, Capricorn, Leo, Pisces, Libra, Taurus, Sagittarius, Cancer, Aquarius and Virgo. The lords thereof are those in whose Rasis they fall.

Notes: The idea is that the counting of Saptamsa for odd signs starts from that sign itself. In the case of even Rasis, it is from the opposite house. For example, the counting of 1/7th Amsa starts from Leo itself for Leo, the second is Virgo, third is Libra and so on and so forth. For Cancer, an even Rasi, the counting is done from Capricorn. Each Rasi is made in seven equal parts to be called as Saptamsa. Each Saptamsa thus equals to  $4 ^ { \circ }$  17’ 8-l/2^{\*}.

Till now in the previous pages, we have had the following divisions:

1. Rasi 2. Hora 3. Drekkana 4. Navamsa 5. Dwadasamsa 6. Trimsamsa 7. Saptamamsa. There are other. nine essential divisions to complete the Shodasa Varga (16 Varga) scheme. Some are satisfied with 10 Var-

gas and yet some with 7 Vargas. For the information of the readers, the lacuna is completed as under:

(8) Turyamsa: Each sign is made in four equal parts of 7° 30' each. The first Turyamsa is lorded by the lord of the same Rasi, the second one by the lord of the 4th sign, the third one by the lord of the 7th sign and the 4th one by the lord of the 10th sign. For example, the lords of 4 Tuyramsas in Aries are respectively:

Mars (Aries), Moon (Cancer), Venus (Libra) and Saturn (Capricorn). Similarly, for other positions in various Rasis these may be noted.

(9) Dasamsa: Each Rasi is divided in 10 parts of 3° each. In odd signs, the counting is from the sign itself. For even signs, the counting is from the ninth thereof. For example, the Ist Dasamsa of Aries, is in Aries itself, the 2nd in Taurus, the 3rd in Gemini and so on and so forth. Take Scorplo an even Rasi.' Its first Dasamsa falls in Cancer which is the 9th Rasi from Scorpio, The second falls in Leo, the third in Virgo, the fourth in Libra and so on and so forth.

(10) Shodasamsa (i.e. 1/ 16th of a Rasl): This is also called Khalaamsa. Each Rasi is made in 16 equal parts i.e. 1-7/s degrees. The counting commences from Aries for Chara Rasis, from Leo for Sthira Rasis and from Sagittarius for Dwiswabhava Rasis. To give an example, in the case of movable signs, the first Shodasamsa is Aries, the 2nd is Taurus, and so on; the 13th is again Aries, the 14th is again Taurus the 15th is again Gemini and the last one, 16th is again Cancer. Similar calculations should be made for fixed and dual (common) signs accordingly.

(11) Vimsamsa: Each Rasi is made in 20 equal parts each of 1° 30'. The counting for movable signs commences from Aries, for dual signs from Sagittarius and for common signs from Leo. For example take a common sign, for which counting is to be done from Leo. The first Vimsamsa is in Leo, the second in Virgo, the 12th in Cancer, the 13th in Leo again and so on and so forth, with the 20th Vimsamsa

ending in Pisces. Similarly, for other signs, suitable deductions can be made.

(12) Chaturvimsamsa: Each Rasi is made in 24 equal parts. This is also called Siddhamsa. One such division is made up of 1° 15' of arc. For odd signs, the 24 divisions are counted from Leo while for even signs the counting is from Cancer. For example, the first Siddhamsa of Aries is Leo, the 2nd is Virgo, the 3rd is Libra, the 13th is again Leo, the 14th is Virgo while the 24th one is Cancer. In the case of Taurus, an even sign, the Ist one is Cancer, the 2nd one is Leo, the 3rd is Libra, the 13th is again Leo, the 14th is Virgo while the 24th one is Cancer. In the case of Taurus, an even sign, the 1st one is Gemini, the 13th is again Cancer, the 14th is again Leo while the 24th is again Gemini. The intermediary ones may be suitably understood accordingly.

(13) Nakshatramsa or Sapthavimsamsa: This is 1/27th part of a Rasi. The counting starts from the Rasi itself, whether it is an odd one or an even one. For example, for Aries, start from Arlcs and count upto Pisces, you get 12 Sapthavimsamsas. Then the 13th one is again Aries, the 14th is Taurus, the 24th is Pisces, the 25th is again Aries, the 26th one is Taurus and the 27th one is Gemini.

(14) Swa-Vedamsa: Each Rasi is made in 40 equal parts, each of 45' of arc. The counting for odd signs commences from Aries while Libra is the starting point for even signs. For example, for odd Rasis, we complete 3 rounds from Aries to Pisces to get 36 Swavedamsas. The 37th one is again Aries, the 38th one is Taurus, the 39th one is Gemini and the 40th one is Cancer. Similarly for even Rasis, starting from Libra, we complete 3 rounds upto Virgo to get 36 Swavedamsas. The 37th, 38th, 39th, and the 40th ones are respectively Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius and Capricorn.

(15) Akshavedamsas: Each Rasi is divided into 45 equal parts, each of 40' of arc. In the case of movable Rasis, the rulership is counted from Aries; from Leo in so far as fixed Rasis are concerned and from Sagittarius for

dual signs.

(16) Shashtiamsas: 30' of arc makes one Shashtiamsa and each Rasi has 60 such Shashtiamsas. In the case of an odd sign, the designations of Shashtiamsas under. The suffix "M" indicates that it is a are as malefic

Shashttamsa while “B" tells you that it is benefic.

For even signs, the counting is reverse. That is the 60th one of the odd Rasi will be the first one of even Rasi. The second one of an even Rasi is the same as the 59th one of an odd Rasi, and so on and so forth.

Saptavargas: These consist of Rasi, Hora, Decanate, Sapthamsa, Navamsa, Dvadasamsa and Trimsamsa.

Dasa Vargas: Khalamsa, Dasamsa and Shashtiamsa calculations are added to the above-mentioned Sapthavarga scheme, to get Dasavargas.

Shodasa Vargas: These are sixteen dtvisions, which I have explained in the previous pages.

The use of the said 16 vargas is explained below:

(1) Rasi Lagna (2) Hora (3) Decanate (4) Turyamsa (5) Sapthamsa (6) Navamsa (7) Dasamsa (8) Dwadasamsa (9) Khalamsa (10) Vimsamsa (11) Chaturvimsamsa (12) Nakshatramsa (13) Trimsamsa (14) Swavedamsa (15) Akshavedamsa (16) Shashtiamsa Appearance, shape, etc. Financial status Brothers and happiness from them Bhagya or prosperity Children and grand children Wife Profession Father and mother Conveyances Success Education Strength Malefic results Good and bad Good and bad Good and bad

वट्टिहोरा ब्यंशाः सोडुरदा सा हविसप्ततिसमेता ।

लिप्तानामट्टादशवतानि परिवर्तनेः स्वगृह्हात् ॥ १७।

17. The total of the number of Horas and that of decanates is sixty (24 + 36), in the entire Zodiac. The figure is increased by 72 if the total of Horas + Navamsas is required, i.e. 60 + 72 = 24 + 108 = 132. Each Rasi has 1800

Kalas (30 degrees each of 60' of arc).

लग्नादीनां लिष्ता क्षेयाः स्वगृह्तादिकर्यसंतुष्पिताः ।

श्र्नादशवात भक्ता लब्धः स्वादोष्सितो वर्यः ।१८।

18. DIFFERENT VARGAS: To know any kind of Varga (i.e. Hora, Navamsa, Drekkana etc.) adopt the following method: convert the longitude into minutes of arc and multiply by the Varga figure concerned. Divide the product by 1800. The resultant figure will reveal the required Varga.

Notes: Suppose a planet is in 15° of Aries and we want to know the Navamsa position. 15° converted into minutes becomes 900. This should be multiplied by 9 which is the figure for Navamsa. We get 8100. Divided by 1800, this yields 4 completed Navamsas with a remainder which indicates the 5th Navamsa. The factor of multiplication for Hora is 2, for decanate 3, for Dasamsa 10 and so on and so forth. These Vargas can be found from the tables already given earlier without undergoing this ordalium.

एतेर्षा गुरादोषान् विस्तरतो नहुजातके वक्ष्ये ।

एभिः स्पष्टतरं तत्प्रत्यक्षपरीक्षणं यस्मात् ॥११।

19. The malefic and benefic effects of such Vargas are explained in detall in the Nashta Jataka chapters (at the end).

प्रजावितः क्रू रशुभौ पुमांस्त्री चरः स्थिरो मिश्रतमुझ्ब हहाः । कुलीरमीनालिगृहान्तसन्धि वदन्ति गण्डान्तमिति प्रसिद्धम् ॥२०। जातो न जीवति नरो मातुरपथ्यो भवेत्स्वकुलहन्ता । यदि जीवति गण्डान्ते बहुयजतुर्यो भवेढ्रसूपः ॥२१५

20-21. RASIS—BENEFIC AND MALEFIC IN NATURE; GANDANTA AND ITS EFFECTS: From Aries onwards, alternatively the Rasis are known as malefic and benefic on the one hand and male and female on the other hand. These are also classified as chara (Movable), Sthira (Fixed or Im-

movable) and Dviswabhava (Ubhaya, dual or common) Rasis. The ending portions of Cancer, Scorplo and Pisces are called Gandanta. It is said that one born in Gandanta will not survive. He will either lose his mother or he will end the dynasty, ie. he is the last of his descent and will not have any children. If, however, he survives, he becomes a king with many elephants and horses.

Notes: The Rasis are classified as under:

| Aries       | odd  | male   |
|-------------|------|--------|
| Taurus      | even | female |
| Gemini      | odd  | male   |
| Cancer      | even | female |
| Leo         | odd  | male   |
| Virgo       | even | female |
| Libra       | odd  | male   |
| Scorpio     | even | female |
| Sagittarius | odd  | male   |
| Capricorn   | even | female |
| Aquarius    | odd  | male   |
| Pisces      | even | female |

Gandanta: For Gandanta, I have given elaborate notes in my translation of Horasara, vide chapter 5 which the reader may consult.

ऐन्रचादिषु परिवर्ते स्त्रत्यं त्रितयं त्रिभिस्तु मेवार्यः ।

एभिदिक्षु निवर्द्र्यात्रादि विकल्पयेत्कार्यम् ॥२२।।

22. DIRECTIONS OF RASIS: The four Rasis from Aries onwards indicate East, South, West and North, while the remaining Rasis repeat in the same way. A journey undertaken by a person towards the direction indicated the Lagna or the Moon (at the commencement of journey) yields fruitful results.

Notes According to the above rule, for example, if Aries is the Lagna at the commencement of a journey, or if the Moon be in Aries, there is success in the eastern

direction.

Now, the directions indicated in this Sloka are:

| Aries, Leo and Sagittarius  | East  |
|-----------------------------|-------|
| Taurus, Virgo and Capricorn | South |
| Gemini, Libra and Aquarius  | West  |
| Cancer, Scorpio and Pisces  | North |

According to Prasna Marga (chapter 2), the directions are divided among the zodiacal signs as under:

| Aries—Taurus       |   |  | East       |
|--------------------|---|--|------------|
| Cancer—Leo         |   |  | South      |
| Libra—Scorplo      |   |  | West       |
| Aquarius—Capricorn |   |  | North      |
| Gemini             |   |  | South-East |
| Virgo              | : |  | South-West |
| Sagittarius        | : |  | North-West |
| Pisces             |   |  | North-East |

Planetary rulerships are denoted in sloka 8 of the chapter 4 infra.

नरपञ्चुव््षिवकजलजा यथाक्रमं प्राग्दियादिगा बलिनः । निजि दिवसे सन्ध्यायां प्वः पुरुषो मृगालिककिक्षाः ।२३।

23. DIGBALA AND KALABALA: Human signs, quandruped signs, Scorpio and watery signs are respectively strong in the directions commencing from the east. Further during night time quardruped signs, day time human or biped signs and the second half of Capricorn, Scorplo, Cancer and Pisces are strong at the time of day break and or night fall.

Note: Digbala of signs: Each Rasi assumes strength when it happens to be a certain Bhava. East represents lagna, South represents the 10th house, West represents the 7th house (ie. the opposite of Lagna) while the 4th house (i.e. opposite of the 10th, the South) is the North.

The northern point is also called Nadir; the Western point is known as descendant and the southern point is

the zenith of the horoscope. The ascendant and the planets (from the Sun to Saturn) in their usual motion move from East to West via the North. That is their motion is anticlockwise. Rahu, Ketu and others (i.e. Mars to Saturn) when retrograde move from West to East via North i.e. clockwtse.

Human signs are: Gemini, Virgo, Libra, Aquarius and first half of Sagittarius.

Quadruped signs are: Aries, Taurus, Leo, second half of Sagittarius and first half of Capricorn.

Watery signs are: Cancer, Scorpio, Pisces and second half of Capricorn.

Keeta Rast or insect sign is Scorpio as per Garga and Mantreswara. But Parasara includes Cancer also. Horasara classifies the 3 watery signs as Kecta Rasi with particular emphasis on Scorplo. Now, the first part of the sloka can be well-conceived. Human signs are strong in the east or ascendant. Quadruped signs are strong in south or the 10th. Scorpio is strong in the west or the 7th while watery signs (excluding Scorpio) have strength in the north or the 4th. Similar view is found in Horasara of Prithuyasas (ch. 1, s. 12) and Jataka Parijatha (ch. I, S. 16-18). The Kalabala of Rasis is clear, as given in the text.

नन्तंबरा मियुनक्किम्गाजगोदवा

द्यश्रेष्ठका हरितुलालिघटान्त्यकन्याः । पृष्ठोदयाः समियुना मियुनं विहाय

शेषाः विरोभिर्वयन्त्युभयेन सीनः ॥२४॥।

24. DAY AND NIGHT SIGNS—SIRSHODAYA AND PR- ISHTODAYA SIGNS: Gemini, Cancer, Capricorn Aries, Taurus and Sagittarius are night signs. Leo, Libra, Scorpio, Aquarius, Pisces and Virgo are day signs. The signs Cancer, Capricorn, Aries, Sagittarius and Taurus are called Prishtodaya which rise with hind part. Sirshodaya signs (rising with head) are: Leo, Libra, Scorpio, Virgo, Aquarius and Gemini. The sign Pisces is Ubhayodaya i.e. that rises with both head and hind part).

Notes: The use of day signs and night signs is manifold in lost horoscopy. For details see my English translaton of NASHTA JATAKA.

श्रात्मीयनाचहट्टः सहितस्तेनेव ततिप्रवर्वापि । शाश्चिमुतजीवाम्यामपि राशिर्बलवान्न चेच्छेर्षः ।२५।।

25. STRENGTH OF RASI: If a Rasi is aspected by its lord, or by a planet that is friendly to its lord or by Mercury or by Jupiter, it is said to be strong. Planets other than the above do not lend strength by aspect.

तन्वर्य सह्जबान्धवपुत्रारिस्त्रीविनावपुष्यानि कर्मायव्ययभावा लग्नाद्या भावतक्षचन्त्याः ।२६।। शक्तिधन पौरुषगृहप्रतिभाव्र्यकामवेह्विवरार्षि । युद्मानभवव्यमिति कवितान्यपरार्गि नामानि ।२७१।

26-27. SYNONYMS OF 12 BHAVAS

| Lagna    | Thanu and Sakthi      |     |
|----------|-----------------------|-----|
| Second   | Artha and Dhana       |     |
| Third    | Sahaja and Parakrama  |     |
| Fourth   | Bandhava and Griha    |     |
| Fifth    | Putra and Pratibha    |     |
| Sixth    | Ari and Vrana         |     |
| Seventh  | Stri and Kama         |     |
| Eighth   | Vinasa and Dehavivara |     |
| Ninth    | Punya and Guru        |     |
| Tenth    | Karma and Mana (π)    |     |
| Eleventh | Aya and Bhava (       | व ) |
| Twelfth  | Vyaya                 |     |

Notes: The ascendant denotes the physique and vigour of the native. The second house is indicative of money while co-born and valour are to be noted from the 3rd. Residences and relatives are governed by the 4th. Children and intelligence are reflected by the 5th. Enemies and wounds come under the 6th. Sexual acts and spouse

fall under the 7th. The 8th house holds death. One’s past merits, preceptors and elders should be estimated from the 9th. Profession (work) and honour are related to the 10th. Income and prosperity are to be noted from the I 1th while the last house highlights expenses. For more detailed information, see pp 183-187 of Horosara.

संज्ञा वेश्माष्टमयोद्चतुरस्र व ताद्च नवमस्य । होरास्तदशजलानां चतुष्यं कण्टकं केन्द्रम् । २८।। नामानि चतुर्यस्य तु सुखजलपातालबन्धुहिबुकानि । कर्माज्ञामेषूररागगनार् कीर्यते दशमम् ॥२६।। धर्मंसुतयोस्त्रिकोणं सुतस्य घीस्त्रित्रिकोरामिति तपसः । च्नं जायास्तमयं जामित्रं सप्तमस्यास्याः ।।३०१। वट्कोणं रिपुभवनं तृतीयमय कीर्तयन्ति दुक्षिष्यम् । रिःकं द्वादशभवनं हितीयसंज्ञं कुटुम्बं च ।३१।। 28-31. ADDITIONAL SYNONYMS

The 4th (Vesma) and the 8th are called as Chaturasra houses. The 9th is known as Tapa ( a\Psi ) 1.e. penance. The 4th has names like Sukha (happiness), Jala (water), Patala (nether-world). Bandhu (relatives) and Hibuka. The other names given to the 10th house are: Karma (deed), Agna (command), Gagana (sky or zenith) and Meshurana. The 5th and 9th are trines. The 5th is the only house connected with Dhi (ie. intellect) while the 9th house is Tritrikona, i.e. the trine of trine. The 7th house is called Dyuna, Astha (setting), (Jaaya) (T1) and Jamitra. The 6th is Shatkona; the 3rd is Duschikya and the 12th Rippha. Kutumba is the 2nd house.

Notes: The word Jamitram |(ft%) is dertved Trom ( ) and is indicative of the future goodluck of one's wife. The word is obviously connected with the Greek word diametron.

Regarding trines, only the 5th and the 9th are strictly

trines while the ascendant is not included in this classification. Our author uses the words " f " to indicate the first, 5th and 9th houses separately in sloka 30 of this Chapter. Such separate mention for the ascendant can be noticed while the 5th and 9th are discussed, in Horasara and others. Also refer to Jataka Parijata (Ch. 1), Phal Deepika (1-17/18) and my English translations of Brihat Parasara Hora (Ch. 8, sloka 35) and Horasara (Ch. 1, sloka 33). Thus, we may know that the ascendant is not designated as a trine in any standard astral literature.

क्षेन्द्रात्परतः पखफरमापोकिलमसंज्ञितं तयोः परतः ।

क्षालयुवस्थविरत्वे कमेखा फलवा प्रहास्तेषु । ३२।।

32. KENDRA, APOKLIMA AND PANAPHARA-EFFECTS- THEREOF: The adjoining houses with reference to angles (Kendras) are Panaphara. The houses next to Panapharas are Apoklima houses. The planets in angles give effects in one’s boyhood. The effects of planets in Panapharas are felt in the middle age while the planets in Apoklima give result at the conclusion of the native's life.

Notes: Kendras are Ist, 4th, 7th and 10th houses. Panapharas (also called cadent houses) are the 2nd, 5th, 8th and 1 ith. Apoklima houses (succedent) are the 3rd, 6th, 9th and 12th houses. The good or bad effects are specially revealed in the boyhood, youth and old age according to their placement in angles, cadents or succedents.

षद्ववाभवदुदिचक्यान्युपचयसंज्ञानि

कीरत्यन्ते ।

स्वतनुमुखसुतास्ततपदिछ्रव्ययसंजितानि चान्यानि ॥ ३३।।

33. UPACHAYA AND ANUPACHAYA: The 6th, 10th, 11th and 3rd are called upachaya houses while the Ist, 2nd, 4th, 5th, 7th, 8th, 9th and 12th are Anupachaya houses.

Notes: The word U

is derived thus: # 1

सिहवृषमेषकन्याकार्मकम्त्तौलिकुम्भधराः । सुर्यादीनामेते त्रिकोखाभवनानि कथ्यन्ते ।३४।

34. MOOLATRIKONAS OF PLANETS: Leo, Taurus, Aries, Virgo. Sagittarius, Libra and Aquarius are the respective Moolatrikonas for the planets from the Sun onwards.

Notes: For more details, see Horasara, page 20.

सुर्यावीनामुच्चाः क्रियवृषमृगयुवतिकाकिम्मोनतुलाः । स्वोच्चगृहकथितभागा यथाक्रमेर्णव परम्मोच्वाः ॥३प्॥ विग्वह्लचष्टा विशतितिथिवारात्रिधनविशतयः स्वोच्चात्सप्तमराविनींचः स्यादंशकात्परमम् ॥३९१।

35-36. EXALTATION AND FALL: Aries, Taurus, Capricorn, Virgo, Cancer, Pisces and Libra respectively are the signs of exaltation for the Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn. These are deeply exalted at 10, 3, 28, 15, 5, 27 and 20 degrees in the respective Rasis. The Rasis opposite exaltation are the places of debilitation for the planets concerncd. And the respective degrees arc of deep fall.

Notes: Planets derive strength by virtue of exaltation and are weak if in all. Even if a planet is in exaltation in Rasi but in its fallen Navamsa, its effects are neutralized. Conversely though it may be in its debilitation Rasi, but in exaltation Navamsa, its good effects only incrcasc. Now, see the table of exaltation and fall for various planets:

| Planet  | Deep exaltation degree                                | Deep fall degree |
|---------|-------------------------------------------------------|------------------|
| Sun     | Aries 10º                                             | Libra 10º        |
| Moon    | Taurus 3º                                             | Scorpio 3º       |
| Mars    | Capricorn 28°                                         | Cancer 28°       |
| Mercury | \$\mathrm { V i r g o } ~ ~ 1 5 ^ { \mathrm { o } }\$ | Pisces 15°       |
| Jupiter | Cancer 5°                                             | Capricorn 5°     |
| Venus   | Pisces 27°                                            | Virgo.27°        |
| Saturn  | Libra 20°                                             | Aries 20º        |

The nodes are differently discussed in this context. Various schools have allotted differing Rasis as exaltation and debilitation zones for Rahu and Ketu. Without bothering oneself at this stage about the controversy, one can gather consolidated information from my notes given on pages 20-21 of Horasara.

हस्वास्तिमियोजघटा मियुनधनुकाकिमुगमुखाइ्च समाः । वृविविककन्यामृगपतिवखिजो दीर्धाः समास्याताः ।।३०१। एभिर्लग्नादिगर्तः शीर्वप्रभृतोनि बे शरीरार्ति । सहवानि विजायन्ते युलगगनचरंश्च तुल्यानि ॥३८।

37-38. LONG, MEDIUM AND SHORT ASCENSIONS OF SIGNS: Pisces, Taurus, Aries and Aquarius are signs of short ascension. Geminl, Sagittarius, Cancer, and Capricorn are of medium ascension. The long ascension signs are: Scorpio, Virgo, Leo and Libra. The head and other organs of the native will be similar to the sign ascending at birth and other relative signs. The planets occupying such houses will also influence the form and shape of the various organs.

Notes: If, for example, Scorpio ascends at birth, the head of the native will be long. Should Aries rise, the head is small. The influence lent by planets will also have a say on this matter. For significations of organs, see next chapter.

In Jataka Parijatha, Ch. I, Pisces is stated to be of medium ascension.

Also see Horasara, Ch. I, sloka 24.

भवनापिपदिङ्नाम प्लव इति यवनेः प्रयत्नतः कथितः । तत्त्वगो विनिहन्यादचिरेखा सहीपतिः वात्रून् ॥ ३८१।

39. FAVOURABLE DIRECTIONS FOR JOURNEYS: The direction ruled by the lord of a Rasi becomes Plava direction for that Rast. This is Yavana's school of thought. If the king undertakes his journey towards such direction

indicated by the Rasi concerned, he attains quick success.

Notes: The direction ruled by the planets are indicated in sloka 8, Ch. 4, infra. From the same, we can see, for example, Saturn rules west. If one proceeds to the west when Capricorn or Aquarius rises (or the Moon being so posited) he will attain victory effortlessly.

लोहित्सित्युक्हरिताः पाटलपरियू स्रपाण्डुचित्राश्च । कुष्कनकामपिष्धाः कर्बुर्बधा त्वजाविवराः स्युः ।४०। ज च्चोद्यचुह्वर्ता तदधिपतेः पूजिता प्रतिमा । हुन्ति समस्तान् शात्रूनिन्ध्वजिनोव बेवरिपून् ।४१।।

40-41. COLOURS OF RASIS: From Aries onwards the following 12 colours are allotted to the various Rasis respectively: red, white, green, pink, brown, grey, varlegated, black, golden, yellow, deep brown and white. If an idol of the lord of the ascendant is made in the colour alloted (as above) and is worshipped, the native will destroy his enemies just as Lord Indra destroyed the demons.

इति इह्याल्बरमेविरचितायां सारावल्यां राशिभेदो नाम तृतीयोऽध्यायः ।३।

Thus ends the 3rd chapter "Rast Description" of Kalayana Varma's Saravali.

## Chapter 4 थर प्रहद्ग्ययाऽध्यायः ।।।

## Planetary Characters

◦ क्यात्मा रविः शीतकरस्तु चेतः सर्वं घराजः श्विजोऽय वारपी । शुवःसितो ज्ञानशुले मदश्च राहुः वानिः कालनरस्य दुःखम् ॥१। श्यात्मादयो गगनर्गबँलिभिर्बलवत्तराः । हुर्बलर्बुर्बलास्ते तु विपरीतं धानेः फलम् ॥२।

1-2. KALAPURUSHA’S SOUL ETC. AND THEIR USE: The Sun is the soul of the zodiac personified, the Moon mind, Mars strength, Mercury speech (voice), Jupiter intelligence, Venus comforts, Rahu ego and Saturn grief. If at birth, planets indicative of soul etc., (as above) are strong, these significations will equally be strong. If these planets are weak, the effects are only meagre. But in the case of Saturn it is different.

ययायया लग्नम्हात्रयारणां समुद्रगमो मूरिविकल्पमानाच् । तयातया शेलनवादिसंध्याः ऋरमेख कालावयवाः प्रसुताः १३। उवितं तत्वणं लग्नात् वाममङ्गमथाबलम् । सव्यार्षावितरत्तस्य नोट्र्गतं सबलं च तत् ॥४।। मूर्यालोचनकर्णगन्धवहनं गण्डौ हनुश्वाननं प्रीवास्कन्चभुजं तु पारदर्वह्वयक्रोडानि नाभिः पुनः । बर्ताल्गुदे च मुष्कयुगलं चोरुह्यं जानुनी जर्धे पादयुयं विलग्नभवनत्रपंवेद्च त्रेषा क्रमात् ॥५ पापा वरणं लाञ्छनमेषु सौम्प्राः स्वांधे स्वराशावयवा स्थितास्ते । कुर्बन्ति जन्मोत्थितमेषु चिल्लमेव्यं प्रहास्तद्विपरीतसंस्थाः ॥६।। 3-6. The counting of limbs according to the decanate rising at birth should be identified as under:

If the first decanate rises, count from head; if the second decanate rises from neck and if the 3rd one rises, count from pelvis. The 6 Rasis backwards from the ascen-

dant denote the left side of the body while the 6 forward Rasis from Lagna indicate the right side of the body. Should the first decanate of a sign ascend the lapsed part of the ascendant denotes left side of the head and the part to rise yet indicates the right side of the head, the 2nd and 12th denote right and left eyes respectively; the 3rd and 11th right and left cars; the 4th and 10th right and left nose; the 5th and 9th right and left cheeks; the 6th and 8th right and lcft chins and the 7th left side of the face (by area yet to rise) and the 7th right side of the face (by portion already descended). If the 2nd decanate rises neck, shoulder, arms, side, heart, stomach, and navel are to be understood in the same order. Should thc third decanate rise, pelvis, anus, penis, testicle, thigh, knee, calf, and feet will be counted in the same order. The decanates occupied by malefics denote wounds etc., while the ones by benefics indicate marks (or moles) on the limbs conccrned. Should these planets be in their own Rasi or Amsa at birth, then the wound or mole are right from birth; otherwise these will be caused later on (in appropriate periods).

Notes: If the first decanate be rising, the limbs denoted are as under:

| Lagna     | – | head (right/left side) |
|-----------|---|------------------------|
| 2nd/12th  | — | eyes (right/left)      |
| 3rd/ 11th | — | ears (right/left)      |
| 4th/ 10th | - | nose (right/left side) |
| 5th/9th   | — | checks (right/left)    |
| 6th/8th   | — | chins (right/left)     |
| 7th       | - | face (right/ left)     |

In the case of 2nd decanate ascending, the counting is from neck, as under:

| Lagna     | — |                           | neck (right/left side)    |
|-----------|---|---------------------------|---------------------------|
| 2nd/12th  | — |                           | shoulder (right/left)     |
| 3rd/11th  | — |                           | arm (right/left)          |
| 4th/ 10th | - |                           |                           |
| 5th/9th   | - | 1 heart (right/left side) |                           |
| 6th/8th   | – |                           | stomach (right/left side) |
| 7th       |   |                           | navel (right/left side)   |

If the 3rd decanate ascends, then the arrangement takes place from pelvis as under:

| Lagna     |  | — | pelvis (right/left)      |
|-----------|--|---|--------------------------|
| 2nd/12th  |  | — | annus/penis (right/left) |
| 3rd/11th  |  | — | testicle (right/left)    |
| 4th/ 10th |  | - | thigh (right/left)       |
| 5th/9th   |  | — | knee (right/left)        |
| 6th/8th   |  | — | calf (right/left)        |
| 7th       |  |   | feet (right/left)        |

If a decanate is occupied by a malefic, say, Mars, there will be wound etc., on the limb. A benefic denotes mole. Should this be in a Rasi/Amsa owned by it, then the effect is from birth itself. If not, this will occur in its dasa bhukti later on.

राजा रविः शत्रपरस्तु बुष कुमारः सेनापतिः क्षितिमुतः तचिवौ सितेज्यौ। मृत्यस्तयोर्व रविजः सबला नराख्यां कुर्वन्ति जन्मसमये निजमेव "रूपम् IIII

7. PLANETARY PORTFOLIOS AND THEIR USE: The Sun and the Moon are said to be the kings. Mercury is the prince, Mars army chief, Jupiter and Venus ministers while Saturn is scrvant. The native acquires such qualities as of the strongest planet.

भानुः शुक्रः क्षमापुत्रः संहिकेयः वनिः सशी । सोम्यस्त्रदक्षमन्त्री च प्राच्याविदिगघीव्वराः ॥चII

8. PLANETARY DIRECTIONS: The Sun, Venus, Mars, Rahu, Saturn, the Moon, Mercury and Jupiter are respectively the lords of the directions commencing from the east.

Notes: The planetary rulerships of the 8 quarters are clarified below:

1. The Sun-East 2. Saturn-West 3. Venus-South-east 4. The Moon-North-west 5. Mars-South 6. Mercury-North

7. Rahu-South-west 8. Jupiter-North-east.

The directions are related to the zodiacal signs also vide sloka 22, Ch. 3, supra.

गुरुबुचचुकाः सौम्याः सौरिकुजार्का निपार्गतः पापाः । क्षक्षिजोऽधुभसंयुक्तः क्षीराञ्च निशाकरः पापः NEI

9. PLANETARY NATURE: Jupiter, Mcrcury and Vcnus are benefics in nature. Saturn, Mars and the Sun are natural malefics. Mercury in the company of malefics and the Moon of decreasing are also considered malefics.

Notes: Thus, Jupiter and Venus are the only natural benefics. Mercury, if not in the company of malefics, remains a natural benefic. Otherwise, if he gets assoclated with natural malefics, he turns into a malefic. As regards Moon, she is benefic in the bright half and a malefic in dark half. According to some, she is good from bright half Dasamt to Dark half Panchami (Sce also sloka 16, Ch. 5, infra).

Apart from natural benefics and natural malefics, planets are good or bad according to the sign rising at birth. For details, see my English translation of Brthat Parasara Hora, (Ch. 34).

हेलिर्भानुः शाशो चन्दः क्रूरहक् क्षितिनन्दनः । श्रारो रक्तस्तथा वक्रो हेम्नो विङ्ग ज्ञोऽय बोषनः ॥१० ईड्येत्याव्ड्भिरा जीवो ह्यास्फुजिच्च सितो मृगुः । बन्दः कोखो यमः कुष्यो विद्यावन्यानि लोकतः ॥११।

10-11. SYNONYMS OF PLANETS: Heli and Bhanu are the other names given to the Sun. The Moon is called Sasi. Krooradrik, Bhomisuta, Aara ( ¶\_1). Rakta, and Vakra are indicative of Mars. Hemna, Vid, Gna and Bodhana denote Mercury. Eedya (), Ijya (q), Angira and Jiva all the Jupiter's names. Aasphujit, Sita ( fe) and Bhrigu means Venus. Manda, Kona, Yama and Krishna are Saturn's synonyms. For these planets, there are various other

names which should be elicited from other texts.

Notes: There are many other names with which the planets are referred to in traditional astrological literature. The curious reader may consult notes given under sloka 41, Ch. 2 of my English version of Horasara.

ता स्रसितारुराहरितकनीतविचित्रासिता ह्नादीमाम् ।

पतयः ।१२।

पावकजलगुह्केराववाङ्वाची वेघराः श्र्काविग्रहर्दवतमन्त्रेः \_\_\_ संग्पूज्य तामाचाम् । कनकगजवाहनादीन् प्राप्नोति गतोऽरितः शीघ्रम् ।।१३।

12-13. PLANETARY COLOURS, DEITIES AND THEIR USE: From the Sun onwards, the respective hues of the planets are: copper-red, white, blood red, green, yellow, variegated, and black. The deities for these planets are respectively: Fire, Water, Kartikeya, Vishnu, Indra, Sachi Devi and Brahma. The planets from the Sun onwards should be worshipped by the Mantras concerned and if trips to the respective directions are undertaken, one can gain gold, gems, elephants etc.

Notes: The colours described can be noted from Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra (Ch. 1), Brihat Jataka (Ch. II), Horasara (Ch. 2), Jataka Parijatha (Ch. II), Prasna Marga (Ch. 16), Phala Deepika (Ch. Il) etc.

The planetary directions are given in sloka 8, of this chapter. If one undertakes a journey to the directions concerned after worshipping a planet, he will stand to gain. For example, one wants go to the north-east; he must worship the planet Jupiter (and also Indra) before proceeding. Then, he can achieve his aim unhindered.

स्त्रीरां चन्त्रक्सितो नपुंसकपती सोमात्मजार्कात्मजो पुंसां जीवविवाकरक्षितिसुता, विप्रस्य चुक्रोऽङ्कराः । राज्ञां सूर्यकुजौ विज्ञां व्घरो मिश्रस्य मन्दो बुबः शुद्राख्यां विलिमुखतोयमस्तां भौमादयः कोतिताः ॥१४।।

14. SEX, CASTE AND ELEMENTS: The Moon and Venus are females. Mercury and Saturn rule neutrals (1.e. these two are neutrals). Males are Jupiter, the Sun and Mars. Brahmins are ruled by Jupiter and Venus. The Sun and Mars preside over the royal lot. The Moon rules Vaisyas (business community). Saturn rules those of mixed breed while Mercury presides over Sudras, (the 4th caste people). The five primordial compounds, viz. Fire, Earth, Ether, Water and Air are respectively ruled by planets from Mars onwards.

कदुलवखातिक्तमिश्रितमधुराम्लकवायरस विशेषात्ताम् ।

सुरतोया मिन्निविहारार्यश्यनपांसुत्करह्तां T 1 0

15. PLANETARY TASTES AND ABODES: The planets from the Sun on are attributed tastes as under:

acrid, saline, bitter, mixed, sweet, sour (aciduous) and astringent. Thelr abodcs respectively are: temples, waterresorts, fire houses, sports ground, treasury, bed-room and dirty places.

Notes: The planets from the Sun to Saturn, leaving Mercury have been allotted individual Rasas or tastes while Mercury reveals a mixture of all the six tastes.

For planetary abodes, aiso see Sarvartha Chintamani, Ch. 1, sloka 80.

वस्त्रारां स्प् नाहतशिखिजलहतमध्यहृढसुजीर्रानाम् ।

ता त्रमरिपिहेम मिश्रितर्प्यकमुक्तावसां बाऽपि ।१६।

16. PLANETARY ROBES AND SUBSTANCES: From the Sun onwards, the apparels attributed are: coarse, new, burnt, water-soaked, neither new nor old i.e. recent, durable and a rag. Similarly, copper, gem, gold metal alloy, silver, pearl and tron are in their order denoted by the Sun etc., ending with Saturn.

Notes: Rahu is allotted a multi-coloured apparel while Ketu a much torn plece of cloth, see Sarawartha Chinta-

mani, Ch. 1. sloka 81. Similarly for Rahu lead and for Ketu sapphire is indicated by Venkatesa Daivagana.

स्रयनक्षरणदिवसर्तुकमारातवर्धशरदां दिनेवाद्याः ।

## विविरादीनामोवाः शनिसितभौमेन्दुबुषजीवाः ॥१७।

17. PERIODS AND RITUS OF PLANETS: Ayana ( वग ), Muhurtha, Ahoratra (day and night), Ritu (season), month, fortnight and year are respectively ruled by the planets from the Sun to Saturn. The seasons, viz. Sisira, Vasanta, Grishma, Varsha, Sarat, and Hemanta are respectively ruled by Saturn, Venus, Mars, Moon, Mercury and Jupiter.

Notes: Apart from Mars, the Sun also gets rulership of Grishma Ritu. These Ritus can be used in lost horoscopy also. For details, consult my English translation of Nashta Jataka.

## लग्ना थिपतेस्तुल्यः कालो लग्नोदितांशकसमास्यः । वक्तव्यो रिपुविजये गर्भेषु व कार्यसंयोगे ।१८ा।

18. USE OF THESE: In the matter of winning enemies, delivery of child or such questions, materialization of the objective concerned will be as per the period allotted to the Lagna Lord. This should be understood according to the ascendant passed.

Notes: Suppose Jupiter is the Lagna Lord in a query relating to child birth, with the Prasna Lagna having passed 10 degrees. Jupiter indicates the period month as per the previous sloka and hence one third of a month should pass for the delivery to take place. Such information can be elicited in regard to return of missing person etc. Rahu and Ketu have not bcen allotted such periods by Kalayana Varma in sloka 17, Ch. 4. Sarvartha Chintamani, however, denotes 8 months for Rahu and 3 months for Ketu, vide its chapter 3 sloka 90.

ऋस्वेदाधिपतिर्जीवो यजुवेवपतिः सितः । सामवेदाधिपो वत्रः शविजोऽयर्ववेदराट् ॥ १६। सुरपूज्यः शविश्चुक्रौ दिनकरभौमी बुधार्कजी नायाः । विबुधमनुष्यपितुर्तां तिर्यङ् नरकाधिवासानाम् ॥२०॥

19-20. LORDS OF VEDAS AND WORLDS: Jupiter rules Rig Veda, Venus Yajur Veda, Mars Sama Veda and Mercury Atharva Veda. Jupiter denotes the Heavens, the Moon and Venus the world of the Manes, the Sun and Mars the world of mortals and Mercury and Saturn hell.

स्वल्पाकुञ्वतमूर्धजः पटुमतिर्मुस्यस्वरूपस्वनो नात्युच्चो मधुपिज्जचावनयनः शुरः प्रचण्डः स्थरः । रक्तद्यामतनुनिगूढबरखः पित्तास्थिसारो महान् गम्भीरव्चतुरलकः पृषुकरः कौसुम्भवासा रविः ।।२९।

21. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF THE SUN: The Sun has curly hair, sharp mind, prominent appearance, majestic voice, and is not very tall. His eyes are honeycoloured. He is courageous, steady, and of a complexion which has a mix of red and green. His feet are not conspicuous (i.e. small in size). He is bilious and has sturdy boncs. He is great and majestic. He has fiery rays and square body, and wears saffron coloured apparels.

Notes: The Sun has no hair on his head. He causes reddish clouds. He denotes tuberculosis, fever, physique, head, father, king etc. (Ref. Poorva Parasaryam.).

Mantreswara also describes the Sun as dealt with in various texts. He uses the words "qg g:” which indicate that the Sun has long hands. Kalyana Varma's words : have been rendered as fiery rays as also denotes fire and the rays (or hands). Similarly, Prithuyasas has used TEEKSHNAMSU in Horasara. The Sun has predominance of Satwa. He is said not to live in one place for long. His weakness causes damage to eyes.

If in a nativity, a satwtk planet is strong, he will have kindness, firmness, truthfulness, softness and respect for Gods and the wise. If Rajasik planet is strong, he will be skilful in poetry, literature, fine arts, sacrifices (religious), will have interest in the fair sex and be valorous. If Tamasik planet is strong, he will be short-tempered, foolish, indolent, and long-sleeping. The Sun, the Moon and Jupiter are Satvic planets. Mercury and Venus are Rajasic planets. Mars, Saturn and Rahu are Tamasic planets. These descriptions are found in our present work and in various other texts, in many contexts. Now, the reader can easily understand these.

सौम्यः कान्तविलोचनो मधुरवाग्गौरः हुन्याङ्जो युवा

प्रांधुः सुक्ष्मनिकुल्न्वतासितकचः प्राज्ञो मृदुः सात्रिवकः । चार्वातकफात्मकः प्रियसखो रक्तकसारो घरी

बृद्धस्त्रीषु रतञ्चलोऽतिसुभगः शुद्राम्बर्बन्ध्रमा ।२२।

22. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF THE MOON: She has an auspicious appearance. Her eyes are beautiful. She has mellifluous voice. She is fair in complexion, has an emaciated body, is youthful, tall, curly and short hair, is learned, soft, of Satwik disposition, beautiful, phlegmatic, amicable to friends, has pure (excellent) blood, is kind, fond of old ladies, fickieminded, very fortunate, and wears clean apparels.

Notes: The Moon is a cold body in the planetary scheme, is black in complexion( g), has a round body, is windy and phlegmatic, peaceful, soft-spoken, auspicious in sight, indicates queen, mother, fare, water-born products, mind, flowers, salt, palatable articles, lead, pearl, zinc, ram etc. She drinks milk and water: (Ref Poorva Parasaryam).

The Moon does not have much hair. She is more phlegmatic than windy. She has a mix of both old age and youth, Phala Deepika).

ह्वस्वः पिज्जत तोचनो हृडवपुर्दोप्ताम्निकान्तिश्चलो मज्जावानरुताम्बरः पद्रुतरः शूरङ्च निरन्नवाक् । हिल्नः कुञ्चतबीप्तकेवातर्णः पित्तात्मकस्तामस-

ब्चण्डः साहतिकोऽपि घातकुवालः संररतगौरः कुजाः ।२३।

23. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF MARS: The planet Mars is short; his eyes are reddish-brown in colour. Hc has a strong body. He is as bright as burning fire. He is not steady in disposition. He has marrow in bones and flesh. He wears red-coloured apparels. he is more intelligent and is courageous. He is an accomplished speaker (faषव). He causes injury. He has short and shining hair. He is billous in disposition and is Tamasic. He is adventurous and wrathful and is skilful in hurting. He is blood-red in appearance.

Notes: POORVA PARASARYAM states that Mars has crucl sight ( ), is bilious, liberal, lean and thick red. He lives through weapons. He is blind and is a poet. He rulcs ncck. Hc indicates an army head, younger brothers, darkness, forts, vomitting, eating warm food, itches, wounds, accidents and diseases caused by fire etc., kingdom, wheat, gold, coral, abdomen, lizard, ass, cat, tiger and such other violent animals, dog, poverty, tale-bearing, injuring, hatred etc.

Mars has slender waist. He has attractive limbs. (Ref.

Jataka Parijata, Ch. II, sloka 55.)

He has rough voice and a depressed belly. He is modest. Vide Horasara, p. 24).

Varaha Mihira in his Brihat Jataka, Ch. Il states that the planet Mars is which is denoted to be Asubha Drishti or inauspicious aspect by the Tameus eommentary Dasadhyayi.

रक्तान्तायतलोचनो मधुरवागदूर्वादतव्यामल-

स्त्वक्तारोऽतिरजोधिकः स्फुटवचाः स्फोतस्त्रिदोषा त्मक ।

हुष्टो मध्यमल्पबान्मुनिपुखो वृत्तः शिराभिस्ततः

## सर्वस्यानुकरोति वेषवचरनः पालादावाता बुवः ।२४।।

24. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF MERCURY: Mercury’s cyes are reddish and broad. Hc spcaks swectly. His complexion is of green grass. He has strong skin. He is an extremely Rajasic planet. He is clear in his speech and is i pure. He has a mixture of the three humours, i.e. phlegm, bile and wind. He remains delighted and has moderate beauty. He is skilful, and sinewy. He is agreeable to all in dressing and specch and wears green robes.

Notes: Mercury is learned, emaciated, Valsya (business community), has a mix of the three humours, and is a commercial planet. He is young and is a mathematician. His disposition is to be jovial. He indicates drawing, fine arts, maternal grand-mother, friendship, relatives, heart, pearloyster, Rajasic disposition, delirium fever, forgetfulness, diseases of various kinds and mixed eatables. He rules skin and has reddish, large eyes. He is of medium staturc, somctimes firm and sometimes unsteady. He speaks sweetly but stammers. He is adulterous and does not possess firm valour. He is truthful. This is how Mercury is described in Horasara, Sarvartha Chintamani, Phala Deepika and Poorva Parasaryam.

ईवत्पिङ्गललोचनः श्रुतिधरः सिहाच्छनादः स्थिरः

स्वाढचः सुविशुद्धकाञ्चनवपुः पीनोन्नतोरः स्थलः । शाश्वङ्ग घर्मरतो विनीतनिपुखो बद्धोत्कटाक्षः क्षमो

स्यात्पीताम्बर नृत्कफात्मकतनुमेदः प्रधानो गुरुः ॥२५।।

25. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF JUPITER: The eyes of Jupiter are somewhat reddish-brown. He is learned in Vedas. His voice resembles that of a lion. He is firm and prominently Satwic. His physical complexion is akin to pure yellow metal. He has broad and prominent chest. He is always fond of virtues and is modest. He has firm and large eyes and is of forgiving disposition. He wears yellow

apparels, is phlegmatic, fat and pre-eminent.

Notes: " %z1” has been interpreted by some commentators as “wearing excellent rosaries”. This view cannot be subscribed to, while I have rendered the same as having firm and large eyes. See¶ব= firm,\epsilon= large and #% = eyes.

Jupiter has large eyes and hence his aspect on or occupation of the 2nd house confers large and beautiful eyes.

He has large belly. He is a minister, has sharp mind, and is gold-coloured. He denotes abdomen, wisdom, work, hearing and auspiciousness. He consumes honey, ghee and sweet food. He is virtuous and meritorious and is an exponent of mythology. He indicates happiness, Vodic rituals, Sacrifices etc. He rules Brahmins, sages and ascetics and possesses empirical disposition. (Vide Poorva Parasaryam).

चार्वीर्घमुजः प्यूर्वदनः शुभ्राधकः कारतमान् इष्याकुल्चितसृक्षमलम्बितकचो दूर्वाङ्कुरद्यामलः । कामी वातकफात्मकोऽतिसुभगदिचत्राम्रो राजसो लीलाबान्मतिमान्विधालनयनः स्थूलासदेशः सितः ।२९।

26. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF VENUS: The planet Venus is beautiful, has long hands, broad chest and face, is very seminal, splendorous, has dark, short and extended hair, is of complexion which is a mix of yellow and green, is sensuous, windy and phlegmatic, very fortunate, wears multi-coloured apparels, is of Rajasic disposition, sportive, intelligent, broad-eyed, and has prominent shoulders.

पिङ्गो विम्यविलोच्यः कुन्त्युर्वोरधः सिरालोऽ्नसः हव्लाङ्गः पवनात्मकोऽतिपिच्युनः स्माम्याततो निर्षु राः । सुर्जः स्प्ननक्षद्विचोऽतिशतियो कक्षोऽध्यचिस्तामतो रोहः कोवपरो अरापरिस्तः कुब्जाम्बरो मास्करिः ।२७। 27. DESCRIPTION AND NATURE OF SATURN: Saturn

has red and depressed eyes, emaciated body, prominent veins, is indolent, black-bodied, and windy in temperament. He is a tale-bearer. He is muscular, unkind, foolish, has large nails and teeth, is very dirty, impure, Tamasic in disposition, flerce, short-tempered and old (worn out). He adores black apparels.

Notes: He is cruel, lame and is indicator of misery, longevity, livelihood, death, disputes, windy diseases, indolence, devils, base men, untouchables, sins, and legs. He is a eunuch.

The present verse is taken verbatim by Mantreswara for his Phala Deepika, (except for minor replacement), vide Ch. II, sloka 14.

Since our author has described the nodes, please find following addition:

RAHU: He is of thievish disposition and belongs to a base caste. He is black in complexion, and denotes fits, fear from snakes, leprosy, loose motions, small-pox, tastelessness of food, worm infections, elephants, death and weaith.

KETU: He indicates poisonous creatures, monkeys, rats, animals, poverty, snakes, evil spirits, wounds, fits etc. He eats insipid food. He also denotes debts, foolishness, illusion etc. He is a spiritual planet. Also see Phala Deepika, Ch. II, slokas 33 and 34 for more information on the nodes.

Now, we have to understand the use of these planetary descriptions, qualities etc., as explained in the foregoing slokas. If a planet influences the ascendant, its lord, its occupant, Moon sign lord, a bhava lord etc., the sphere concerned changes accordingly. For example, if the 7th lord is influenced by Venus, the wife of the native acquires the qualities ascribed to Venus. She may be beautiful, be of Rajasic disposition, intelligent, fortunate etc. etc. If the ascendant or its lord comes in contact with the Sun the native may be baldy, courageous, may like saffron apparels, may have sturdy bones, small feet, and be susceptible to T.B., fever etc. Simllar inferences may be suitably made by the wise.

मित्रारि सूर्याङ्गुरुभौमचन्द्राः सूर्येन्दुपुत्री रविचन्द्रजीबाः । मानुः सञ्चुकः शशिसुर्यभीमा मन्देन्दुजौ चुक्रबुधौ क्रमेख ।२८II धुकार्कजौ चन्द्रम पो न कदिवत्सौम्यः वाली चुक्बुधौ रवीन्दू । सोमार्यवका रवितस्त्वमित्रा मित्रारिशेषो न सुत्न्न क्षत्रुः ।२६।।

AND

28-29. NATURAL FRIENDSHIP, NEUTRALITY ENMITY BETWEEN PLANETS: The Sun’s friends are Jupiter, Mars and the Moon. The Moon's friends are the Sun and Mercury. The Sun, the Moon and Jupiter are friendly to Mars. Venus and the Sun are Mercury’s friends. The Sun, Moon and Mars are friendly disposed to Jupiter. Saturn and Mercury are friends of Venus. Mercury and Venus ai . Saturn’s friends. The Sun's enemies are Venus and Saturn. The Moon has no enemies. Mercury is inimical to Mars while the Moon is Mercury’s enemy. Venus and Mercury are the encmies of Jupiter. The Moon and the Sun are enemies of Venus. Saturn’s enemies are the Sun, the Moon and Mars. If a planet is neither a friend nor an enemy of another one, there exists neutrality among them.

व्ययाम्युचनलायेषु तृतीये सुह्दः स्थिताः ।

सत्कातरिपबः षव्यसप्तार्ष्टकत्रिकोर्णगाः ।३०।।

BE- 30. TEMPORARY FRIENDSHIP AND ENMITY TWEEN PLANETS: If a planet is in the 12th, 4th, 2nd, 10th, 11 th and 3rd from another planet, then there is temporary friendship between the two. If the position is in the 6th, -7th, 8th, Ist, 5th, or the 9th, then there is temporary enmity among them. (This may be applied to a birth horoscope, horary horoscope or any such other context).

हिमे-सम-रिपुसंत्ञा ये निसर्गान्निकर्क्ता

हिततम हित मध्यास्तेऽपि तत्काजमित्रः।

दिपुरामसुद्वास्याः सृतिकाले प्रहेनत्रा श्रघिरिपुरियुमध्याः वात्रुभिशिचन्तनीयाः ॥३१।।

31. FIVE KINDS OF CONSIDERATIONS OF RELATION- SHIP: (a) Should there be temporary friendship and natural friendship between two planets, then they become cxtremely friendly(gfufgx),(b)If there is natural neutrality and temporary friendship, there cxists only friendship between two planets. (c) If there is natural enmity and temporary friendship, they are neutral in relationship. (d) Should they be naturally neutral and temporarily inimical, they are only inimical. (e) If there is enmity both naturally and temporarily, then they are greatly inimical to each other.

Notes: These rules are simple and need no clarification. However, an example is given thus: Suppose the Sun is in Taurus and Jupiter is in Cancer. They are mutually in 3rd and 11th. Hence, they are temporary friends in the nativity. They are by nature friendly to each other. Hence on account of these two sources of friendship, they become mutually extreme friends as far as the concerned horoscope is concerned. Now, see point (e) above. The Sun and Saturn are mutually inimical. If these two are mutually in 6/8 positions in a chart, they act in a highly inimical manner for each other.

संपद्यन्ति स्यानात् सदा प्रहाद्वर्णवृद्धितः सर्वे । त्रिवशत्रिकोर्णवतुरस्नसप्तमानि च फलं क्रमेर्णवम् ।।३२। पूर्णं पत्यति रविजस्त्तीयववामं त्रिकोर्तमपि जीवः । चतुरसं मूमिसुतो द्यूनं सर्वे ग्रहाः क्रमवः ।३३।

32-33. PLANETARY ASPECTS: Plancts lend 1/4th aspect on the 3rd and 10th, half aspect on the 4th and 8th, 3/4 aspect on 5th and 9th and full aspect on the 7th, from their positions. Saturn has special full aspect on the 3rd and 10th, Juplter on the 5th and the 9th and Mars on the 4th and 8th. All planets aspect the 7th fully.

दिव्स्यानकालचेष्टाङतं बलं सर्वनिर्णयविधाने । वक्ये चतुःमकारं प्रहर्तु रिक्तो भवेद्बलः । इडा।

34. PLANETARY STRENGTHS: To assess the auspicious and inauspicious effects of a planet, its directional, positional, temporal and motional strengths are important; for, without these, it is considered to be weak.

लग्ने जीव-बुधौ दिवाकर-कुजी व्योम्नि रक्रे भास्करि-

बन्धाविन्दुसितौ विशाहतमिदं स्वोच्चे स्वकोणे स्वभे । मित्रस्वांवकसंस्थितः शुभफर्लहेष्टो बलीयान् ग्रहः स्त्रीक्षत्रे वाविभार्गवौ नरगुहे शेषा बले स्थानजे ।३५॥।

35. DIRECTIONAL AND POSITIONAL STRENGTHS: Jupiter and Mercury have directional strength while in the ascendant (ie. East), the Sun and Mars in the 10th (ie. South), Saturn in the 7th (i.e. West) and the Moon and Venus in the 4th (i.e. North). If a planet is in exaltation, Mulatrikona, own house, friendly house or own Navamsa and aspected by a benefic, it assumes strength. The Moon and Venus are strong in even Rasis while the rest are so in odd Rasis. This is called positional strength.

Notes: Digbala or directional strength is found thus: Take the case of Jupiter. He gets full strength, under this heading, of 60 shashtlamsas, if he is on the cusp of the ascendant. If he is on the seventh cusp (ie. 7th Bhava Madhya), his Digbala is nil. In other places, it should be worked out by rule of three process.

To speak deeply, Sthana Bala or positional strength is made up of:

(a) Uchcha Bala (exaltation strength) (b) Sapta Vargaja Bala (c) Ojayugma Rasyamsa Bala (i.e. strength on account of odd and even Rasis and such Navamsas) (d) Kendra Bala (angles) (e) Drekkana Bala (Decanates)

The five kinds of strength denote the positional strength of a planet, which from part of shadbala. For fuller information, please see Ch. 27 and Ch. 28 of my English version of BRIHAT PARASARA HORA SASTRA.

श्रीबार्कास्फुजितोऽद्धि विच्च सततं मन्देन्दुभौमा निश्षि होरामासदिनाब्दपादव बलिनः सौम्याः सितेऽन्येऽतिते । संग्रामे जयिनो विलोमगतयः संपूर्णगावो ग्रहाः

सुर्येन्दू पुनरुत्तरेखा बनिनो सत्योक्तचेष्टाबले ॥ ३११।

36. TEMPORAL AND MOTIONAL STRENGTHS: The Sun, Jupiter and Venus acquire strength during day time while Saturn, the Moon and Mars become strong during night time. Mercury acquires vigour at all times. The Horabala goes to Hora lord, masa bala (मIW = month) to the lord of the month, Dina bala to the lord of the week day of birth and Abda (year) bala is credited to the lord of the year of birth. Malefics get strength in Dark Half while benefics become strong in Bright Half. The planet that is victorious in war, that is in its retrograde motion, or that is not combust become strong. The luminaries get strength in northern course. This is how Satyacharya detailed motional strength.

Notes: Each day from one Sun rise to the next one is divided into 24 horas (or hours). The planet ruling the hour of birth is called Hora 'lord and gets 60 shashtiamsas. The first hora of the day is ruled by the lord of the week day itself. For example, Mars rules the opening hora of Tuesday. The second one is ruled by the lord of the sixth week day. The third one is ruled by lord of the sixth week day counted from the second hora lord. Still an easy method is suggested here, so that the reader need not memorise or make cumbersome calculations. Just note the first hora lord and count every alternative week day lord backwards. You get the hora lord successively. For example, Saturday has first hora ruled by Saturn, second by Jupiter, third by Mars, fourth by the Sun and so on and so forth. And calculations of Kala Hora should be done with reference to mean ocal time.

उत्तरमयनं प्राप्ताः चुक्रकुजार्केन्द्रमन्त्रिस्तो बलिनः ।

याम्यं शशिरविपुत्रौ इयेऽपि शविजः स्ववर्गस्थः ॥३७।

37. AYANA BALA: Venus, Mars, the Sun and Jupiter are strong in Uttarayana while the Moon and Saturn are strong in Dakshinayana. Mercury is strong in both the cases. This is called Ayana Bala.

पुंस्त्रीनपुंसकास्याः क्षेत्रेष्वाद्यन्तमध्यसंप्राप्ताः ।

## सूर्यान्निर्गत्य सदा नवोविता यवनराजमतम् ॥३८।।

38. DREKKANA BALA: Male planets are strong in the first decanate, female planets in the third decanate and neutral planets in the second decanate of a Rasi. Planets are strong only when they are away from the Sun, as per Yavanas.

प्राप्रात्रिभागेऽतिबलः शशाङ्कः चुक्रो निशाधड्वनिज्ञो निशान्ते । प्रातर्गुषो मध्यविने च सूर्यः सर्वत्र जोवोऽर्कसुतो दिनान्ते ॥ ३ह।।

39. TRIBHAGA BALA OR TRIAMSA BALA: The Moon, Venus and Mars get this strength in the first, second and third parts of the night respectively. This is similarly enjoyed by Mercury, the Sun and Saturn in day time while Jupiter has it always irrespective of day or night.

Notes: (a) Whether it is day birth or night birth, Jupiter gets 60 shashtiamsas of Triamsa Bala. (b) Additionally, one of Moon, Venus and Mars, or one of Mercury, Sun and Saturn get this strength according to birth being in night or day.

For Example, there occurs a birth at 14 ghatis after night fall while the night duration is 30 ghatis. So to say, birth is in the second one third of the night. As suggested, the strength of 60 shashtiamsas, on this score goes to Venus. This is again 60 shashtiamsas.

मन्दारबुधगुरुसिताः शक्षिसूर्यावुत्तरोत्तर बलिनः ।

स्वाभाविकबलमेतब्ब नसाम्ये चिन्तयेत् प्राज्ञः ।४०।

40. NAISARGIKA BALA (NATURAL STRENGTH): Sat-

urn, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Moon and Sun are stronger than the other in the ascending order. (This is called natural strength). Should there be similarity in other strengths, then the one with greater natural strength prevails.

Notes: Naisargika bala accrues to a planet according to its brightness. This is constantly expressed and is not variable. The Sun is the most luminous body and hence its natural strength is the highest. The darkest body Saturn gets the least. Sce the following table for an easy grasp:

| Sun     |  | 1 |  | 1.000 Rupa |
|---------|--|---|--|------------|
| Moon    |  |   |  | 0.857 Rupa |
| Mars    |  | : |  | 0.286 Rupa |
| Mercury |  |   |  | 0.429 Rupa |
| Jupiter |  | : |  | 0.571 Rupa |
| Venus   |  | : |  | 0.714 Rupa |
| Saturn  |  |   |  | 0.143 Rupa |

(One Rupa equals 60 Shashtiamsas)

इति कत्याएत्रमंतविरचितायां सारावल्यां ग्रह्योनिभेदो नाम चतुर्योऽध्याव: (I४)। Thus ends the 4th chapter "Planetary Characters” of Kalyana Varma's Saravali.
