The portion that expired in Lagna is indicative of the content to which the wick has burnt already. The quantity of oil in the lamp will correspond to the waxing or waning state of the Moon.

बलवति सूर्ये हृष्टे बहून् प्रदीपान् बदेत् कुपुत्रेखा ।

ग्रन्वेरपमतवीयः सृती ज्योतिस्तृरोर्भवति ।। २६ ॥ सौराविध्य जलचि चन्द्रेर्षजसंयुतेऽयवा हिबुके । तद्वृष्टे वा कुर्यात्तमसि प्रस्वं न सन्देहः ॥ २७ ।।

26-27. There were many lights (in the delivery chamber) if the Sun is aspected by Mars. Should other planets be all weak, light was obtained by burning grass. Should the Moon be in a navamsa of Saturn or be in watery navamsa or in the 4th house and be in aspect to or in the company of Saturn, delivery was in a place of darkness. There is no doubt about this.

Notes: Since the Sun and Mars indicate brightness, their mutual aspect suggests many lights. In sloka 27, combinations for birth in darkness are given: Satun is a dark planet and hence the Moon’s connection therewith, lead to birth without lights. Watery signs are brought in the picture, because water is not congenial for a lanp to burn. The conjunction of the Moon and Saturn in 4th (house related to mother) also causes darkness at the time of birth.

होरामनीक्षमाणे शक्षिति परोक्षस्थिते पितरि जातः । मेघुरखाच्व्युते वा चरभे भानौ विद्देश्यते ॥ २८ ॥ युनिश्योर्कासितयोः कुवेन सन्हष्टयोः पिताप्यभवत् । चरराशौ परवेधे युक्तेक्षितयोस्तु तत्र मृतः ।। २८ ।। पञ्चमनवम्युने व पार्पर्कातु पापसंहृष्टेः । बद्धः पिताऽन्यवेश्ष राक्षिवश्यात् स्वेऽ्यवा मार्गे । ३० ।

28-30. BIRTH OUT OF FATHER’S SIGHT: Should the ascendant be without the aspect of the Moon, the birth is behind the father, i.e. the father was absent from the place. If the Sun is in the 9th identical with a movable Rasi, the father was in a foreign place. The Sun in the case of day birth and Saturn in the case of night birth, if aspected by Mars, delivery is out of father’s sight. If the said Sun or Saturn be in a movable sign, death of father (in a distant place) should be declared. If the 5th, 6th and 7th from the Sun are aspected or occupled by malefics, the father was under confinement. Movable, fixed and common signs here indicate confinement, respectively in foreign place, own place and on the way.

जायात्रिकोरासंस्थे: क्रूरेरानन्दर्वाजतः प्रसवः । व्यामचतुर्थोपगतः सौम्य: संपत्तयो विपुलाः ॥ ३१ ॥

31. DELIVERY—COMFORTABLE OR OTHERWISE: Should the 7th house and the trines be occupied by malefics, the delivery is not without trouble. Benefics in the 10th and the 4th cause easy deltvery in a large room apart from giving wealth.

व्यति न गुरु: दाविनं लग्नं च दिवाकरं सेग्दुम् । वापयुतं वा सार्के चन्द्रं यदि जारजातः स्यात् ।। ३२ II गुरक्षिक्षिरवयो नीचे सृतो लग्नेऽथवार्यसूनुष्च । लग्नोडुपम्गुपुत्रा: \_\_\_ घुर्रवृष्टास्तचाग्यजातञ्च । ३३ ।

32-33. BIRTHS OUT OF FATHER'S LOINS: The following lead to birth outside wedlock:

(a) Moon or Lagna sans Jupiter’s aspect. (b) The luminaries being together without Jupiter's aspect. (c) The luminaries along with malefics not receiving the aspect of Jupiter. (d) The Sun, Moon and Jupiter simultaneously in fall

while Saturn is in Lagna and Lagna, Moon and Venus not being in aspect to Jupiter.

Notes: The following are additional traditional rules for illegal births:

(1) The Moon in the 4th and in aspect to malefic or inimical planet. (2) Lords of the 3rd, 6th, 2nd and 5th occupying the ascendant. (3) Saturn in the 10th, malefics in Lagna while benefics arc confined to the 7th. (4) Mars and Venus in the 3rd while the Moon is in Lagna. (5) Sun in the ascendant and Rahu in the 4th. (6) Sun and Moon in Lagna or Sun and Mars in 7th. (7) Lagna with Mars and Rahu while the luminaries are in the 7th. (8) No planets in anglcs. (9) All planets relegated to 2nd, 6th, 8th and 12th. (10) Lords of Lagna and 7th in conjunction. (11) Lords of Lagna and 4th in exchange (which is otherwise a Raja Yoga). (12) Lagna and Moon not belonging to any Varga of Jupiter. (13) The Sun, Moon and Jupiter together in Lagna while one of them is in fall. (14) Karakamsa Lagna related to only malefics. (15) Any one group of the following prevalent at birth: (a) Sunday—Dvitiya Thithi—Swati Nakshatra, (b) Wednesday—Saptami—Revati, (c) Sunday— Dvadasi—Dhanishta.

Some of these combinations do not call for literal application. In this context Jupiter in the 4th or Mars aspecting Jupiter or any Kendra occupied by a planet or Lagna Lord in Lagna or benefics aspecting the ascendant or Jupiter and Moon in mutual Vargas or the Sun and the Moon in exchange cancel the combinations for illegal birth.

बलेशो मातुः क्रर्बन्ध्वस्तगर्तेः श्याज्युक्तेवा। चन्द्रात् सप्तमराशौ पापा मरखाय वक्षसम्हष्टाः ।। ३४ । चन्त्राह्यामे भानुर्मातुमरखं करोति पापयुतः । शुक्कात् पञ्चमनवमे सौरियुतस्तेन वा हृष्टः ।। ३५ ।। चन्द्रारित्रकोरा राशी रविजो मातुर्वधं दिश्ति रात्री । शुक्रात्तचैव विवसे, भौमः पापेन उम्हृष्टः ।। ३६ ।।

34-36. EVILS TO MOTHER: If the Moon is in the 4th or 7th along with malefics, it is evil to mother. The native’s mother dies (immediately after delivery) if there are malefics in the 7th from the Moon, and aspected by Mars. The Sun in the 10th from the Moon along with malefics indicate death of the mother. The same effect prevails if the Sun is in the 5th or 9th from Venus (??) and with Saturn’s company or aspect. If birth takes place during night time while Saturn occupies 5th or 9th from the Moon, the mother soon leaves the world. For a day birth, if Mars is the 5th or 9th from Venus and aspected by malefics, the mother dies (early).

Notes: According to Jataka Sarvasva, should the Sun be in the 5th or the 9th from the Moon and be aspected by malefics, the mother dies carly. If Mars is in the 5th or 9th from Venus and aspected by malefics, the paternal grandmother dies early.

कुत्सौरयोस्त्रकोणे चन्द्रेऽस्तगते वियुज्यते मात्रा । दृष्टे सुरेन्द्रगुरुखा सुखान्वितो वोघ जीवो च ।। ३७ ॥ स्रियते पापेद् ष्टे वाशिनि विलग्ने कुजेऽस्तगे त्यक्तः । सग्नात् स्वलाभगतयोर्वसुधासुतमन्दयोरेवम् ॥ ३८ ॥ प्रयति सौम्यो बलवान् याद्ग्यृद्धाति तावृशो जातम् । च्युमपापत्रहवृष्टे परेगु होतोऽबवा स्रियते ॥ ३६ ॥ एकांशस्यितयोर्वा यमारयोस्त्यव्यतेऽयवा मात्रा । लग्नात् सप्तमभवने भौमे शानिवीक्षिते नियतम् । ४० ॥

37-40. MOTHER FORSAKING THE CHILD: If Mars and Saturn join in the 5th or the 9th from Ascendant, while the Moon is in the 7th, the child is forsaken by the mother. Jupiter’s aspect in that case bestows happiness and long life. The child given up dies if the Moon is in Lagna and Mars is in the 7th, in aspect to malefics. Mars and Saturn in 1 1th bring similar effects. Should a benefic aspect such combinations, the child is rescued by the respective relative. Both malefics and benefics aspecting indicate death in

the hands of others. Saturn and Mars joining in the same Amsa indicate that the child will be foresaken by the mother. Mars in the 7th from the ascendant in aspect to Saturn produces similar effects.

पादृष्पद्यति सौम्यस्त्तुल्यगुणं सुतः समाधले । पितृजननोसावृद्यं रवेः श्राजूस्य बलयोगात् ॥ ४१ ॥

41. RESEMBLANCE DESCRIBED: The child acqutres the qualities corresponding to the benefic’s aspects. Should the Sun be strong, it resembles the father. If the Moon be so, resemblance is that of the mother.

सिहाजयोभिरुदये जातो नालेन वेष्टितो जन्तुः । लग्ने कुजेऽ्य सौरे राद्यंश्चसमानगात्रे च ।। ४२ ।

42. BIRTH WITH COIL AROUND: When Leo, Aries or Taurus rises with Mars or Saturn, there will be coil around the limb as indicated by the Navamsa lagna Rasi.

भौमशनिवेक्काणे पापे लग्ने स्थिते श्झियुते वा ।

द्वघेकादशार्गः सौम्येरभिवेष्टितको भुजङ्गन ।। ४३ ॥ सुर्यश्चतुष्पदस्थः शेवा द्विवारोरसंस्थिता बलिनः ।

कोर्शवे च्टितबेहो यमलौ खलु संप्रजायेते ॥ ४४ II

43-44. COILING BY NAVEL CORD: If the Lagna falls in a decanate of Mars or Saturn, while malefics or Moon occupy Lagna, with benefics relegated to 2nd or 11th denote that the child was born with navel cord coiled around. Should the Sun be in a quadruped sign, while others with strength remain in dual (common) signs, it indicates that twins were born coiled by same navel cord.

सम्मनवभागवुल्या

मूर्तिबलसंयुताङ्प्हाहृर्पि ।

नवमायाद्रोक्तिः शधियुक्तात् तत्र सुतस्य ।। ४५ ।

## बहवो यदि बलपुक्ता मिवा मूर्तिस्तदा वाच्या । कुलजातिवेश पुरुषान् बुद्ध्वाऽदेशं समाविद्योसञ्जः ॥ ४६ ।

45-46. FEATURES AND CHARACTER: The physical appearance will correspond to the Navamsa Lagna or the strongest of planets. It may also correspond to the lord of the Navamsa occupied by the Moon. If many are strong, appearance and complexion will be mixed. Race, caste and country of birth should be considered while estimating features and character of a person.

## त्रिशाद्धागे भानुग्रहस्य यस्य स्थितो भवति ।

## तत्तुल्याः प्रकृतिः स्यादेवं मुनयोऽब्यवस्यन्ति ।। ४७ ॥

47. NATURE OF THE NATIVE: Sages say that the nature of the native will correspond to (that of the lord) the Trimsamsa occupied by the Sun at birth.

तत्कालमुह्वरित्वं बलं च नीचोच्चमध्यसंश्रितताम् ।

ज्ञात्वा प्रहस्वभावास्तिम्यः संचिन्त्यमन्यदपि ।। ४८ ॥

48. Other information must be understood according to the friendship, enmity and debilitation, exaltation or placement in between, of the planets at birth.

## कीणे शशिनि सपापे माता च्रियते पिता रवौ तहत् ।

## पार्पदुष्टे मिश्रेव्याधिः सौम्ये: श्चुभं भवति ।। ४१ II

49. DEATH OF PARENTS: If weak Moon be with malefics, mother’s death takes place while the Sun being so causes father's death. These planets indicate sickness (to the concerned parent) if aspected by both benefics and malefics. Benefics however, cause auspiciousness (in such a case).

विपुलविमलमूर्तिः स्वोच्यगो वा स्वराक्षौ

गुरुसितयुत इन्दुर्बोधनेनानुवृष्टः ।। प्रतित्यत्नुभवाता पञ्चमे वाऽपि मातुः पितुरपि खलु तहत् भास्कर: सर्वदेव । इ०।

50. HAPPINESS OF PARENTS: The Moon, being bright, if be in her exaltation Rasi or in her own Rasi along with Jupiter and Venus in aspect to Mercury, or the Moon in 5th is capable of giving extreme auspiciousness in respect of the native's mother. The Sun so placed proves auspicious for father.

इति कल्याणात्रमविरचितायां सारावल्यां जन्मविधानाध्यायो नवमः ।

Thus ends the 9th chapter entitled “JANMA VIDHA- NADHYAYA" of Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## Chapter 10

## - ऋषाऽरिष्टाध्यायः ।। १० ।।

## Evils At Birth

द्वायुक्ानातावे सर्वं विकलं प्रकीतितं यस्मात् । वस्थात्तज्ज्ञानाथे रिष्टाध्यायं प्रवक्ष्यामि । १ ॥

1. NEED TO KNOW OF LONGEVITY: In the absence of knowledge of longevity, attempts to know other information are futile. So, I explain below Arishtas (or evils), in order to determine longevity.

श्रीजे स्थिताः पुर्मांसः घुक्लेऽ्हनि सूरिभिः समार्याताः । युग्मभवनेषु सवे\* कृष्णे निशषि योधितो बलिनः ।। २ ॥

2. STRENGTH OF MALE AND FEMALE PLANETS: Male plancts are strong and in odd Rasis, bright lunar half and day time. Female planets are strong in even Rasis, dark lunar half and night time.

त्रिविधमिह वास्त्रकारा नियतमनियतं च योगजं प्राहुः । योगसमुत्यं तावदक्ये पद्चालु परिक्षेषो । ३ ॥

3. KINDS OF EVILS: There are three kinds of evils, explained by astrological expounders. These are: preordained, accidental and Yogaja (arising of planetary combinations). The third kind is dealt with below while others are explained later on.

बृहस्पतिभौ मम्हेऽलष्टमस्थः सूबग्दुभौमार्कजहच्टमूति । ध्रब्बेस्त्रिभिर्मार्गव्ष्टिहीनो लोकान्तरं प्रापयति प्रसुतम् ॥४।

4. LONGEVITY CURTAILED TO MINIMUM: Should Jupiter be in the 8th and in a house of Mars (i.e. Aries /Virgo rising at birth), and aspected by the Sun, the Moon, Mars

and Saturn, the child dies in 3 years.

Notes: For Aries, Jupiter rules the 9th and 12th while for Virgo, he rules the 4th and 7th houses, Even though he is aspected by Mars—the dispositor, Saturn-Ayushkaraka and the luminaries, his closc friends, he is harmful in respect of longevity if in the 8th for the said ascendants. If Jupiter is in the 8th house belonging to Mars, but be without aspect to all the said 4 planets, the longevity may be more than 3 years, but not full.

◦ वक्री वनिभी मगृहं प्रप्नदचन्द्रेऽप्टवष्ठेऽ्य चतुष्टये वा । कुजेन सम्प्राप्तबलेन हृष्टो वर्षद्यं जीवयति प्रजातम् ॥ ५ ॥ 5. If Saturn is retrograde in a house of Mars while the Moon is in the 6th, 8th or an angle, and aspect Mars, the child born lives for two years.

भास्करहिमकरसहितः शानद्चरौ मृत्युदः प्रसवक्काले ।

वर्षेनविभिर्यार्तेरित्याह ब्रह्यशौण्डार्यः ।। ६ । भीमदिवाकरसौरादिछिन्ने जातस्य ववंगृहे। स्रियतेऽव्यं स नरो यमक्तरक्षोऽपि मासेन ।। ७ ॥

6-7. If Saturn joins the Sun and Moon at birth, the life span is only nine years, according to Brahmasaunda. Should Mars, the Sun and Saturn be in Taurus identical with the 8th, the native lives for one month even though Lord Yama may try to save him.1

एकः पापोऽ्टमगः शुक्रगृहे पापवीक्षितो वर्षात् । मारयति वरं जातं सुधारसो थेन पीतोऽपि । द रविवाविभवने शुक्रो द्वादशारिपुर्ध्रमः श्चुभेः सर्वेः । बृष्टः करोति वड्भर्ववर्मरणं किमत्र चित्रं हि ।। & II

8-9. Even if there be a single malefic in the 8th house, owned by Venus and aspected by other malefics, the native 1. Some texts read भौमगृह्धे while still some read चुक् म्हें, In place of

lives for one year only inspite of the fact that he may drink nectar. If Venus is in the 12th, 6th or 8th house, owned by the Sun or the Moon, and is aspected by all benefics, the life span is six years.

कर्कटवामनि सौम्यः षष्ठाव्टमसंस्थितो विलग्नर्षात् । चन्द्रेता हृष्टमूरतिर्ववं चतुष्केरा मारयत ॥ १० ॥ तीवफलराजयोगा यवनाद्येये विनिमितास्तेषु । जायन्ते खलु कुलजा रिष्टं तेषु प्रसूतानाम् ॥ ११।

10-11. If Mercury occupies Cancer identical with 6th or 8th from ascendant and is aspected by the Moon, life is only upto 4. The efficacious Raja Yogas expounded by Yavanas may produce persons of high descent; even such children are subject to premature end, in this case.

Notes: For the first rule, the ascendant should either be Aquarius or Sagittarius, so that Mercury is in 6th or 8th in Cancer. Thus, the Moon becomes the lord of the 6th for Aquarius and the lord of the 8th for Sagittarius. Her aspect on Mercury is deemed to curtail longevity to four years.

In sloka 11, the author hints at the fact that even though a person may be born with powerful Rajayogas, if Arishta yogas indicating premature death are simultaneously present, the latter prevails. Of what use are Rajayogas in the face of Arishtas?

केतुर्यस्मिग्न्केऽस्युवितस्तस्मन्त्रसूयते यो हि । मासद्येन मरणं विनिदिशेत्तस्य जातस्य ।। १२ ।। गगनस्यो विवसक्षर: वार्वरबलिभिनिरीक्षितः सच् । मार्यति भौमधामनि क्षनिमे च न संक्षयो भवति । १३ ॥

12-13. If the birth star is identical with the one in which Ketu1 rises, the native lives only for two months. Should the Sun be in the 10th house identical with that of Mars or Saturn and aspected by strong malefic,2 the native faces immediate death.

1. Ketu has been interpreted as Dhuma Ketu by some translators. 2. Some say speclally Saturn/Mars.

लग्ने बढ्देक्काखा निगडाहिविहज्जपावापरसंज्ञाः । मरख्याय सप्तवर्षः क्रूरयुता न स्वपतिहृष्टाः ॥ १४ II राहुब्चतुष्टयस्थो मरताय निरीक्षितो भवति पापेः । बर्वैर्बंदन्ति दशभिः बोडश्षभिः केचिवाचार्याः ।। १५ II

14-18. Should the ascendant fall in Nigala, Sarpa, Pakshi or Pasa decanate and be occupied by malefic planets, without the aspect of the decanate lord concerned, the native lives upto 7 ycars only. If Rahu is in an angle and aspected by malcfics, the longevity is only 10, while according to some, it is 16.

Notes: For cxample, Sarpa drekkana in Pisces is the 3rd decanatc, i.c. 20 degrees-30 degrces. Suppose Rahu is in 20-30 degrec arca of Pisccs. He should be aspected by Mars, the ruler of Scorpio where the 3rd decanate falls, so that the child lives beyond 7 years of its age. Similarly for other decanates.

पापारित्रक्षोर्णकेन्द्रे सोम्याः वष्ठाष्टमव्ययगताव्च । सूर्वोदये प्रसूतः सथः प्राखास्त्यजति जन्तुः ।। १६ । संशाधिपजन्मयती लग्नपतिद्चास्तमुपवता यस्य । संवत्सरस्तु शरणं निर्ष्याजं कतिपर्यरेव । १७ ॥ -

16-17. If malcfics are angles and trines, while benefies are relegated to 6th, 8th and 12th, one born at the time of Sun rise dics at once. If the three planets, viz. the lords of Navamsa Lagna, Moon sign and natal ascendant, are combust, the person lives just for a few years.

Notes: Bcnefics posited in cvil houses as malefics occupy Lagna, 4th, 7th, 10th, 5th or 9th, death is imminent if birth takes place at Sun rise. With the combustion by the Sun to Lords of Lagna, Navamsa lagna and the Moon sign, the longevity is highly afflicted.

राशिप्रमिर्तवर्वेर्मारयति विलम्नपो रिपुस्थाने । मासेब्रेक्कार्णपतिदिवसंरंशा धियो

18. If the 6th house (from the ascendant) is occupied by the ascendant lord, death occurs in the year corresponding to Rasi concerned; if the occupant is the lord of rising decanate, death occurs in the month corresponding to the Rasi concerned and if it is lord of Navamsa Lagna, the Rasi concerned causes death in so many days.

Notes! Suppose Virgo rises at birth and the sixth house, Aquarius, is occupied by Mercury, Lord of lagna, death occurs in the 1 1th year as Aquarius is the 1 Ith Rasi of the zodiac. Death in 11 months should be expected if in the said casc, sixth is occupied by the natal decanate’s lord. The native dies in 11 days, if the said Aquarius is occupied by the lord of Navamsa Lagna.

## धारयति षोडश्ञाहै: शर्नेश्वरः पापवोक्षितो लग्ने । संयुक्तो मासेन तु वर्षाच्छुद्धस्तु मारयति ॥ १९ ।।

19. If Saturn is in the ascendant in aspect to malefics1 longevity is only 16 days; if Saturn in the ascendant is in the company of malefics, death occurs in a month and if he is alone in the ascendant without malefic connections, the child lives for one year.

Notes: Saturn alonc in Lagna identical with Libra, Sagitiarius, Capricorn, Aquarlus or Pisces is not adverse for longevity. His lonely occupation in other Lagnas are pronouncedly harmful for longevity. According to Sage Jambunada's Jambu Nadeeyam, one with Saturn in ascendant identical with Libra, Sagittarius, Pisces, Aquarius or Capricorn will possess attractive limbs, become a king, be learned and rule cities and villages.

वर्षाम्भारयति शाश्षी वष्ठाष्टमराविसंस्थितो लग्नात् । सौर्यरब्दाष्टकाञ्चव ।। २१ ॥ सञ्चः क्रूरंव्ष्टः क्षीरात्रारीरद्चन्द्रो लग्नस्थः क्रूरवोक्षितः कुरुते। स्वर्गगमनं हि पुंसां कुलोरगोऽ्जान् परित्यज्य १ २० ।

1. Some texts read as Sun and Mars instead of malefics.

श्रशुभशुभः सम्दृष्टे वर्षचतुष्केरा निविशेवन्तम् । स्नुपातः कर्तव्यः प्रोक्तादूनाधिर्कर्वे ष्टे ।।२२।।

20-22. If weak Moon is in the ascendant excepting Cancer, Taurus and Arics, and is aspected by malefics the child passes away (soon). The Moon in the 6th or 8th if aspected by malcfics, causes death in one year, while when aspected by bencfics, she kills in the 8th ycar. Should the aspect be from both bencfics and malefics, the lifc span is only 4. This should be decided according to the strength of such bodies.

Notes: Vide Jambu Nadeeyam, if weak Moon occupies the birth ascendant identical with Cancer or Aries: the native will be wealthy, and virtuous. If the Moon occupies ascendant coinciding with a human sign or centiped sign, the native will be wicked, deaf, dumb or will be stammering in speech.

सौम्याः षष्ठाष्टमयाः पार्यर्वक्रोपसञ्चतदुष्टाः ।

मासेन मृत्युवास्ते यदि म धुभस्तत्र सम्बृष्टाः ।२३।। लग्नादद्वादशाधनर्गः क्रीस्रयते च रम्ध्ररिपुयुक्त” : । शुभसम्पर्कमयार्तरमासि षष्ठेऽ्ष्टमे वाऽपि ।।२४।।

23-24. Should benefics be in the 6th or the 8th and be aspected or conjoined with retrograde malefic, without the aspect of (another) benefic, the child lives only for one month. If malefics are in the 12th, 2nd, 6th and 8th, without connection of benefics, the child dies in 6 or 8 months.

लग्याषिपजन्मपती वष्ठाष्टमरिव्यगी प्रसवकाले। श्वस्तमिती मरराकरौ राशिप्रमितवंदेहर्बे: ।।२५।। ह्वोराधिपतिर्चूने पापजितो मरखमेव विक्षधाल्षि । मासेन जन्मनाथस्तदध्वन्त्रो यवा न शुभदृष्टः ।। २६ ।।

25-26. If the lords of lagna and the Moon sign are together in 6th, 8th or 12th and be combust, the child dies in the year corresponding to the Rasi.1 Should the lord of the ascendant be in the 7th, defeated2 by a malefic and be without a benefic’s aspect, death occurs in a month. Similar clues should be drawn from the Moon and her dispositor.

चन्त्रः कुजर वियुक्तः स्वसुतस्थाने न चापि श्युभहछ्टः ।

मरणं शिशोः प्रयच्छति वर्ष नवमे न सन्देहः ।२७।

होरेववरस्तु मृत्यो पार्पेः सकलंश्च हृद्यते ब्लिभिः ।

मासि चतुर्ये मरणं जातस्य करोति मुनिवाष्यम् ॥२८।।

27-28. If the Moon is conjoined with Mars and the Sun and be in the 2nd or 5th unaspected by benefics, the child doubtlessly dies in the 9th year. Sages declare that if the ascendant lord is in the 8th in aspect to all the malefics, who are with strength, the child dies in the fourth month.

जन्माधिपतिः सूर्यः स्वपुत्रसहितोऽ्मे भवति राशौ ।

वर्षे राक्षिप्रमितमरखाय व सितेन सम्हष्टः (२६। व्ययाम्रेषव्ठोदयगे वाशाङ्क पापेन युक्ते चुभहङ्निहीने ।

केंडरेषु सौम्यग्रहवजितेधु प्रार्णवियोगं ब्रजति प्रजातः ।३०।

29-30. If the lord of the Moon sign, the Sun and his son are in the 8th house, the child dics in the year corresponding to the Rasi concerned.3 Should the Moon be with malefics, be in the 12th, 8th, 6th or ascendant and be not aspected by benefics, while benefics are non-angular, the child dies at once.

चक्रस्य पूर्वभागे पापाः सौम्यास्तथेतरे चेव । वृक्रिकलग्ने जाता गतायुषो ब्रमुष्टियोगेऽस्मन् ॥३१। क्षीणे बाज्ञिनि विलग्ने पार्षः केन्द्रेषु मृत्युसंस्थर्वा ।

भवति विपत्तिरव्यं यवनाधिपतेरमतं चंतत् ॥३२।।

1. See notes under sloka 18 of this chapter. 2. This obviously refers to planetary war. 3. See notes under Sloka 18 of this chapter.

31-32. If malefics are in the oriental half of the zodiac, with bencfics relegated to the occidental half, birth in Scorpio Ascendant causes what is called Vajra Mushti yoga. One born in such a yoga does hardly live. According to Yavanas, if weak Moon is in Lagna while malefics are angular or in the 8th house (instant) death is caused.1

Notes: The oriental half or eastern half (i.e. ण) in Hindu astrology is the area from the 10th bhava madhya to the 4th bhava madhya i.e. second half of 10th, then the 11th bhava, 12th Lagna, 2nd, 3rd and first half of 4th bhava. The rest of the horoscope i.e. first half of 10th, then the 9th, 8th, 7th, 6th, 5th and the second half of the 4th is called occidental half or ¶% WI%of the horoscope. (These two are different from visible and invisible halves of the chart). In the sloka, the ascendant is fixed as Scorpio. Hence malefics posited in the zone of Leo 2nd half through Aquarius first half and benefics posited in sccond half of Aquarius through first half of Leo can cause death at the very boyhood.

रादयन्तगर्तः पार्षः सन्ध्यायां तुहिनरविमहोरायाम् । मृत्युः प्रत्येकस्थः केन्द्रेषु शाशाङ्कपापंदच ।३३। छूनचतुरश्रसंस्थे पापद्यमध्यमे वाश्चिनि जातः । विलयं प्रयाति नियतं देवरपि रक्षितो बातः ।३४।।

33-34. One born in twilight does not live long, if the ascendant falls in lunar hora, while malefics are posited in the end of the Rasis which are angular, along with the Moon. Should the Moon be amidst two malefics and be in the 7th, 4th or 8th, even Gods cannot save one from (instant) death.

Notes: Sandhya means the junction of day and night or the night and day. If one is born at this time while the Lagna falls in the Hora of Moon (i.e. Lagna being 2nd half of an odd Rasi or the first half of an even Rasi), and while malefics with Moon are in the last portions of the Rasis 1. See notes under Sloka 20, chapter 10.

occupied by them, and be angular, death is early. Individually, the Moon should not be in the 4th, 7th or 8th hemmed between two malefics for a long life.

पापद्यमध्यगते होरासप्ताष्टमस्थिते चन्े। सोर्म्यरबलंहब्टे जातो स्रियते ध्रुवं ह्वयत्र ।३५।। शूनान्टमर्गः पार्षः क्रूरप्रहवीक्षितः सह जनन्या । श्रियते शुभंसंहर्ष्टः सत्यस्य मताढवेद्वचाधिम् ॥३६।।

35-36. If the Moon is hemmed between two malefics, be in the ascendant the 7th or the 8th and be in aspect to weak benefics, (infant) death follows. Should malcfics be in the 7th and 8th and be aspected by malefics, the child dies along with its mother. If there be aspect from bencfics in such a case, the mother becomes sick, (i.e. there will not be immediate death).

ग्रह्तोपगते चन्रे सक्रूरे लग्नगे कुजेऽङ्टभने। मात्रा सार्ष स्रियते चन्द्रवदकं व शस्त्रेख ।३। क्षीणे शशिनि विलग्ने कष्टकनिधनाश्रितस्तथा पार्षः ।

सौम्याहृष्टे मृत्युः सद्यः सत्यस्य निर्देशः ॥३८।

37-38. If the Moon be eclipsed (i.e. lunar cclipse operating at birth) and be in the ascendant along with malefics, while Mars is in the 8th house, the child quits the world along with its mother. The mother dies of weapons along with the child, if it be a solar eclipse with Sun and malefics in the ascendant while Mars is in the 8th house: Should weak Moon be in the ascendant, and malefics be angular or in the 8th, without bencfic’s aspect, dcath is imminent. This view is of Satyacharya.

च्यनगतेऽके लग्ने यमे कुजे वा विपर्यये वाऽपि । - श्रन्यतर्युते वेन्दावचुर्भंह क्टेऽविरात्मृत्युः ॥३६ ह्होरानिधनास्तगतः पार्पः क्षीणे व्ययस्थिते चन्ने । जातस्य भवेन्मरणं सद्यः केन्द्रेषु चेदधुमाः ॥४०।।

39-40. The undermentioned combinations death soon: cause

(a) The Sun in the 7th while Saturn or Mars is in the ascendant. (b) The Sun in the ascendant, whilc Saturn or Mars is in the 7th. (c) Saturn or Mars joining the Moon and be not aspected by benefics. (d) Malefics in the ascendant, 8th, 7th, 4th and 10th the Moon be decreasing in the 12th. लग्नान्त्यनवमनंधनसंयुक्ताऽबन्द्रसूर्यसौराराः I जातस्य वधकृतः स्युः सद्यो गुरुता न चेद्टट्राः ॥४१। लग्ने चन्द्रेऽके वा पापा बलिनस्त्रकोरानिधनेषु । सौर्वरहृष्टयुक्ताः सद्यो मरखाय कीतिता यवर्नः ॥४२।।

41-42. The Moon, the Sun, Saturn and Mars are capable of inflicting infant death' if they are together in the asccndant, the 12th, 9th or 8th unaspected by Jupiter of the ascendant is tenanted by the Sun or the Moon while malefics are in trines or the 8th and be not aspected by or conjoined with benefics, according to Yavanas, infant death is caused.

Notes: As per the first rule, the four planets, viz. the Sun, the Moon, Mars and Saturn should join either in the ascendant or in the 8th, or in the 12th or in the 9th to cause child mortality. If they are, however, aspected by Jupiter, the yoga stands nulified. Secondly, when the ascendant is occupied by the Sun or the Moon while malefics are in the 5th/9th/8th without relief from benefics, infant death is indicated.

शुक्रो रविज्ञनिसहितो मारयति नरं सदा प्रसवकाले । हृष्टोऽपि देवगुरुखा नवमिर्वर्वर्ने सन्देहः ॥४३। यत्रस्यस्तत्रस्यो रुधिरार्कशर्नश्वरेक्षितस्यन्त्रः । जननीमृत्युं कुर्यान्न तु सौम्यनिरीक्षितः सख्यः ॥४४१।

43-44. Venus joining the Sun and Saturn causes instant death. This combination doubtlessly gives death in the 9th year if aspected by Jupiter. Death in childhood occurs if the Moon is conjoined with Mars, the Sun and Saturn, provided unaspected by benefic planets.

Notes: Should Venus join the Sun and Saturn, the child will die immediately at birth. If the combination is aspected by Jupiter, then the longevity is extended upto nine. Similarly the Moon, Mars, the Sun and Saturn in conjunction will cause child mortality, if benefics give help, this yoga does not prevail and the child will Itve long, if favourable yogas are present.

दधिर्चार्दबर्च्टो दिवसकरो दिक्षसजन्मनि तु यस्य । पापयुतो बा हन्यात् पितरं निःसंश्यं जातः ।४५॥ रहितो बुषयुद्धुकर्जन्मनि रुधिराङ्गसौरसहितोऽर्कः । कथयति पितरमतीतं पितुरपि च श्रोरकर्तारम् ।४६।।

45-46. If the Sun is aspected by Mars and Saturn in the case of a day birth, it soon leads to death of the father. The Sun joining malefics (for day birth) also indicates the same effect. The child's father and paternal grand father will face early death if the Sun is in the ascendant along with Mars and Saturn but unrelated to Mercury, Jupiter and Venus.

Notes: For a native born in day time, the Sun in aspect to Mars and Saturn or the Sun in the company of malefics (i.e. Rahu, Ketu, Saturn or Mars) can cause father's early death. This is more sure to follow if the house involved is the 9th or the 10th. If the Sun be in the ascendant with Mars and Saturn, not connected with Mercury, Venus or Jupiter (or even the Full Moon aspecting from the 7th), it may kill the father or grand-father. Incidentally, if the (Full) Moon in 7th is aspected by Mars, the Sun and Saturn, unhelped by benefics, it may prove adverse for the mother of the child.

पापद्यमध्यगतो दिवसकरो दिवसजन्मनिर्तस्य । पापयुतो वा हन्यात् पितरं निःसंशयं जातः ॥४७। सूर्याव्रमराज्यौ यादि युक्तो सौरलोहितौ प्रसवे। सौम्याहट्रौ निधनं कुर्यातां सख्य एव पितुः ।४८।।

47-48. If the Sun is hemmed between two malefics or is conjoined with malefics, one born during day time loses his father early without any doubt. The child's father at once quits the world if the planet Sun has Mars and Saturn in the 8th from him unaspected by benefics.

Notes: The Sun is the indicator of father for day births. His being free from malefic connections is essential for a long life of father when the birth takes place during day time. Similarly, Saturn rules father for night birth and hence he should be well placed to help the longevity of father in the case of night birth.

According to the above slokas, if the 8th from the Sun is occupied by both Saturn and Mars, longevity of the father is affected. This applies to a day birth.

- In the case of a night birth, if the 8th from Saturn is occupled by Mars and Rahu (or even by the Sun), the child's father faces quick death. Hence, the 8th house from the. Sun for a day-birth and 8th from Saturn for a night-birth should be well-disposed so that the father has a long life.

पापप्रहसंयुक्तश्चरराशिगतो दिवाकरः प्रसक्षे । विषशस्त्रजलान्मृत्युं कचयत्यस्पायुधं nee चन्त्रावृष्टमरासौ नवमे वा सप्तनेऽपि वा थापाः । सर्वे तत्रान्सतमे हन्युर्जातं सह जनम्या ॥५०।

49-50. If at birth the Sun is in a movable Rasi along with malefics, the father of the child dies of poison, weapons etc. Malefics (or even a malefic) in the 8th, 9th or 7th from the Moon can cause death of the child along with its mother.

चरराक्षिगते सुर्ये दिनजन्मनि बीक्षिते कुपुत्रेश । कथयति विवेवयातं जातस्य शरीरकर्तारम् ।५१॥ चरराशिगतं सौरं यद्यर्को रात्रिजन्मनोक्षेत । छत्रापि विवेशस्यं कययति पितरं प्रसूतस्य ॥५२।।

51-52. The father of the child was away at the time of birth if the Sun is in a movable sign, in case of day birth, and aspected by Mars. Saturn in a movable Rasi and in aspect to the Sun in the case of night birth, denotes some results.

इधिरसहितस्तु सौरद्वरभवने रात्रिजन्मनिरतस्य । कथयति पितरमतीतं परदेशे नात्त्र सन्देहः ॥५३।। यत्रस्यस्त्रस्थः स्वपुत्रधिराञ्चसङ्गतः सूर्यः । प्राम्जन्मनो निवृत्तं कथयति पितरं प्रसूतस्य ॥५४।।

53-54. In the case of night birth, if Saturn is in a movable sign along with Mars, the father undoubtedly dies in a distant place. If the Sun joins Mars and Saturn in any Rasi, the father dies before the birth of the native.

Note: See sloka 46 of this chapter and notes there of.

जन्माणसप्तवब्ठहादशासंस्थेषु चैव पापेषु । भाता सुतेन सार्ष स्रियते नास्त्यत्र सन्येहः ॥५५। जोवति माता स्रियते सुनुः बब्ठान्त्यगेषु पापेषु । जन्याइसप्तमेवु च जोवति सूनुस्रयेत तम्माता ।५६।

55-56. If malefics are disposed in the Ascendant, 8th, 7th, 6th and 12th, the child dies along with its mother. There is no doubt in it. Should only the 6th and 8th be occupied by malefics, the mother does not die but the child dies. The reverse is true if malefics are in the ascendant, 8th and the 7th.

Note: Both the child and mother die early if malefics are in Lagna, 8th, 7th, 6th and 12th. Only the mother dies

if the Lagna, 7th and 8th are occupied by malefics. It is the child that dies if the 6th and 8th are occupied by malefics.

वक्रो वा सौरो वा दादशसंस्थो नयनहन्ता। दक्षिखनयनं सौरो वाममथाङ्गारको हन्यात् ॥५७। युगपच्वन्द्रा दिस्यो द्वाद्भे निष्ठितो ग्रही स्याताम् । कुरुतः प्रसुतमन्धं पापः षष्ठेऽ्थ वा 'निघने ॥५८।। श्रथ वाप्यन्यतरयुते दाद्ञाने वापि जायमानस्य । श्रत्रापि हरेन्नयनं दक्षिरामर्क: शषसो सव्यम् ॥५६। स्वर्भानुनोपसुष्टा यदि होरा दिनकरद्व जामित्रे। जातस्तत्र मनुष्यो निःसन्दिग्धं भवत्यन्धः ॥ ६०।। घनभावे द्वादवामे चन्द्रः सूर्यश्च निष्ठितो यत्र । तत्रापि भवत्यन्धो यद्यक्रमषध्ठयोः पापौ ।६१। रजनिकरः षव्ठ्गतो निघने सूर्यो रवेः सुतस्तु बुने । वक्रः कुदुम्बराज्ञावत्राप्यन्धी भवेल्जातः ॥६२।। वषिराङ्गतौरयुक्तव्जनत्रो मिधनेऽ्ध वाऽपि वष्ठे वा । पित्तदलेव्मविकारहं झि हन्यावधुभयुक्तः ।६३। बेविरामंष्ट्मसंस्यः सब्यं तु हरेत्समाध्रितः वव्ठ्य् । सौम्यनिरीक्षिततनुः सद्यो न हरेल पञ्चाह्वा ॥६४।। विनकरसुतेन सहितो निधने चाऽन्ये समाश्रित्न्रः । बातिवलेस्मविकारहं छि हन्यावधुभदृष्टः ।६५। निषने वक्षिखानयनं त्यागे सब्यं हरेलु नियमेन । सौम्यैस्तु हृव्यमाने न हरेदथवा हरेत्यश्चात् ॥६९। एतेनैव तु विविना सौरारविवाकरावितक्षन्द्रः। कुर्याङ्रहृष्टिविकारं नानारीर्र्धुबं अन्तोः ॥६७।। 57-67. YOGAS ADVERSE FOR EYES: Mars or Saturn in 12th can deprive the native of his eyes—Saturn right eye and Mars left eye. The native becomes blind if the Sun and

the Moon are in 12th while 6th and 8th are occupied by malefics. Even if one among the Sun and the Moon is in the 12th damage to eye is indicated; the Sun affects the right eye while the Moon affects the left one. If Rahu is in the ascendant while the Sun is in the 7th, the native is blind by birth. Should the 2nd and the 12th be occupied by the Moon and the Sun, while malefics are in the 8th and 6th, person born is blind. If the Moon is in the 6th, the Sun in the 8th, Saturn in the 9th and Mars in the 2nd, simultaneously, the native is blind. Should the Moon be in the company of Mars and Saturn and be posited in the 8th or the 6th, the native loses his eye sight duc to billious and phlegmatic imbalances. The said Moon should be conjunet other malefics, apart from Mars and Saturn. In this combination if the house involving the Moon is the 8th, the damage is to the right eye and if it be the 6th, the left eye is damaged. Should, however, this combination be aspected by benefics, the damage is not at birth but will be later on. If the Moon is in the 8th or the 12th along with Saturn and be aspected by malefics eye sight is lost due to windy and phlegmatic complaints—here also, the 8th indicates right eye while the 12th the lcft eye. And if benefics aspect the said Moon in the 8th/ 12th, the loss is not at birth but in the course of time. If the Moon is thus associated with Saturn and the Sun (in several ways), damage to eyes is to be predicted.

Notes: There are umpteen combinations to know about eye afflictions, thus:

If the 2nd lord joins Venus or is aspected by him or is trinal or angular to him, adversity of sight should be ex- - pected. The same result occurs if the 2nd lord is connected with lagna Lord as above. The luminaries in the 2nd cause night-blindness. Should the lords of Lagna and 2nd house be in an angle along with the Sun, the child born is blind. Should these join the 9th lord, the father becomes blind. (vide Parasara Samhita)

See what Sarvartha Chintamani says in this regard: Should the lord of the 2nd along with the Lagna lord be in

an evil house, loss of sight is caused. Night-blindness occurs if Venus and Moon join the 2nd lord. Mars in the 12th damages the left eye. Saturn so posited damages the right eye. The Sun and Moon in Leo Lagna in aspect to Mars or Saturn afflict the eycs. Eye discases are caused if thc 2nd lord is conjunct Gulika, Mars and Saturn. If the Navamsa dispositor of the 2nd lord joins malefics in a malefic Rasi, same effect prevails.

एकावशे तृतीये होरायां पापसंयुते शक्षिनि । कर्णविकलो नरः स्यात्पापग्रहविक्षिते सद्यः ॥६८।। नवमे पञ्चमराखौ पापग्रहवीक्षिती ग्रही स्याताम् ।

श्रोत्रोपघातमतुलं कुर्यातां जातमात्रस्य ।६६।। नवमे दक्षिराकर्ण वामं वे पञ्चमे ग्रहो हन्यात् ।

श्रत्रव सौम्यमे वा शुभहब्टे वा शुभं वाच्यम् ॥७०।

68-70. YOGAS ADVERSE FOR EARS: The Moon in 11th, 3rd or the ascendant along with malefics causes defects of the ears. If she is aspected by malefics, the cffcct is along with birth—ie. immediate. Should the 9th and the 5th be aspected or occupied by malefics, ear defects at the time of birth exist. Malefics in the 9th affect the right ear while in the 5th, they damage the left ear. Benefics aspecting or occupying these houses, cause on the other hand good in respect of ears.

राझौ होरान्तरं प्राप्य यो यस्मिन् व्याधिमाप्नुयात् । तस्माच्च होरा प्रसवे चन्द्रस्थान च याद्भवेत् ।७१।। सव्यापसव्यभागे योगमयंवं ग्रहास्तु संप्राप्ताः । कुर्युन् तां च विद्ध' व्यङ्गभयं पापवीक्षिताः सौम्याः ।।७२।। विवित्वा त्रितयं ह्येतत् कृत्स्नस्य तु विशेषतः । बुभाञ्चुभौ तु विज्ञेयो ग्रही संयोगकारखौ ।०३।

71-73. Whatever Rasi is indicative of a disease shall be treated as the limb to be afflicted by such disease. The

Moon sign can also be similarly considered. Benefics cause birth marks on the right or left side of the body according to their occupation. Should malefics aspect these bencfics loss of concerned limb should be expected. Malcfics and benefics according to their association with the Moon and the visible and invisible halves (counted from Lagna) cause bad and good results in this respect.

चन्द्रादित्यो तृतीयस्यौ मोनक्षेत्रस्य यस्य तु। व्याँध तत्र विज्ञानीयात् त्रिरात्रं तस्य जीवितम् ॥७४।। श्रतस्तृतीये नक्षत्रे समस्ते व्यस्तगेऽपि वा। रवी रात्रि परां जीवेच्चन्द्रे दशममात्रिते ॥७५ सहितौ चन्द्रजामित्रे यस्याङ्चारकभास्करौ। जातस्य तस्य हि तदा भवेत्सप्ताहजीवितम् ॥७६।

74-76. DEATH IN FEW DAYS: The Sun and the Moon in the 3rd house identical with Pisces cause illness from birth and the child lives only for three days. If the Moon is in the 10th from the ascendant while the Sun singly or with all others is in the 3rd asterism from that occupied by the Moon, the child lives only for one night. The life span is just one week, if the Sun and Mars are in the 7th from the Moon.

चतुरश्रस्थिताः पापा बामदक्षिख्या यदा । तवा यो व्याधिमाप्नोति दवारात्रं स जीवति ।।७७।। त्रिकोणे दक्षिणे सुर्यद्चन्द्रो वामे यदा भवेत् । यस्तवा लभते व्याधि द्वाववाहं स जीवति ।७८।। त्रिकोरास्थो यदा चन्द्र्तुरश्रेऽय- मास्करः । तदा दुर्ध्याचिना युक्तस्त्ररात्रं नातिवर्तते ।७६।। यदा होरा-वतुयंस्यक्चन्द्रः वध्ठस्थितो रविः । श्रष्टाववाहं च नरस्तदा व्याभ्रिसमन्वितः ।८०।। रविर्धवा चन्द्रमसस्त्रकोलस्थानमात्रितः । वर्षाति विवसान् जीवेत्तदा व्याधिभयावितः ।८१।

होराष्टमस्थितः सूर्वः सौरभौमनिरोक्षितः । यः पुमान् प्राप्नुयादचाधि न स जोवेद्विपद्यते ।८२।।

77-82. EFFECTS OF TIME OF STARTING OF DIS- EASES: The following combinations prevailing at the time of a disease cause death in so many days as indicated:

(1) Malefics in 4th and 8th from Lagna—10 days. (2) Malefics in 2nd and 12th from Lagna—10 days. (3) The Sun in 5th while Moon is in 9th from the ascendant—12 days. (4) The Moon in angle while the Sun is in 4th or 8th— 3 nights. (5) The Moon in the 4th while the Sun is in the 6th— 18 days. (6) The Sun in the 5th or 9th from the Moon—20 days. (7) The Sun in the ascendant or the 8th and in aspect to Saturn and Mars—will finally inflict death on the patient.

Notes: If the time of commencement of a disease is not properly noted, then the application of these principles and such othere will lead to pit falls only. For detailed information, see Ch. 13 of Prasna Marga.

हेरायां कष्टके भौमो भवेद्यस्य प्रजावतः । न च केन्द्रगतो जीवो जायते मृत एव सः ॥:३॥ श्च होरागतः सुर्यों न च केनद्रे बहस्पतिः । निषने पापकः कश्चित् जातमात्रो विन्द्यति ।।४।। होरायां कष्टके चन्द्रो न च केन्दरे बृहस्पतिः । निषने पापकः कश्चित् जातमात्रो विन्यति ॥ex11 ब्रेष्काराजामित्रयती यस्य स्याङ्रदार्लाग्रहः । होरागतः शशाखूव्च सद्यो हरति जीवितम् ।८६१ प्रहाः समेपुर्वह्यो निचने यस्य जन्मनि । मासं वा सप्तरात्रं वा तस्यायुः समुदाहृतम् ।८७।।

## क्षनद्वर्च होरायां निषने च महीसुतः । न च देवगुरुः केन्द्रे मृतगर्भः प्रसूयते Icall

83-88. Mars in Lagna or other angles while Jupiter is not so, causcs death. The Sun in Lagna, Jupiter nonangular and malefics in the 8th cause immediate death. Similar results should be predicted if the Moon is in the ascendant, while Jupiter is in a place other than angles and malefics are in the 8th. Malefics in the 7th from the rising decanate while the Moon is in the ascendant will also cause death at birth. (In this case, the Moon shall be in the rising decanate while malefics shall occupy the 19th decanate). A •majority of planets in the 8th—primarily malcfics—indicate longevity ranging from seven days to one month. A dead child is born if Saturn is in the ascendant while Mars is in the 8th, with Jupiter being not in an angle.

Notes: Mars in Lagna, 4th, 7th or 10th can affect longevity, if not capable of causing infant death, when Jupiter does not protect the native by being angular, according to sloka 83 above. Other principles in the above group of slokas may accordingly be understood.

यः प्राग्विलग्ने द्रेक्कारस्तत्समानो यवा ग्रहः । भवेत्त्रिकोर्यः पापस्तत्समानफलो भवेत् ।।८१। सौरे व्याधिमवाप्नोति मरणं धरखीसृते। सूर्षे स्याद्धाचिर्वकल्यं मरणं नात्र संशयः ।E०।।

89-90. MALEFICS IN RISING DECANATE AND TRINES THEREOF: Saturn in the rising decanate while a malefic is in the 5th and another in the 9th—the latter two should be exactly on trinal decanates as Saturn is—indicates disease. Mars in the place of Saturn in the said combination denotes (infant) death while the Sun so placed indicates discase, deformity or death.

Notes: If Saturn is in the first decanate of the rising sign—another malefic in the first decanate in the 5th Rasi

and yet another malefic in the 9th Rasi will cause discase of a serious nature. If Saturn is in the 2nd or 3rd decanate in the rising sign, then the other malefics referred to should also be in corresponding decanates. Similarly, Mars replacing Saturn will denote early death while the Sun replacing will cause deformity, discase or death.

श्रथ होरागतो भौमः केन्द्रस्थञ्च भुगोः सुतः ।

स बै मरखमाप्नोति होरायां पुनरागते ।६१।।

91. DEATH: If at birth Mars is in the ascendant while Venus is in an angle. Mars causes death when he resumes the same point in the next round in his transit.

Notes: Let us assume that Mars is in Aquarius ascendant and Venus is in Lco, an angie. After the birth, when Mars reached Aquarius again, it is detrimental to the longevity of the child. So to say, such a child will not live for more than the pcriod identical with one full course of the zodiac by Mars. He normally takes fortyfive days to traverse one sign of the zodiac.

गुरुस्त्रिकोणे होरायां होरेवद्च महीमुतः । तस्यान्यतरकेन्द्रस्थः सद्यो जोवितनाशनः ।९२।

92. If Jupiter is in a trine, ascendant lord in the ascendant and Mars in an angle to one of them, it can cause immediate dcath.

Note: In this case, Mars should be angular to Jupiter or Lagna.

न नंघने ग्रहः कविवत् पापो होरागतोऽय वा । केन्द्रब्बन्यतमे जीवो जीवत्यष्टशतोसरम् ॥६३॥ न केन्द्रे कविचिदाग्नेयो न त्रिकोणे न नेघने । गुरुचुक्रो च केन्रस्यौ जीवेदष्टशताधिकम् ।९४। यदि होरागतः चुक्रः केन्द्रब्वन्यतमे गुरुः । नंधने न च पापः स्यात् स्विशं जीवते शतम् ॥E५।

राशी कर्कटहोरायां गुरुचुको समन्वितौ। शुश्वन्नयुतो वाऽपि निघने न च कञ्चन ॥0६। न स केन्द्रगताः पापा न त्रिकोणे न नैषने ।

## तह्वायुरमरप्रह्यं निश्चयेन च कोर्यते ।६७।।

93-97. EXCEEDINGLY LONG LIFE: The native lives for 108 years if the 8th house is not occupied, while ascendant is free from malefic residence and Jupiter is in an angle. A span of over 108 years is granted if Jupiter and Venus are in an angle, while there are no malcfics in trines and the 8th. Predict 120 years of life if Venus is in the ascendant, Jupiter is in an angle other than the ascendant and the 8th house is not occupied by malefics. Similar span of life is given if Jupiter is in Cancer ascendant along with Venus or the Moon while the 8th house is not occupied. If angles, trines and the 8th house are not occupled by malefics, one’s longevity is comparable to that of Gods.

## विधतास्तव्ययलग्नत्रिकोखागाः क्षीराचन्द्रसंयुक्ताः । वाक्त क्लिमः शुभदेशहृद्यमाना गतायुरेष स्याव् IE-I

98. ELAPSED LONGEVITY: Weak Moon along with malefics if posited in the 8th, 7th, 12th, 1st, 5th or 9th and unaspected by benefics will only grant elapsed longevity, i.e. immediate death.

Note: The occupation of weak Moon and malefics in the said houses, without benefic aspect is capable of causing death in childhood.

## थोगे ब्लिनः स्थानं स्वं वा लग्नं गतेऽपि वा चन्द्रे ।

## बलवति पार्यट्च्टे वर्यान्तरमुत्युकालः स्यात् ।६६।

99. TIMING INFANT DEATH: When the child with powerful infant mortal combinations is in its first year, death will come to pass if the Moon with strength passes over the natal position of another qualified planet, or on its

own natal position (i.e. Janma Rasi) or natal ascendant. In all these cascs, the Moon should be aspected by malefics.

Notes: The sloka gives clue to the effect of finding out the day of death in case of powerful child mortality. The Moon with strength in transit if moves over her own natal position or over the natal ascendant or over another planet's natal position capable of curtailing longevity can cause thc end of the child.

रविज्न्द्रभौचनुरुभिः कुजगुसौरेन्दुभिस्तर्यकस्यः । रविश्निभौमशशाङ्कर्मरणं खलु पञ्चभिर्वषेः ।।१००।। रक्षित्ता युक्तः स्विजोऽसौर्यह क्रो विनाशयति नूनम् । एकादशा भिर्ववेदेवाङ्केऽपि स्थितं जातम् ॥१०१। लग्ने रविमन्दकुजाः शुक्रम्हे सप्तमेशची क्लीराः । हट्रो म देवगुर्णा सप्तभिर्वविनावायति ॥१०२।। केन्दे र विमुषिततनुः क्षितिमुतमन्दावलोकितोऽ्य युतः । वर्धचतुष्के चन्दो मार्यति किमत्र परितेन ।१०३।

100-103. DEATH IN CHILDHOOD: Any one group of the following planets joining in one house will cause death in five years: (1) The Sun, the Moon, Mars and Jupiter, (2) Mars, Jupiter, Saturn and the Moon, (3) The Sun, Saturn, Mars and the Moon. Even if a child is on the God’s laps, it will die in 11th year if the Sun conjunct Mercury is aspected by malefics. Death within 7 years of age will occur if the Sun is in the ascendant along with Mars and Saturn while declining Moon is in the 7th house in a house owned by Venus, providcd this combination is not aspected by Jupiter. Should the Sun and the declining Moon be in angles and be conjunct with or in aspect to Mars and Saturn, the child has a longevity of only 4 years. In these cases, t.e, where child mortality is indicated, there is no use of making any calculations.

Notes: Each group of planets as above being together at birth can cause child mortality. The Sun and Mercury being in conjunction and in aspect to severe malefics can

cause death in the eleventh year. If the ascendant is Aries or Scorpio, and is occupied by the Sun, Mars and Saturn while the 7th house is tenated by declining Moon (i.c. the lunisolar angle being less than 180 degrees), death in the 7th year is denoted. Jupiter’s aspect (on the 7th or the ascendant) is a redeeming feature. The Sun in one angle, the Moon of dark half in another angle (i.e. the Moon with diminishing angular distance with reference to the Sun— for example when the Sun is in the 10th, dark half Moon can be in the 7th), and the company of or aspect to Mars and Saturn (ie. Mars and Saturn can individually be connected with the luminaries or both of them either connccted with the Sun or the Moon), the 4th ycar of age wil be fatal for the child. The author says that when child mortality is powerful in the horoscope, no other calculations need be made in the matter of longevity.

## लग्नाधिपतेशचन्द्रो मरखपदस्थोऽतिकृष्णतां यातः ।

क्रूरेः सक्लहट्रो न चुर्मः सर्बेस्त्रिभिस्तु मार्यति ॥१०४।।

104. DEATH IN THIRD YEAR: If waning Moon or Lagna Lord be in the Marana Pada and aspected by all the malefics, but not benefics, the length of life is only 3 years.

Notes: Marana pada has boen interpreted as 8th house by some. But it actually means the Rasi counted so many signs away from the Rasi in which the 8th lord is as he is away from the 8th. Note how many signs away the 8th lbrd is from the 8th. So many Rasis away from the 8th lord’s Rasi is Marana Pada. For example, take a Capricorn native. His 8th Rasi is Leo lorded by the Sun. Assume that the Sun (8th Lord) is in Aries, i.c. 9 Rasis away from Leo, the 8th. So count 9 Rasis from Aries, the Sun sign. We get Sagittarius, thus, as Marana Pada.

लग्नाषिपतिः पापः शशिनोऽशे रिःफगो यदि च चन्त्रात् । श्रूरविलोव्यमानो मारयति शित्युं नवभिरव्हः ॥१०५।।

दर्शानभागे सौम्याः क्रूराद्चाहृद्यके प्रसवकाले । राहुर्लग्नोपगतो यमक्षयं नयति पञ्न्वभिर्ववेः ।१०६१। राहुः सप्तमभवने वातिसुर्यनिरीक्षितो न चुभटङ्टः । बशभिर्दाम्यां सहितरब्दर्जातं विनावायति ॥१०७१।

105-107. If all benefic planets are in the visible half of - the zodiac while malefics are relegated the invisible half- Rahu be specifically in the ascendant—at the time of birth, the longevity is only 3 years. Should Rahu be in the 7th house in exclusive aspect to the luminaries and be unaspected by others, death before the age of 12 is denoted.

घटासिह्वृदिचकोदयङ्तस्थितिर्जीवितं हरति राहुः । पार्य निरीक्ष्यमार्षः सप्तमिर्तनिविचितं वर्षेः ।।१०८।। केतोर्वयः पूर्व पश्चादुल्कादिपवननिर्धाताः ।

रौद्रे सार्यमुहतें प्रार्णः सन्त्यज्यते जन्तुः ॥१०११। क्षीणं यदा राशाङ्क पश्येब्राहुः समागतं पार्पः। मार्यति तदा विवर्सेनिर्व्याजं कतिपर्यरेव ।११०।

108-110. If Rahu is in Aquarus, Leo or Scorpio identical with the ascendant and is aspected by malefics, the life span is only 7. If Dhuma Ketu rises followed by fall of meteor and strong gale and if birth occurs in Raudra Muhurta or Sarpa Muhurta, the subject invites early death.

Notes: Rudara Muhurta is for four ghatis from Sunrise while Sapra Muhurta rules for 48 minutes from Sun set. If declining Moon is conjunct malefics, aspecting Rahu, death within few days of birth is to be declared.

कुम्मे विवाति शशाडको भागे मृत्युं तथेकाविशाख्ये । सिंहे च पञ्गमेशे वृषे च नबमे तर्थबोवतः ।१११॥ श्लिनि त्रिव्युक्ते मेषे च तथाङ्कमे दिवाति मृत्युम् । करकाँटके द्वाराविधे तुलिनि चतुर्ये मृगे विश्ने ।११२।

कन्यायां प्रयमेवे घनुर्धरेऽङ्वादशे भवे दशमे । मियुने च द्वाविशे शशी प्रसुतस्य मरराकरः ॥११३। ये भुक्ताः वाविनोंशा जन्मनि वर्वर्गतेस्तु तावद्धः । मरणं हि जन्मभाजामप्यन्तकबद्धरक्षाराम् ।।११४।

111-114. FATEFUL DEGREES OF THE MOON: The Moon is capable of inflicting death if she is in any of the following degrees at birth: Aquarius 21°, Leo 5°, Taurus 9°, Scorpio 23°, Aries 8°, Cancer 12º, Libra 4º, Capricorn 20°, Virgo 1°, Sagittarius 18°, Pisces 10° and Gemini 22°. If the Moon is in such degrees at birth, death is indicated in the corresponding year and the native cannot be protected by even God Yama.

Notes: Should the Moon be in any degree as above, death occurs in the corresponding year. For example, if the Moon is in 23 degrees of Scorpio, the person dies at the age of 23.

Horasara states that the fateful degrees for the Moon from Aries onwards are 26, 12, 13, 25, 24, 4, 26, 14, 13, 25, 5 and 12 and the same for Lagna are from Aries onwards: 8, 29, 22, 22, 25, 14, 4, 23, 18, 20, 21 and 10, Phala Deepika (Ch. 13) has the same view of Prithuyasas.

Jataka Tatwa (B- 138) also denotes fateful degrees for the Moon from Aries onwards respectively thus: 8, 9, 23, 22, 5, 1, 4, 23, 18, 20, 21 and 10. These are death inflicting degrees in the respective year of age.

These can be used in horary also relating to questions on death of an ailing person.

एवं सर्वं प्रयत्नेन जायमानस्य देहिनः । होरास्यानानि केंद्राखि चिन्तनीयानि तद्यया ॥1११५१। चिन्तयेज्जायमानस्य स्थानं राक्षिषु वित्यज्ञः । वृहस्पतिन्न खां जीवस्तस्मान्मृत्युं बृहस्पतेः ॥ ११९।

सहजे तुर्ये पञ्चम-सप्तम-नवमेषु क्षशामे । एकावशे च लग्ने प्रोक्ता जीवस्थितावब्दाः ॥।११७॥। पञ्चवशषट्समेतद्चत्वारिशत्तर्यव विश्च । त्रिशच्चत्वारिशत् पञ्चाशत्तु प्रमाच्च होरायाः ॥ ११८।।

115-118. Assess the strengths of ascendant, angles etc., at birth. Jupiter is life spirit of all natives. The time of death should be known through Jupiter. If he is in the 3rd, 4th, 5th, 7th, 9th, 10th, and 11th or ascendant, he proves fatal at the age of 5, 10, 46, 20, 30, 40 and 50 respectively.

Notes: Jupiter in 3rd will prove fatal at the agc of 5, in the 4th at the age of 10, in the 5th at the age of 46 ard so on. Should however thc ascendant and kendras are with strength, he does not prove fatal in the years specifed.

इति कस्यारावर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां चरिष्टाध्यायो दश्मः ।१०।।

Thus ends the 10th chapter entitled Evils at Birth in Kalyana Varma’s Saravali.

## Chapter 11

## ऋथ चन्द्रारिष्ठमंगाध्यायः ।।११।

## Evils and the Moon

संमूतारिष्टास्या भङ्गस्तेषां यया यथा भवेश्योगः। तानर्गमतो बक्ये प्रधानभूता यतस्तेऽत्र ।१॥ उडुपतिक्तरिष्ठानां भड्गस्तावन्विक्प्यते पूर्वम् ।  
• सम्यक् वेषार्तामपि ययामतं ब्रह्मपूर्वाताम् ।।२।

1-2. I now explain the combinations that are capable of nullifying the said evils, for these are important in the Science of Horoscopy and initially explained below are the yogas to counteract the evils emanating from or afflicting the Moon, as done by expounders like Lord Brahma.

Notes: In this chapter, the combinations to counteract the evil influences of the Moon are discussed, so that there is no fear of child mortality. fz means child mortality' or f in this chapter and 12thchapter. But they should not be profitably interpreted as though there will be no blemish in other matters, like marriage, living etc.

सर्वर्गगनभ्रमर्णह ब्रश्वन्द्रो विनावायति रिष्टम् । श्रापूर्यमाखमूर्तिर्यथा नृपः सन्नयहेब्दन् ।३। चन्त्रः सम्पूर्णतनुः चुक्रा निरीक्षितः सुहुद्धागे। रिच्टहराखां श्रेष्ठो वातहराखां यया बस्तिः ॥४II

3-4. If the Moon is Full and is aspected by all heavenly bodies, all evils are countered just as lawbreakers are destroyed by the king. Venus aspecting the Full Moon while the latter is in friendly divisions is capable of obstructing evils just as abdomenal remedics are ablc to cure windy diseases.

Notes: The full Moon in aspect to all planets is capable of nullifying evils against longevity. If she is full and is aspected by Venus, then also the evils for short life will

vanish. In the latter case, the Moon should simultaneously be in friendly divisions.

In this chapter, the high degree of knowledge of medical branch of Kalyana Varma should be usefully noted for its worth.

परमोच्चे विविर्तनुभू गुतनयनिरीक्षितो हरति रिष्टम् । सम्यत्विरेकवमनं कफपित्तानां यया दोषम् ॥५॥ चन्दः बुभवर्गस्थः क्षोखोऽपि चुनेक्षितो हरति रिष्टम् । फलमिव महातिसारं जातीफलवल्कलक्वथितम् ६।।

5-6. Should the Moon be in her deep exaltation and be aspected by Venus, evils are countered just as phlegm and bile are controlled by induced vomitting. If the Moon be in the divisions of benefic planets and be aspected by benefics, she will not prove evil though she may be waning, just as nut meg bark treats dysentery.

सप्ताच्टमवव्ठस्थाः शाशिनः सौम्या हरन्त्रिव्टफलम् । पार्यरमिश्चाराः \_\_\_कल्यारावृतं बचोन्मादम् ।। युक्तः चुक्रफलबायिभिरिन्दुः सर्वेनिहत्यरिच्टानि । तेषामेव \_\_\_ ब्रयशे लवरात्र तिपूरवच्छ्रब्रोगम् ।।।

7-8. Benefics in the 7th, 8th and 6th from the Moon remove all adversities, just as madness is removed by Kalyana Grita. Same is the case if the Moon is conjunct benefics and be in the decanates owned by benefics, just as saline water controls ear diseases.

Notes: So to say, a powerful Adhiyoga can give great longevity. Further, if the Moon is conjunct benefics and falls in benefic's decanate, long life is indicated.

श्रापूर्यमार्मूतिर्दावतभागे बुभस्य यादि चन्द्रः । रिच्टं नयति विवारं तक्राम्यासो यया गुबजम् ॥eI सौम्यक्षेत्रे चन्द्रो होरापतिना विलोकितो हुन्ति । रिष्टं न वीक्षितोऽत्येः कुलाञ्चनाकुलमिवान्ययता ११०४

benefic's 9-10. Should the full Moon be in a Dvadasamsa, all evils are removed just as butter-milk checks piles. If the Moon occupies a benefic’s Rasi and receives the Lagna Lord’s aspect, adversities for short-life are counteracted as even a noble housewife can destroy the famc of the descent if she seeks union with other men.

क्रर्भवने व्याङ्को भवनेशनिरोक्षितस्तदनुवरमे । रक्षति विद्युं प्रजातं हुप् इव घनं प्रयत्नेन ॥११॥ जन्माधिपतिर्बलवान् सुट्ट्द्धिरभिवीक्षितः धुर्भमंङ्गम् । रिष्टस्य करीति सदा भीररिव प्राप्तसंग्रामः ।।१२।।

11-12. Even though the Moon may be in a house owned by a malefic or in such Vargas, she does not prove evil if aspected by her dispositor. This is comparable-to a miser protecting his money. If the dispositor of the Moon is strong and be in aspect to benefics or friendly planets, the Moon is not capable of harming, in comparison to a timid person who cannot face others even though he may be in war field.

Notes: A planet proves favourable though it may be in malefic divisions if it is aspected by the lord of the Rasi it occupies. Secondly, the strength of the Moon sign lord is essential to promote longevity of a person.

जन्माविपतिर्लग्ने हृष्टः सर्वेविनाशायति रिष्टम् । घुष्टोषश्विवलाम्यां प्रत्येककृताञ्जनं यथा शुक्लम् ॥ १३॥ स्वोच्चस्यस्वमुहेऽयवापि सुट्दा बगऽपि सौम्येऽथवा संपूर्णः शुभवोक्षितः श्षघरो वगे स्वकीयेऽथबा । शत्ररामवलोकने न पतितः पार्यर्युक्तेक्षितो

रिष्टं हन्ति सुदुस्तरं दिनपतिः प्रालेयराक्षि यथा ॥१४॥

13-14. If the lord of the Moon sign is in Lagna and is aspected by all planets, all evils are removed, just as the ointment of holy fig and bamboo bark removes whiteness. The Moon in her exaltation, own, friendly or benefic Vargas, aspected by benefics but not malefics, counteracts all evils

as the Sun does remove fog.

वशिनोऽन्त्ये बुधसितयोराये क्रू रेषु वाक्पतौ गगने । दुरितं चातुथिकमिव न्यति मुनिकुसुमरसनस्यः ॥१५।

15. Just as quartan fever is removed by inhalling Agashya flower, all evils are checked by Mercury and Venus in the 12th of Moon, malefics in the I Ith and Jupiter in the 10th.

Notes: Mercury and Venus in 12th, malefics from 1 1th and Jupiter in 10th, all with reference to Moon, are capable of contributing to a long lease of life. When Mercury and Venus are in the 12th of Moon, the Sun will also be close to her. Even though she may be weak, her angularity to Jupiter is the kcy to the above Yoga at birth.

लग्नेश्वरस्य चन्द्रः षट्त्रिदशायहियुकेषु शुभहृष्टः । क्षप्यति समस्तरिष्टान्यनुयाते नृपतिरोष इव ।१६। एको जन्माधिपतिः परिपूर्णबलः धुर्भटव्टः । हन्ति निवाकररिच्टं व्याध्र इव मृगान् वने मत्तः ॥१७।।

16-17. Should the Moon be in the 6th, 3rd, 10th, 1 1th or 4th from the natal ascendant, and be aspected by benefics, evils are removed as though a king destroys his opponents. The lord of the Moon sign is singly capable of removing all evils if he is with full strength and aspected by benefics, similar to an inebriated tiger throwing away a thousand deers.

Notes: In this chapter, the author has described the role of the Moon in the matter of longevity and these combinations be usefully analysed.

## इति कल्याखवमेविरचितायां सारावल्याँ एकादशः ।।११।

Thus ends the I1th chapter, entitled Evlls and the Moon” in Kalyana Varma's Saravalt.

## Chapter 12 अथ सकलारिष्टमङ्गाध्यायः ॥१ २।। Evils Cancelled at Birth

सर्वानिमानतिबलः स्फुरदंभुजालो लग्ने स्थितः प्रशमयेत् सुरराजमन्त्री। एको बहूनि दुरितानि सुदुस्तराखि भक्त्या प्रयुवत इव चक्रघरे प्रर्यामः ।१॥

1. A very brilliant Jupiter (i.e. not being devoid of rays) is capable of counteracting all evils if he is in the ascendant just as a single sincere prostration before Lord Vishnu, the wearer of Sudarsana Chakra, relieves one of the greatest sins.

Notes: The previous chapter dealt with evils countered with reference to the Moon while the present chapter deals with evils checked by other planets, viz. Jupiter etc.

Jupiter will be in fall in Capricorn and his position in Lagna may not give long life as the 12th lord will be in fall in Lagna. He also rules the 3rd, which is another house concerning longevity. If, however, in being so, he has strength acquired by number of rays (vide ch. 36), he can turn a mender and confer long lease of lifc.

अलैम्यग्रहैर तिवर्ले विवलंदच

पार्ष-

लग्नं च सौम्यभवनं शुभ्हृष्ट्युक्तम् । सर्वापवामिरहितो भवति प्रसुतः प्रजाकरः खलु यथा दुरितेप्रहार्ताम् ।२।।

2. Benefics with strength and malefics without it remove all evils if ascendant falls in a benefic’s house and is aspected by benefics, Just as those who worship planets will have no sins left to their liability.

Notes: There are four conditions to be free from longevity evils: These are (I) Ascendant should fall in benefic's house, like Pisces, Libra etc., (2) It should be in aspect to a benefic, (3) Bencfics in general should be strong, (4) Malefics should be weak.

In the above bargain, 8th lord though may be a malefic should have strength as his weakncss will only imbalance the good of the yoga referred of above.

पापा यदि शुभवर्गे सौम्येटब्टाः शुभांधवर्गस्थः। विष्यन्ति तदा रिष्टं पति विरक्ता यथा युवतिः ॥३।। राहुस्त्रिष्ठलामे लग्नात् सौम्येनिरीक्षितः सद्यः । वाशयति सर्वदुरितं मार्त इव तुलसंधातम् ।४। शीर्षोदयेषु राशिषु सर्वर्गनाधिवासिभिः सुतौ। प्रह्तिस्यैव्चारिष्ट विक्रियते घृत्तमिनाग्नवडम् ॥ 1

3-5. If malefics occupy the Rasis and Amsas of benefics (l.e. such benefic divisions) and be in aspect to benefics, evils are removed just as a dejected lady remains away from her husband. Should Rahu be in the 3rd, 6th or I 1th from the ascendant and be aspected by benefics all evils are destroyed as though wind carries away a heap of cotton with it. If all planets be in direct motion and in Seershodaya signs, all blemishes are removed just as ghee is burnt out in fire.

Notes: (1) Malefics should be in benefic's divisions,. (2) Rahu in 3, 6 or 11 is aspect to benefic, (3) All planets should be in Seershodaya signs and be in direct motion.

Each of the three combinations is capable of giving long lease of life.

तत्काले यदि विजयी शुभग्रहः शुभनिरीक्षितोऽ्वद्यम् । तर्जयति सर्वरिष्टं मारुत इव पादपान् प्रबलः ॥६। परिविष्टो गगरवरः क्रंद्च विलोकितो हुरति पापम् । स्वानं सन्तिहितायां कुतं यथा भास्करप्रहुषे ।७।

6-7. A benefic planet victorious in planetary war at birth and essentially aspected by another benefic, destroys all evils just as a gale can uproot trees. If a planet has a halo ‘(in the sky) and is aspected by a malcfic, cvils arc countcred just as the solar eclipse day bath in Kurukshetra docs.

Notes: There must be a benefic involved in planetary war and must be victorious and be in aspect to another benefic. Then only, the yoga comes to pass. Two planets are said to be in war (except the Sun and the Moon), when they are in conjunction and within a longitude of one degree. The one with less longitude wins the war. For example, Jupiter and Mcrcury are in Taurus at 24° 10' and 24° 29' respectively. Jupiter is declared successful as he is behind Mercury.

स्निग्धमृदुपवनभाजो जलदाञ्च तर्थव खेचराः शास्ताः । स्वस्याः क्षराच्व रिष्ट वमयन्ति रजो यथाम्बुधारौघः IcII उदये चागस्त्यमुनेः सप्तर्षोखां मरीचिपूर्वाखाम् । सर्वारिष्टं नव्यति तम इव सुर्योदये जगतः ।।EI

8-9. If there is a plcasant brecze at the time of birth, and the sky is with pleasing clouds and the heavenly bodies be with splendour, the evils disappear in a fillip just as rains settle dust. If birth takcs place when Agasthya and others of Saptharshis (Alfa Carini or Great Bear) rise, all evils are destroyed, just as darkness disappears when the Sun rises.

Notes: The Seven Seers (or Saptarshis or Alfa Carini or Great Bear) traversc each lunar mansion in 100 years. They are Marichi, Vasistha, Angirasa, Atri, Pulaha and Kratu. Arundhati is always close to the sage Vasistha. The star Marichi is in the east, Vasishta is to his West, Atri is to the west of Vasishta; close to Vasishta is Paulastya followed by Pulaha and Kratu.

श्रजब्वरकाकविलग्ने रक्षति राहुः समस्तपीडाम्यः ।

पृथ्योपतिः प्रसन्नः हतापरावं यया पुरुषम् ॥१०१।

योतेस्त्रभागमपरेः स्वं स्वं गगनेचरेरिदं क्रियते ।

## सज्ज्ञं नष्टमनिष्टं शामयति तिमिरं यथार्ककरः ।११।।

10-11. Rahu in the ascendant identical with Aries Taurus or Cancer protects the native from the clutches of all evils Just as a pleased king forgives the crimes of a person. If all the planets (not of course the nodes) are in their own decanates, all evils are removed just as the Sun removes darkness.

Notes: If Rahu is in ascendant identical with Aries or Taurus, or Cancer, the person enjoys great longevity. This view is supported by Horasara (p. 63), Jataka Martanda, Saravartha Chintamani (ch. 11, S. 16), Jataka Parijata' (ch. 4, S. 90), and Jatakadesa Marga (ch. 4).

बहवो यदि शुभफलदाः सेटास्तत्रापि - शीर्यते रिष्टम् । सूर्यात् त्रिकोख इन्दी यर्यव वात्रा नरेन्त्रस्य ।१२।।

12. If at birth, many planets are capable of giving benefic results, even then there is no blemish just as the king gets success in journey undertaken when the Moon is in a trine to the Sun.

Notes: There are two important clues in this sloka. Firstly, if a majority of planets are beneficial to the native, the other malefic combinations get nullifed.  $H e r e$  ts  $a$  clue to the effect that a wisdom-based Judgement is necessary in astrology and rushing to conclusion by surface study is detrimental. The second clue is: if the Moon is trine the Sun, at the time of a query, success in journey is indicated.

गुरुचुक्रो च केन्द्रस्थो जीवेद्वर्षशातं नरः ।

गृहानिष्टं हिनस्त्याशु चन्द्रानिष्टं त्येव च ।१३।

13. Should Jupiter and Venus be in angles, evils arising out of the various Rasis (i.e. Bhavas) and of the Moon are countered. And the native lives for hundred years.

Notes: Although there may be affliction to longevity through other bhavas, yogas and other planets, if only Jupiter and Venus are angular from Lagna, the subject has a long lease of life of 100 years. In such yoga, even the blemish to Moon, if any, is perfectly balanced and the Moon remains actually unafflicted.

बन्ध्वास्पदोदयविलग्नगतः कुलीरे

गोर्वाख्नाथसचिवः सकलर्च वर्त्रः। जूके रविन्दुतनयावपरे च लामे दुदिचक्यात्रुभवनेव्वमितं तंदायुः ।१४।

14. If Jupiter and the Moon be in Cancer, identical with 4th, 10th, or the ascendant, Mercury and Saturn be in Libra while others are in the 11th, 3rd and 6th, one has illimitable longevity.

Notes: The moon and Jupiter should be in Cancer as Lagna or in 4th in Cancer for Aries ascendant or in 10th in Cancer for Libra ascendant. Additionally, Saturn should be

exalted joining Mercury, while the Sun, Venus and Mars should confine to the 3rd, 6th or 1 1 th from Lagna. This rare yoga bestows a very long lcase of life on the native concerned.

एते सर्वे भड्गा मया निरुक्ताः पुरातनाः सिद्धाः ।

यर्जातिदेवविदो नरेन्द्रवाल्लम्यमायान्ति ॥१५।

15. I have thus explained the Yogas that are capable of countering the evils, as laid down by our great ancestors. May the astrologers please thc king with the knowledge of these.

Notes: The yogas explained in this and the previous chapter are guidelines only to know the combinations that counter child or infant mortality and relate only to longcvity. As alrcady cautioned, thesc do not necessarily work for all kinds of defccts as antidoets. For example, a certain combination explained in this context will not necessarily offsct an imbalance in one’s education, marriage, progeny etc.

इति कल्पाखाबर्मविरचितायां साराबल्यां स्लारिष्टभज्ो नाम ह्वादशोऽध्यायः ।।१२।।

Thus ends the 12th Chapter entitled “Cancellation of evils at birth" in Kalyana Varma’s Saravalt.
