## Chapter 13

## झाथ चन्द्रयोगाप्यायः ॥ १३।

## Lunar Yogas

सुनफाऽनफा-दुरुधरा भवन्ति योगाः क्रमेख रविरहितः ।

वित्तान्त्योभयसंस्यः

केरववनबान्धवाद्विहगः ।१।

1. SUNAPHA, ANAPHA AND DURUDHURA YOGAS: Excepting the Sun, any other planet in the 2nd from the Moon causes Sunapha yoga, in the 12th Anapha yoga and in both the 2nd and the 12th Durudhura yoga.

Notes: Just as the Sun, the nodes are also not considered in this kind of Raja yogas.

The Moon should have a planet cither in the 12th or in the 2nd (or at least with it) so that she remains balanced. Otherwise, Kemadruma yoga is caused, as could be seen in the following verses and notes thereof. Unless the Moon is thus well-balanced, the native is not likely to fecl comfortable in life. He will face only obstacles in day-to-day affairs. The said 3 yogas as per the present sloka are caused only by Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Vcnus and Saturn. Each position by a different planet manifests varied effects, vide infra.

एते न यदा योगाः केन्ग्रह्वाजितः सवाडुब्च । केम्मोऽतिकष्टः शाशिनि समस्तग्रहाहृष्टे ।२।।

2. KEMADRUMA YOGA: Kemadruma yoga is formed if at least one of the said three yogas (viz. Sunapha, Anapha and Durudhura yogas) is not present or if thc Moon’s angles are devoid of planets or if she is unaspected by all the planets. Such yoga causes extreme difficultics.

Notes: Elaborate notes and comments can be found in my English translation of Horasara (P. 230) which the reader may consult.

Kalyana Varma uses म uे thereby requiring the

Moon to be in aspect to all planets in deviation to standard textual versions. As otherwisc, he states, the Moon produces Kemadruma yoga. This condition is quite strange.

सुनकानफासरूपास्त्रिवाद्योगा (३०) स्त्रसंगुखा वष्टिः (१८०) ।

संख्या दौर्धुराखां

प्रस्तारविधौ समाख्याताः ।३।।

3. DIFFÈRENT PERMUTATIONS: By all possible permutations and transmutations, one can see 30 kinds of Sunapha yoga, 30 kinds of Anapha yoga and 180 kinds of Durudhura yoga.

Notes: We have learnt that any planet other than the Sun in the 2nd of the Moon causes Sunapha yoga. For example Mars in the 2nd of the Moon causes Sunapha yoga. Similarly, the other four i.e. Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn individually cause Sunapha yoga. Thus 5 kinds of this yoga is formed in the first instance. With group of two, these 5 plancts can severally form 10 kinds of additional Sunapha yoga. For example Mars plus Mercury or Mars plus Jupiter or Mars plus Venus and so on and forth. This is the second set of 10 permutations. In group of 3 cach, these 5 plancts can form again ten permutations in the third instance. For example, Mars + Mercury + Jupiter + Venus or Mars + Mercury + Venus and so on and so forth. In the 4th instance, these 5 plancts in group of 4 cach can causc  $5$  diffcrcnt permutations, such as Mars  $\star$  Mcrcury  $\dagger$  Jpitcr  $\star$  Vcnus or Mars  $\star$  Mcrcury  $\star$  Juplter  $\star$  Saturn ctc. In the 5th instance all the 5 planets in the 2nd give rise to one transmutation. Thus these 5 diffęrent transmutations cause in thcir order 5 + 10 + 10 + 5 + 1 = 31 kinds of Sunapha yoga.

Same transmutations can be conceived with these 5 plancts in the 12th for Anapha yoga also and we get 31 different yogas. Similarly, disposing thesc 5 planets in the 2nd and 12th in group of 1 to 5, we can conceive 180 kinds of Durudhura yoga transmutations.

Parasara in his Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra (ch. 37; sloka 13-my Engllsh translation) obscrves thus: The yogas

(like Sunapha, Anapha, Durudhura, Kemadruma, Chandradhi yoga etc.) prevail over the other good or bad yogas in the horoscopc. Hence one must beforehand analyse the good and bad effects of yogas from the Moon.

श्रीमान् स्वबाहुविभवो बहुधर्मशीलः

शास्त्रार्थ विद्रबहुयवाः स्वगुराभिरामः । शान्तः सुखी क्षितिपतिः सचिवोऽव वा स्यात्

सूतः पुमान् विपुलधीः सुनफाभिधाने ।।

4. EFFECTS OF SUNAPHA YOGA: One born with Sunapha yoga will be wealthy, will have self-made prowess (or will reach an exalted position of his own efforts), be very virtuous, be learned in Sastras and their meanings, be very famous, virtuous (like Lord Rama)1, be peacefully disposed2, happy, be a king or a minister and be highly intelligent.

Notes: The word¶%apart from meaning “happy” has various meanings like: agreeable, charming, sweet, joyful etc.

One born with Sunapha yoga will be a king or equal to a king, be wise, rich, famous and will earn wealth by his own efforts, (vide my English translation of Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra ch. 37, sloka 8). Prithuyasas in Horasara (Ch. 19) also adds that this yoga gives self-made wealth. The native will have rich knowledge of Sastras and be a commentator thereof.

Jatakabharana in chapter on "Chandrayogas” asserts that the native will have self-made wealth, will enjoy fame, wisdom, and all kinds of happiness, be a scholar and a minister of the king.

Thus, planets other than the Sun in the 2nd house counted from the Moon promote the prospects of the native’s having self made riches. If the planet in question be Venus or Jupiter or Mercury, the subject can earn a fair 1. f is another reading.

2.W1; is another reading meaning splendorous.

amount of wealth. Exalted Saturn or exalted Mars will also give good results in this regard.

वाग्मी प्रभुईवित्वानगढः सुशीलो

भोक्तान्नपानकुसुमाम्बरका मिनोनाम् । स्यातः समाहितयुखः सुख्यस्तचित्तो

योगे निशाकरकृते रबनके सुबेवः ॥५।

5. EFFECTS OF ANAPHA YOGA: One born in Anapha yoga will be eloquent in speech, magnanimous, virtuous, will enjoy food, drink, flowers, robes and females, will be famous, calm (\_t) in disposition, happy, pleased and will possess a beautiful body.

Notes: According to Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra (ch. 37), this yoga makes one a king, free from diseases, blessed with all kinds of happincss, virtuous, famous and beautiful.

One born in Anapha yoga will have political wisdom and moral training, be steadfast, will know many sastras, be blessed with enormous wealth, be capable of attracting the fair sex and be honoured (vide P. 228 of Horasara.) Thus, this yoga gives plenty of wealth, if for example 12th from Moon is occupicd, particularly by exalted Venus; or when the Moon is in Leo while Jupiter is in Cancer; or when Mercury is in Virgo with Moon in Libra.

Dundhiraja in his Jatakabharana also praises Anapha yoga. The effccts are: good conduct, kingly status, beauty etc.

The Anapha and Sunapha caused by a debilitated planet will only yield adverse results. For example when the Moon is in Libra with Venus in 12th in Virgo, Anapha yoga produces only immoral conduct instead of good conduct. Such natives cannot enjoy physical comforts, long life etc.

वाग्युठिविक्रमगुणः प्रचितः पृथिव्यां

स्वातन्त्रयसौव्यधनबाह्नभोगभोगी

## वाता कुटुम्बजनपोषरपलब्धकेदः सङ्गत्तवान् दुरुपुराप्रभवो धुरिस्थः ।।।

6. EFFECTS OF DURUDHURA YOGA: One born in Du. rudhura yoga will be famous on the earth for his speech. wisdom, valour and virtues. He will enjoy freedom, comforts, wealth and conveyances. He will be liberal but will come to grief by maintaining his family members. He will have good behaviour.

Notes: One born with this yoga, will enjoy the comforts generated by him, be charitable, and be endowed with wealth, conveyances and attendants (See ch. 37 of BRIHAT PARASARA HORA SASTRA).

According to Horasara, P. 228, such a native will enjoy luxuries and wealth. He will be an eloquent speaker, be fond of battles and be an army chief. He will be in a state of bewilderness, be kingly and sacrifying in nature. He will have cattle.

Vide Jatakabharana, the native will enjoy wcalth, conveyances, lands etc, be equal to a king and be happy with women.

Compared to Sunapha and Anapha yogas, Durudhura yoga is more effective as it is not that frequent to occur. The Moon gets enormous strength to yield good effects if she is planked by planets on both the sides. The native, according to Parasara, is destincd to experience comforts of his own.

कान्तान्नपानगृह्वस्त्रसुहृद्धिहीनो

दारिद्युदुःखगदर्दग्यमर्लरुपेतः

प्रेष्यः खलः सकललोकविरुद्धवृत्तिः ।

केमद्रुमे भवति पार्थिववंशजोऽपि (1911

7. EFFECTS OF KEMADRUMA YOGA: The native with Kemadruma yoga will be deprived of life, drinks, food, residencc, robes and friends, though he may belong to regal scion. He will suffer from poverty, grief, sickness and be

dirty. He will live by hard labour, be wicked and be inimically disposed to one and all.

Notes: The native with this yoga will be blameworthy, be devoid of intelligence and learning and will expcricnce penury and misfortunes, vide BRIHAT PARASARA HORA SASTRA, ch. 37, sloka 12. Prithuyasas in Horasara emphatically declarcs that even a king with this position will beg (p. 230).

According to Jatakabharana, one with this yoga will resort to unacceptable professions, be dirty, will present himself in bad dress, be a servant, will live in distant countries, and be devoid of wife, sons, friends and money even though he may be of a royal scion.

Manasagari, however, attributes long life to the native while describing other bad effects. It adds that the said kemadruma is defunct if the Moon is in a bencfic’s Rasi and is conjoined with Mercury or Venus or Jupiter.

केन्द्रादिस्थेग्रहैर्योगाः कीर्तिता येऽनफादयः । तान् प्रवानान् समानान् स्वांश्चन्द्ररूपान् विचिन्तयेत् ।।८।। भौमादीनां बलं वेशं जासस्य च कुलं बुषः । विज्ञाय प्रवदेत् सम्यक् सुनकादिक्तं फलम् १६।

8-9. OTHER KIND OF SUNAPHA ETC.: The kind of Sunapha yoga etc. arising from the Moon’s angles are (also) important. These yogas give effects according to the state of the Moon (i.e. depending on her rays, being Full, New etc.). The strength of the planets, viz. Mars etc., causing these yogas, native's country and the race he belongs to must be properly understood before evaluating Sunapha etc.

Notes: A diffcrent set of Sunapha etc., may be noted as under:

If a planet other than the Sun is in the 4th of the Moon, Sunapha yoga is formed. Similarly the 10th house position causes Anapha and occupations of both the 4th and 10th cause Durudhura yoga.This account is given by Shrutakirti.

Another view is of Jeevasarman which indicates that the 2nd and 12th houses in the Navamsa chart with reference to the Moon shall be considered for these yogas.

It seems that Srutakirti's yogas too have meaning in this context.

विक्रमवित्तप्रायो निष्ठुरवचनदचमूपतिद्चण्डः । हिस्रो बम्भविरोघी सुनफायां भौमसंयोगे ॥१०।। सुतिश्ञास्त्रगेयकुक्लो धर्मपरः काव्यकन्मनस्वी च । सर्वहितो इचिरतनुः सुनकायां सोमजे भवति ॥।११।

10-11. RESULTS OF SUCH YOGAS CAUSED BY MARS ETC.: If Sunapha yoga is caused by Mars (by being in the 2nd of the Moon), the native will be valorous, wealthy, cruel in speech, be an army chief, will be fierce, torturous, proud and inimical. If Mercury be the cause of Sunapha yoga, the native will be expert in Vedas, Sastras and music. He will be virtuous, and a poet. He will be high-minded, intent on thinking of good for all and will possess a brilliant physique.

Notes: Sunapha yoga caused by different planets from Mars to Saturn have different effects. While analysing a given horoscope, these effects further vary according the Rasi involved and the lordships of the planet concerned. For example, Mars may cause Sunapha yoga for a Capricorn native which can give enormous wealth while for a Virgo native this planet will only give very ordinary wealth as he is the lord of the 8th also.

Jataka Parijata, ch. 7, slokas 86 and 87 attribute similar results as per Kalyana Varma for Mars and Mercury individually causing this yoga. According to Manasagari, this yoga by Mars causes perennial enmity with others. For Mercury same results as per our text and Jataka Parijata are mentioned.

In fact, Manasagari and Jataka Parijata have exactly followed our author’s view for effects of Sunapha, Anapha and Durudhura yogas by various planets.

विद्यावायँ स्यातं नृपति नृपतिप्रियं वाऽपि । सुकुटुम्बधनसमृद्ध सुनकायां सुरगुरुः कुर्ते ।१२।। स्त्रक्षेत्रवित्तविभवञ्चतुष्पदाढचः सुविक्रमो भवति । नृपसत्क्तः सुधीरो दक्षः शुक्रेर सुनफायाम् ॥१३।। निपुरामतिर्यरामपुरेनित्यं संपूजितो घनसमृद्धः । सुनकार्यां रविपुत्रे क्रियासु युक्तो भवेद्धीरः ॥१४।।

12-14. Should Jupiter cause Sunapha yoga, a person so born will have high learning, will be famous and himself be a king or dear to a king. He will have a good family and have plenty of wealth. Venus will give wife, lands, wcalth, prowess, quadrupeds and valour. The native will further be honoured by king and will also be courageous. Should Saturn be in the 2nd of the Moon causing Sunapha yoga, the native will be skilful, be worshipped by the people in his village and city and will have wealth galore. He will be devoted to his work and be brave.

चोरस्वामी घृष्टः स्ववश्यो मानी रखोत्काः क्रोधी। श्रेष्ठः इलाच्यः सुतनुः कुजेऽनफार्यां सुलाभञ्च ।१५। गन्धर्वलेखनपटुः कविः प्रवक्तः नृपाप्तसत्कारः । दचिर्तनुस्त्वनफार्यां प्रसिद्धकर्मा बुषेन भवेत् ॥१६।। गरम्भीर्यस्त्वमेवास्थानरतो बुद्धिमान् नृपाप्तय्षाः । श्यनफाया त्रिव्यगुरी संजातः सत्कविर्भवति ॥१७१। युवतीनामतिसुभगः प्रखायी कलितिपस्य भोगवान् कान्तः । श्यातः कनकसमृद्धस्त्वनफायां भार्गवे भवति ॥१८।। विस्तीर्णभुजो नेता गृहीतवाव्यञ्चतुष्पदसंमृद्धः । दुर्वनिताया भक्तो गुरा सहिनञ्चार्कपुत्रेख ।१९ ॥ 15-19. ANAPHA YOGA’S EFFECTS GIVEN BY MARS

ETC.: If Anapha yoga is caused by Mars-being in the 12th from the Moon—the native will head a band of thieves, will be arrogant in disposition, be honoured, fond of war, given to anger, good, praiseworthy, good-bodied, and proud. If by Mercury, the native will be an expert in singing, dancing and writing, will be a poet, and an ablc orator. He will be conferred honours by the ruler, will have a brilliant body and will perform famous deeds. Should the yoga be caused by Jupiter, the native will be majestic, strong, intelligent, fond of assembly, famous through a king, and will be a poet. Venus causing this yoga gives an exceedingly great liking for women. The native will be dear to the king and will enjoy pleasures. He will be splendorous, famous and will have abundant gold. If Saturn causes this yoga, the subject will be broad-shouldered, be a leader, be inclined to keep up his. commitments, be rich with quadrupeds, devoted to an infamous lady and be virtuous.

22. If the Moon has Venus and Mars on either side, the native will have a virtuous wife, will be fortunate, argumentative, pure, skilful, will likc doing excrcises and be bold in war.

23. If Saturn and Mars plank the Moon, the native will be the husband of a bad lady. He will save a lot of money. He will have vices. He will be given to anger and be a talebearer. He will win over his enemies.

## घर्मपरः शास्त्रज्ञो वाचालः सत्कविर्धघनोपेतः ।

त्यागयुतो विख्यातो बुधनुरुमध्ये स्थिते चन्द्रे ।२४१।

24. Should Jupiter and Mercury be on either side of the Moon, the native will be virtuous, learned in Sasthras, garrulous, be a good poet, be wealthy, capable of making sacrifices and be famous.

प्रियवाक् सुभगः कान्तः प्रवृत्तगेयादियु प्रियो भवति ।

## सेव्यः शूरो मन्त्री बुधसितयोर्दुश्धुरायोगे ॥२५॥।

25. If Durudhura yoga is caused by Mercury and Venus, the person will be a sweet speaker, be fortunate, splendorous, bcautiful, be fond of dance, singing etc., be served, vallant and be a minister.

## वेशाङ्गदेशं गच्छति वित्तपरो नातिविद्यया सह्हितः ।

चन्त्रेज्येषां प्रज्यः स्वजनविरोधी ज्ञमन्द्योर्मध्ये । २९१।

26. If Mercury and Saturn plank the Moon, the native will move from one country to the other to earn money. He will not be much educated, will be worshipped by others but be inimical to his own men.

◦ घृतिमेधाशौर्ययुतो नोतिज्ञः कनकरत्नपरिपूर्णः ।

## स्यातो नृपकृत्यकारो गुरुसित्योर्वुद्धुरायोगे ॥ २७॥।

27. Should Jupiter and Venus cause this yoga, by being on either side of the Moon, the native will be courageous, intelligent, will have political wisdom, will possess gold and gems, will be famous and be in the employ of a king.

## सुखनयविज्ञानप्तः प्रियवाग्विद्वान् धुरन्धरोऽप्यार्यः ।

## शान्तो घनी सुरूपद्बन्ने गुरुभानुजान्तस्थे ।२८।।

28. If Jupiter and Saturn be such planets as to cause Durudhura Yoga the native will be happy, will have knowledge of politics, will be sweet in speech, learned, peaceful, wealthy and good looking.

## वृद्धचरितं कुलाइयं निपुणं स्त्रीवल्लभं घनसमृद्धम् ।

## नृपसत्तं बहुधनं कुरुते बन्द्रः सितासितयोः ।२६।।

29. One who has Durudhura yoga caused by Venus and Saturn will conduct himself like an old man (i.e. so mature), be chief in his race, skilful, dear to women and will have plenty of money. He will be honoured by the king and gather abundant wealth.

Notes: In assessing these Sunapha, Anapha and Durudhura yogas—for that matter any Raja yogas, we should take due precaution. Should the planet be in fall, be in an inimical house and the like, the efficacy of the yoga is reduced. On the other hand if it is exalted, is in a friendly house etc., the results will be still better.

## सूर्यात् केन्द्रादिगतो निशाकरः स्वल्पमध्यसूयिष्ठान् ।

कुर्यातक्रमेरा धनचीर्नपुर्विज्ञानविनयांश्च ।३०।।

30. LUNAR YOGAS FROM THE SUN: If the Moon is in

an angle from the Sun, little effects will be felt in regard to money, wisdom, skill, learning and modesty. These are medium if the Moon be in Panaphara (i.e. 2nd, 5th, 8th and 11 th) and exceedingly good in Apoklima (i.e. 3rd, 6th, 9th and 12th).

Notes: Should the Moon be in the 6th or 12th from the Sun, shc promotes one’s learning, wealth, wisdom etc.

Phala Deepika designates these yogas (vide ch. 6, sloka 14) thus: The Moon in an angle of the Sun causes Adhama yoga; in Panaphara it causes Sama (#\Psi) yoga and in Apoklima, the yoga is termed as Varishta yoga. The results ascribed are (sloka 18)—wealth, conveyances, fame, happiness, intelligence, wisdom, learning etc., in little quantity for Adhama yoga, in medium quantity for Sama yoga ( # T) and the highest for Varishta yoga.

श्रौत्पातिकः कुत्तनुनिज्ञि चाष्यहृद्यो दृश्यो दिवा शिक्षिरगुर्मयज्ञोकदः स्यात् । एवं स्थितः समकनं पृथिवीपतिबं यातोऽन्यया प्रकुरुते परिपूर्णमूतिः ॥३१।

31. MOON IN VISIBLE AND INVISIBLE HALVES: In the case of a night birth, if the Moon is with Parivesha (halo), and be declining in the invisible half of the zodiac (ie. being posited between lagna Bhava madhya and the centre of the descendant—7th house), or declining Moon in day time be in the visible half (i.e. between the centres of the descendant and the ascendant), even results will be felt in respect of fear, grief etc., (that is these positions confer bad results). The Full Moon gives kingdom, if she be contrarily posited.

Notes: The Full Moon in the case of day birth in the invisible half and in the case of night birth in the visible half confers kingdom.

लग्नादुपचयसंस्यः शुर्मः समस्तरमहाधनो द्वास्याय् । मध्यं चकेनाचममेवं चन्त्रादपि तद्ूनः ।३२।।

32. DHANA YOGAS: If all benefics are posited in upachayas l.e. 3, 6, 10 and 11 the native will acquire much wealth. Two benefics confer moderate wealth while one confers little wealth in the said upachaya. These can be deduced from the Moon also.

## श्रधियोगादयोऽन्येऽपि मयाऽत्रेव न कीर्तिताः । नृपयोगा यतस्ते हि बव्ये सत्त्रंब तानहम् ॥३३।।

33. There are other Raja yogas like Adhi yoga arising from the Moon. I have not dealt with them here which I have done infra.

Notes: The other yogas, namely Adhi yoga etc., are still superior that are capable of conferring high status on the native. The yogas like Sunapha, Anapha and Durudhura are not comparable to such kingly combinations but are only moderate.

इति कल्याएवर्मविरचिताया सारावल्यां चन्द्योधो नाम त्रयोदशोऽध्यायः ॥१३।।

Thus ends the 13th chapter entitled “Yogas from the Moon” In Kalyana Varma's Saravali.

## Chapter 14 ऋथ सूर्ययोगाध्यायः ॥१४।

## Yogas from the Sun

सूर्याडिचयर्गत्राक्चिडितीयर्गश्वन्दर्वजितेब्रेशिः । उभयस्थितंग्रहेन्दर्भयवरी नामतः प्रोक्ता ।१।।

1. VASÀ, VESI, UBHAYACHARI YOGAS FROM THE SUN:

Excluding the Moon (and nodes) a planet in the 12th from the Sun causes Vasi yoga, in the 2nd Vesi yoga and in both the 12th and the 2nd Ubhayachari yoga.

मन्वह्रां स्थरवचनं परिमूतगरिश्रमं नतोरध्वतनुम् । क्ययति यवनाधिपतिवेशिसमुत्वं तथा पुरुवम् ।२।। 2. RESULTS OF VESI YOGA: One born in Vesi Yoga will be weak-sighted, firm in his word, be hard working and will have a bent body. So say Yavanas.

Notes: Sage Parasara observes in his Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra (ch. 38) that such a native will have even sight, be of truthful disposition, has a long body, will be indolent, be happy but not be much wealthy.

Phala Deepika distinguishes these yogas caused by benefics as Subha Vesi, Subha Vasi and Subhobhayachari; and by malefics as Papa Vest, Papa Vasi and Papobhayachari. The adjectives themselves are suggestive. The ones, so to say, by benefics cause good results while 'the ones by malefics give adverse effects.

वसुसंचर्यवित्ससुहृत्स्याहेक्षी सुरगुरो भवति जातः । भीवः कार्योद्विग्नो लघुचेष्टो भूगुसुते पराचीनः ।३। परिकर्मको दरिदो मुदुविनीतो बुचे सलज्जस्च । मार्गलधुः क्षितिपुत्रे प्रोपकारी नरो बेशौ ।४।।

## पर्दाररतद्चण्डो वृद्धाकारः शठो वृखी । भवेन्मनुष्यः सघनो याते वेशि शनैश्चरे ।५।।

3-5. EFFECTS OF VESI YOGA CAUSED BY DIFFERENT PLANETS: Should Jupiter cause Vesi Yoga, the native will accumulate money, be learned and be good-hearted. If Venus produccs this yoga, the person will be timid, will face obstacles in his missions, will have pleasant (or swift) movements1 and will be defeated. Mercurial participation indicates that the native willbe a servant (ufi), will suffer penury, be soft-spoken and be modest. He will be bashful. Mars denotes that the native will resort to base means (Tif), but be helpful to others. If it is Saturn that causes Vesi yoga, one will join others' wives, be wicked, will resemble an old person in appearance, be fraudulent in disposition and be contemptuous. He will, however, have wealth.

उत्सृष्टवचाः स्मृतिमानुद्योगयुत्तो निरीक्षते तिर्यक् ।

## पूर्वशरीरे पुचुलो नृपतिसमः सातिविको वाशौ ॥६।

6. EFFECTS OF VASI YOGA: One born in Vasi yoga will possess excellent speech (voice), good memory, be employed, will have looks towards, sideways, will possess a strong physique about the waist, will be equal to a king and be a genuine person.

Notes: Satwika has several meanings thus: genuine, virtuous, amiable, vigorous, good, real etc.

षुतिससर्वबुद्धियुक्तो भवति गुरौ वाज्ञिके वचनसारः । शुरः व्यातो गुर्वान् यशस्करो भार्गवे पुरुषः ISI प्रियभाषी रुचिर्तनुर्वाद्यां स्याङ्गबोधने पराज्ञाङत् । सङ्ग्रामे विष्यातो भूमिसुत्ते नतन्यमाग्य्वच ।८।। बरिक् खलस्वभावः स्यात्परदर्यापहारकः । गुरहेवी मुनिस्त्रशो गते वाश्चि प्रानंश्चरे IEI 1. Wygl may also indicate 'mean behaviour'.

7-9. EFFECTS OF VASI YOGA BY DIFFERENT PLAN- ETS: If Jupiter should cause Vasi yoga, the native will posscss courage, strength and wisdom. He will kecp up his word. In the case of Venus, the person will be valorous, famous, virtuous and reputed. Mercury emanating this yoga will make onc speak swectly, be beautiful and obey others’ orders. The Vasi yoga caused by Mars indicates that the subjcect will be victorious in war, famous and will live with his own fortuncs. If by Saturn, the native will be a busincssman, wicked, will stcal others' moncy, will hate eldcrs and will be the husband of a pious lady.1

## संनिरीध्य - रवेर्वीर्य ग्रहवार्तां चाप तर्दतः।

रार्पंवासङ्गमात् सर्वे कलं ब्रूयाद्विचक्षणः ॥१०11

10. The strcngth of the planet Sun and that of the plancts causing these yogas and their association with various Rasis and Amsas should be undcrstood and all results be declared accordingly.

## सर्वसहः सभदः समकायः सक्षिधरो विपुलसत्त्वं।

नात्युच्चः परिपूर्तो विद्यातृवतो भवेदुभयचर्याम् ॥ ११।।

11. EFFECTS OF UBHAYACHARI YOGA: One born under Ubhayachari yoag will have forebcarance, will be very fortunate (NNz), even bodicd, firm, profoundly strong, not very tall, full of everything, lcarncd, happy, will have many servants, will protcct his relatives, be equal to a king, cvcr enthusiastic, and will enjoy all plcasures.

## सुभयो बहुभृत्यधनो बन्धूनामात्रयो नृपतितुल्यः ।

नित्योत्साही हुट्टो भुङ्क्ते भोगानुभयचर्यांम् ॥१२।। इति कस्याखवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां सूर्ययोगो नाम चतुर्दशोऽध्यायः ।।१४)।

Thus ends the 14th chapter entitled "Yogas from the Sun” in Kalyana Varma's Saravali.

## Chapter 15 श्थ दियहयोगाप्यायः ॥१९॥

## Conjunctions of Two Planets

यवनाचार्येवं द्वेद्विग्रह्योगेषु यत्फलं प्रोक्तम् । तदहमपहाय मत्सरमधुना व्ये विशेषेख ।१।।

1. INTRODUCTION TO TWO-PLANETARY YOGAS: Vriddha Yavanas have stated the effects of combinations of two planets (in one house). I state those below sans ego.

(४० चैं०) युवतीनां वसगः स्यादविनोतः कूटवित्प्युलवित्तः । श्रासवविक्र्यकुजलो रव्युडुपत्योः ्रियानिपुल्ः ।।२।।

2. SUN-MOON YOGA: One who has the Sun and Moon together will be at the disposal of his wife (or females), immodest, be a metallurgist ( can be interpreted also as "able to decetve"), will be quite wealthy, and be an cxpert in sale of intoxicants.

Notes: Should these two be together, the native's wife will have an upper hand. He will be an expert machinist, will have affected eye sight and will suffer eye diseases. This combination makes one an expert, immodest and lighthearted. He may take things casually.

According to Jatakabharana, with this combination, the nattve will be skilful in poison, machinery, selling and buying, be capable of making fictitious articles, be very libidinous, and honourable.

(र० मं०) प्रोजस्वी साहसिको सूर्वो बनसत्वसंयुतोऽन्तवाक् । पापमतिर्वघनिरतो रविकुजयोः स्यात् प्रचण्ड्च ।३।।

3. SUN-MARS YOGA: Should the Sun and Mars be in one house the native will be splendorous, valorous, dullwitted, strong, be a liar, be sinful, disposed to kill (or torture) and be fierce.

Notes: This combination makes one a liar, foolish and strong. The native may be engaged in Defence, police service etc.

According to Jatakabharana, this combination makes one very splendorous, strong, foolish, very emotional and valorous.

(१० बु०) सेवा कृदस्थिरधनो रविज्ञयोः प्रियवचा योर्थः स्यात् । श्रार्यः क्षितिरतिर्दायितः सतां च बलरूपवित्तविद्यावान् ॥४।।

4. SUN-MERCURY YOGA: If the Sun and Mercury are in one house, the native will be in service, will have unsteady wealth, be sweet in speech, will have fame and money, be noble, dear to king and good people and will possess strength, beauty and learning.

Notes: The Sun and Mercury together make one learned, gentle, honourable and famous. This conjunction goes in the name of Buddhaditya yoga. Jataka Martanda is however of the view that this Raja yoga is effective only for Leo, Aries and Virgo but not for other ascendants. Such people can easily attain fame in life.

Jatakabharana states that one with the Sun and Mercury yoga will be an eloquent speaker, be a king's minister, will earn money through service, and be skilful in arts and Vedas.

(४० यु०) बहुपर्मों नृपत्तचिवः सुबुद्धिमान् वित्रसंअधान्तर्वः । सूरवे बृहत्पतिवुते भवेटुपाध्यायसंश्च ॥५।।

5. SUN-JUPITER YOGA: If the Sun and Jupiter be together, one will be virtuous, be a minister of king, will gain through friends, be with good mind1 and be a preceptor.

Notes: The conjunction of Jupiter and the Sun is favourable for those pursuing teaching profession. This yoga makes one wcalthy and very famous. One becomes spiritual if these are well-placed being together.

As per the statement of Jataktbharana, one with this yoga will live by Purohitya (performing religious rites of various familics as laid down in Sastras), be a king's minister, will gain wealth through friends, be wealthy, helpful to others and skilful.

(र० घु०) वास्त्रप्रहरराविद्यावाक्तियुतो नेत्रुर्बलदचरमे । रङ्गज्ञो रविसितयोः स्त्रीस्ञ्चाल्लब्धबहुलधनः ॥६।।

6. SUN-VENUS YOGA: If the Sun and Venus be together, one will be skilful in use of weapons, be mighty, weak-sighted in old age, will be able to amuse the public and will have abundant money earned through women.

Notes: The Sun and Venus affect eye sight. One with this combination is highly libidinous. If Venus is ahead of the Sun in longitude, then the former is very favourable in general matters.

Vide Jatakabharana, the native with the yoga of the Sun and Venus will be interested in music, archery etc., be weak-sighted and be happy in regard to wife and friends.

## (१० ३०) घातुज्ञो धर्ममयः स्वकर्मनिरतः प्राङ्वसुतदारः । निजवंदागुर्णः शुद्धः शानिरव्योरस्प्षील्च ।।।

7. SUN-SATURN YOGA: If the Sun and Saturn are together, the native will be a metallurgist, be virtuous, mindful of his own duties, will lose his wife and son, will 1. पggाq is another reading which means plentiful

have the qualities suitable to his race, will be pure but not so upright.

Notes: One becomes learned with this combination. He is very mature in behaviour as an elderly person but however will be disadvantageously placed in family front, if these two are adversely placed in the 6th/8th/12th.

According to Jatakabharana, one with the Sun-Saturn combination will be skilful in sale of minerals, be virtuous, will possess good qualities, be happy with wife and sons and be wealthy.

## (चं० मंठ) शूरो रस्त्रतापी मल्लोऽसृग्वेदनार्तदेह्र्च ।

मृच्वर्मधातुश्चित्री

कूटज्ञ्चन्त्रकुजयोगे IcI

8. MOON-MARS YOGA: Onc who has the Moon and Mars together, will be valorous, brave in war, be a boxer, will suffer bloody imbalances or diseases, will manufacture articlcs of mud, skin and minerals and will be an artisan and a metallurgist.

Notes: This is not a good position for maternal relationship. This combination often throws heavy affliction to blood. This is favourable for pottermen, skin dealers and leather dealers, business in minerals etc. One can achieve proficiency in boxing, with the help of this combination apart from riches.

According to Jatakabharana, this combination makes one bereft of conduct, mean-minded, valorous, businessman, belligerent, inimical to mother and sick.

(चं० बु०) काव्यकवास्वतिनिपुखाः सघनः स्त्रीसंमतः सुरूपश्च । स्मितवदनः क्षशिबुधयोर्धर्मरुचिः स्याहिशिष्टगुरः ।EI

9. MOON-MERCURY YOGA: One who has the Moon- Mercury combination will be expert in poems and fables, be wealthy, amiable to his wife, beautiful, smiling-faced and will be endowed with distinct virtucs.

Notes: Mercury and the Moon together cause fame

and honour through women, literary success etc. This can however weaken one's virility.

Jatakabharana states that the native who has Moon and Mercury together at birth will be an eloquent speaker, rich, beautiful, kind, modest, affectionate to wife and religlous.

## (चं० गुठ) हृढसोह्दो विनोतः स्वबन्धुसंमानकृद्धनेशादत्र ।

## गुविन्होः शुभक्षीजः सुरद्धिजानां हितो भवेत्पुर्षः ॥१०।।

10. JUPITER-MOON YOGA: One with Jupiter and the Moon together will be firm in friendship, modest, respectful towards his relatives, wealthy, virtuous and regardful of Gods and the wise.

Notes: The Moon-Jupiter yoga gives great intelligence and female children. The native will be broad-minded and wealthy. Should this conjunction occur in the 10th, the Moon simultancously being waxing, the subject will attain high state in life.

Vide Jatakabharana, if these two planets are together at birth, the native will be modest, be attached to his duties and helpful to others.

## (चं० घु०) स्रग्गन्धासम्बरयुक्तः क्रियात्रिधितः कुनप्रियोऽत्यनसः । क्र्यविक्रपेषु कुवतः श्शिभार्गवयोः सदा योगे ।११।।

11. MOON-VENUS YOGA: Should the Moon and Venus be together, the native will be endowed with flowers, incense and clothes, will know to perform duties (fut also means rites), be dear to his race, very lazy and expert in buying and selling.

Notes: This combination gives fighting tendency according to Kasinatha, author of Lagna Chandrika.

According to Jatakabharana one will deal with textiles, will have vices, be of good conduct, and will like good clothes etc., if this yoga is present at birth.

## (चं० श०) जीर्णवप्जनरमरो गजाश्वसम्पालक्षो 'विगतज्ञीलः ।

वश्यो वियनः पुरुषः पराजितः स्याच्छन्ञाजुशनियोगे ।१२।।

12. MOON-SATURN YOGA: Should the Moon and Saturn be together, the native will possess a decayed wife, be dear to public, will breed elephants and horses, be devoid of virtues, be under the control of others, unwealthy and defeated.

Notes: These two together promote tendency of seeking sexual pleasures with aged women. The native's wife may be elder than him. If found in woman's horoscope, widowhood will follow.

Vide Jatakabharana, the Moon-Saturn company will make one attend upon many women, attached to whores, be of bad conduct, born of others' loins and unwealthy.

## (मं० बु०)स्त्रीदुर्भगोऽल्पवित्तः सुबर्चलोहप्रकारकः स्थपतिः ।

बुष्टस्त्रीविधवानां कुजबुषयोरोषधत्रिया निपुर्यः ॥ १३1

13. MARS-MERCURY YOGA: If Mars and Mercury are together at birth, the native will have unlucky wife, little wealth, will work with gold and iron, be an architect, will keep a wicked widow and be expert in making medicines.

Notes: efि means king, architect, master-carpenter, women’s attendant, kubera etc. But, as Mercury rules architects, the proper interpretation is architect. It is not however wrong to apply the various meanings as above, except "Kubera” denoting wealth.

Mars-Mercury concjunction promotes thieving disposition. The native will pursue jobs related to gold, metals etc. Good salesmanship will follow this combination, but however it is unfortunate for marital bliss.

According to Jatakabharana, the nattve with Mercury-Mars conjunction will be an expert in boxing, be attached to females, will deal with medicines, and will produce golden and steel articles.

(मं० यु०) शिल्पश्रतिज्ञास्त्रज्ञो मेधावी वाविविज्ञारदो चतिष्यान् ।

## प्रस्त्रपियत्रधानः सुरगुरुकुजयोः सच्चागतयोः ।१४॥

14. MARS-JUPITER YOGA: If Mars and Jupiter be together at birth, the native will be an artisan, Vedic expert, intelligent, expert in speech, wise and fond of using weapons.

Notes: One with this yoga will be skilful in Sastras and mantras, wise, be an army chief, king or the head of a town or village, as per Jatakabharana.

## (मं० घु०) पूज्यो गाह्तप्रधानो गखितज्ञः पर्युबतिभी रतो घूर्तः । चूतानृतशाठ्चरतो विटद्च सितदधिरसंयोगे ॥१५।।

15. MARS-VENUS YOGA: One who had Mars and Venus together at birth will be worshipped, be chief among his men, be a mathematician (or an astrologer), and be fond of gambling, untruth etc.

Notes: Mars and Venus in conjunction show unhappy sign in marital front. The native may seek extra-marital relationship unless well-aspected by other favourable planets. Diseases of vagina or penis will result from this yoga.

According to Jatakabharana, with these two planets together, one will enjoy many women, will gamble, be a liar, be honourable and be inimical to all.

## (मं सं०) धातिवन्द्रजालकुवालः प्रवञ्वकस्तेयकर्मकुदलइच । कुजसौरयोविघर्मः शस्त्रविवध्नः कलिरचिः स्यात् ॥१६।

16. MARS-SATURN YOGA: If Mars and Saturn be together, one will be a metallurgist, be expert in jugglery, be deceitful, skilful in thieving, troubled by weapons and poison and be fond of quarrels.

Notes: The native with this yoga will make weapons etc., be a skilful warrior, robber, liar and be unhappy, vide Jatakabharana.

(बु चु०) नृत्तविधेवज्ञाता राज्ञोऽपि च गेयवाद्यविन्मनुजः ।

बुधगुरुयोगे मतिमान् सौख्ययुतो जायतेऽवद्यम् ॥१७१।

17. MERCURY-JUPITER YOGA: One who has Mercury and Jupiter together will be a noted dancer, scholar, singer and instrumentalist. He will be wise and happy.

Notes: According to Jatakabharana, the native with this yoga will have knowledge of music, be a justice, be modest, very beautiful, courageous, liberal and fond of scents.

(बु०ु०)म्रतिशयबनो नयज्ञो बहुविस्पो वेदवित्सुवाष्यः स्यात् ।

गोतज्ञो हास्यरतिर्बुधसितयोर्गन्धमास्यरविः ।१८॥।

18. MERCURY-VENUS YOGA: Should Mercury and Venus be together at birth, the native will be abundantly rich, a politician, an artisan, will study Vedas, be good in speech, will know to sing, make fun and like scents and flowers.

Notes: According to Jatakabharana one, with this yoga will be supreme in his descent, be sweet in speech, be delighted, will maintain many people, be virtuous and wise.

(बु० शठ) ऋ्खवान् बम्भप्रायः प्रपञ्चकः सत्कविर्गमनशीलः ।

निपुखः सोभनवाक्यो बुबशानियोगे पुमान् भवति ॥१ह11

19. MERCURY-SATURN YOGA: One who has Mercury and Saturn together at birth, will contract debts, be proud, deceiving, an able poet, intent on moving, expert and will possess auspicious speech.

Notes: According to Jatakabharana, one with this yoga will be fickle-minded, fond of quarrels, skiiful in arts, and will maintain many.

(चु झुठ)ज्जीवति विद्यावादेविश्चिष्टधर्मस्थिता प्रमार्युतः । श्रीवतितयोर्मनुष्यो विशिष्टदारो भवेन्मतिमान् ॥२०॥।

20. JUPITER-VENUS YOGA: One who has Jupiter and Venus together at birth will live by education and arguments, will follow a highly virtuous path, will have accurate conception or notion (of things), and will have a supreme wife.

Notes: Vide Jatakabharana, one with this yoga will be learned in many branches, will arguc cven with scholars and be happy with friends, sons and wealth.

## (गुर शक) धूरो वितसमृद्धो नगराधिपतिर्यशास्वी च ।

## श्निजीवयोः प्रधानः श्रेर्तिसभाप्रामसंघानाम् ।।२१।

21. JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: A native who has Jupiter and Saturn together at birth will be heroic, will have plenty of wealth, will be chief of the city (mayor etc.), famous, and will be the head of an assembly, a village or an association.

Notes: The combination is congenial for political positions, like assembly speakers, village hcads, mayors etc., as well as heads of associations etc. Such people are also found as successful doctors and advocates.

Jatakabharana has almost similar views as of Kalyana Varma, but adds that the native will achieve his aims.

1 (छु० श०) दावविवारख्वक्षः क्षुरचित्रादमादिकर्म्चिल्पी च ।

## मल्लोऽ्टवः पञ्चुपतिः शनिसितयोगे पुमान् भवति ॥२२।।

22. VENUS-SATURN YOGA: If Venus and Saturn are together at birth, one will be expert in breaking wood, be a barber, painter, or sculptor, boxer, be intent on wandering and be owner of quadrupeds.

Notes: If these two are strong in a good house, one will take to good professions described above, viz. painting, boxing etc. Should they be weak, the native will resort to mean professions, viz., barber, wood-breaking etc.

उक्तं क्ल गगनगा मञ्चन्योग्धगर्वस्थिताः ।

श्रघमादिविकल्पेन कुर्वन्ति विक्ति तथा ।२३।।

23. Whatever results have been stated as above are likely to become increased or decreased according to mutual good or bad vargas occupied by the planets in question.

इति कल्याराबर्मविरचितायां साराबल्यां द्विग्रह्योगो नाम पञ्चदशोध्यायः ॥ १५।।

Thus ends the 1 5th chapter entitled “Conjunction of two planets” in Kalyana Varma’s Saravali.

## Chapter 16

## अथ त्रिपहयोगाध्यायः ॥१६। Conjunctions of 3 Planets

निर्लज्जः पापरतो यन्त्रज्ञः शत्रुदारणे घूरः। श्लिलक्रियासु कुवतः सहस्थितेः सूर्यवशिभौमः ॥१।

1. SUN-MOON-MARS YOGA: Should the Sun, the Moon and Mars be together at birth, the native will be bereft of shame, be sinful, will have knowledge of machincry, will destroy enemies, be brave, and will be expert in any kind of work.

Notes: the yoga of Sun-Moon and Mars in combination will promote the ability to produce machinery, spurious articles etc. This can also cause seminal defects. The native will be hard of progeny.

तेजस्वी निपुरामतिः शास्त्रकलागोष्ठिपानरतः । नृपकृत्यकरो घोरो रविशाशिक्षिजेः सहेकस्थः ।२।।

2. SUN-MOON-MERCURY YOGA: If the Sun, Moon and Mercury are together, the native will be splendorous, very intelligent, will be fond of arts, assembly and drinks, be in royal service and bold.

Notes: The native with this yoga will do well to take up government jobs or agencies from government. This is congenial for political success, literary pursuits and achieving wealth.

## क्रद्घो मायानिपुखः सेवाकुशलो विदेशगमनरतः । मेघावी चपलमतिः सहस्थितर्कश्चिजीवः ।३।।

3. SUN-MOON-JUPITER YOGA: Should the Sun, Moon and Jupiter be in one house, the native will be given to anger, be cunning, expert in service, intent on going to

foreign places, be intelligent and fickleminded.

Notes: These three planets together can make one pious, affectionate to relatives, a minister and firm in disposition. He is more cunning than sincere. He is fickleminded, learned and skilful in his profession.

परघनहरणे निपुखः पर्दाररतञ्च शास्त्रनिपुर्श्च ।

रविचन्द्रदेत्यपूज्य रेकर्स्थर्जायते

मनुजः ।४।।

4. SUN-MOON-VENUS YOGA: Should the Sun, Moon and Venus be in onc housc at birth, the native will steal others' money, will be a debaucher and be expert in Sastras.

Notes: The combination under discussion can give thieving tendency, vices (particularly immoral nature) and aversion to good acts. The native will be pronc to dental diseases or may have unsightly teeth. He may not get on well with his mother. The marriage may be a delayed one. It gives physical beauty if undisturbed by Saturn or a node. This is a good position to win over the encmics..

कामी विवादकुशलो मूर्खः परतन्त्रगो दरिद्रद्च ।

सुर्य निवाकरर वर्ज रेक्स्यर्जायते मनुजः ॥५।।

5. SUN-MOON-SATURN YOGA: If the Sun, Moon and Saturn be together in one house, the native will be lustful, be expert in arguments, be a dunce, be at the disposal of others and be poor.

Notes: The combination as above is good for legal profession. If these are not well-aspected, poverty will result. The native will be quite libidinous and be not virtuous. Horse-breeders will do well with this yoga. Metallurgists can also look forward to good position. In all, this yoga causes many general failures in one’s undertakings.

भबति स्यातो मल्तः साहसिको निष्ठुरो विगतलज्जः ।

घनमुसकलत्र रहितः सहस्थिरतर्कंकुजसौम्यः ।६।।

6. SUN-MARS-MERCURY YOGA: If the Sun, Mars and Mercury are in one house at birth, the native will be famous, hard-hearted, shameless and be devoid of wealth, sons and wife.

Notes: This combination may have more telling effects on the marital life than on the question of children. If it occurs in the 7th house, marriage is very adverse. Success in money matters is very limited. If, however, the influence of Mars is less in this yoga, the native will be learned and wealthy.

बचसि निपुर्गो महार्बः क्षितिपतिमन्त्री च भूपति्वांपि ।

सत्यवचनः प्रचण्डः : सहस्थितेभौंमगुरुसूर्यः ।OII

7. SUN-JUPITER-MARS YOGA: Should Mars, Jupiter and the Sun be together at birth, the native will be an expert speaker, very wealthy, either a king’s minister or even a king himself, be truthful and fierce.

Notes: These planets together at birth can make the person easily and highly irritable. The native will be a gifted speaker and be skilful in work and can hope to reach high position in life depending upon the house of the conjunction. For example, in the 10th, this yoga can produce parliamentarians, political stars, judges, army heads etc. Should the Rasi involved be Cancer or Capricorn or Libra as the 10th (where one of these will be in fall) the native though can acquire prosperous position, is not free from downfall.

## नयनातुरः कुलीनः सुभगो वाक्वल्यसंयुतो मनुजः ।

## मृगुभौमदिवसनार्थः सहस्थितेः स्थाद्विभवयुक्तः ।।।

8. VENUS-MARS-SUN YOGA: One, who has Venus, Mars and the Sun in one house, will be subjected to eye diseases, belongs to a good race, will be fortunate, harsh and wealthy.

Notes: The native with this combination will be more

materialistic and sensuous. He is prone to eye diseases. (Particularly, the ascendant with this trio can deprive one of eye-sight.) He does not speak much but harshly and ably. This is a good position for intelligence of high order and for achievements in movie line, photo studios, music etc.

## विकलाङ्गो धनरहितो निर्यं रोगान्वितो मनुजः । स्क्जनरहितोऽतिमूर्खः क्षितिजार्कजभानुभिः सहितः ।EI

9. MARS-SUN-SATURN YOGA: Should Mars, Saturn and the Sun be together, the native will have deformed limbs, be without wealth, always sick, without his people and be a big fool.

Notes: This yoga deprives one of his father and relatives. He cannot earn much wealth. He will be devoid of wisdom. Deformation of limb will depend on the house involved. (For example, if they join in the 3rd, hearing defect can be predicted) The native will suffer from many diseases and litigations if they join in a house other than the 6th.

नेत्रातुरोऽतिधनवान् शास्त्रकचाकाव्यगोष्ठिविल्परतः ।

वाचस्पतिबुधसुर्ये रेकगतेलिपिकरः पुरुषः ।।१०।

10. JUPITER-MERCURY-SUN YOGA: If Jupiter, Mercury and the Sun be together at birth, the native will be subjected to eye-diseases, be very wealthy, be interested in Sasthras, fables, poetry, meetings and stonework and will become a good writer.

Notes: Should the conjunction occur in the 3rd in particular, one can achieve success in writing books, publishing, book-sales etc. If the Sun is not more powerful than the other two, the yoga in the 3rd can bless one with mellifluous voice.

## श्रवितृप्तो वाचाटो स्रमखर्चिः प्रोवितो गुरुभिः ।

स्त्रीहेतोः सन्तप्तः वाक्षिसुतरविभागंबे: सहितेः ॥ ११।

11. MÉRCURY-SUN-VENUS YOGA: If Mercury, Sun and Venus be together, the native will be insatiable, talkative, intent on wandering, and subjccted to grief on account of women.

Notes: If they join in the 7th, other than in Libra or Taurus, the marital front will be greatly disturbed. In Libra, if the Sun is predominantly strong in Shadbala it may even deny marriage.

क्लीबाचारो इच्प्रः सर्वजितो बन्धुभिः परित्यक्तः ।

सौरादित्येन्दुसुर्त रेक्स्चर्जावते

पुरुषः ।१२।

12. SATURN-SUN-MERCURY YOGA: One who has Saturn, the Sun and Mercury in one house will be a neuter, will hate others, and will be given up by his relatives.

Notes: This yoga will tell upon the virility of the person. Particularly in the Ascendant, these can make one look likc a eunuch. This is an adverse combination for paternal affinity. The nattve will house many vices and will incur enmity with others without any reason. He will be a loser in liligations. He will always be in the company of bad , people.

## दुर्वलचलू ः शूरः प्रज्ञो निःस्वद्च भूपतेः सचिवः । परकार्यरतो नित्यं भार्गवयुरुभास्करे: सहितः ॥१३।।

13. VENUS-JUPITER-SUN YOGA: Should Venus, Jupiter and the Sun be together at birth, the native will be weak-sighted, bold, intelligent, indigent, be a minister and be devoted to others' jobs.

Notes: Jatakabharana declares that this is not a good yoga for money just as Kalyana Varma does. In our opinion, this combination, can give abundant riches, if none of them is weak in the horoscope. According to Horasara, one will be a king with this yoga, but will lose in gambling etc.

## श्रसहरकायः पूज्यः स्वजनद्रेब्यः सुरदारसुतमित्र । नृपतोष्टो विनतभयो जीवार्कजदिनकरंः सहितेः ।१४।।

14. JUPITER-SATURN-SUN YOGA: If Jupitcr, Saturn and the Sun be togcther at birth, the native will not have a prominent physiquc, will be worshipped, will hatc his own men will have good wife, children and friends, be dear to the king and be fcarless.

Notes: One with this combination will have reasonable cxpenscs, be honoured by all and will hold a prominent position. He will play to the tuncs of his spouse according to Manasagari.

## क्षत्रुभयात्सोदेगी मानकलाकाव्यवाजिती मनुजः । कुत्सितखारितः कुष्ठी सिताकिर विसंयुतेर्भवति ।१५।।

15. VENUS-SATURN-SUN YOGA: Onc who has Venus, Saturn and the Sun togcther at birth will be emotionally upset due to fear from cncmies, will be devoid of honour and knowledge of arts and poctry, will have a mean history and will suffer from leprosy.

Notes: He will be a chcat of first order. His life will be miserable. He will be sick but will acquire some money in his old agc. (Horasara, P. 282).

Notes: This yoga will affect the married life of the native. The native will suffer from cardiac defects.

## पापकराः जायन्ते नोवाचाराः सुहुत्त्वजनहोनाः । श्राजीवनञ्च पुरुवाः वाशाडुबुचमूमिर्जः सहितेः ॥१६।

16. MOON-MERCURY-MARS YOGA: Should thc Moon Mercury and Mars be together at birth, the native will be sinful, mcan in habits and conduct, and devoid of friends and relatives throughout life.

Notes: He will not have wealth according to Jatakabharana.

Vide Horasara, p. 282, the native will attain fame, be honoured by the public, be learned, be eloquent in speech and kind. Prithuyasas adds that the native will be penniless in the middle of his life.

विनताङ्गः स्त्रीलोल्चोरः कान्तव्च संमतः स्त्रीसाम् ।

भौमताशा जुमुरेज्ये रेकस्यैदैचण्डरोषश्च

11801

17. MARS-MOON-JUPITER YOGA: A native who has Mars, the Moon and Jupiter together at birth will have an even body, be addicted to women, be a thief, be splendorous, dear to women and given to anger.

Notes: According to Manasagari, the native's face will be equal to that of Moon, sec ु q +

This combination can leave wounds and marks on the body and make one short-tempered.

दुःशोलावा: पुत्रः पतिव्च तस्याः स्वेव निविष्टः । कुजभुगुक्षक्षिभिः सहितंत्र मखारुचिः शीतभीतव्च ॥१८भ

18. MARS-VENUS-MOON YOGA: One who has Mars, Venus and the Moon together at birth is the son of a bad woman and also will wed such a lady. He will be of wandering disposition and will have fear from cold.

The native will be susceptible to afflictions from colds. Both Manasagari and Jatakabharana also state that the native’s mother will have bad history and the subject will marry a bad female. Venus in conjunction with Mars affects the wife while Mars afflicting the Moon causes adversity in regard to mother.

## क्षाल्ये मृतजननोक क्षुद्रो विषमञ्च लोकविदिष्टः ।

श्रादेत नरो योगे भूसुत्वाक्षिभास्करसुतानाम् ॥१९।

19. MARS-MOON-SATURN: If Mars, Moon and Saturn join at birth, the person concerned will lose his mother in boyhood, be mean, wicked and inimical to people.

Notes: The relationship of Moon with the said two malefics can deprive of mother in boyhood itself. The native will be ever fond of promoting quarrels.

पनवान्कल्यो वाम्मी तेजस्वी स्यातिमान् विपुलकोरतिः ।

बहुपुत्र्रास्युतो बुबेन्दुमुरपूजिर्तर्युक्तः ॥२०।।

20. MERCURY-MOON-JUPITER YOGA: Should Mercury. Moon and Jupiter be together, the native will be wcalthy, skilful, eloquent in speech, bright, famous and will have many sons and brothers.

Notes: These three in conjunction confer good conduct, wcalth, friends, wisdom and scholarship, according to Jatakabharana.

This is an advantageous position to achieve a high degree of success in astrology.

विद्यासंस्कृतमतिरपि मोवाचार: पुमान्भवेञ्जातः । सेर्यो घनप्रमुब्धो बुधभार्गवचन्द्रसंयोगे ।२१।

21. MERCURY-VENUS-MOON YOGA: One who has Mercury, Venus and Moon together at birth, will be mean in conduct in spite of his being learned and cultured, be Jealous and miserly.

Notes: According to Manasagari, he will also be blameworthy. This is not a good position for smooth relationship in the family. The native will be too much attached to money.

श्रस्वातन्तर्यो विकलः प्राज्ञो वाग्मी सुपूजितः क्षितिर्ष । भवति नरः संयोगे सौरेन्दुश्ञाडुपुत्राखाम् ।२२॥

22. SATURN-MOON-MERCURY YOGA: If Saturn, Moon and Mercury be together, the person concerned will be dependent, deformed, learned, be an able speaker and respected by the king.

Notes: One with this combination will be famous, skilful in many arts, be head of a village or town and be dear to king, vide Jatakabharana.

The conjunction of Saturn-Mercury and the Moon can promote cardiac and asthmatic problems. It is more so if it directly relates to the 6th house.

## साध्वीतनयः प्राज्ञः कलास्वभिजो बहुश्रुतः साधुः । भार्गवनुर्षक्षियोगे जातः सुभगो भवेत्पुरुषः ।।२३।

23. VENUS-JUPITER-MOON YOGA: Should Venus, Jupiter and the Moon be in one house at birth, the nativc will be a chaste woman’s son, be a scholar, will have knowledge of arts and many Sasthras, be virtuous and beautiful.

Notes: This conjunction can give wealth of high order if related to 2nd, 11th or 10th house. One can hope to achieve literary success with these 3 planets being together at birth.

शास्त्रार्थतसत्वबुद्धिव् द्वसत्रीसङ्गतो विगतरोषः । शत्तिवाचस्पतिसौरं रेकल्चर्गाम्न्दपतिः ।२४ा।

24. MOON-JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: One who has Moon, Jupiter and Saturn combination at birth will know the inner meaning of Sasthras, will seek union with aged women, be devoid of shame and be the head of a village and people.

Notes: The native will yield to his wife, according to Manasagari. One will be addicted to women of higher age bracket as Saturn partakes in this yoga.

लिपिकरपुस्तकवाचकपुरोधसां भवति जन्म सुङ्गतोनाम् । देवविदां पुर्वाखां शशिभार्गवसौरिसंयोगे । २५।।

25. MOON-VENUS-SATURN YOGA: If the Moon, Venus and Saturn are in combination at birth, one will be a writer,

reader of books, Purohit (family guru), will have to his credit merits of past birth, and be an astrologer.

Notes: Should these 3 planets be together at birth, one will go after women, will have a bad married life or may lose his spouse early.

सुकविः क्षोरीनाथः सद्यवतिपतिः परार्थ उद्यवतः । गान्धर्ववेदकुशलः स्याव्बुधगुरुमूतुतेः सहितेः ।२६।।

26. MERCURY-JUPITER-MARS YOGA: One who has Mercury, Jupiter and Mars together will be a good poet, lord of a group, will marry a virtuous lady, be intent on helping others, and an expert singer.

ग्रकुलीनो विकलाञ्चद्चपलो दुष्टद्व जायते मनुजः । मुखरो नित्योत्साही कुजबुषभुगुनन्दर्नः सहितंः ।।२७१।

27. MARS-MERCURY-VENUS YOGA: Should Mars, Mercury and Venus be together at birth, the native will give up his caste, be deformed, fickle-minded, wicked, and garrulous.

प्रेष्यः व्याम उनेत्रः प्रवासशीलो भवेहदनरोगी ।

रमते प्रहसनशीर्लर्बुधारकिरुधिरेः सहेक्स्यः ।२८।।

28. MERCURY-SATURN-MARS YOGA: Should Mercury, Saturn and Mars be together at birth, one will be a servant, will have dark eyes, be intent on living in foreign places, will have facial diseases and will be witty.

नृपतोष्टः सत्युतवान् विनासिनीभ्यः सबाप्तक्षहुसौख्यः ।

सकलजनानन्दकरो भार्गवगुरुमूमिर्जः सहितः । २११।

29. VENUS-JUPITER-MARS YOGA: One who has Venus, Jupiter and Mars together at birth will be dear toking, will have good sons and enjoy pleasures through women. He will please all persons.

## नृपसंमतः क्षताङ्गो नीवाचारो विगहितो मित्रः। भवति नरो विगतवृखः सुरेज्यकुजसौरिसंयोगे । ३०।।

30. JUPITER-MARS-SATURN YOGA: If Jupiter, Mars and Saturn be together at birth, the native will be honoured by the king, will have wounds on the body, be mean and blamed by friends. He will also be unkind.

सुबरित्रविहीनायाः पुत्रो भर्ता भवेत्मुखविहीनः। नित्यं प्रवासशीलः संयुष्र्तः सौरिकुजधुक्रः ।३१।

31. SATURN-MARS-VENUS YOGA: If Saturn, Mars and Venus are in one house at birth, the native is born tc an immoral lady, will marry such a lady will be devoid of happiness, and will be intent on wandering in distant places.

सुतनुः क्षपितारिगखो नृपतिः सुभगस्तया पृथुतकोतिः । बुधगुरख्चुर्कः सहितर्मवति नरः सत्यवचनश्च ।३२।।

32. MERCURY-JUPITER-VENUS: Should Mercury, Jupiter and Venus be together, the native will have good physique, be successful over his enemies be a king, fortunate, widely famous and truthfully disposed.

मानघनश्वर्ययुतं प्राज्ञ बहुभोगिनं स्वदाररतम् ।

षृतिसौस्ययुतं सुभगं जनयन्ति बुधार्किजीवास्याः ॥३३।।

33. MERCURY-SATURN-JUPITER: When Mercury, Saturn and Jupiter are together at birth it denotes that the native will enjoy honour, wealth and sovereignty, be learned, will have abundant pleasures, be attached to his wife, be courageous and fortunate.

मुसरो घर्तोऽ्नृतवाक् पर्युबतिर्तो भवेद्विचमशीलः ।

बुषचुक्सूर्यतन्यः कलास्वभिज्ञः स्वदेशरतः ।।३४।।

34. MERCURY VENUS-SATURN: One who has Mercury, Venus and Saturn together at birth will be scurrilous in speech, fraudulent, be a liar, addicted to other women, vehement, (f\Psi r has several other meanings, viz., irregular, uneven, mysterious, inaccessible, rough, painful, troublesome etc.), will have knowledge of arts and be attached to his own country.

## न्यूने कुलेऽपि जातो भवति नरो सूपरतिविपुलकोतिः ।

शुर्भार्ववदिनकरज रेकस्यः

## शीलसम्पन्नः ।३५॥

35. JUPITER-VENUS-SATURN: Should Jupiter, Venus and Saturn be in one house at birth one will become a king even though he might be of mean natal order, will be very famous and be a repository of virtues.

Notes: This is a superior combination to attain fame and high position in legal felds.

पार्पयुक्ते चन्द्रे मातुरभावः प्रकीतितप्रायः। सुये पितुस्तथार्यः शुभं बढेन्मिश्र्तेविधम् ॥ ३६।

36. PARENTS: If the Moon is in the company of malefics, it is detrimental to the mother of the native. Predict similar results regarding the father of the native if the Sun joins malefics at birth. Benefics in the place of malefics in the said yogas will prove auspicious to the respective parent. If the planets joining be of mixed nature (ie. one benefic and another malefic), the effects are medium.

प्रायः शुभाः समेता घनमूतियक्षोन्वितं नृपतिचेष्टम् ।

उत्पादयन्ति मनुजं भूमण्डलमण्डनं श्रेष्ठम् ॥३७।

पापास्त्रयोऽपि मिलिताः कुर्बन्ति गरं सुदुर्गं लोके ।

बारिप्रचदुःखतप्तं गहितरूपं विनयहोनम् ॥३८।

37-38. BENEFICS IN CONJUNCTION AND MALEFICS IN CONJUNCTION: If benefics are in conjunction at birth,

the native will enjoy wealth, sovereignty and fame. Such a combination produces an emperor capable of ruling the earth. Should three malcfics join at birth, the native wil be unlucky, indigent, grief-stricken, ugly and immodest.

Notes: The combination of three bencfics will produce splendid results even in the 6th and 8th—not to spcak of other non-trika houses, for Adhi yoga is produced. Even in the 12th, the conjunction of 3 planets, one of them being the 2nd lord, causcs powerful monetary yoga, (sec Sarvartha Chintamani). Malefics conjunct prove adverse and it is better that they are separated from each other. This concentration on one particular Bhava—by way of conjunction—proves severely inauspicious for that particular housc while their separation distributes the imbalances evenly.

इति कत्याखवर्मविरचित्तायां साराबल्यां विग्रह्योगो नाम

## षोडशोऽध्यायः ।।१ ६।।

Thus ends the 16th Chapter entitled "3 planets in combination” in Kalyana Varma’s Saravali.

## Chapter 17

## अथ चतुर्महयोगाध्यायः ॥ १७ ॥

## Four Planets in Conjunction

लिपिक्रतस्करमुखरो रोगी मायाप्रपञ्चकुशलद्च । बुधरविभौम्शाङ्का रेकर्णगर्तः पुमान् भवति ।।१।

1. SUN-MOON-MARS-MERCURY YOGA: Should these four plancts be together at birth, the native will be a writer, a thief and be scurrilous in speech. He will be sickly, cunning and be capable of decciving others.

घनवान् वनितावन्यस्तेजस्वो नीतिमान् विगतशोकः । कर्मसमर्थो निपुखः वाक्षिकुजगुरुभास्करः सहितेः ।।२।।

2. MOON-MARS-JUPITER-SUN YOGA: One who has these four plancts in one house at birth, will be wealthy, dear to women, splendorous, will maintain decorum, be free from grief and expert in work.

श्रार्योंखितवाग्वृत्तिः सुलभाङ् निपुख्तोऽर्थसंग्रहराशीलः । विद्यासुतवारयुतः वाविकुजभूगुभास्करं: सहितेः ।३।

3. MOON-MARS-VENUS-SUN YOGA: If these four planets join at birth, one will speak and conduct himself as a great person, be happy, expert, intent on gathering money and will have learning, sons and wife.

विषमशरीरो ह्वस्वो घनरहितो याचितवनो मूर्जः । गम्यः सर्वस्य तथा रविश्विकुजसौरिसंयोगे ॥४।। 4. SUN-MOON-MARS-SATURN YOGA: One who has

these four planets together at birth, will have uneven physique, be short in stature, be unwealthy and will collect food by begging, and be a proven dunce.

## सौक्षरतिकः प्लुताक्षः शिल्पकरो वा महापनो धीरः । जातः स्याद्रुविरतनुः शविज्गुरुमास्करः-सहितः ॥५I

5. MOON-MERCURY-JUPITER-SUN YOGA: If these four planets are in one house, the native will be a goldsmith (or a gold dealer etc.), be long eyed, be a sculptor, be very wealthy, bold, and beautiful bodied.

## विकतः सुभगा वाग्मी ह्यत्वो नृपसंमतो मनुजः ।

## जातः स्यादेक्स्य रविवशिबुधभार्मवः सहितः ।६।।

6. SUN-MOON-MERCURY-VENUS YOGA: The native who has these four planets together at birth, will be deformed, lucky, short-statured, and dear to king.

मातृपितु विप्रयुक्तो घनसौख्यविवाजितो स्रमखीलः ।

## भिक्षाशनोऽप्यन्तवाक् रवीन्दुसौम्यारकिर्भिनियतम् ॥19I

7. SUN-MOON-MERCURY-SATURN YOGA: If these four planets are together at birth, the native will lose his parents (in childhood), be devoid of money and happiness, be wandering, will earn food by begging and will be a liar.

सलिलमृगारण्यानां स्वामी स्यात्सौख्यभाक् नृपतिपुष्यः ।

8. VENUS-JUPITER-MOON YOGA: Should these planets be in one house at birth, the subject concerned will be head of water, animals and forests (i.c. his livelihood may be through such sources), will be happy, honoured by the king and be an expert.

ताक्तनेत्रस्तीक्यो बहुसृतवित्तो वराख्नासुभगः ।

सूर्वज्यचर्सौरं रेक्स्चर्जायते

: Hen

9. SUN-JUPITER-MOON-SATURN YOGA: If the sald plancts join at birth, the native will be dark-eyed, flerce, will have many sons, be wealthy, and fortunate through women.

बनितासहवाचारः पुरःसरोऽत्यन्तदुर्बलशारीरः ।

भोरः सर्वत्र भवेदकन्दुसिताऽसितः सहितेः ।१०।

10. SUN-MOON-VENUS-SATURN YOGA: Should one have a combination of these planets in one house at birth, the native will have habits like a female, be very weak, desirous of coming up and timid at all times.

शुरोऽ्थ सत्रकारञ्चक्रबरो वा विपन्नदारधनः ।

दुःख्चार्णवोऽ्टनपरः सुस्तर्वजोवबुधभौमः ।११।।

11. SUN-JUPITER-MARS-MERCURY YOGA: These four planets together at birth indicate that the subject will be brave, be a composer of Sastras ( ¶%1x) or a ruler of province ( \equiv ), will losc his wife and money, be undesirable and will wander.

Notes: In spite of being a "ruler”, the native may not be free from mental debacles—as indicated by the sloka.

पर्दाररतद्बोरो वियमाङ्गी दुर्जनो विगतसत्वः । भवति प्रसवे पुरुयो रविसितभौमेन्दुर्जः सहितः ॥१२।।

12. SUN-VENUS-MARS-MERCURY YOGA: If these four planets be together at birth, the native will be addicted to other women, be a thief, will have uneven limbs, will be a bad person and will be bereft of energy.

ध्योद्धा प्राज्ञस्तीक्यो नीचाचारः कविप्रधानश्च ।

श्रन्त्री ख मूप्तर्वा बुर्धाकंकुजसौरिसंयोगे ॥१३।

13. MERCURY-SUN-MARS-SATURN YOGA: If thesc four planets are together at birth, the subject will be a

warrior, scholar, be fierce, will be meanly disposed, be chief among poets, and be a minister or a king.

सभयः पूज्यो लोके घनवान् नृपसंमतो भुवि स्यातः । द्विभीमजीवचुक्र रेक्स्चनीतिमान्पुर्षः 1X0

14. SUN-MARS-JUPITER-VENUS YOGA: with the conjunction of these four planets, one will be fortunate, worth worship by the people, be wealthy, dear to king and famous.

सोन्मादो गखमान्यः सिद्धार्थो बन्धुमित्रसंपृक्तः।

भानुकुजजोवसौरंः संयुक्तर्बा नृपाभिमतः ॥१५॥

15. SUN-MARS-JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: Should these join at birth, one will be rash, prime among his group, will have cherished desires, will bc endowed with relatives and friends and dear to king.

विकलो मोचावारो विषमाक्षो बन्धुविद्विहुः।

सुर्यकुजशुक्सौरीः पराभवं सर्वतो याति ॥१९॥।

16. SUN-MARS-VENUS-SATURN YOGA: One who has these four planets together at birth, will be deformed, mean in conduct, oblique-sighted, will hate his relatives, and will always be insulted.

षनवान् सुखप्रवानः सिद्धार्थों वन्धुमान् प्रकूबूद्।

भानुबुघजोवशुक्र्भवति पुमानेकराशिगर्तः ॥१७१।

17. SUN-MERCURY-VENUS YOGA: With these planets in one house, one will be wealthy, happy, chief, will have cherished desires, will have relatives and be noble.

बलोबाचारो मानी कलहरुचिः सहजवान् निरुत्साहः ।

श्र्हाकिबुषसुरेज्ये रेकर्त्यर्जायते पुरुषः ।१८।।

18. SATURN-SUN-MERCURY-JUPITER YOGA: One who has these four plancts together at birth, will have a neuter’s habits, be prestigious, fond of quarrels, will have co-born, and be not enthusiastic.

मुखरः सुभगः प्राज्ञो मृदुसौर्यः सत्वशौचसंपन्नः ।

धोरो मित्रसहायो

19. SUN-MERCURY-VENUS-SATURN YOGA: Should these four planets be in conjunction at birth, onc will be scurrilous in speech, fortunate, learned, soft spoken, happy, energetic, pure, wealthy, bold and helpful to friends.

सुब्धः कविः प्रधानः कारकनायोऽधिपद्च नीचानाम् ।

श्वादित्याकिसितारये राज्ञां जातो भवेदिष्टः ।२०।।

20. SUN-SATURN-VENUS-JUPITER YOGA: These four planets in conjunction at birth will make the person a miser, a poet, chief, leader of sculptors and mean.

शास्त्रकुवलो नरेन्द्रः सुमहामन्त्रोऽ्य वा महाबृद्धिः।

शाशिकुलसोमजजीर्व रेकस्थेयंः

पुमाञ्जातः ॥२१।

21. MOON-MARS-MERCURY-JUPITER YOGA: One who has the conjunction of these four planets at birth, will be an expert in Sasthras, be a king or a great minister and be extremely intelligent.

कलहरचिनिद्रासुर्नीचः स्यादर्घकीपतिः सुभगः ।

बन्धुद्रेष्टा न सुखी दाविकुजबुषभार्गवः सहितेः ।२२।

22. MOON-MARS-MERCURY-VENUS YOGA: If these four planets are together at birth, one will be fond of quarrels, will sleep much, be mean, will marry an unchaste lady, be fortunate, will hate his relatives, and be not happy.

शुरो विमातृपितुको दुष्कुलजो बहुकूलत्रमित्रसुतः ।

भर्षाति सुकर्माभिरतः वाशाकुजबुधसौरिसंयोगे ।२३11

23. MOON-MERCURY-MARS-SATURN YOGA: One who has these four planets together will be bold, be without parents, is from an ignoble race, will have many wives, friends and sons and will havc good conduct.

## बिक्लाङ्कः सुकलत्रः सकलसहोऽतीव मानसंयुक्तः । प्राज्ञो बहुमित्रसुखः वाशिकुजगुरुभार्गवः सहितः ।२४।

24. MOON-MARS-JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: If these four planets are together at birth, onc will be deformed, will have a good wife, be highly tolcrant, be self-respected. learned will have many friends, and be happy.

ब्धिरो धनवान् शुरः सोग्मादो वाक्पटुः स्थरप्रकृतिः । मतिमानुदारचित्तो भौमेन्दुशानंद्वरसुरेज्यैः ॥२५॥

25. MARS-MOON-SATURN-JUPITER YOGA: One who ,has these four planets conjunct at birth will be deaf, wealthy, bold, rash, able in speech, firm in nature, wise, and liberal.

## कुलटापतिः प्रगल्भः सर्पाक्षो नित्यमेव सोद्रेगः ।

## ज्ञातः पुरुषोऽवद्यं कुजेन्दुयमभार्गवर्भवति ।२६।।

26. MARS-MOON-SATURN-VENUS YOGA: If these four plancts are together, one will marry an unchaste lady, will be proud, will have eyes resembling that of a snake, and will be always emotional. This is certain.

## विद्वान् विमातृरित्कः स्पो धन्युतोऽतिसुभगञ्च ।

## भवत्ति नरो विगतात्िर्बुधनुरुवाक्षिमार्गवेः सहितेः ।२७।।

27. MERCURY-JUPITER-MOON-VENUS YOGA: Onc who has these four planets together will be learned, be devoid of parents, good looking, wealthy, very lucky and be without enemies.

कृतबमँकीतिरग्युस्तेजस्वी बन्धुबल्लजमो मतिमान् ।

नृपसचिवः प्रवरकविः शशिबुधजीवाकिभिः सहितेः ।२८।

28. MOON-MERCURY-JUPITER-SATURN: Should these be conjunct one will be virtuous, famous, noble and splendorous, fond of relatives, wise, be a king's minister and be a chief poet.

## परवारगमनशीलो विज्ञोलभार्यो विपन्नबन्धुश्च ।

प्राज्ञो लोकदिष्टः स्यादिन्दुबुघाकिम्गुपुत्रैः ।२६।।

29. MOON-MERCURY-SATURN-VENUS YOGA: If these planets are together at birth, the native will be intent on seeking sexual pleasures with others’ wives, will have an unchaste wife, be devoid of relatives, learned and will hate people.

मात्रा रहितः सुभगस्त्वम्दोषी दुःखितो भ्रमणशीलः ।

## Chapter 19 झथ पढ्यह्योगाप्यायः ॥१ ६॥

## Effects of 6 Planets in Conjunction

विद्याघनचर्मरतंः क्षामो बहुभावको विशिष्टमतिः ।

एकभवनोपयारतर्बुषेन्दुरव्यार गुर्छुकः 181

1. MERCURY-MOON-SUN-MARS-JUPITER-VENUS YOGA: If these six planets join together, the native will be learned, virtubus, will have an emaciated body, be acquainted with many languages, and will have distinguished knowledge.

दाता परकार्यकरद्वलस्वभावो विच्युद्धसत्वव्च । रमते विजनोहेधे रवीन्दुवक्र्गुरुरविजः ।२।

2. SUN-MOON-MARS-MERCURY-JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: Should these planets be together, the native will be liberal, helpful to others, fickleminded, of satwic disposition and will grafity his sexual desires in loncly places.

चोरः परदाररतः कुष्ठी स्वजर्न निराङतो मूर्णः । स्थानभ्रङ्ो विसुतो बुषेन्दुरव्यारवानिच्युक्रः ।३।

3. MERCURY-MOON-SUN-MARS-SATURN-VENUS YOGA: Should these six planets join in one house, the native will be a thief, be addicted to others' women, subjected to leprosy, discarded by his own men, be a dunce, deprived of position and be issueless.

श्रोखः वर्हर्रतः क्षयरोगी द्वासकासपरिमूतः । हित्यः स्याष्वर्न्यूमां सितेन्दुरव्यारगुरुरविर्जः ॥VI

4. VENUS-MOON-SUN-MARS-JUPITER-SATURN YOGA: If these six planets are together, the native will be mean, will do others' jobs, suffer from tuberculosis, breathing and sneezing troubles (i.e. sneezing etc.) and will be blamed by his relatives.

मन्त्री नृपस्य सुभगः क्षान्तियुतो भवति शोकपरितप्तः ।

स्रकलत्रो धनरहितो रवीन्बुबुघजीवसितसौरंः ॥५।

5. SUN-MOON-MERCURY-JUPITER-VENUS-SATURN YOGA: One with this six-planetary conjunction will be a king's minister, fortunate, patient and will suffer from grief. He will have no wife and wealth.

तीर्थेचु सदा रमते पुत्रेनित्यं घनेन रहितव्च ।

बनपर्वत्तोपसेबी बुधारशविजोवशनिशुक्रेः ।६।

6. MERCURY-MARS-SATURN-JUPITER-VENUS-SUN YOGA: If the said six planets are together the subject will always wander in shrines, be deprived of issues and wealth and will live in forests and hills.

नित्यं श्रुचिः प्रतापो बहुयुवतिरतो नृपप्रियो मन्त्री ।

घनसुतसौभाग्ययुतः कुजाकिसितचन्द्बुधजीर्वः ।७।।

7. MARS-SATURN-VENUS-MOON-MERCURY-JUPITER YOGA: One with these six planets will be pure at all times, valorous, will seek sexual union with many women, be dear to king, be a minister and be endowed with wealth, issues and happiness.

प्रायो दरिद्रदुःखी मुर्खः पद्पञ्चसंयुर्तविह्यः ।

श्वन्योन्यवर्शनावपि फलमेतत्कन्दलाः प्राहुः ।।८।।

8. PLANETARY CONJUNCTIONS: If 5 or 6 planets join at birth, normally such natives will be penniless, subjected to grief and be dullwitted. Even aspects among such

planets will yield similar results according to Kandalas.

Notes: In general, even if there be mutual aspects between five or six planets, they yield unpleasant results as though they are in conjunction. The reason is most of the Bhavas in the chart come under adverse effect, as all planets except the luminaries own two houses each.

इति कल्यारावर्म विरचितायां साराबल्यां बड्गाह्योधो

## नार्मंकोनविशोऽध्यायः ।। १६।।

Thus ends the 19th Chapter, entitled "Effects of sixplanet combinations” in Kalyana Varma's Saravali.

## Chapter 20

## बाथ प्रवज्यायोगाष्यायः ॥ २०॥।

## Combinations For Renunciation

योगा विभक्ताद्वतुरादिसंस्वव्यासाङ्गप्रहेः करपि तापसानाम्। जन्मादि तेषामार्मुनीर्त्रः संवेदितं तत्कथयाम्यवेवात् ।१।। 1. Various sages have explained widely the combinations of four or more planets producing an ascetic. I now explain below such of the yogas.

सूर्यन्दुधुकार्यमहोसुतेषु सूर्यम्बुसोमात्मजमूमिजेषु । एकस्थितेषु प्रभवेत्तपस्वी भान्वारमन्दज्ञसितेषु चंव ।२।

2. One born with the conjunction of the Sun, the Moon, Venus, Jupiter and Mars; or the luminaries, Mercury and Mars; or the Sun, Mars, Saturn, Mercury and Venus becomes an ascetic.

कुजेन्युसुर्यतपुरोहितंश्च तोक्तांशुचन्दाकिवदाजुर्जद्च । सूर्येन्दुभूपुत्रार्नदचरः स्यावेकार्षगः प्रव्रजितो मनुष्यः ॥ ३।

3. The native having the combination of Mars, the luminaries (i.e. the Sun and the Moon), Mercury and Jupiter; or the luminaries, Saturn, and Mercury; or the luminaries, Mars and Saturn takes to renunciation and become a mendicant.

भावित्यगुर्वारकिशशास्पुत्रा भौमार्कचन्त्रात्मजसूर्यद्च । एकर्जसंस्थास्तपति स्थितानां कुर्वन्ति जन्मप्रसबे ग्रहेन्द्राः ।।४।। 4. The Sun, Jupiter, Saturn and Mercury being together or Mars, the Sun, Mercury and Jupiter joining denotes a mendicant.

सितार्कभौमार्कसुता महाबलाः सुरेज्यमूपुत्रकसूर्यसौरयः । कुजेन्दुबायीशवार्नश्चरा इमे समं गता वं जनयन्ति तापसम् ॥५।।

5. Should Venus, the Sun, Mars and Saturn; or Jupiter, Mars, the Sun and Saturn; or Mars, the Moon, Jupiter and Saturn with strength be in conjunction at birth, native takes to ascetism.

कुजाकिसोमात्मजवेववन्दितः कुजाकिचन्द्रात्मजसूर्यभार्गवः । रवीन्दुभौमासितदानवप्रियर्भवन्ति सूता ब्रजसंयुता नराः ॥६।।

6. Onc will be initiated into a religious vow if anyone of the following group of plancts be found together at birth: (1) Mars, Saturn, Mercury and Jupiter; (2) Mars, Saturn, Mercury, the Sun and Venus; (3) the luminaries, Mars, Saturn and Venus.

सितारसूर्यात्मजजीवभास्करंः कुजेन्दुवेवेडपबुधार्शनन्वनेः ।

सिदेन्द्युत्राकिशान्याडुमूमर्ज्भवेत्तपस्वी बनपर्वताश्रयः ।७।

7. One becomes a mendicant to live in forests and hilly areas if the planets group themselves in conjunction at birth as under: (1) Venus, Mars, Saturn, Jupiter and the Sun; (2) Mars, the Moon, Jupiter Mercury and Saturn; (3) Venus, Mercury, Saturn, the Moon and Mars.

चन्देन्दुपुतारसुरेडचभास्करं: क्षशाजुसूर्वन्दुजभुक्यूक्षिजः । स्थिरतरमीभिः सहितेन् सम्भवा भवन्त बन्च। मुनयोऽन्नदूषक्काः ॥e1।

8. The native will become a sage, giving up (even) his food and be honoured by the people, if the heavenly bodies form conjunction as under: (1) The Moon, Mercury, Mars, Jupiter and the Sun; (2) The luminaries, Mercury, Venus and Mars.

रपीन्दु भौमेस्युजजीवमार्गवेः वाञ्षाजू भौमार्शब्धेडच्भार्करंः । कुनेन्दुसुर्याकिसितेन्दुस भर्वर्भवेदमीभिः सहितर्रो ती ।ET1

9. The native will be initiated into a religious vow, if (1) the luminaries, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn or (2) The Moon, Mars, Saturn, Mercury, Jupiter and the Sun or (3) Mars, the luminaries, Saturn, Venus and Mercury, join together at birth.

सितेन्दुओवार्कजसूयतोहितः सितार्कंजोवारकिश्ञाजूसोमर्जः । एकत्र यार्तरगंगनेखरेः सवा भवन्ति जाता मुनयो यन्नस्विनः ॥१०।

10. A famous sage is indicated by the combination of (1) the luminaries, Jupiter, Saturn and Venus or (2) Venus, the luminaries, Jupiter, Saturn and Mercury.

कुजत्वागीवासिताकिभास्करः सिताकिजोवेन्दुजबन्द्रभूमिरजः ।

बलप्राधनः सहिते विहङ्गमंत्र जेत्रजातः पुरुपस्तपस्विताम् ॥११॥।

11. Note the conjunction of the following planets: (1) Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn and the Sun. (2) Venus, the luminaries, Saturn, Jupiter and Mercury. It indicates an ascetic.

रवीम्बुवागीधावानंद्चरंऽ्च वार्नद्वरेग्हक सितैरवद्यम् ।

रवीन्दुपुत्रक्षितिजेन्द्रपुर्यस्तपसिवमः स्युः फलमुलभक्षिर्णः ।१२॥।

12. If conjunction is found among the planetary group, viz. (1) the luminaries, Jupiter and Saturn; (2) Saturn, the luminaries and Venus; (3) Sun, Mercury, Mars and Jupiter, the native will become an ascetic living on roots and fruits.

बक्रार्कसोमात्मजवानवेडचा भौमेन्दुवागीववावाडूपुत्राः ।

एकाक्षंगा जन्मनि यस्य जन्तोर्भवेद्वती बल्कलजोरघारी ॥१३।

13. The native will become an ascetic with barkgarments if Mars, the Sun, Mercury and Venus are found together at birth. The same effect follows the conjunction of Mars, the Moon, Jupiter and Mercury.

क्षश्रीन्वुपुत्रक्षितिजार्कपुत्रा बुधक्षमापुत्रसुरेड्यसौराः । एकक्षंगा जन्मनि यस्य सुतौ कुर्वन्ति तं तापसमेव शान्तम् ॥१४11

14. A patient ascetic (awaiting the blessing of the Supreme) is indicated by either the combination of the Moon, Mercury, Mars and Saturn or of Mercury, Mars, Jupiter and Saturn.

खमद्रार्कभार्गववाशाडूकसुता बलस्था

भौमेन्दुपुत्रसितभास्करनन्दनाश्च । मन्वेन्दुवाष्पतिसिता नियतं यतीनां कुर्वन्ति जन्म फलपाकहताशनानाम् ॥१५।

15. A sage living only by eating fruits is denoted by the combination of the luminaries, Venus and Mercury; or Mars, Mercury, Venus and Saturn; or Saturn, the Moon, Jupiter and Venus.

रविकुजशशिशुक्रे बन्द्रभौमतसूर्ये-

शुरुसितरविमन्देः शुक्रजीवेन्दुवक्नेः । कुजबुधसितच्रे रेभिरेकर्यार्त-

भवति गिरिवनौकास्तापसः सर्वबन्धः ॥१६।

16. An ascetic, who will be highly honourable, lving in forests, is denoted by each of the undermentioned planetary conjunctions: (1) The Sun, Mars, the Moon and Venus, (2) the luminaries, Mars and Mercury, (3) Jupiter, Venus, Saturn and the Sun, (4) Venus, Jupiter, the Moon and Mars.

सितधातिकुजनुरुम्वदेशचन्देन्दुजभीमवेवपुर्युकः रविश्चशिकुजबुषजोवरभवति यतर्वुःखिती बीनः ॥१७।।

-17. Find the planets together in individual groups as under (1) Venus, Moon, Mars, Jupiter and Saturn, (2) the Moon, Mercury, Mars, Jupiter and Venus, (3) The luminar-

ies, Mars, Mercury and Jupiter. The result is that the ascetic will be sorrowful.1

कुजाकिवेवेड्चसितेन्दुपुत्रः जनीनसोमात्मजचन्द्रभौर्मः । समं गर्तः स्युः सबलेर्यचोक्तर्जटाधरा वल्कलघारिख्ञ्च ॥१८।।

18. Mars, Saturn, Jupiter, Venus and Mercury with strength, or Saturn, the Sun, Mercury, the Moon and Mars with strength in conjunction produce one with clotted hair and bark-garments.

भान्विन्युजेन्दुकुजजीवसुरा रिपूर्यः सुर्यन्दुभौमुरक्षुक्रवान्चरेश्च । प्राप्नोत्यवद्यमिह तापसरूपमेमि- रेकर्षगर्गगनवासिभिरेष जातः ।१६।।

19. The luminaries, Mercury, Mars and Venus or the luminaries, Mars, Jupiter, Venus if be together, the native certainly will become an ascetic.

प्रव्रज्वेशे दिनकर्गते भक्तमन्तो न वक्ता:

प्रवरज्यायाः सुबलसहितः स्वर्यमाहुर्हेश्वः ।

सम्पूर्तानां बदामचुगतः प्रच्युतिर्त्रबहुत्वे

बीर्योपेतभंवति बहुभिः सब्बलस्यानुपूर्यात् ॥२०॥।

20. If the Sun denotes ascetism (i.e. being the strongest in the yoga), there will only be faith in the religious order while competence (to fulfil the order) will lack. If the planet, contributing to such order is overpowcred by another planet (i.e. in ecltpse, planetary war etc.) the native will give up the order after being initiated into it. Should many planets denote such an order, there may be many kinds of religious orders, but these will depend on the order of the planet.

1. Unsuccessful mendicant.

प्रव्रज्यायाः स्वामी रविमुषिततमुनिरीक्षितो वार्म्यः । याचितवीक्षा भवति च यवनाधिपतेर्यया बाव्यम् ।२१।

21. If the planet indicating ascetism is combust in the Sun or is aspected by another heavenly body, the native will only have an earnest desire to be initiated into the religious order but will not attain fruition of his aim. So say Yavanas.

शाक्षी हकाणे रविजस्य संस्थितः कुजाकिरष्टः प्रकरोति तापसम् । कुजांशके वा रविजेन हट्टो नवांशातुल्यं कययन्ति तत्पुनः ।२२।

22. The native will attain religious order if the Moon, posited in a decanate of Saturn, is aspected by Mars and Saturn. The same effect is produced if the Moon occupies the Navamsa of Aries/Scorpio in aspect to Saturn.

जन्माषिपः सूर्यसूतेन दृष्टः शेर्वरहन्नः पुरुषस्व सुती । सात्मीयवीक्षां कुरुते ह्यावध्यं पूर्वोक्तमत्रापि विचारखीवम् ॥ २३॥1

23. If the lord of the Moon sign receives thc solus aspect of Saturn (i.e. unaspected by others than Saturn), one will enter religious order. Even in this context, the earlier yogas if present will only prevail.

## जन्मपतिविपुलाञ्चः पद्यति सौरि चतुझ्ये प्रबलम्।

पस्य स भाग्यविहीनः प्रव्रज्यां प्राप्नुयात् पुरुषः २४१।

24. If the lord of the Moon sign, bright with rays, lends his aspect to Saturn while Saturn is in an angle, the native will lose his fortunes only to enter into a religious order.

## पुरुहिमगुरवीरगामेक एवोदवस्थो

मयनतलगतो वा रिःफग्त्रास्प्युतिः । अविकलबलभाजा सूर्यपुत्रेष हवो जनयति खलु जातं तापसं दुक्षभाजम् ॥२५॥

25. If one among the Sun, the Moon and Jupiter be devoid of strength, and occupy the ascendant or the 10th or the I2th in aspect to strong Saturn, the native will become distressed ascetic.

कुमुदवनसुबन्युं सौम्यभागे बलस्यं

वियति गमनसीलान् स्वोच्चमस्थाद्च शेवान् । यदि विनकरपुत्रः पद्यति प्राप्तवीर्यों

भवति भुवननायो बोक्षितव्च स्वतन्त्रः ।२९।

26. Should the Moon with strength be in the 10th, falling in a benefic’s Navamsa while others occupy their exaltation marks and all being in aspect to strong Saturn, the native will become the lord of the carth to only become an ascetic.

श्रतिवायबलयुक्तः शीतयुः शुक्लपक्षे

बलविरहितरिक्तं प्रेक्षते लग्ननाथम् ।

## - यदि भवति तपस्वी वुःखितः क्षोकतन्तो

## पनजनपरिहीनः कृच्छ्लब्धान्नपानः ११२911

27. If waxing Moon, with superior strength, aspects the ascendant lord, the latter being bereft of strength and being alone (in that Rasi), the native will be devoid of wealth and own men, be miserable, sorrowful, will take to ascetism and will obtain his food with great inconvenience.

सौरिः शुभभागस्यः प्यति चन्दं प्रहास्तर्थवान्यान् ।

कुस्मभियु प्राप्तान् जनयति बीक्षान्वितं पुष्वच् । २८३।

28. One will become a religious mendicant if Saturn occupying a benefic's navamsa aspects others including the Moon. The Moon in this case should only be in Aquarius Navamsa.

एकर्षगर्तः सर्वेर्जन्माधिपतिनिरीक्षितो दस्य ।

। बीक्षा तस्यावद्यं भवतीति पुरात्ने: शृचित् ॥२३11

29. If thc lord of the Moon sign aspects all the other planets, while these are all together in one Rasi, the native will become an ascetic.

श्रत्नीनां परिचारका गिरिनदीतीराश्रमे तापता:

सूर्याराधनतत्परा गतपतेर्मव्ता उच्चायाइब ये ।

गायत्री जपतां वने नियमिनां गङ्लाभिवेकाचिन्।

कोमारव्रतमिच्छतामधिपतिस्तेषां सदा भास्करः ।।३०11

30. The Sun rules the ascetics of the following orders: Those who worship the Fire God in forests or on river banks, those who worship (the omnipotent) Sun God, Elephant-faced Ganesa and His benevolent mother Parvati, those who recite the hymns of Gayatri (which are very sacred), those who are vowed to bathe in the sacred Ganges everyday and those who maintain (absolute) celebacy.

बृद्ध्रावकमस्यबूलिववलाः शेवव्रते ये स्थिता

बाह्याः पातकितां गता भगवतोभक्तादच निःसङ्गिनः । सिद्धान्ते खलु सोमनाम्नि निरताः कापालिका निष्ठुरा-

स्लेवां नावकतां गतः शत्रघरः लद्वाङ्पारिद्युतिः ।३१।

31.
