## अथ चन्द्रचाराध्यायः । २३।

## Effects of the Moon in Various Rasis

सौवर्णाङ्गः स्थिरस्वः सहजविरहितः साहसी मानभन्नः कामार्तः क्षामजानु: कुनखतनुकचञ्चदञ्चलो मानवित्तः । पद्यार्भः पांखिपादेविततसृतजनो वर्तुलाकारनेत्रः सस्नेहस्तोयभीरत्र राविकृतक्षिराः स्रोजितो मेष इन्दौ ॥१॥

1. THE MOON IN ARIES: Should the Moon be in Aries, the native will have a golden coloured body, will be endowed with lasting wealth, be devoid of co-born, be valorous, selfrespected, auspicious, prosperous, libidinous, will have weak legs, ugly nails and little hair, be fickle-minded, will consider honour as wealth, will possess hands and legs like the lotus, (i.e. these will be lotus-red), will have more sons, - round eyes, be friendly, be afraid of getting into watery places (like river, well etc.), will have wounds on the head and be won over by females.

Notes: Should the Moon be in Aries at birth, the limbs of the body will be red as Mars is red like blood. If she is related to Mars, the native can have famous brothers. There will be wounds on the body/head as Mars indicates wounds. The native will be prone to accidents from water, like drowning. He will seek sexual pleasures from many women.

If the Moon is in Aries identical with ascendant she causes Raja yoga. (According to Jambunadeeyam, ch. 1, rule 46, the Moon though weak is good in Aries/Cancer identical with ascendant. She gives wealth, virtues, quadrupeds etc.) If it is weak Moon, the person will not be outspoken and will earn wealth through bad means. If it is

full Moon, the person acquires physical strength, beauty and good qualities. This is a very good position for lasting wealth. Should the Moon be in the 2nd house indentical with Aries, i.e. the ascendant being Pisces, the native will enjoy Raja Yoga, par excellence, as she is the 5th lord. The native will have more daughters if it is full Moon and will -lose children if it is weak Moon. If Aquarius rises at birth while increasing Moon is in Aries, the native will have many younger sisters. Even if it is decrcasing Moon, her dasa will give good effects but loss of co-born. If Capricorn be the ascendant with the increasing Moon in Aries, the native will have many younger sisters. Even if it is decreasing Moon, her dasa will give good effects but loss of co-born. If Capricon be the ascendant with the increasing Moon in the 4th, the native will enjoy abundant lands, learning, conveyances, maternal happiness etc. If the Moon is weak, these will be in a lesser degree and the native will keep his wife's mother to be maintained in his house. Should Sagittarius rise, while the Full Moon is in the 5th, the native will acquire one son (as she is the lord of the 8th) who will become very famous and if it is decreasing Moon, the native will beget issues with great difficulty. For Scorpio ascendant, the Full Moon in the 5th is capable of removing debts, diseases, enemies etc., and if weak, then, these are quite limited, instead of being nil. If Libra rise at birth with Full Moon in the 7th, the native will enjoy paternal bliss, paternal wealth etc., acquire a beautiful wife, but be occasionally subjected to physical distress. If it be weak Moon, the native will not be happy from the view-point of marriage. Even though the Moon may be Full and be in Aries for a Virgo native, it causes infant mortality if Moon's dasa occurs at birth; if it be weak Moon, it may without fail cause death in its dasa coinciding with childhood. Should Leo rise at birth with Full Moon in Aries in the 9th, the native will be very fortunate and will enjoy paternal wealth, and his coborn will be greatly benefited; if it is weak Moon, the native will seek union with females equal to mother. If Cancer ascends with Full Moon in the 10th in Aries, the native will lead over monetary transactions and will enjoy rich posi-

tion, conveyances etc., and with a weak Moon, the person will losc his parents early. For a Gemini native, the Full Moon in the 1 ith will supply money uninterruptedly and will give more daughters; if she be weak, the native will not financially prosper. If one born in Taurus has Full Moon in the 12th in Aries, he will spend away all his wealth on charitable acts, and will be free from diseases etc., if it be weak Moon, he will contract various kinds of venereal diseases and will be immoral.

श्रत्युप्रतरो नृपतिः प्रखतानां मार्दवं भजति जातः । बीरः सङ्ग मर्ची रविरा हल्टे वाक्षिनि मेषे ।२। दन्ता क्षिरोगतप्तः शिखिवातादिक्षत्रीरः । माण्डलिकः स्यान्मेषे कुजहर्टे वाविनि मृत्परतः ।३। मानाविद्याचार्य: सद्गाष्यः स्यान्मनोऽभोन्नः । बुषहब्टे मेवस्थे निज्ञाकरे सत्कविविपुलकीतिः ॥४।। बहुमृत्यधनसमृद्धो नृपतेः सचिवदचमूपतिर्याऽपि । मेषगृहे हिमर्मौ इष्टे गुरुखा पुमान् जातः ।५।। सुभगः सुतघनयुक्तो वरयुवतिविभूषर्ोऽल्पभोक्ता च । मेषे शिक्षिरमवूले भूगुतनयनिरीक्षिते भवति ॥६। विहिष्टो बहुदुःखौ दारित्रचतनुर्मलीमसोऽनृतवाक् । मेषे विशिरमयूबे रवितनयनिरीक्षिते भवति ।७।।

2-7. THE MOON IN ARIES IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in Aries at birth, and be in aspect to the Sun, the native will be quite irascible, be honoured by the king, be soft, be valorous, and fond of war. If the Moon in Aries is aspected by Mars, the person will suffer from diseases of eyes and teeth, will have wounds caused by arrows (or horse/cow etc.) and will suffer windy diseases and urinary disorders. Should Mecrcury aspect the Moon in Arics, the native will teach various disciplines, will possess good speech, will achieve his desires, be a great poet and be widely famous. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in Aries, the person will be epdowed with servants and abundant riches,

and be a king or a king’s minister. Venus aspecting the Moon in Aries denotes that the subject will be lucky, be endowed with sons and wealth, will own (i.e. marry) a supreme lady and costly ornaments and will not eat much. The Moon in Aries aspected by Saturn indicates that the native will be jealous, miserable to a great extent, be very poor, dirty and untruthful.

Notes: The effects of the aspects of other planets on the Moon in Aries (or in any other Rasi) will depend on the rulership of the aspecting planet. For example, if Taurus rises with Moon in the 12th and is aspected by Saturn, the native will not be poor but instead he will be rich. Conversely if Jupiter aspects the said Moon for a Taurus ascendant, the native will not be very rich, but only rich in the ordinary sense. These results thus differ according to the lordship of aspecting planet concerned.

व्युढोरस्कोऽतिदाता घनकुटिलकचः कामुकः कीति्ञाली कान्तः कन्याप्रजावान् वृपसमनयनो हंसलीलाप्रचारः । मध्यान्ते नोगभागी प्रपुकडिचर्स्कन्धजान्वास्यजज्धः साधुः पार्वास्यपृष्ठे काकुदि शुभगतिः क्षान्तियुक्तो गक्षीन्दौ Ic।

8. THE MOON IN TAURUS: If the Moon be in Taurus at birth, the native will be large-hearted, highly charitable, short-haired, libidinous, famous, brilliant, will have (more) daughters, will possess eyes resembling that of a bull, be in a position to differentiate between good and bad, be happy during middle and concluding parts of his life, will have strong waist, feet, shoulders, face etc., will have (some) identity on the side and back of the body, will walk beautifully and be endowed with forbearance.

Notes: This is the exaltation Rasi for the Moon and hence she will normally give good results. If she is Full and in good bhavas, benefic effects are sure to follow. If she is decreasing, then the results are proportionately less. See the following to know as to how she will act remaining in Taurus for various ascendants:

If Full Moon is in Taurus ascendant, it shows birth in affluent and noble family. This will make the native a famous personage. On the contrary if she is with diminishing rays and is in Taurus ascendant, the native will suffer from rheumatic diseases, cough, T.B.etc., and will have curtailed longevity. If she is Full and in the 2nd house identical with Taurus, i.e. for Aries ascendant, the native will enjoy Raja Yoga in Moon’s dasa and will have abundant wealth but be subjected to sickness; if it is weak Moon, monetary gains are limited and diseases will be more. For Pisces ascendant, the Moon, if Full, in Taurus, will give famous children, wealth etc. If she is weak in the 3rd, a Pisces native will be subjected to throat disorders and will suffer lack of progeny. If an Aquarian has the Full Moon in Taurus, he will have fame but will have pressure of delts and diseases and cannot enjoy much wealth; if it is weak Moon, there will be no maternal happiness at all while learning, property etc., will be quite meagre. For A Capricom native, the Moon in Taurus, if Full, will confer early and happy marriage; if she is weak, the native will have strained marital relationship and will seek pleasures out of marital bonds. In the case of birth in Sagittarius, the Moon in Taurus, if Full, will give medium longevity but will give some pressure on the front of diseases, debts etc., and if be weak, the longevity is quite curtalled and adverse effects will be in full. For Scorpio ascendant, the Full Moon in the 7th will give a beautiful and chaste wife, property from women and also political fame and wisdom; if she be weak, marriage is almost in a pipedream and if at all it takes place, it will not prove happy. If Rahu/Ketu/Saturn give company to weak Moon in the 7th for a Scorpio native, the wife may develop illicit relationship with other men and may even elope from the house. If Libra be the ascendant with Full Moon in the 8th, the native will enjoy fair longevity, good position (with some downfall), but will not retain his earnings; if it is week Moon, the native will face umpteen obstacles in his career and may be shortlived while money will be very scarce with him. Should Virgo rise at birth having full Moon in the 9th in Taurus, the native will add

to his paternal property and have all his own desires fulfilled while weak Moon will sometime or other cause debacles in money matters. For a Leo native, Full Moon in the 10th in Taurus will bestow good conduct, property, position etc., but cannot reach the native to heights unless well-aspected by Mars, while weak Moon will give many hindrances in career and show heavy expenses. If Cancer be the ascendent with Full Moon in Taurus, the native is born in wealthy family, enjoys all paraphernalia and is never short of anything in life; if the Moon be weak, the native will have to struggle hard to come up in life. Lastly if Gemini ascend at birth, the Full Moon in the 12th in Taurus is less problamatic while a weak Moon is a headload.

कर्षकमतिकर्मकर द्विपदचतुष्पदसमृद्धमत्यादधम् । प्रायोगिक प्रकुस्ते वृषभे रविवीक्षितव्चन्द्रः ॥eII श्रतिकामं कुजहट्टो युवतिक्ते नष्टवारमित्रजनम् । हुव्यहरं नारीखां मातुर्न घुमं वाशी वृषे कुर्ते ।१०।। प्राज्ञं वाक्यविधिनं प्रमुदितमिष्टं समस्तभूतानाम् । जनयति बुषेन दृष्टः रज्षी वृषेऽनुपमयुर्णयु क्तम् ॥११।। स्षरपुत्रवारसुहृदं मातापितृभक्तिमन्तमतिनिपुर्म् । धामिकमतिविस्यातं गवि गुरुहङ्ग: क्षश्ती कुरुते ।१२।। मूषखायानगृहाखां शयनासनगन्धवस्रमाल्यानाम् । मागिनमुपभोक्तारं सितेक्षितो गवि क्षज्षी कुश्ते ॥१३। घनहीनमनिष्करं वृषभे मातुस्तथा च युवतोमास् । सुतमित्रबन्धुसहितं रविसुतहट्टः क्षश्षी कुर्ते ॥१४।

9-14. THE MOON IN TAURUS IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should the Moon be in Taurus, and be in aspect to the Sun, the native will be a farmer, be very industrious, be very rich with servants and quadrupeds, and will lend money on usury. If Mars aspects the Moon in Taurus, the subject will be highly libidinous, will lose his wife and friends on ac-

count of another lady, will steal the heart of the fair sex, and will prove adverse for the mother. Should the said Moon be in aspect to Mercury, the native will be highly learned, will know the code of speech, be of pleasing disposition, be dear to everyone, and will uncomparably be of good qualities. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in Taurus, the native will have long-living wife and children and lasting wealth, be respectfully disposed to his parents, be virtuous and very famous. Should Venus aspect the Moon in Taurus, one will be cndowed with ornaments, conveyances and houses, and will possess comforts of sleeping and sitting (i.e. will possess luxuries of bed and sitting paraphernalia), scents, robes, garlands etc. If it is Saturn that aspects the Moon in Taurus, one will be devoid of wealth, be inauspicious for mother and wife and will be endowed with sons, friends and relatives.

Notes: The Moon in Taurus (or Libra) is a favourable position for money lenders. If she is aspected by the Sun (or Jupiter) the native will have money-lending as profession. This is very favourable for carcer in banks, financial institutons etc. Mars aspecting her in Taurus, makes the person very sensuous and he is sure to be of questionable character. When Mercury aspects the Moon in Taurus, such natives will do well to pursue some independent profession, trade etc. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in Taurus, one will have a well-settled family life. Venus aspecting the exalted Moon indicates that the native can command anybody and will earn respect from anyone. If Saturn, will reasonably be rich. I do not know why the author indicates poverty in this context except for the baneful lordship (9th house) of Saturn. Saturn aspecting the Moon in Taurus, can however throw some financial ups and downs, depending on the rising degree. This aspect can give a long life to the native, but may affect mother's longevity in general.

पूर्वोषें सम्भूतो जननीमृत्युं करोति न चिरेख ।

पद्चाद्ये वृषमे पितुवियोगं शाशी कुस्ते ॥१५॥

15. AFFLICTION TO PARENTS: Should the Moon be in the first half of Taurus, the mother of the native is not longlived. Similarly, the father is short-lived if the Moon occuples the second half of Taurus.

Notes: This rule should not be verbatim applied. If the Moon is in the first 15 degrees of Taurus and the 4th lord and Venus are simultaneously afflicted, then the mother’s longevity may be at stake. Similarly, the Moon in the 2nd half of Taurus, while the Sun/Saturn and the 9th lord are heavily blemished, longevity of the father may be at stake. Alternatively, if the Moon occupies the 8th house in the first or sccond half of Taurus, mother /father are respectively affected.

उन्नास-दयामचक्षुः सुरतविधिकलाकाव्यकृद्धोगभोगी

हस्ते मत्स्याधिपाड्को विषयासुखरतो बुद्धिवक्षः सिरालः । कान्तः सौभाग्यहास्यप्रियवचनयुतः स्त्रोजितो व्यायताङ्को याति क्लोबेऽ्च सर्यं वाश्चिनि मियुनगे मातृयुम्मप्रपुष्टः ॥१६।।

16. THE MOON IN GEMINI: If the Moon is in Gemini at birth, one will have prominent nose and dark eyes, will be skilful in the art of love, poetry etc., will enjoy sexual pleasures, will have lines of fish in the palm, will be fond of worldly enjoyments, will be sinewy, be very intelligent, splendorous, be endowed with happiness, jocular disposition and eloquent speech, be won over by the females, will have a long body, will befriend neuters and will have two mothers.

Notes: The Moon in Geminl ascending, at birth, gives the qualities of Mercury to the native in general. He will have a prominent nose, which makes him look beautiful. As Mercury is a eunuch, the native will make friendship with neuters. He will enjoy sexual pleasures with barren females. He will be attached more to worldly pleasures rather than the Supreme Spirit. People born under this sign may be gifted speakers and if the Sun occupies the 10th, politlcal aspirations can be noted to one's advantage. The Moon

remaining in Gemini acts differently  $for$  different ascendants as under:

Aries ascendant: The Moon \$ $eminl gives expenditure galore and the native's mof! 4$  face separation from his father. If the Moon is wih^ishing rays, this is more certain. Taurus ascendant: Wwan the Moon in the 2nd in Gemini the native will have significant savings and will live long if she is Full. If she is decreasing, the co-born will suffer from mental diseases and lack of intelligence. The native will be rough in speech. Cancer ascendant: The Moon in the 12th if full will keep the native free from discases and if waning causes diseases from childhood till the end. Such waning Moon will be an impediment in the overall growth of the native. Leo ascendant: With Full Moon, the native will acquire wealth through fair means and with waning Moon, the native will have frequent losses and will lack ability to satiafy his female in coition. Virgo ascendant: The Full Moon in Gemini is capable of conferring high position and good income in the fields of publication, communication, radio/television etc. The native can amass wealth in Moon’s dasa. If she be waning, the native will resort to illegal profession, will have mean conduct, be subjected to vices and will lead a miserable life. Libra ascendant; If Full Moon is in Gemini, the person will undertake to visit Ganges, shrines etc., and will acquire paternal wealth. If it is declining Moon, there will be frequent misunderstandings with father, cousins etc. Scorpio ascendant: Even the Full Moon in Gemini cannot make any satisfactory contribution to longevity, not to speak of declining Moon. If the Full Moon is helped by Jupiter/Saturn, then a fair longevity can be expected. Weak Moon in the 8th in Gemini is an insurmountable menace to the prosperity of the native. Sagittarius ascendant: The Moon in Gemini will give a cunning and belligerent wife, though beautiful. Marriage is consummated with great difficulty. If she is weak and is particularly with the Sun, then the spouse lives only for a short period. Capricorn ascendant: The Moon in Gemini, if-full, can reduce the scope of diseases to be suffered by the wife while declining Moon almost gives a

perennially sick wife. Gemini Moon for this ascendant will cause diseases of private parts both for the male and his female. Aquarius ascendant: The Moon in Gemini does not promote the descent of the race and the native will not be able to fulfil his aims at any period of his life. If she is full, some redemption can be expected. Pisces ascendant: Progeny is delayed while birth of a male issue is doubted unless Jupiter is strong. The eldest issue will be doubtlessly a female. The results so far stated will depend on the state of the planet Mercury also.

प्रजावनं प्रकाशं मियुने रूपान्वितं सुर्धामिव्ठम् । ब्रतिदुःखितमस्पार्यं करोति सूर्ये क्षितश्चन्त्रः ॥१७। प्रतिश्चुरमतिप्राज्ञं सुखवाह्नविभवरूपसम्पन्नम् । कुर्ते मिबुने चन्द्रो बक्रेर निरीक्षितोऽव्यम् ॥१८।। श्र्योत्पादनकुवालं कुर्ते ह्वयापराजितं सुधौरं च । पार्थिवमखण्डिताज्ञं मियुने बुधवीक्षितञ्चन्द्रः ।१। विद्यावास्त्राचार्यं विष्यातं सत्यवाचमतिरूपम्। मान्यं वाम्मिनमिन्दुः करोति गुरुवीक्षितो मिथुने ।।२०॥। बर्युवति-माल्यत्वस्त्रवर-वाहन-यान-मूर्णम्िभ्भिः । कीर्डा कुरुते पुरुषो भृयुहृष्टे शाविनि मिथुनस्थे ।२१। कुर्ते बान्धवरहितं युवतिसुखविमूतिवाजतं चापि । श्रघनं लोकदेव्रं जितुमे शनिवेक्षितञ्चन्द्रः ।२२॥1

17-22. THE MOON IN GEMINI IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in Gemini at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the nattve will be quite learned, be splendorous, very beautiful, be charitable, be very miserable and be not rich. If Mars lends aspect to the Moon in Gemini, the person will be very valorous, very learned, be endowed with happiness, conveyances, wealth and beauty. This is certain. The Moon in Gemini aspected by Mercury makes one skilful in producing money, always successful and inviolable king. If Juplter aspects the Moon in Gemini, one will be.a teacher of sastras, be famous, truthful, very beautiful, honourable

and be an eloquent speaker. If Venus aspects the Moon in Gemini, the person will be endowed with the company of supreme females, garlands, robes, conveyances, ornaments and jewels, and will be sportive. Should Saturn aspect the Moon in Geminl, the subject will be devoid of relatives, wife, happiness and wealth, and will be inimical to the public.

Notes: Kalyana Varma states that when the Moon in Gemini is aspected by the Sun, the native will not be rich. It may be more aptly applicable when one of these two happens to be in evil houses from the ascendant or be an evil lord from the ascendant. Should they be in good house, shortage of money may not exist. If the Moon in Gemini is aspected by Mercury, the native may pursue his own profession, business etc., and will gain abundantly. The sign Gemini itself is a sign of wisdom, intelligence etc., and if the Moon posited therein is aspected by Jupiter, he will achieve great name in sastras, literatures, teachings etc. For Aries ascendant, this aspect will confer a mellifluous voice on the native and he may become a famous singer. The aspect of Venus to the Moon produces great riches while Saturn’s aspect is adverse for wealth.

युक्तः सौभाग्यर्रवेगु हरसुद्दटनज्योतिष्ञानशीलः कामासक्तः कृतज्ञः क्षितिपतिसचिवः सत्यवक्ता प्रवासी । सोन्मादः केवकतापो जलकुमुमरुचिहानिवृद्धच्यानुयातः प्रासावोद्यानवापीप्रियकरर्रतः पीनकण्ठः कुलीरे ।२३।

23. THE MOON IN CANCER: If the Moon is in Cancer at birth, the native will be fortunate, valorous, be endowed with residence, friends, journeys and astrological knowledge, be sensuous, grateful, be a minister, be truthful, will live abroad, be passionate, hairy bodied, fond of water and flowers, interested in construction of houses, wells etc., and will have a prominent neck.

Notes: Even though the Moon may be weak, if she is in Cancer ascendant, the person will enjoy fair longevity (Jambunadeeyam Ch. 1 rule 46). If she joins Jupiter in

Cancer the native rules Kashmir according to classic texts, see Karkasthita devaguro sachandro Kashmir desam raja bhavatt, In the modern context, if the native becomes a minister or a significant person the effects be deemed fulfilled. If Saturn aspects the Moon in Cancer, a female will have long bunch of hair. Saturn aspecting Moon in Cancer may cause occasional ups and downs in one’s career. The Moon in Cancer is generally a good position for occult studies. Now scc the different effects given by the Moon remaining in Cancer for the 12 ascendants:

Aries ascendant: Strong Moon in Cancer is capable of conferring position with distinct identity, happiness, learning etc., while weak Moon causes defects of the heart. Taurus ascendant: An afflicted Moon causes long-lasting infection, colds etc., while a strong Moon brings fame through music, gives paternal property, luck etc., and makes the native highly intelligent. Gemini ascendant: If Mars joins the Moon in Cancer, it can bring death in childhood and if Mars dasa or Moon’s dasa coincides with childhood, it is all the more certain. Full Moon contributes to lasting wealth. Cancer ascendant: If the Moon is full, the native will rise high in life and lives without obstacles. Weak Moon causes occasional downfall and eye discases, blood disorders etc. Leo ascendant: With weak Moon, the native joins bad people while with Full Moon he will be free from diseases, unnecessary expenses etc., and will respect other women. Virgo ascendant: The native with strong Moon will have only sisters born prior to him, will gain through good deeds and will obtain only female issues; if Saturn joins the full Moon in the I Ith the native will beget twins. If the Moon is weak at birth, the native will not beget children and will resort to bad means to earn money. Libra ascendant: If the Moon is along with the Sun, in Sun’s dasa, the native will make gains while his Moon’s dasa will give moderate results. Scorpio ascendant: Should the Moon and the Sun join in Cancer, the native will enjoy enviable position in life and will have all paraphernalia at his beck and call. Should Mars join the Moon he will have defective limbs. Sagittarius ascendant: Unless the Moon is Full, she

cannot make any worthy contribution to longevity. Death by drowning is indicated. Capricorn ascendant: Full Moon in 7th will confer bride from known circles and in the native place of the person. Weak Moon will make the native remain at loggerheads with others. Aquarius ascendant: Weak Moon makes the native seek sexual pleasures in illegal ways and contract various venereal diseases. Full Moon proves a powerful antidote against enemies. Pisces ascendant: The person will enjoy good luck, intelligence, honour, position and female issues.

नरपतिपुर्यमधन्यं धनरहितं लेलहारकं वाऽपि । कुस्ते स्वगृहे चर्दो रविडट्टो दुर्गपालं च ।२४। शूरं विक्वारीरं मातुरनर्थावहं क्रियादक्षम् । क्षितितनयवीक्षिततनुर्जनयति चन्द्रो नर स्वग्हे ॥२५॥ स्रविकलमति नयतं जनयति बुधवीक्षित: झशी स्वगुहे। बनदारपुत्रबन्तं नृपसचिवं सौस्यवन्तं च ।२६।। नृपति नृपयुखायुन्तं जनयति चन्द्रः सुरेज्यसंह्यः । स्वगृहे मुखितसुभार्य न्यविनयपरात्रमाक्रान्तम् ।२७। घनकनकवस्त्रयोविद्रत्नानां भाजनं झाक्षी कुर्ते । कर्कटके सितदृष्टो वेद्याजननायकं कान्तम् ।२८। यटनमतुखं दरिदं मातुरनिष्टं प्रियानृतं पापम् । शानिना दृष्टः स्वगृहे करोति चट्ट्रो नरं नीचम् ॥२eII

24-29. THE MOON IN CANCER IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: Should the Moon be in her house at birth and be aspected by the Sun, the native will be in the employ of king, be not rich, be a letter-bearer and will protect forts (1.e. will be a security officer in royal service). If the Moon in Cancer is aspected by Mars, the subject will be valorous, be deformed, will prove ominous to his mother and be skilful in his jobs. Should Mercury lend his aspect to the Moon in Cancer, he will be spirited in disposition, be endowed with political wisdom, wealth, wife and sons, will be a king’s minister and be happy. Jupiter aspecting the Moon in her

own house indicates that the person will be a king, be endowed with royal qualities, be happy, will have a good wife, will behave well, be modest and valorous. If Venus aspects the Moon in Cancer, the subject will be endowed with money, gold, wife, robes and jewels, be head of prostitutes and be splendorous. Should Saturn aspect the Cancer-posited Moon, he will be of wandering disposition, be miserable, very poor, be a llar, a sinner and be mean.

Notes: The Moon in Cancer in aspect to the Sun is an ideal position for national Ambassadors, employment in Defence units etc. The native may suffer occasional debacles in monetary affairs. If Mars aspects the Moon in Cancer, the native may lose his mother early, particularly if the aspect is from Capricorn, where Mars acquires great strength. Mercury’s aspect over the Cancer-Moon is favourable for political success while the spouse may face nervous breakdown, rheumatic disorders etc. Jupiter aspecting the said Moon, except from Capricorn, confers very great wealth, fame, name etc., on the native while the aspect of Jupiter from Capricorn may give limited results if the Moon has not significant pakshabala. Venus lending aspect to the Moon in Cancer will give association with whores and money in questionable manner. However wealth will be plentiful while two marriages may be possible. With Saturn if in aspect to the Moon in Cancer, he may not achieve much financial success but will be a rolling stone.

स्यूलास्थिर्मन्दरोमा पृयुवदनगलो ह्वस्वपिश्चाक्षियुम्मः स्त्रीद्वेवी क्षुत्पिपासाजठररदर्जापीडितो मांसभक्षः । वाता तीव्योऽल्पपुत्रो विपिमनगरतिर्मातृवद्यः सुवक्षा

विक्रान्तः कार्यलीनः शवामृति रविमे सर्वगम्भीरदृष्टिः ।।३०।।

30. THE MOON IN LEO: If the Moon is in Leo, one will have sturdy bones, sparse hair, wide face, small and yellowish eyes, will hate women, will suffer from hunger and thirst, will incur stomachial disorders and tooth-decay, will eat flesh, be charitable, harsh, will have few sons, will seek sexual union in forests and hills, be respectfully disposed

to his mother, will have broad chest, be valorous, dutiful and will have majestic looks.

Notes: With the Moon in Leo, the native will tend to sexual union in forests/hills (or in remote places) as the lion seeks. His voice resembles that of a llon—so majestic it is and he eats flesh. He may not have so good a marital front unless Venus, Sun or Jupiter comes to the Moon’s rescue. Now see how the Moon reacts remaining in Leo for the various ascendants prevailing at birth.

Aries ascendant: The person lacks progenic ability or with great difficulty (i.e. after propitiations, medical treatments etc.) he will beget a female issue. The native may not be upto mark in respect of intelligence. Taurus ascendant: Weak Moon cannot give many brothers/sisters but will cause braindrain. Full Moon gives abundant landed property (around deserts or barren lands). Geminl ascendant: The person will have many brothers if the Moon is full. If weak, loss of co-born, lack of intelligence, burning scnsation in the throat, Tonsils etc., will be experienced. Cancer ascendant: Child mortality is indicated if the Moon is weak. Full Moon confers great longevity. Leo ascendant: The native will be night-blind with weak Moon right from his boyhood while Full Moon may cause eye defects at a later part of life. However, longevity will depend on Saturn and other factors. Virgo ascendant: The elder sister may be barren while the native may be in penury for a greater part of his life. Libra ascendant: The native will spend on auspicious functions like marriage, will have a decent living and will spend away his paternal property in life time. Scorpio ascendant: He will have loving parents, high education etc. The father may however be at loggerheads with the mother. Sagittarius ascendant: The native’s father will be shortlived while the native will be subjected to misfortunes. Capricorn ascendant: The native will not enjoy a happy married life while his spouse may not be virile. Aquarius ascendant: The native’s spouse will be sickly while his enemies will always trouble hfm. Pisces ascendant: He will not have healthy children, will experience

miseries throughout life and will be holding only low positions in lifc.

नृपतिसमत्वं कुर्ते प्रात्हर्टगुणं महास्वनं वीरम् । रविश्या दृष्टः सिहे पानरतं विश्रुतं चन्द्रः ।३१।। सेनापति प्रचण्डं वरयुवतिसुतार्यवाहनोयेतम् । जनयत्युत्तमपुरुषं कुजेक्षितश्चन्द्रमाः सिहे । ३२। स्त्रोसत्वं स्त्रीललितं स्त्रीव्यं युवतिसेवकं सिहे । कुरुते बुधेन दृष्टो घनमुखभोगान्वितं चन्द्रः ॥३३।। श्रभिजातं कुलमुख्यं बहुश्रुतं गुरासमृद्धं च । कुस्ते नरेन्द्रतुल्यं गुरुदृष्टद्वन्द्रमाः सिहे ।३४। प्रमवा विभर्व्युक्तं रोगिर्मपि युवतिसेवकं कुर्ते । सुरतविधिज्ञं प्राज्ञं शशी हरो चुक्तन्दुष्टः ।३५। कर्षकमधनं कुर्तेऽनृतवाचं दुर्गपालक सिहे । रविजन तथा दृष्टो युवतिसुर्खहोंनमल्पकं च शाशी ॥३६।

31-36. THE MOON IN LEO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should the Moon be in Leo in aspect to the Sun, the native will be equal to a king, will have excellent qualitics and majestic voice, will be valorous, be fond of intoxicants and be widely famous. If Mars aspects the Moon in Leo he will be an army chief will have excellent wife, sons, wealth and conveyances and be superior among people. Should Mercury aspect the Moon in Leo one will be endowed with the characteristics of a female, and also the grace akin to that of a female; will be in the custody of females, will serve females and will enjoy money, happiness and pleasures. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in Leo, one will be excellent among his castemen, be wide-famed, highly virtuous and will equal a king. Should Venus aspect the Moon in Leo, one will possess a wife, wealth, and high knowledge, be sickly disposed, will be a female’s servant, and be skilful in sexual union. If the Moon in Leo is aspected by Saturn, the person will be an agriculturist, be not wealthy, be a liar, will protect forts, be devoid of happiness from wife and be mean.

Notes: If the Moon in Leo is aspected by the Sun, while the ascendant is one of Aries, Leo and Scorplo, the native will beget big fortuncs. Similarly if aspected by Mars for these three ascendants. Mercury’s aspect on the Moon in Leo in general is a good combination for astrology. Saturn aspecting the Moon in Leo will make the native resort to extra-marital relationship. The aspect of Jupiter or of Venus will confer wealth.

स्त्रीलोलो लम्बबाहुर्ललिततनुमुखद्चा रुदन्ताक्षिकर्णो विद्वानाचार्यधर्मा प्रियवचनयुतः सत्यत्यौचप्रधानः । घीरः स्वानुकम्पी परविवयरतः क्षान्तिसौभाग्यभागी कन्याप्रायप्रसूतिर्वहुमुतरहितः कन्यकायां शाशाङ्क ।३७।।

37. MOON IN VIRGO: If the Moon occuples Virgo at birth, one will be addicted to women, will have long hands, attractive body and face, beautiful tceth, eyes and ears, be learned, be a religious preceptor (teaching Vedasetc.), be an eloquent speaker, be truthful and pure, valorous, be kind to living beings, be interested in others' affairs, be of forgiving disposition, be fortunate, will have more daughters but not many sons.

Notes: The Moon remaining in Virgo will give varied results as under for the different ascendants:

Aries ascendant: The natve will lose his landed property and will incur heavy expenses on litigations. He will not be happy in regard to his mother, will suffer diseases in boyhood and will generally have limited achievements. Taurus ascendant: He will have a daughter who will become very wealthy in her life. The native will not have good terms with his fricnds and relatives. Gemini ascendant: If the Moon is Full the person will have lasting maternal happtness, learning etc. If Jupiter or Mercury give company to the Moon in Virgo the person will reach high position in life and be wealthy. Cancer ascendant: Even the Full Moon will not give satisfactory results if in Virgo. The native will lack happiness in every respect. Leo ascendant: If the Moon is

weak, the subject will be subjected to poverty and if Full, some wealth can be expected. Virgo ascendant: The Full Moon makes the native beautiful, very affluent and skilful in speech. Weak Moon gives adverse results in her dasa. Libra ascendant: Expenses will be incurred only on good deeds while the prosperity of the native will be obstructed off and on. Helping hand can come only from Venus or Saturn. Scorpio ascendant: The dasa of Full Moon will confer abundant riches while that of weak Moon gives bad conduct, penury etc. Sagittarius ascendant: Full Moon gives association with good affairs, visits to shrines and a noble and great profession. Weak Moon gives attachment to mean profcssions. Capricorn ascendant: Full Moon causes widc fame to the native’s father and luck and longevity to the native while weak Moon deprives one of paternal bliss. Aquarius ascendant: The Full Moon gives medium longevity but no physical comforts. Weak Moon begets a chaste wife with Full Moon and enjoys married life. Progeny is delayed. Weak Moon is not a good augury for married life.

नृपकोशकरं स्यातं गृहोतवाक्यं विशिष्टकर्मारगंम् । कन्यायां रविवृष्टो मार्याहीनं वाशो कुर्ते ।३८।। झिल्पाचार्यं स्यातं धनवन्तं शिक्षितं सुघोरं च । कन्यायां कुजवृष्टो मातुरनिष्ट शवी कुर्ते ॥३६।। ज्योतियकाव्यविधिज्ञं विवादकलहेषु विजयिनं सुतराम् । सातिक्षयं कन्यायां जनपति निपुणं बुबेक्षितञ्चन्द्रः ॥४०।। बन्धुजनाढच सुखिनं नृप त्यकर गृहीतवाक्यं च । कन्यायां गुरुदृष्टो जनयति विभवान्वितं चन्द्रः ॥४१।। कन्यायां बहुदारं विविधानद्धारभोगिनमथाढधम्। सततमिहोजितमुदितं करते भुगुरा निरो वितञ्चन्द्ः ॥४२। श्रवृदस्मृति दरिदं सुखरहितममातृकं युवतिवद्यम् । कन्यार्या यमवृष्टः स्त्रीभागधनं शवी कुरुते ।४३।। 38-43. MOON IN VIRGO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon occupies Virgo at birth and is aspected by the Sun,

the native will be incharge of royal wealth, be famous, will keep up his wor, and perform distinguished acts. If Mars aspects the Moon posited in Virgo, the subjcct will be skilful in mechanical or fine arts (of 64 kinds), be famous, affluent, disciplined, courageous and will be inauspicious for mother. Should it be Mercury that lends aspect to the Moon in Virgo, the native will be expert in astrology and literature, be successful in disputes/quarrels, and be highly skilful to a surprising extent. Jupiter aspecting the Moon in Virgo indicates that the native will be supreme among his relatives, be happy, will carry out royal duties, will keep up his word and will be endowed with wealth. If Venus aspects the Moon in Virgo, the person will have many wives, be endowed with many kinds of makeups, pleasures and wealth and will always be blessed with fortunes. The Moon in Virgo aspected by Saturn indicates that the native will not have firm memory, will suffer from poverty, will not have happiness, will be bereft of mother, will be at the disposal of women (or be controlled by them) and will derive wealth through females.

Notes: If the Moon in Virgo is aspected by the Sun, the native can occupy positions like bank manager, treasury officer etc., and will become a distinguished person. Mars aspecting indicates that the person will be an expert artist while that of Mercury denotes proficient astrologer, lawyer, literator etc. Venus lending its aspect points out to plurality of wives, more so when the ascending sign is also Virgo. Saturn's aspect on the Moon in Virgo denotes that the person will run brothels and the like and will earn money in mean ways.

उन्नासो व्यायताक्षः कुशवदनतनुर्भूरिदारो वृषाढधो गोभूम्यः सौर्यसारो वृषसमव्षयो विक्रमादद्यः क्रियेवः । भक्तो देवद्विजानां बहुविभवयुतः स्त्रीजितो हीनदेहो धान्यादार्नकबुदिस्तुलिनि वाञधरे बन्धुवर्गोपकारी ॥४४।।

44. MOON IN LIBRA: If the Moon is posited in Libra at birth, the natiye will have elevated nose, broad eyes, will

have weak (i.e. not prominent) face and emaciated body, will have many wives, many bulls (or cattle) and abundant landed property, be valorous, will have testicles resembling that of an ox, be skilful in work, will honour Gods and the wise, will be endowed with various kinds of wealth, will be conquered by females, will have an emaciated body (this is a repetition), will donate corns, will not be firm in disposltion and will be helpful to his relatives.

Notes: The Moon is capable of bestowing following effects remaining in Libra, for the various ascendants:

Aries ascendant: If the Moon is Full, she may even inflict death; the native will not have freedom from diseases. If the Moon is weak, she may not pose death on the native, but the mother of the native will not be happy. Taurus ascendant: The native will have, if thc Moon is Full, still troubles from litigations, debts etc., though to a minimum. extent; if she be weak, the subject is always subjected to miseries and inauspicious effects. Gemini ascendant: If the Moon is Full one makes lot of money and maintains abundant savings. He will have physical beauty, mental chastity etc., to full cxtent. If the Moon is declining, loss of female issues, lack of learning, sufferances from brain diseases, blood pressure and having moderate luck should be predicted. Cancer ascendant: If the Moon is Full, he enjoys maternal happiness, landed property, learning, conveyances etc. Paternal inheritance will be quite significant. He will do business across the seas and enjoy abundant riches. If she is decreasing, carly death of parents, obstruction to education, running away from the family etc., can be denoted. Leo ascendant: If the Moon is Full, one will derive good fortunes through sisters. He will achieve fame as singer, actor etc. If she is declining, enmity with co-born, diseases of the throat, blood-clot etc., may be foreseen. Virgo ascendant: If it is Full Moon, one will amass wealth to a surprising extent, will have a beautiful face, pleasant teeth, a rich elder brother and uninterrupted gains. His Moon's dasa will confer a supreme Rajayoga. If she is waning, there will be only an ordinary yoga. Libra ascen-

dant: If the Moon is weak, the native’s longevity is short and he will not enjoy good position. If she is full, one becomes a very notable personage, very famous and very rich. If Venus aspects such Full Moon, the native is richer than the richest. If she is declining, the native is a 'repository' of all kinds of miseries, subjected to rheumatic/blood disorders and will lose his mother very early. Scorpio ascendant: The Moon in the 12th cannot give much wealth but the native will become famous and will spend on meritorious and noble acts. If she is waning, the expenditure will be on bad deeds and one becomes illfamous. Sagittarius ascendant: If the Moon is Full, the earning will be abundant and by fair means. The person will be long-lived but will face occasional losses through theft, machination etc. If she is waning, there will not be much wealth but short-life. Capricorn ascendant: If the Moon is Full, one will be endowed with good conduct, Independent profession and spiritual activities. If she is declining, he will be devoid of character, will be proud and will resort to ignoble means for livelihood. Aquarius ascendant: Moon dasa can give ordinary results if she is full and adverse effects if the Moon is declining. Pisces ascendant: Same as for Aquarius. Additionally, Full Moon bestows long life on Pisces native while declining Moon will cause infant mortality.

ग्रधनं व्याधितमटन परिभूतं भोगविप्रयुक्तं च । स्रसुतमसारं जूके जनयति रविवोक्षितश्चन्द्रः ।४५।। तीक्णं चोरं क्षुद्रं परयोविङ्गगन्धमाल्यसंयुक्तम् । मतिमन्नयनातुरगं जनयति वक्र क्षितदयन्द्रः ॥४६। दृष्टो बुधेन चन्त्रः कलाविदग्धं प्रभ्रूतधनधान्यम्। शुभवाष्यं विद्वासं देशस्यातं तुलाधरे कुरुते ॥४७। जीवेक्षितस्तुलायां जनयति सर्वत्र पूजितं हिमनुः। क्यविष्येषु कुवलं रत्नादिषु भाण्डजातेषु ।४८। ललितमरोगं सुभगं समुपचिताङ्ग' धनान्वितं प्राज्ञम् । विविचोपायविधिज्ञं कुस्ते भुगुबीक्षितः शाक्षी तुलके IYEI)

## कुर्ते वाची घनाढचं प्रियवावयं वाहनेर्युतं जूके। विषयराति सुखरहितं भात्करिदृष्टो हितं मातुः ॥५०।

45-50. MOON IN LIBRA IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in Libra and is aspected by the Sun, the person will be bereft of wealth, be diseased, will wander here and there, be insulted, be bereft of enjoyment, sons and strength. If Mars aspects the Moon in Libra, the person will be sharp, be a thief, be mean, adulterous, will enjoy scents and garlands, be wise, and will suffer from eye diseases. If Mercury aspects the Moon in Libra, the subject will be skilful in arts, will have abundant riches and grains, will be an auspicious speaker, be highly learned, and be famous in his country. Should Jupiter aspect the Moon in Libra, the person will be always worshipped, and be skilful in sales and purchases of gems etc. If Venus lends his aspect to the Moon in Libra, one will be beautiful, be free from sickness, fortunate, strongbodied, be learned, and will have knowledge of many means. Should Saturn aspect the Moon in Libra, the native will be very affluent, be sweet in speech, be cndowed with conveyances, be very much interested in sexual affairs, be devoid of happiness and be favourable to his mother.

Notes: If the Moon in Libra is aspected by the Sun, it is inauspicious for wealth, progeny etc. But if Venus is exalted simultancously or is in Aries (along with the Sun) the malefic effects denoted will not come to pass but good effects will increase in these areas. Still the native will desire frequent changes. If Mars aspects the Moon in Libra from Cancer, the person will be of thievish disposition, will join other women and will contract many diseases. If the aspect is from Pisces, these effects will vanish and the native will be free from diseases, be virtuous and rich. If from Aries, the native will however, acquire an extravagant, irascible wife whose longevity will depend on the strength of Mars. Should Mercury aspect the Moon in Libra, the native will gain paternal property which will however be spent away. If Jupiter lends his aspect to the Moon in Libra,

the subject's father will suffer from diseases while the native’s fortunes will be many. Saturn's aspect on the Moon will make the native very rich, spiritual and philosophical.

सुब्धो वृत्तो रुजड्चः कठिनतरतनुर्नास्तिकः क्ररचेष्टः चोरो बाल्ये रुगार्तो हृतचिबुकनखञ्चार्नेत्रः समृद्धः । कर्मोद्य वतः प्रदक्षः परयुवतिरतो बन्धुहीनः प्रमत्तः चण्डो राजा हुतस्वः पृथुजठरवाराः कीटभे शीतरदमौ ॥५१।।

51. MOON IN SCORPIO: Should the Moon be in Scorplo at birth, one will be a miser, will have round thighs (i.e. wellgrown thighs), will have coarse physique and nose, (i.e. the physique and nose will not be soft), will be cruel in acts, be a thief, be sick in childhood, will have spoiled chin and nails but beautiful eyes, will be plentiful, be industrious, skilful, fond of others’ houscwives, devoid of relatives, insane (or infatuated with passion), valorous, will lose wealth due to royal wrath and will have a big abdomen and a big head.

Notes: The Moon when in Scorpio will be in fall and hence it proportionately reduces the potence of the horoscope. If however she occupies further favourable houses, this defickency is offset to a great extent. Or if she is Full or is aspected by Venus, Mercury, Jupiter or Mars, much betterment can be expected. Posited in Scorpio, the Moon can manifest the following different effects for the various rising signs.

Aries ascendant: If the Moon is in Scorpio at birth, the native and his mother, as well, will be sick and shortlived. There will always be poverty. Taurus ascendant: If the Moon is Full, the wife of the native will be amicable, beautiful and will bring fortunes with her. If the Moon is waning, the native's marriage is delayed and the married life will not be happy. The second issue of the native will be afflicted or short-lived. Geminl ascendant: The subject will contract several debts and will always be in wants. Full Moon mitigates these defects sufficiently. Cancer ascendant: The

subject will suffer abdominal disorders. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in this case, dropsy will result and death will be caused in the major or sub periods of Moon/Jupiter. Leo ascendant: If the Moon is in 3rd degrce of Scorpio, all the good combinations and merits of the horoscope are marred and the native cannot prosper. If Rahu joins the Moon, the mother is unchaste. Virgo ascendant: The native will be deaf or will suffer from diseases of the ear. If the Moon is Full, treatments will be of some help. Libra ascendant: The person concerned will not make any headway in profession and will face acute poverty. Scorpio ascendant: The subject will be dullwitted, foolish and poor, Sagittarius ascendant: If it is New Moon, death will take place at the age of 8. If Full Moon survives the native, he will resort to unfair means of earning and will spend away all his wealth. Capricorn ascendant: If Moon is Full, her dasa will bestow somewhat better effects but not marital bliss. If weak, there will be all round adversities apart from quite an unhappy married life. Aquarius ascendant: In this case weak Moon in fall in the 10th gives excellent results, abundant Luck etc., while Full Moon gives litle favourable effects, see the sloka con- ध्यास्थ्यः दशमे पूर्खा चन्द्रस्ये स्वल्पयोगप्रदायकः जीक्षश्वेत्पर्ण सौभाम्ययोगमेव )ा(with reference toAquarius).Pisces Ascendant: Full Moon gives moderate results but grief on account of children while declining Moon does not confer any auspicious results.

कुस्ते लोक्द्ध्यं बुधमटनं चैव वित्तवन्तं च। दिनकरदृष्टोऽलिगतञ्चन्द्रः सुखर्वाजतं पुरुषम् ॥५२। स्रनुवमर्धयें कुरुते नृपतिसमं वृदिचके विभूतियुतम् । सूरमजय्यं समरे प्रभक्षणं मूमिजेन संदूषः १५३। ग्रचतुरममुष्टवाक्यं यमलापत्यं च युवितमन्तं च । जनयति बुयेन दृष्टः कूटकरं वृदिचके च गीतज्ञम् ॥५४।। कर्मासक्तं कुरुते लोकट्वेच्यं च वित्तवन्तं च। गुरुता दृष्टोऽलिगतो निज्ञाकरो रूपवन्तं च ।५५॥

## स्रतिमतिमतीव सुभगं धनवाहनभोगललितमिह कीटे । युवतिविनाशितसारं जनयति भुगुवीक्षितवचनद्रः ॥५६। नोचास्त्यं कृपणं व्याधितमधनं च सत्यहीनं च । जनयत्यन्तकदृष्टो नरमधनं चन्द्रमाः कीटे ॥५७।।

52-57. MOON IN SCORPIO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon at birth is in Scorplo and aspected by the Sun, the native will hate people (i.e. will not be friendly with others), be learned, wandering natured, and rich but be not happy. Should Mars aspect the Moon in Scorpio, the native will have incomparable courage, be equal to a king, be endowed with wealth, be valorous, inconquerable in battle, and be a voracious eater. If Mercury aspects the Moon in Scorpio, the person will not be skilful, be hard in speech, will obtain twins, be tricky, will produce fictitious things and be an expert singer. If Jupiter lends aspect to the Moon in Scorpio, the native will be intcrested in performing his duties, will be biased towards people, be wealthy and be beautiful. If Venus aspects the Moon in Scorpio, the native will be highly intelligent, be fortunate, be endowed with riches, conveyances and beauty, and will lose strength on account of women. If Saturn aspects the Moon in Scorpio, the native will have base sons, be a talebearer, be sick, poor and untruthful.

Notes: While the aspect of the Sun on the Moon in Scorpio has its brighter side, it has its adverse phase as well in that the luni-solar relationship involving Scorpio-Taurus brings strained married life. The spouse is oftener found irascible, upper-handed and selfish. The native is generally not happy in most fields except that he reaps some wealth. This is good for higher education if the Moon is beyond her deep fall zone. Mars lending aspect to the Moon adds to the courage of the person. He will be more disposed towards eating frequently hot foods. Financial achievements will be splendid if Mars aspects from Aries. Mercury’s aspect takes away the self-confidence invested by other planetary combinations and gives problems in speech, like stammering.

The native will indulge in cheating, trickery, jugglery etc. He will be skilful in forgery, and will invite risk if Rahu is associated with the Moon or Mercury. Jupiter’s aspect on the Moon gives physical beauty but slender waist. This aspect may produce problems like intestinal ulcer, or indigestion etc. If the aspect of Jupiter is from Cancer/Pisces, it may result in dropsy and consequent death. Venusian touch from Taurus on the Moon gives abundant wealth, beautiful and libidinous wife, and business in beautifying articles, textiles, jewellery, interior decoration etc. The combination is also capable of causing imbalances in excretory and generative system. Saturn (singly) aspecting the Moon in Scorpio denies marriage altogether but the native tends to seek pleasures of sex out of the way. If at all marriage is granted, it is only namesake. This aspect triggers of various discases like heart trouble, windy complaint etc. This is also a dysmelic indication.

कुब्जाङ्गो वृत्तनेन्नः प्पुट्दयकटिः पोनबाहुः प्रवक्ता दोर्घासो दीर्घकष्ठो जलतटवसतिः शिल्पिविङ्ग गूढविज्ञः । बूरो हुटोऽस्थिसारो विततबहुबलः स्यूलकण्ठीष्ठ्योखो बन्धुस्नेही कृतज्ञो घनुषि वशिधरे संहताङ्ध्रिः प्रगल्भः ॥५८

58. MOON IN SAGITTARIUS: If the Moon is in Sagittarius at birth, the native will be dwarfish, will have round eyes, big heart, waist and hands, be a good speaker, will have prominent shoulders and neck, will live near watery zone, will have knowledge of arts and secret affairs, be courageous, will possess strong bones, be very strong, will have strong neck and lips, be attached to his relatives, be grateful and distinguished. His legs will not be wider when in position.

Notes: With the Moon in Sagittarius, the native will be short in stature and will have a heart which will be big in size. If Jupiter is strong, the native will grow to normal size but will have a bulky physique. The Moon's placement in this sign is favourable for study of occult sciences and working in departments related to investigations, enquiry

commissions etc. With reference to the 12 ascendants, the Moon in Sagittarius, produces the following effects:

Aries ascendant: Though the Moon has blemish she will prove beneficial in the 9th in Sagittarius. Prosperity, luck, wealth etc., are conferred, as a consequence. If the Moon is weak, one will be highly irreligious and will not be favourably disposed to good acts. Taurus ascendant: The native will be subjected to physical distress, irrespective of the Moon being Full or New, Weakness of thighs, hips and bad lungs will result in the relative periods of the Moon. Gemini ascendant: With weak Moon, the married life will be short while full Moon gives unexpected riches in speculation. The Moon—whether Full or New—in Sagittarius makes a Geminian prone to accidents caused by fall from height. Fear from animals, loss of memory and premature fall of teeth will follow. Cancer ascendant: One will be addicted to frequent drinking of liquor, gambling, debauchery and contracting loans. If, however, the Moon is strong, beneficlal results will be reaped and the person will be free from diseases and be long-lived. Leo ascendant: Bereavement on account of children, gains from animal husbandry etc., will result while the dark side denotes cardiac/respiratory disorders. Virgo ascendant: The person will be highly learned and will be affectionate to his mother. Libra ascendant: The native will gain in profession through his coborn. A female with Full Moon will have prominent breasts while with weak Moon, she will have depressed breasts. Scorpio ascendant: The Full Moon in 2nd will give excellent results in respect of wealth, patrimony etc., in her dasa while weak Moon will give ordinary results. Weak Moon with malefics shall cause early death of parents, particularly father. Sagittarius ascendant: Full Moon in the ascendant can contribute to moderate longevity while weak Moon is adverse and shall cause perennial diseases right from childhood. Capricon ascendant: If the weak Moon is in Sagittarius, the native’s spouse will tend to live separately while Full Moon keeps the tempo soft. Aquarius ascendant: The native will be free from diseases while his children may be prone to these. Pisces ascendant: Full Moon offers

exemplary and independent career and the native will leave behind a huge fortune for his children. Weak Moon does not ensure cordial relationship with children and the native will be angrily disposed.

नृपतिमथाड्यं कुर्ते शूरं विस्यातपौरुषं चापे । भास्करहब्टदचन्द्रस्त्वनुपममुखवाहनोपेतम् uxen सैनार्पात समृद्धं सुभगं प्रस्यातपौर्वं पुरुषम् । 'जनयत्यनुपमभृत्यं क्षितिसुतहङ्कः शाशी धनुषि ॥६०।। बहुन्त्यंत्वक्सारं ज्यौतिष शिल्यक्रियादिनिपुणं च । बुबहृष्टो हिमर्ष्मर्नाटच्याचार्यं ह्ये कुर्ते ॥६१। घनुपमदेहं कुर्ते पृथ्वीपालस्य मरित्रणं चापे। त्रिदवागुरुहृष्टमूर्तिर्धनधर्मसुखान्वितं चन्त्रः ।६२।। सुखिनमतीव हि ललितं सुभयं पुत्रार्थकामबन्तं च । चापे सुमित्रभार्यं भार्यवदुष्टः करोतीन्दुः ॥ ६३।। पियवादिनं सुवाष्यं बहुश्रुतं सत्यवादिनं सौम्यस्। श्रभिजातं नृपपुरुषं जनयति सौरेक्षितः शशी घनुषि ॥६४।

89-64. MOON IN SAGITTARIUS IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: If the Moon in Sagittarius is aspected by the Sun, the person will be a king, be affluent, valorous, famous and will have uncomparable happiness and conveyances. Should Mars aspect the Moon in Sagittarius, the person will be an army chief, will be very rich, fortunate, famous for his valour and will have a large working force. If the Moon in Sagittarlus is aspected by Mercury, one will have many servants, will be an expert astrologer and artist, and be a skilful dancer. If Jupiter aspects the Moon in Sagittarius, one will possess very attractive physique, be a king's minister, and will be endowed with wealth, virtues and happiness. Should Venus aspect the Moon in Sagittarius, the nattve will be happy, beautiful and fortunate, be endowed with sons, wealth and sexual desires and will have good friends and wife. If Saturn aspects the Moon in Sagittarius, the person will be sweet in speech, will speak good

words, will have wide knowledge of Sastras, be truthful, soft and be a king’s man.

Notes: The Moon in Sagittarlus, aspected by Sun/ Mars/Jupiter is favourable for Sagittarius, Scorpio, Aries, Leo and Cancer ascendants. In the general context, the Moon in Sagittarius aspected by the Sun gives riches, fortune, vehicles etc. Also if by Mars or Jupiter, Venus aspecting the Moon in this Rasi, though bestows excess desire for sexual connections, the native shall not go out of the way. His married life will be quite happy. Even Saturn's aspect on the Moon in Sagittarius gives good tendencies, knowledge of sciences galore, truthfulness and so on and so forth; but Jupiter’s aspect simultaneously or his conjunction with Saturn lends fortune etc.

गीतज्ञः शीतभीरः पृशुलतरतिराः सत्यधर्मोप्सेवी प्रांधुः स्यातोऽल्परोषो मनसिभवयुतो निर्जु खास्त्यक्तजव्जः । चार्वक्षः शामदेहो गुर्युवतिरतः सत्कविबु सजड्चो मन्वोत्साहोऽतिलुब्धः शशिनि मकरगे दीर्घकण्ठोऽतिकर्णः ॥ ६५।।

65. MOON IN CAPRICORN: If the Moon at birth be in Capricorn, the native will be a singer, will be averse to cold articles (or season), will have stout body, will be fond of truth and charity, be distinguished, famous, less irascible, be libidinous, unkind and shameless; will possess beautiful eyes and emaciated body, will violate teacher's bed, be a poet, will have round thighs, be not very enthusiastic, be very miserly and will have long neck and ears.

Notes: The Moon in Capricorn (or in the other sign of Saturn, ie. Aquarius) will make one susceptible to colds etc., and such people should avoid cold articles etc., and be cautious in winter in the matter of food and other habits. Since the position of the Moon in Capricorn is otherwise a good augury, the native will receive help if Jupiter or Venus is in the 3rd in Pisces or if one of these two aspects the Moon. The Moon remaining in Capricorn will produce different effects, as under for the various ascendants.

Aries ascendant: The Moon in the 10th causes Rajayoga and spiritual achievements. Such natives will head religious missions. If Saturn aspects, one takes to monkhood and is deprived of marital ties. Taurus ascendant: There will be acquisition of plenty of wealth. Gemini ascendant: If Moon's dasa coincides with boyhood (before 12), the native may face early end. If Mercury lends his aspect, then the situation is mended. Cancer ascendant: The native obtains beautiful and amicable wife. He will be fortunate and wealthy. Leo ascendant: The Moon, if weak, brings insult/defeat from enemies while Full Moon gives association with many branches of learning but mastery in none. Virgo ascendant: The native begets one daughter only. If the Moon is weak, the sustenance of the child is doubtful. Libra ascendant: High position and smooth career is bestowed on the native. Weak Moon does not give a long-living mother. Scorplo ascendant: Weak Moon destroys other Rajayogas while Full Moon makes the native give away the patrimony to his youngsters. Sagittarius ascendant: If the Full Moon is in this Lagna the nattve will have a copulent body, while the weak Moon, he is emaciated, sick and shortlived. Aquartus ascendant: The native will be a spend-thrift. Pisces ascendant: The native will obtain happiness through children. He will be fortunate if the Moon is Full.

श्रषनं दुःखितमटनं परकर्मरतं मलोमसं कुर्ते । मकरे कुवलयनाथः विल्पर्माति बीक्षितो रविरणा ॥६६। श्रतिविभवमत्युदारं सुभगं धनसंयुतं मृगे पुरुषम् । बाह्नयुतं प्रचण्ड करोति बक्रेश्चितश्वग्रः ॥६७।। मूर्वं प्रवासञ्चीलं गतयुर्वाति चञव्तं मूगे तोक्तम् । जनयति बुघेन वृष्टः सुखरहितं निर्धनं पुर्षम् ॥ ६८।। भूपतिमनुपमवीयं नृपतिगुणः संयुतं मृगे जातम् । बहुवारपुत्रमित्रं जनयति गुरुवोक्षितव्बन्द्रः ।६e।। पर्युवतिघनविभूषखवाहनमालान्वितं नरं मरे । सोपक्रोदामपुत्रं जनयति भुनुवीक्षितश्चन्द्रः ॥७०।।

## श्रलसं मलिनं सधनं मदनार्त पारदारिकमसत्यम् । दिवसकरपुत्रदुष्टः करोति चन्द्रो नर मकरे ।७१।।

66-71. MOON IN CAPRICORN IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should the Moon in Capricorn be aspected by the Sun at birth, one will be penniless, miserable, wandering-natured, interested in others' work, dirty and clever. If Mars aspects the Moon in Capricorn, one will enjoy abundant riches, be highly liberal, be fortunate, wealthy, will have conveyances and be brave. If Mercury aspects the Moon in Capricorn, one will be dunce, be interested in living in other places, be bereft of wife, be fickleminded and be devoid of happiness and money. Should Jupiter aspect the Moon in Capricorn at birth, the native will be a king, be uncomparably brave, will have royal qualities, and will possess many wives, children and friends. If Venus aspects the Moon in Capricorn, one will join others' wives, be cndowed with wealth, ornaments, conveyances, garlands etc., be blameworthy and be issueless. If Saturn aspects the Moon in Capricorn one will be indolent, dirty, be endowed with money, be troubled by sexual feelings, will join others’ housewives, and be untruthful.

Notes: Normally, the Sun’s aspect on the Moon in Capricorn deprives one of affluence as both these planets are not well disposed towards Saturn, the Moon sign lord. However, if the ascendant be one of Scorpio, Aries, Leo, or Cancer—particularly Cancer, it brings wealth etc. The aspect of Mars on Capricon — Moon is very favourable for riches as Mars is unsullied yogakaraka for this ascendant. Mercury’s aspect is not a good augury for money while it is auspicious for father's success. This aspect gives a sickly wife. However, Jupiter's aspect on the Moon in Capricorn tends to plurality of wives and number of children. If Jupiter falls in Capricorn navamsa in the process the effects are only moderate. The Moon in a Rasi of Saturn and in aspect to Venus gives a tendency to be immoral but favours the native with vehicles, jewels etc. Saturn’s aspect

also promotes immoral character, distress out of sexual feelings and wealth. In addition, this aspect makes the spouse prudent and loving.

उद्धोखो सूक्षदेहः - प्युकरचररो मत्यापानप्रसक्तः सङ्गदष्यो धर्महीनः परसुतजनकः स्थूलमूर्धा कुनेत्रः। शाठचालस्थाभिभूतो विपुलमुखकटिः शिल्पविद्यासमेतो दुःश्षोलो दुःखतप्तो घटभमुपगते रात्रिनाथे दरिद्रः ।७२।।

72. MOON IN AQUARIUS: If thc Moon is in Aquarius at birth, the person will have clevated nose, rough or uneven body, and stout hands and legs; will be addicted to intoxicants, will be aversc to the virtuous, be not himself virtuous, will obtain illegal sons, will have stout head, ugly/discased eyes, bright face and prominent waist, be an artisan, will have bad mentality, be miserable, and will be very poor.

Notes: The Moon in Aquarius is capable of producing the following different effects for the various rising signs:

Aries ascendant: The Moon produces favourable Yoga in the I I th and the native resorts to fair means of earning. Taurus ascendant: The native will resort to ignoble acts if the Moon is weak, Full Moon confers visits to shrines and all kinds of wealth. Gemini ascendant: This is a very fortunate position if the Moon is full and the native will be never in want. If the Moon is with diminishlng rays the native will be ominous for father and will lose his father early. Cancer ascendant: Unless the Moon is Full, one may not enjoy fair longevity but will be sickly. Leo ascendant: If the Moon is strong, the native will beget a chaste and beautiful wife and will enjoy fortunes. If weak, he will marry twice. Virgo ascendant: If the Moon is Full, the native will (at least) beget some loans; if weak even that will be difficult. So to say, his financial condition will be awful. Libra ascendant: The native will acquire happiness through children and will enjoy power, position etc. Scorpio ascendant: If the Moon is Full, the subject will be a great scholar, will enjoy abun-

dant properties, conveyances etc., and will be happy with his mother. If she is weak, these are proportionately meagre. Sagittarius ascendant: If the Moon is full, one will have prospering sisters; if weak he will only lose them. Capricorn ascendant: If the Moon is Full, he will have a beautiful face, be affluent and learned; and will enjoy favours in Moon’s dasa while it may sometimes cause death. Weak Moon does not prove a killer but gives no wealth. Aquarius ascendant: The Moon in the ascendant is not auspicious in this case. If she is Full, the native will suffer from windy complaints and be sickly. His Moon's dasa will not be good in effect. If she is weak, he will have crooked mind and will experience only difficulties. Pisces ascendant; If the Moon is Full, one will be famous, virtuous and will enjoy a superior Raja Yoga. If weak, the dasa will give ordinary result and some misery in respect of children.

ग्रतिमलिनमति व शूरं नृपर्यं धारमिकं कृषिकरं च । कुरुते दिनकरदृष्टो घटबरसंस्थः क्षपानाथः ।७३। कुम्मेऽतिसत्यवाष्यं मातृतुर्धन वियुक्तमनसं च । विषमं परकार्यरतं करोति भौमेक्षितञ्चन्त्रः ।७४।। ह्ञनोपचारकुवलं गीतविधिज्ञं प्रियं च युवतीनाम् । तनुविभवसुखं युरुषं करोति बुपवोक्षितः शरी कुम्मे ।७५। प्रामक्षेत्रतर्रां वरभवनानां बराज्नानां च । कुर्ते भोगिनमार्य साघुं गुरुधीक्षितः वाली कुम्मे ॥७६।। नीचमपुत्रममित्रं कातरमार्चार्येनिन्दितं पापम् । कुरुते जसी कुयुर्वाति सितेक्षितो घटघरेऽल्पसुखम् ॥०७।। नखरो्धरं चलिनं परदाररतं शठं विवर्माराम्। स्थावरभामिनमाद्यं शाशी घटे सौरसंदुष्टः ।७८।। 73-78. MOON IN AQUARIUS IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should the Moon at birth be in Aquarius and be aspected by the Sun, one will be very dirty in disposition, be valorous, will be like a king in guise, be virtuous, and be an

agriculturist. If Mars aspects the Moon in Aquarius, one will be quite truthful, will not inherit moncy from mother and clders, be indolent, and mysterious, and interested in others' work. If Mercury aspects the Moon in Aquarius, the native will be skilful in treating others well while hosting them, will be proficient in music, will be liked by the fair sex, and will possess less money and less happiness. Should Jupiter aspect the Moon in Aquarius, the native will own villages, agriculture lands and trees, be endowed with superior castles and superior ladies, and will be devoted to (sensual) enjoyment (Bhogi also means rich person or a king.) If Venus aspects the Moon in Aquarius, the person will be base, issueless, friendless, timid, be censured by preceptors, be sinful, will have a bad wife and will be least happy. Should the Moon in Aquarius have Saturn's aspect, the native will have (prominent) nails and hair, be dirty, wili seek union with other women, be a dunce, be irreligious and be rich possessing many immovables.

Notes: The luni-solar aspect relating to Aquarius is generally not a good augury for wealth and other matters. The native will only be mistaken to be rich by others but will actually not be so. If Mars aspects the Moon, the Lagna being Capricorn or Aquarius, it may help the native financially and positionally. Mercury’s aspect over the Moon will bring windy diseases, rheumatic breakdown, asthmatic complaints etc., monetary flow will be limited while association with females will be significantly marked. It is extremcly good for the Moon in Aquarius to be aspected by Jupiter so that the native will enjoy high position, own landed properties in abundance and willbe amidst superior women (not for physical pleasures but for beneficial association). Venus aspecting the Moon in Aquarius may give some ordinary wealth but the marital sphere is severely strained. The person will be attached to whores, be not happy with his wife, will lack general happiness, will not have children and will be dismissed by his family members. Capricorn, Libra, Taurus and Virgo are somewhat better in effect if Venus aspects the Moon in Aquarius and the adverse results cited will get slightly improved towards

favourable side. If Saturn aspects the Moon in Aquarius, it will bestow great wealth and properties but will make one go after women and sexual satisfaction will be the only aim of the native. In some cases, even his spouse may not be loyal, depending on her natal Venus/Moon.

शिस्पोत्पन्नाधिकारोऽहितजयनिपुखः शास्त्रविच्चा दवेहो गेयज्ञो धर्मनिव्ठो बहुपुवतिरतः सौम्यवाक् भूपसेवी । ईवत्कोपी महत्कः सुखनिधिधनमाक् स्त्रोजितः सत्स्वभावो वानासक्तः सभु्रे तिमित्रुगलगते शीतगो दानशीलः ।७६।।

79. MOON IN PISCES: If the Moon is in Pisces, the native will be expert in fine arts, be capable of winning even unfavourable people, be learned in Sasthras, beautifulbodied, proficient in music, very religious, will cohabit with many women, be a polite spcaker, will serve the king, be somewhat irascible, will have a big head, be endowed with happiness and wealth, will be won over by the fair sex, be virtuous, be interested in sailing and be liberal.

Notes: The Moon in this Rasi will normally give good effects, but a great deal naturally depends on the rising sign, Jupiter and the associated planets. She gives the following varied effects depending on the ascending sign at birth:

Aries ascendant: The subject will lack maternal happiness, be inimical to his cousins and will spend away all his earnings, if the Moon is declining in the 12th in Pisces. If she is Full, the native will be learned and wealthy while his savings will depend on the strength of Jupiter. Taurus ascendant: With Full Moon, the earnings will be abundant and through fair means. Even weak Moon will give some wealth but through unfair means. Generally, the native will enjoy good health, learning etc. Gemini ascendant: The native will obtain a superior profession and gain through travels. Cancer ascendant: If Moon is full, the native will enjoy abundant ancestral wealth, but will also spend in equal proportions. Weak Moon will make the native occa-

sionally unfortunate and will bestow only moderate gains in journeys. Leo ascendant: Neither the Full Moon nor weak Moon will be in a position to confer position on the native. If this dasa coincides with boyhood, it will cause premature death. Virgo ascendant; If the Moon is waxing, the native will frequently visit shrines, will acquire a bcautiful and rich wife and will beget many daughters. Waning Moon in Pisces will delay the marriage of the native and will give a sickly wife. She will suffer bloody discases. Libra ascendant: The native will contract heavy loans. Depending upon the state of the Moon, he will advance in his career. Scorpio ascendant: The native’s children will be very fortunate. In the dasa of the Moon, the native will prosper highly and will achieve his life’s aims. Sagittarius ascendant: The native will lose his mother early if the Moon is waning. If waxing, he will enjoy maternal happiness and acquire landed property, high education etc., and be happy. Capricorn ascendant; If the Moon is waning, the native’s wife will have defect of some limb. Full Moon gives name, fame, wealth etc., to the native but he will be timid in disposition. Aquarius ascendant: The native will incur many losses in the matter of money and will mostly be in debts. Pisces ascendant: The native will achieve high political/business position if the Moon is strong. If weak, it will give ordinary results.

तीव्रमदनप्रकारां सुखिनं सेनापति घनसमृद्धम् । जनयति दिनकरदृष्टः सुमुदितमार्य श्रो मोने ।८०।। परिम्तं सुखरहितं कुलटापुत्रं च पपानिरतं च। जनयाति नक्षत्रपतिः क्षितिसुतवृष्टो भषे घूरम् ॥८१। जनयति बुधेन दृष्टो मोनस्थ्चन्द्रमाः पुरुवम्। भूपतिमतीव सुखिनं वरयुवतिसमावृतं वद्यम् ॥२्11 गुरुरुष्टो मीनस्यो ललितं चन्दोऽप्रमाण्डलिक्षम् । भत्यादच मुकुवारं बहुभिः, स्त्रोभिवृतं अन्येत् ।३। कुच्ते वासी सुरोलं रतिमन्तं नृत्ववाद्यमेवर्तम् । शुक्रमितो भयस्यो हृदयहरं कामिनोनां च ॥eg1

## विकलमहितं जनन्याः कामार्त पुत्रदारमतिहीनम् । कुर्ते रविसुतदृष्टो नोचविरूपाङ्गनासक्तम् ॥८५।।

80-85. MOON IN PISCES IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon posited in Pisces is aspected by the Sun, the native will be highly libidinous, be happy, be an army chief, be very affluent and will have delighted wife. If Mars aspects the Moon in Pisces, the person will be insulted, be devoid of happiness, is an unchaste woman's son, will be interested in sins, and will be valorous. He will be a king if the Moon in Pisces is aspected by Mercury; will be highly intelligent, be happy and will be surrounded by supreme females and be controlled by them. If Jupiter aspects tic Moon in Pisces, he will be beautiful, fierce, head of a district, be very affluent, beautiful and will be surrounded by many women. If Venus aspects the Moon in Pisces, he will be skilful in coition, interested in dance, instrumental music and songs and will steal the hearts of the fair sex. Should Saturn aspect the Moon in Pisces, he will be deformed, be unfavourable to mother, be sexually distressed, be devoid of sons, wife and intelligence and will be attached to mean and ugly females.

Notes: If the Moon in Pisces is aspected by Jupiter/ Mercury the person will enjoy high position in life. If by Venus from Virgo, his married life will not be happy; he will not earn much wealth but be unhappy in all respects. Saturn aspecting the Moon in Pisces will make one go without marriage and go after prostitutes. If by Mars, his. mother will be of questionable character. The Sun aspecting the Moon in Pisces denotes a person with fat body.

## रातिप्तो बलमुक्ते राती च बलान्विते तथा चन्द्रे ।

## राशिफलं स्यात् सकलं नीचाच्वाविधिना च संचिन्त्यम् ॥ट१।।

86. SAID RESULTS TO MATURE: If the lord of the Moon sign, the Moon sign itself and the Moon are all endowed with strength, the said effects (due to Moon's position as

laid down in this chapter) will fully come to pass. The effects will vary according to positions like debilitation, exaltation etc. (of the dispositor and the aspecting planet).

Notes: For example, assume the Moon is in Aries at birth. If the Moon should give full results due to her position in that Rasi, Mars should be equally strong. There should be good aspects to Aries (and the Moon). If the dispositor, Mars in this case, is in fall, inimically placed etc., the effects will be adverse. Similar estimates should be made through the aspecting planet at well. -

इति कल्यागावर्मेविरचितायां सारावल्यां चन्द्रवारो नाम त्रयोविशोऽध्यायः ।।२३।

Thus ends the 23rd chapter in Saravall, entitled "Effects arising out of the Moon".

## Chapter 24

## झथ चन्द्रनर्वांश्चाराप्यायः ।२४॥

## Effects of Moon in Various Navamsas

भौमांधि कुजदृष्टो निकर्तनञ्चन्द्रमाः प्रचण्डञ्व । जनयति मायाबहुलं प्रवञ्चकं सूर्यजेन किल पुर्वम् ॥१।। सूर्वेता चीरपातकमयवाप्यारक्षक शूरम् । जोवेन मनुजनाथं स्यातं विद्वत्समाराध्यम् ।२॥ शुक्रेख नृपतिक्सचिवं धनान्वितं स्त्रोविलेपनानुरतम् । झलोप्रं वदन्ति चपलं सौम्येन निरोक्षिते चन्द्रे ।३।

1-3. MOON IN NAVAMSA OF MARS ASPECTED BY OTHERS: If the Moon is in the navamsa of Mars aspected by Mars himself, one will win over his enemies and will be valorous; if by Saturn very cunning, deceiving and trifting; if by the Sun thief, sometimes cruel and sometimes protecting and be courageous; if by Jupiter, he will be a king, be famous and honoured by the learned; if by Venus, he will be a king’s minister, be wealthy and be fond of women and cosmetics and lastly if by Mercury, he will be a fast speaker and be fickle-minded.

Notes: A clarification about aspect with reference to Navamsa is necessary herc. The aspect of a planet should not be noted in the Navamsa chart but by longitudinal distance, i.e. in the Rasi chart. For example, take the Moon in the navamsa of Mars to be aspected by Mercury. To fulfil this, the Moon should be in the Navamsa of Aries/Scorpio and be aspected by Mercury in the Rasi chart. Suppose the Moon is in Pisces while Mercury is in Virgo. The sald Moon should simultaneously be in Scorplo navamsa, i.e. between 16° 40'—20° in Pisces and get the aspect of the planet

Mercury from Virgo. In other words, Mercury need be in a certain position in Navamsa vis-a-vis Moon is specified Navamsa. Unless this aspect is properly understood, one cannot effectively and meaningfully apply the relative rules. Similar inference applies to decanate and other such subdivisions.

सितभागे सितदृष्टे योविद्वस्त्रान्नपानधनसौख्यम् । जनयति बुधेन चन्द्रो वार्यज्ञं नृत्तगेयपरम् ।४।। गुर्खा कविप्रधानं नयशास्त्रविशारदं नृपतिसचिवम् । परवारदर्शनपरं कामिनमारेख बहुमृत्यम् ॥५ सुयरण महामूर्ख प्रियंवदं सततमन्नपानरुचिम् । सौरेखा वर्धकीनां गुर्ण्ष्च सदृ्षं दिशाति चन्द्रः ।६।

4-6. MOON IN NAVAMSA OF VENUS IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in Taurus/Libra navamsa and is aspected by Venus, the person will be happy in regard to wife, robes, food and drinks; if by Mercury he will have knowledge of playing musical instruments and be interested in dance and music; if by Jupiter, he will be a great poet, be highly skilful in the branch of justice (or politics), be a king’'s minister, will enjoy sight of others’ housewives, be libidinous, and will have many a servant; if by the Sun, he will be a great stupid, will possess a beautiful face and will always enjoy food and drinks and lastly if by Saturn, the person will resemble a carpenter in disposition (i.e. he will be a carpenter by profession).

बुघभागे बुधदृष्टः शिस्याचार्य कवि शत्ती जनयेत् । शुक्रेक्षितो विशालं गेयज्ञं वचनसाराद्यम् ।।। नृपमन्त्रिणं गुखाड्यं गुरुता दृष्टः प्रतिष्ठितं कान्तम् । भौमेक्षितोऽतिचोर विवादकुदालं नरं रौद्रम् ।।। शास्त्रार्यकाव्यबुद्ध प्राज्ञं विस्पनमवेक्षितः शानिना । रङ्गन्वरं विस्यातं जनयति सूर्येक्षितश्चन्द्रः ॥EI

T-9. MOON IN MERCURY’S NAVAMSA AND IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in the navamsa of Mercury, (1.e. Gemini/Virgo Navamsa) and is aspected by Mercury, the native will be skilful in mechanical or fine arts (of 64 kinds), and be a poet; if by Venus he will have a broad physique, be an expert musician and will have rich knowledge of sacred passages (& C Sastra vachnam, Sruti vachanam and Samriti Vachanam); if by Jupiter, he will be a king's minister, be a repository of good qualities, be famous and splendorous; if by Mars, he will be a notorious thief, be skilful in arguments and be fierce; if by Saturn, he will be skilful in sastras and poetry, be learned in arts and be intelligent and lastly if by the Sun, he will be successful in war and be famous.

स्वांधे दिनकरदृष्टः वशी ह्यातनुमविक्षतवारीरम् । परधनहरणे निपुणं लुब्धं नितरां कुजेनापि ।१०। सौरेखाकृत्यकर बघबन्धविवादसन्तप्तम् । शुक्रेख स्त्रीदेव्यं जनयेदयवा नपुंसकाकारम् ॥११। नृपमन्त्रिणं नृयं वा जनयति गुरुखावलोकितश्चन्द्र । सौम्येनाधर्मरतं निद्राबहुलं च सततवध्वरतम् ।।१२।

10-12. MOON IN CANCER NAVAMSA AND IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in her Navamsa at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the subject will have an emaciated body but be free from diseases; if by Mars, he will be skilful in stealing others' money and be a miser; if by Saturn, he will indulge in prohibited acts and will incur grief due to imprisonment, litigations and destruction; if by Venus, he will be inimical to the fair sex and will resemble a eunuch and if by Jupiter, he will be a king's minister or be a king himself and lastly if by Mercury, he will be not virtuous, will sleep much and will wander at all times.

रविभागे रविवृष्टे सुरोवखः समुपलब्धकोतिधनः । पापो निर्वय इन्वौ सौरेख प्राखिनां हुन्ता ११३।

भौमेन सुवर्णचनं स्यातं नृपसत्क्कतं प्रवण्डतरम् । गुरुखा दृष्टो जनयति चमूर्ति वा नरेन्द्रं वा ।१४।। शुक्लेख दृष्टमुतिः सृताथिनं मृतस्तं वाऽपि । सौम्येन दवचिन्तकमितिहासरतं च निधिभाजम् ॥१५।।

13-15. MOON IN LEO NAVAMSA IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: If the Moon is in Leo Navamsa at birth and is aspected by the Sun himself, the native will be short-tempered, famous and wealthy; if by Saturn, he will be sinful, unkind and will kill living beings; if by Mars, he will possess gold, wealth and fame, will be honoured by the king and be quite valorous; if by Jupiter he will be either an army chief or a king, if by Venus, he will (only) desire to have children and will have (only) dead children (i.e. will have no living children), and lastly if by Mercury, he will be an astrologer, be fond of heroic history (such as Mahabharata), and will be very affluent.

गुरुभागे गुरुदृष्टो विज्ञदं नृपबल्लभं विपुलकीतिम् । जनयति क्षशी सितेन स्त्रीखां भोर्गः सुसंयुक्तम् ।१६। बुधवृष्टो हास्यकरं नुपप्रियं नायकं वरूथिन्याः । श्र्त्राचार्य कुरुते कुजेक्षितः सर्वतः स्यातम् ।१०। दोर्ष विविधः स्यातं दिनकरहृष्टो ननं प्रमावस्थम् । सौरेख वृद्धसीलं बलिभिश्व निराङ्तं नीचम् ।।१८।।

16-18. MOON IN JUPITER'S NAVAMSA IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in the navamsa of Sagittarius/ Pisces and aspected by Jupiter, the native will be beautiful (or spotless), be liked by the king and be widely famous; if by Venus, he will be endowed with conjugal happiness; if by Mercury, he will be jocular, be dear to the king and be an army chief; if by Mars he will be skilful in use of weapons and be very famous; If by the Sun he will have many blemishes, be famous and be subjected to accidents/calamities, and lastly if by Saturn he will be akin to old men,

be neglected by mighty men and be base.
