सौरांच्चे शनिहब्टः कृपणं रोगान्वितं मृतसुतं वा । सूर्येखाल्पापत्यं व्याधिग्रस्तं विरूपतनुम् ॥।१६। भौमेन नरपतिसमं स्वाढ्यं स्त्रीदुर्भगं सुर्खर्युक्तम् । शुक्रस् विषमीलं युवतिभिरवधीरितं घीरम् ॥२०। सौम्येन पाननिरतं कुत्सितचरितं शशी सवा दृष्टः । गुरुखा स्वकर्मनिरतं कुर्ते पुरुषं तथोदात्तम् ॥२१।।

19-21. MOON IN SATURN’S NAVAMSA IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If the Moon is in Capricorn/Aquarius Navamsa and is aspected by Saturn, the native will be miserly, sickly and will have dead children; if by the Sun, he will have a few children, be miserable with diseases and be ugly; if by Mars, he will be equal to a king, be very rich, happy but unfortunate in regard to wife; if by Venus, he will be of crooked disposition, be attached to women and be valorous; if by Mercury, he will be interested in liquors, and will have bad history; and lastly if by Jupiter, he will be devoted to his own work and be great.

वर्गोत्तमे स्वकीये परकीयनवांशाके च हृष्टिफलम् । पुष्ट मध्यं स्वल्पं विपरीतं स्थादनिष्टफलम् ॥२२। रासिफलं यद्र हृष्टं पूर्वेः कयितं ग्रहैः शज्ञाडूस्य । तस्य निरोषो हृष्टो यच्चंशपतिर्बली भवति ॥२३।। शंशपतेश्चन्द्रस्य व फलं विनिदिचत्य दर्शनक्कतानि । कथितानि यवनवृद्धेः फलानि सम्यग्व्यवस्यन्ति ।२४॥।

22-24. SCOPE OF EFFECTS: If the Moon is in Vargothama Navamsa, own navamsa or other's navamsa, the effects will be full, medium and minor respectively. This does not apply to evil effects, so to say these will be reverse. If the Navamsa lord is stronger than the Rasi lord, then only the effects due to navamsa lord will come to pass, 1.e., the Navamsa effects due to the Moon will be felt as against that

of her Rasi position. So say Vriddha Yavanas.

## इति कल्याख्वर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां प्रंशकदर्शने चन्द्रचारो नाम चतुविक्षोऽध्यायः । २४।।

Thus ends the 24th chapter in Kalyana Varma’s Saravall entitled 'Effects arising out of Moon’s position in various Navamsas’.

## Chapter 25

## स्थ मौमचाराप्यायः ॥२९)।

## Effects of Mars in Various Rasis

तेजस्वी सत्ययुतः सूरः क्षितिपोऽ्थवा रख्दलाधी । साहसकर्माभिरतद्चमूपुरग्रामवृन्दपतिः राभसिको दानरतः प्रभूतगोऽ्जाविधान्यकरः। भौमें क्रिये प्रचण्डो बहुयुवतिरतो भवेत्पुरुषः ।२।

1-2. MARS IN ARIES: If Mars at birth occupies Aries, the native will be splendorous, truthful, valorous, be a king, be fond of war, interested in adventurous acts, be an army chief, or head of a village or a band; be delighted, charitable be endowed with a number of cows, goats etc., and grains, be fierce and will join many women.

Notes: Mars remaining in Aries will bestow varied results as under for the different ascendants at birth:

Arles ascendant: Mars in Arles as ascendant gives physical distress. The dasa of Mars will give ordinary results as he is simultaneously the lord of the 8th. In the second half of Mars dasa, more adverse results will be felt, equal to death. Taurus ascendant: The person will contract heavy debts, will spend more on evil fronts and will lose his landed property etc. Gemini ascendant: Mars in Aries will produce a splendid Raja Yoga conferring landed/housing properties and co-operation and help from brothers etc. An adverse Mars can cause loss of issues or may give disobedient children. Cancer ascendant: The native will amass wealth and lands and will acquire prominent position in flelds like Police, Defence etc. This is a good position to run sales of animals, dairy products etc. beo ascendant: Mars in Aries will cause distress in regard to father. If father and son both simultaneously run Mars dasa, death of father will take place. The native will however be wealthy with occa-

sional misfortunes. Virgo ascendant: The subject will undergo various difficulties in life and will not attain success in financial matters. Depending on Venus, his marital life will be prosperous. Libra ascendant: Though the person will earn riches during Mars dasa, he will not be free from marital inharmony. Sometimes, marital life may be short. Scorpio ascendant: The native will, according to one authority, have more enemies, more debts and more diseases. (According to what I have noticed, Mars in 6th in Aries will cause physical defect, makc one a debaucher and successful over enemies. He may be subjected to diseases, like piles). Sagittarius ascendant: Though the native will attain great prosperity, lands ctc., he will not be free from worries on account of children. His retaining a living child is doubtful and dependant on Jupiter. Capricorn ascendant: The native may be separated from his mother but will have longliving mother. He will earn abundant money and gains. His education may be disturbed. Aquarius ascendant: He will grab some property of his brothers or may lose co-born. In courage, he will be like a lion. Pisces ascendant: Mars dasa can give beneficial results while peace in family is scarce.

साब्वीव्रतभञ्चकरः प्रभक्षरो मन्द्धनपुत्रः । देष्यो बहुभररापरो विस्रम्भस्थितिबिहीनञ्च ।३। प्रोडतवेषक्रोडो बहुवुष्ट्वचाः कुजे वृषभसंस्थे । सञ्चततरतः पापो बन्धुविरुद्धः कुलात्साबी ।४।।

3-4. MARS IN TAURUS: If Mars is in Taurus in a nativity, the person will break the vows of chaste women, will eat voraciously, will have little wealth and few sons, be jealous, will maintain many people, will not trust others, will play violently, will speak very harshly, be fond of music, be sinful, be inimical to relatives and will bring infame to his family.

Notes: Depending on the ascendant at birth, as under, Mars in Taurus will mainfest following different effects:

Aries ascendant: Loss of children and blemishes in respect of wife will result. Taurus ascendant: The native will suffer from smallpox and will never be happy. Two marriages are indicated. Gemini ascendant: The native will be troubled on account of debts. He will contract many secret diseases and will have fear of enemies. Loss of wife and loss of limbs may also come into effect. If a malefic is associated with Mars in Taurus a Gemini native may even be imprisoned. Cancer ascendant: The native may have abundant earnings but all by unfair means. Leo ascendant: He will enjoy position, happiness and pleasures. Virgo ascendant: His good fortunes will be ordinary and he will suffer from paternal grief. Libra ascendant: The native will not beget children. He may lose his wife quite early. His own longevity is doubtful and death will be violent in nature. Scorpio ascendant: Marital life will be happy though the wife may suffer from frequent sickness. If Venus is strong, the wife may not be subjected to illhealth. Occasional financial losses will be experienced. Sagittarius ascendant: There will not be amicability with children and the native will not be happy in this field. There will be burden of loans, litigations and expenditure. His fortunes are quite obstructed. Capricorn ascendant: The native will become rich and will expericncc allround gains while diseases of the stomach will prevail. Frequent abortions will be the order of life for a female with this position. Aquarius ascendant: If Mars is in Taurus at birth, the native will be inimically disposed to his mother. Immovable properties will be abundant while learning will not be so. Pisces ascendant: He will only acquire an ordinary livelihood in life.

कान्तः क्लेवसहिष्युर्वहुश्रुतः काव्यविधिनिपुखः । नानाशिल्पकलासु च निपुखो बहुशो विवेशागमवरतः ॥५II धर्मपरो निपुरामनिहितानुकूलः सुतेषु सुहृदां च । मियुनस्थे क्षितिपुत्रे भवति प्रचुरक्रियासु रतः ॥६।।

5-6. MARS IN GEMINI: Should Mars be in Gemini, the native will be splendorous, be capable of enduring miseries,

be very learned, be wellversed with poetical rules, skilful in various kinds of fine arts, fond of going to foreign countries, virtuously disposed, highly intelligent, favourably disposed to his sons and friends and will devote to various kinds of assignments.

Notes: Mars in Gemini will produce the following effects for the different ascendants:

Aries ascendant: Mars dasa will in the beginning give good results while in the later half, goods will be marred. Taurus ascendant: The native will lose co-born, will have sickly wife and will incur loss of lands. Gemini ascendant: He will be of malignant disposition, will not be kind to anybody and will always be distressed due to physical ailments. Cancer ascendant: The native will get into bad people's association and will indulge in reckless loans. In Mars dasa he may lose his eyes. If Saturn is in the 12th along with Mars, imprisonment, wanderings etc., will result. Leo ascendant: He never will attain high success in independent profession, business and the like. Virgo ascendant: There will be frequent disturbances or changes in one’s career. The good aspects depend on Mercury. Such a native will however be a very important person in his family. ,Libra ascendant: In a female’s chart, this position will make her go out of marital bonds and be not loyal to her husband. In general: The native will always suffer from diseases, will not be fortunate and will be short lived. Sagittarisu ascendant: The native will obtain an extravagant and disobedient wife and will lack happiness both in respect of marriage and progeny. Capricorn ascendant: Rhuematic disorders, litigations on account of properties and final success will result. Aquarius ascendant: The native will beget children but will lose some of them. He will however be foolish. Pisces ascendant: Inheritance and gains through enemies or cousins will manifest while learning, maternal happiness and properties will only be ordinary. If Mars dasa occurs in life, these results will improve greatly.

परगृह्निवासशीलो वैकल्य रुगदितः कृषिधनञ्च । बाल्ये च राजभोजनवस्त्रेप्सुः परगृहान्नाशी IsII सलिलाशायता घनवान् पुनः पुन्द्वेनातंद्च । कर्कटके लिमितनये भवति मृदुः सर्वतो दीनः ॥IEI।

7-8. MARS IN CANCER: If Mars is in Cancer at birth, the subject will like living in others' houses, be deformed, sick, will attain riches through agriculture, will enjoy royal food and robes during childhood, will eat food in others’ houses, will becomc wealthy through the source of water, be repeatedly anguished and be always distressed.

Notes: Mars in Cancer is generally found not congenial for maternal happiness. The native will be brought up by other relatives. Though the text says that the childhood of the native will be happy, in actual experience it is found to be reverse. So to say, Mars in Cancer does not give good results in early life but in later part. Depending on the natal ascendant, this planet in Cancer will give following effects.

Aries ascendant: There will be little happincss with Mars in the 4th in Cancer. The native’s mother may be short lived; otherwise separation or misunderstanding will follow. Taurus ascendant: Sickness of wife, loss of co-born and courage and litigations of property will come to pass. The native may have afflicted thyroid glands. Gemini ascendant: Penury, loss of wife, loss of position and fame, contracting facial diseases etc., will occur. Cancer ascendant: The native will be grotesque and ugly in appearance, will suffer planetary blemishes in boyhood and will lack physical comforts. Brain tumours, lack of intelligence, company with mean people etc., will also be experienced. Leo ascendant: The native will not possess any wealth. However, if Saturn joins Mars in Cancer, a Leo native will obtain a Rajayoga par excellence. Virgo ascendant: the native will have a very small family. He will lose elder brother coupled with money. Libra native: Mars in the 10th in Cancer will adversely affect his career. He will not have any permanent

and happy job to do. Patrimony will be misused. Scorpio asccndant: Paternal bliss, good fortune, understanding with co-born will all lack while diseases of the anus (like piles) will exist. Sagittarius native: The native will be absolutely shortlived; otherwise, he will lack progeny, will spend secretly on evil affairs and will be a profligate. Capricorn ascendant: The native’s wife will be weak in constitution and will have dtseases, particularly vaginal. He will not have any good rclationship with others and will frequently contract losses in his undertakings. Aquarius ascendant: The native will be timid in disposition. He will have a checkered career. He will be irreligious in all his activities. Pisces ascendant: Though the native will experience great Rajayoga effects, he will be worried in respect of children. His mental disposition will be akin to that of a very miserable person. If a female has Mars in Cancer in the 5th, abortions, discharge of the vagina and such other disease will come to pass.

The effect due to Mars as above will be greatly modified due to the Moon’s strength simultaneously. Hence a literary application is unwarranted.

प्रसहः प्रचण्डशूरः परस्वसन्तानसङ्ग्रह्तज्ञीलः । प्रदवीनिवासगोकुलमांसंरचिः स्यान्मृतप्रथमदारः 121 व्यालमृगोरगहन्ता न पुत्रवान् , धर्मकलहीनः। भौमे हुरौं सुस्त्वः करियोद्यतः स्याइपुध्यांश्च 11201

9-10. MARS IN LEO: If Mars is in Leo in a nativity, the nalive will be impatient, be valorous, intent upon grabbing others’ money and children, will like to live in forests, be fond of cating cow’s flesh (or beef etc.), will lose his first wife, will kill snakcs and animals, will be bereft of children, be devold of charitable acts and be always active in his jobs.

Notes: Mars in Leo is quite favourable for money. 3 However, progeny will be affected adversely. The native will advance in marital sphere provided Venus/7th lord is sufficiently strong. Mars remaining in Leo will give the fol-

lowing effects depending on the rising sign: Aries ascendant: Progeny is doubtful for the native. If at all he obtains one, he will be happy with his child. Taurus ascendant: The person with Mars in Leo will not achieve any branch of learning satisfactorily. Though he may acquire immovable properties, due to other Yogas in the horoscope, he will have some losses. Gemini ascendant: The effects of Yogas in Mars dasa will only be ordinary. The native will not be well disposed towards his father while his father may be subjected to many diseases. Cancer ascendant: Plentifulness of money, respect, excellent career will be experienced. The adverse side of the nativity will be: unhappiness in family, lack of marital/progenic bliss. Leo ascendant: The native will enjoy little physical comforts while splendid effects in the matter of prosperity will be obtained in Mars dasa. Virgo ascendant: The native will lose some limb in Mars dasa. If a malefic be with Mars in Leo, difficulties like imprisonment, heavy debts etc., will follow. He will incur the wrath of the government, become infamous and will meet a cruel end. Libra ascendant: He will gain through marriage. The spouse will be of disagrecable disposition. Scorpio ascendant: He will be of medium stature, will sometimes lose and sometimes gain, will contact bloody/ cardiac diseases and will not make much progress in education. However, his Mars dasa will confer abundant prosperity, Sagittarius ascendant: Lack of paternal bliss, loss of or misunderstandings with co-born, deafness etc., will follow. Capricorn ascendant: The native will lose his wife or will not be happy in marital life, will be shortlived and will incur huge loans. Aquarius ascendant: Though the wife wlll be rich, marital felicity will be a scarcity. Pisces ascendant: Many Rajayogas will become defunct in the dasa of Mars. If Rahu is in the 6th along with Mars, vital organs will be spollt.

पूज्यः सत्तामतिघनो रतिगीतबनो मृदुप्रियामाबी ।

बिविधव्ययोऽल्प्शौर्यो विद्वान् भवति प्ररीतपादर्वश्च ।११।

## श्रहिते म्योऽधिकभीर्वेदस्मृतिधर्मवान् सुबहुशिल्पः ।

## कन्यायां मूतनये स्नानविलेपनरतः कान्तः ॥१२।।

11-12. MARS IN VIRGO: Should Mars occupy Virgo at birth, the subject will be worthy of honour, be never rich, be very fond of sexual union and music, be soft and sweet spoken, will have various kinds of expenses, be not much valorous, be learned, will have ribs in their advanced position, will fear enemies very much, be skilful in sastras and fine arts, be fond of bathing, make-up etc., and be splendorous.

Notes: Mars in Virgo will produce the following effects for the various ascendents:

Aries ascendant: Mars in Virgo will make the person sickly, shortlived, poor and llable. Taurus ascendant: a female with Mars in Virgo will suffer from secret diseases, abortions, surgical deliveries etc. If Mercury is there along with Mars, she may beget twins. One with Mars in 5th will lose children, will lack amicability with wife and children and will always invite quarrels with others. On the whole, this position of Mars will promote diseases of the abdomen. Gemini ascendant: Ancestral properties will be at stake; the native will not attain high education and will face risk from vehicles. Cancer ascendant: The nattve will have high achlevements in commercial activities. Progeny may lack if Mars has no apprecible strength. Leo ascendants: There may be two marrlages for the native. He will inherit huge paternal property, but will lose the same soon if Mercury is simultaneously in Pisces. Virgo ascendant: He will face all kinds of evils and be sickly. Libra ascendant: Loss of wife, venereal diseases, loss of money etc., will come to pass. Scorpio ascendant: The native will be the eldest issue among the survivors. He will take to evil means to earn money and will earn plentifully. Sagittarius ascendant: The native will author books (particularly scriptures and will be an exponent) and will become very famous in Mars dasa. He will obtain only one but good son. Capricorn ascendant:

Paternal bliss will lack. Landed property, education etc., will be in abundance. Fortunes may be obstructed if Saturn or Venus is not helpful to Mars. Aquarius ascendant: The native will always be miserable. He will enjoy lapsed longevity. Pisccs asccndant: In Mars dasa, there may be difficulties in married life. If Venus is not favourablc then separation, divorce or death of spouse will result.

्रध्वनिरतः कुपष्यप्रसक्तवाक्यो विकत्थनः सुभगः। हीनाङ्गः स्वल्पजनः सङ्ग्रामेप्सुः परोपभोगी च ।१३। योषिङ्रगुरु मित्रार्तां मनोरमो नष्टपुर्वदारञ्च । शौण्डिकवेद्यानिकटे सम्प्राप्तधनक्षयस्तुलिनि भौमे ॥१४।

13-14. MARS IN LBRA: If Mars occupies Libra in a nativity, the person will be liable to wandering, will indulge in bad business, be an able speaker, be fortunate, deformed in respect of some limb, will have few relatives, be fond of wars, will lose his first wife, will deal in liquors and will earn through prostitutes to only lose.

Notes: Mars in Libra will give the undermentioned results for the various rising signs at birth:

Aries ascendant: Though Mars is the lord of the ascendant, if he be in Libra, he will cause imbalances in married life. The native may lose his wife early. This position also tells upon other yogas in general. Taurus ascendant: Hcre Mars acts on the marital front in still worse a manner, apart, the native will suffer from everlasting discases, inimical activities etc. He will spend on immoral purposes. Gemini ascendant: Progeny will be delayed while a female in particular will suffer from menstrual disorders. She will not be chaste in disposition. Cancer ascendant: Mars in Libra confers Raja Yoga and position on the native. He will have many female issues. Leo ascendant: Mars does not cause any good yoga in his dasa. There may be loss of coborn and litigation with them. Virgo ascendant: The native will be poor and will suffer many adversities in life. His marrlage will be not on a happy note. In Mars dasa, the wife

will expire, or be separated. Libra ascendant: The native will lack physical comforts and will suffer urinary disorders. He will not have good terms with his mother and will have obstructed learning. Scorpio ascendant: The native will spend away all the carnings and be indigent. His expenditure will only be on evil sphere. He will lack understanding with his co-born. He will anyhow be free from debts. Sagittarius ascendant: He will gain through females and will have only one male issue. He will become rich through independent business. He will maintain secret relationship with women other than his wife. Capricorn ascendant: For this ascendant, Mars in Libra will confer fortunes, high position, wealth, weapons etc. The native will be sometimes soft and sometimes illtempered. Loss of progeny will. be experienced. Aquarius ascendant: The native's father will be a profligate. The native will attempt at spiritual success but will only fail. Pisces ascendant: Fall from position, loss of honour, litigation etc.—all in Mars dasa—will be experienced. Though the native will not make much advancement in money matters, he will be capable of producing money on loans.

व्यापारश्रतिसक्ताचोरसमूहाधिपः क्रियानिपुखः । युद्धोत्सुकोऽतिनापो बद्धपराधी च वरवाठः ॥१५। द्रोह्वपाहितबुद्धिः प्रसूचको भूमिपुत्रयुवतीशः । वृञ्चिकगे भूपुत्रे विषाम्निवास्त्रवर्णस्तप्तः ।१९॥

15-16. MARS IN SCORPIO: If Mars occupies Scorpio at birth, the native will be attached to trade, will be interested in Vedic knowledge, be leader of thieves, skilful in his duties, be interested in wars, be highly sinful, will do big crimes, will be perfidious towards his encmies, will betray, be disposed towards killing, be unhelpful, be a talebearer, will be endowed with lands, sons and wifc and be troubled by poison, fire, weapons and wounds.

Notes: When Mars occupies Scorpio, the person becomes adept in trading or in pursuing independent professlon. He may also acquire deep knowledge in archaic

branches of knowledge like Vedas. If the planct is adverse, one may take to thieving which is particularly true in the case of an Aries nativity, for Scorpio is the 8th from the ascending sign. 'Interest in wars' as given in the text may turn out to be interest in quarrel, fighting etc. Snake bite, electric shock, fire accident and such other calamities may occur in his life. If Mars is in this position in 22nd decanate, it may even cause death through any of the above cited means. The native will enjoy landed property, wife and sons. If Scorpio happens to be the 7th house, i.e. Taurus ascending at birth, Mars in Scorpio does not hurt the sphere of marriage, vide Bhavartha Ratnakara. On the other hand, he proves auspicious. Natives with Mars in Scorpio may well become police officers, fire brigadiers, chemists, miners, weapon dealers and so on and so forth, which however depends on his relationship with the Lagna and its Lord. If he is adverse, these spheres be better avoided. Now see how Mars in Scorpio proves to different nativities:

Aries ascendant: Though the longevity may be fairly long, the native occasionally faces ups and downs. Financlal emergencies, losses etc., are not ruled out. Married life will be blowing between hot and cold. If Venus is not favourable even loss of spouse may take place. Taurus ascendant: A Telugu canticle however says that Mars in the 7th is not favourable. But in actual practice, more weight may be given to the view of Sri Ramanuja's Bhavartha Ratnakara. The native’s spouse may bring fortunes also. Gemini ascendant: Elder brothers/sisters may not be welldisposed towards the native. The native will have expenditure on vices but may prove lucky to father. Cancer ascendant: Though Mars is capable of bestowing best results in his dasa in respect of position, lands, housing property, real estate business etc. etc., the person will suffer on account of children, may incur stomachial disorders, digestive problems etc. He will be fiery in disposition. If Mars occupies good navamsa position, the native may become a famous author on subjects like chemistry, surgery etc. Leo ascendant: This is a very fortunate position of Mars for a

Leo native to have abundant landed properties and the like. If Mars occupies debilitation/adverse Navamsas, the person is susceptible to enmity with mother, may have impeded learning and the like. If Ketu joins Mars in this sign, the native enjoys very best results in the matter of profession. Virgo ascendant: The native will become wealthy and will possess landed properties. He may have one younger brother/sister only. Libra ascendant: If Mars in Scorpio is aspected by Jupiter, both of them being adverse for this ascendant, the native's longevity will stand reduced considerably. Saturn's strength or that of Venus may prove a powerful antidote in that case. Mars in Scorplo while Libra ascending denotes two wives or loss of wife in the latter part. Scorpio ascendant: The planet Mars gives a wounded body. Scar/wound on the head may particularly be noted. This position gives two wives and addiction to other women. Though the native may enjoy fairly good health on account of Mars occasional breakdown will be experienced. Diseases likely to occur are: venereal, poisonous, bloody etc. Sagittarius ascendant: The subject will be unhappy with children, be a miser, will not enjoy happiness in marriage, and will not be loyal to marital ties. Capricorn ascendant: Mars in 11th in Scorpio will give abundant education, landed/housing properties, plenty of wealth, wide fame, freedom from enmity, and famous but shortlived children. Aquarius ascendant: The native will obtain very enviable position in life, be very affluent and learned. He will achicve fame as an exponent of high latitude. Pisces ascendant: Paternal bliss may be a rarity in life but the native will make enough money and be generally happy.

बहुभिः क्षतः क्वाङ्गो निष्ठुरवाक्यः शठः पराधीनः । रथगजपदातियोधी रचेन वारधारकोऽथ पर्सन्ये ॥।१७१। विपुलत्रमद्च सुखितः बरस्परं क्रोधनध्टसुखवित्तः । कार्मुकसंस्थे वक्रे गुरुव्वसक्तः पुमान् भवति ॥१८।। 17-18. MARS IN SAGITTARIUS: Should Mars be in Sagittarius, the native will have many wounds, be emaci-

ated, be harsh in speech, crafty, allenated, be a warrior endowed with chariots, elephants and armymen, will pass arrows on other's army from his chariot, be happy with hard work, will lose his happincss and money due to anger and will not honour elders.

Notes: Kalyana Varma hints at the bravery and skill of a native with Mars in Sagittarius in war-field. We may take it to be a favourable position for military men etc., instead of literally interpreting as given in our translation. The native will be highly industrious, cunning, harsh in treatment, will have an afflicted body if not literally a wounded body and his own anger will cause his fall. He will not have good relationship with his elders (like parents) and be unpahhy. This planet in Sagittarius will produce the following general effects for the 12 different ascending signs:

Aries ascendant: The native will be very famous and will enjoy position. He may not be however free from misfortunes in respect of mother and co-born. If the Moon and Mercury are strong enough to counteract the evils of Mars just mentioned respectively, some relief will be sure to follow. Taurus ascendant: As lord of the 12th, Mars in the 8th will contribute to the native's longevity. This cannot be a sole factor, however, to reckon with, while the strengths of Saturn/Jupiter are more important. The native will beget a stout wife who will have an upper hand over him. His life will be miserable. Gemini ascendant: Mars in the 7th will tear off the married llfe into pisces. Such a native better not marry before 30 and better not with Mars dasa to come in the way. He himself may be shortlived. His brothers will evolve dirty plots against him. Cancer ascendant: The subject may achicve high position coupled with serious fall some time or the other. Progeny will be many if Mars is favourable in aspect to Jupiter or the Moon. Should Mars be otherwise, weak etc., death of children, mental diseases, financial losses and the like will manifest. Leo ascendant: The native will have brave and honourable sons (female issue is rare with this position) and will be a worthy exponent who will leave an imperishable mark behind him. He

will be posthumously famous. While alive, he may not enjoy much of due fame. Should the Sun aspect Mars, the native will be a highly placed politician or army official and will enjoy great and wide respect in life. Virgo ascendant: The native will enjoy sexual pleasures in unusual manner and in deserted/lonely places. His co-born will be more attached to mother than he could be. The mother may be shortlived or may face marital discord. Libra ascendant: Mars promotes the life span of the native and he will be very long lived. He will receive great help through in-laws. Scorpio ascendant: Goddess Lakshmi will smile on him for ever. The native may face affliction of sight, teeth etc. He will have big and protruding teeth while looking younger than what he is. Sagittarlus ascendant: Weak body with wounds, frequent accidents/falls, spiritual attempt with fallure, expenses on litigations, marital estrangement and the like will be the order of one’s life (with Mars in Sagittarius ascendant). Capricorn ascendant: This position of Mars will make many other yogas defunct and the native will ever suffer from miseries. He will be a synonym of misfortune if not helped by Venus. On the other hand, if Venus gets caught by Mars from Sagittarius, a Capricorn native will land in much more adversities. Aquarius ascendant: Almost similar results will be felt as by a Capricorn native, but an Aquarian is slightly better with Mars in Sagittarius. He can look forward to betterment in the later part of life. Pisces ascendant: The native will be lucky in all respects, will be very rich, will enjoy numerous conveyances, landed properties and will be very respectable.

घत्यो वित्ताहर्ता सुलभोगसमन्वितो भवति सुस्थः । श्रेष्वमतिः प्रव्यातः सेनानाथो नरेन्द्रो वा ।१६।। सद्युवती रर्णविजयी स्वबन्धुविषयस्थितः स्वतन्त्रञ्च ।

श्रारक्षकः सुक्षोलः कुजे स्वतुङ्गे बहूपचाररतः ।।२०॥

19-20. MARS IN CAPRICORN: If Mars is posited in Capricorn at birth, one will be wealthy (or fortunate), be endowed with happiness and pleasures, be wealthy, will

have an excellent disposition, be famous, be an army chief or a king, will possess a good wife, be successful in war, will live in his own country, be independent, be a protector, be virtuous and will be interested in various procedures.

Notes: Mars in Capricorn will confer courage, success over enemies, high achievements in flelds of army, chemical dealing, gunnery etc. Native with Mars in this position with good lordship will acquire immovable properties. For different ascending signs, Mars remaining in Capricorn is capable of bestowing following effects:

Aries ascendant: The lord of ascendant in 10th in exaltation Mars will pave way for obtaining high professional success, fame etc. Being the lord of the 8th it not only makes the native long-lived but also gives a huge paternal inheritance. Taurus ascendant: Father's longevity is cut short unless Saturn is well aspected. Independently Mars can cause sickness to father. The native flourishes through marriage. Gemini ascendant: The dasa of Mars will pose unexpected dangers to the native and throw weight on account of litigations. Loss of money and displacement will also result. Sickly wife, strained marital relationship and landed properties by making debts should also be predicted. Cancer ascendant: Though Mars in Capricorn will give a superior Rajayoga to the native, early demise of the spouse will follow. Leo ascendant: Many gains through cousins and enemies will come to pass. This position may also cause wounds to father, high education to self but with some hindrance, long living mother etc. Virgo ascendant: Loss of children, gain through brothers and friends and a long lease of life to the native will result. In the process, if Mercury and Saturn are both weakly posited, then Mars alone cannot give long life. Libra ascendant: Financial plentifulness, fortunate spouse, high learning, abundant landed property etc., wilI be resultant through Mars in Capricorn. (If Venus or Saturn aspect Mars, these indications will still be greater ). Librans with Mars in Capricorn will have two marriages if Venus is in Gemini/Sagittarius. Scorpio ascendant: Though Mars is the ruler of the horo-

scope, goods derivable during Mars dasa, will only bemeagre. The native will enjoy high physical stamina, but will be prone to many diseases. Sagittarius ascendant: The subject will have numerous children, majority being female, and will substantially gain monetarily. He will be irascible. Even with plenty of resources, he will lack happiness. Capricorn ascendant: The native will live in aristocratic circumstances, will lack physical happiness, be highly short-tempered, be not virtuous and will always be after other women. Aquarius ascendant: Though the subject will have all paraphernalia, occasional downfalls will crop up. Too much of expenditure will mark the life. Pisces ascendant: Fear from theft, loss of wife, huge gains will come to pass.

प्रश्रयशौबविहीनो वृद्धाकारः सुदुर्गतिर्मरणे । मात्सर्यासुयानृतवाग्दोर्ष रपह्तार्थश्च 111

रोमशगात्रो विश्तो द्यूताद्याह्तधनं कुवेवधरः । बुःखसमाह्तवृत्तिः पानरचर्दुर्भयः कुजे कुम्मे ।२२।

21-22. MARS IN AGUARIUS: If Mars is posited in Aquarius at the time of one's birth, he will be devoid of both affection and purity, will look like an old person, will die a bad death, will have spite, jealousy, untruthful disposition, afflicted speech and lost wealth, will be ugly, will have (abundant) hair on the body, will lose money in gambling. will be unsightly, will have miserable profession, be fond of liquor and be unfortunate.

Notes: Mars posited in Aquarius will deprive the native of kindness, chastity etc. Though Mars reflects youth in one’s appearance, in this Old Man’s sign, only aged traits will be acquired by the subject. Death will be violent if particularly Cancer ascends with Mars in Aquarius. Gambling, misfortune addictability to intoxicants, adversity in professlon etc., will be experienced. Remaining in Aquarlus. Mars gives the following effects for various signs ascending.

Ariles ascendant: Mars in Aquarius will prove a source

of miseries. The native will have only one elder brother. Taurus ascendant: Education will be hindered, with Mars in Aquarius. The subject will have falls from heights and conveyances. Loss of children, gain through wife, heavy expenditure etc., will follow. In female’s chart, when Taurus rises with Mars placed in Aquarius, menopause will appear at an unusual age. Gemini ascendant: The native will not have good terms with elders and co-born. He will be unlucky and unhappy. Cancer ascendant: Longevity is cut short with life becoming miserable. Not many children are indicated. Leo ascendant: The spouse will be of questionable character and be highly libidinous. Marriage may however bring fortunes. Virgo ascendant: The native will be long-lived, free from diseases and will prevall over his encmics. There will however be no understanding with coborn. Libra ascendant: The native’s marriage will be delayed; lack of happiness with wife and separation from wife and children are denoted by this position of Mars. The spouse may be sexually cold. For females, puberty will be delayed. Scorpio ascendant: While landed property and learning may be abundant, the subject will not be free from high incidence of debts. Sagittarius ascendant: The native will be cunning and miserly and will grab the wealth of his co-born. He will be short of hearing. Capricorn ascendant: No matter how big may be the earnings, this position of Mars warns heavy financial losses. The subject will also face loss of progeny. Aquarius ascendant: The native will be active, irascible, unhealthy and profligate. Pisces ascendant: There will be loss of paternal wealth and high dose of expenditure.

रोगार्तो मन्दस्तः प्रवासक्षीलः स्वबन्धुपरिभ्तः । मायावञ्चनदोर्षहु तसर्वस्बो विषादी I जिह्योऽतितीक्रमशोको गुरुद्विजावजको दयाहीनः । ईप्सितवेत्ता ज्ञाता स्तुतिप्रियोऽ्त्ये कुजे च्यातः ।२४।

23-24. MARS IN PISCES: Should Mars be in Pisces at the time of one's birth, the person will be troubled by discases, will have indifferent children, will live in foreign

countries, be insulted by his own relatives, will lose all his wealth by his cunning and cheating disposition, will be depressed in spirits, be very miscrable, will disrespect elders and Brahmins, be unkind, will conceive (other's) desires, be fond of praises and be famous.

Notes: Mars in Pisces will make the native timid, less friendly and spiritless. There is a likelihood of his losing wealth by his own evil thoughts. For different ascendants, Mars in Pisces will produce the following possible effects:

Aries ascendant: The native will be subjected to all kinds of miseries with Mars in the 12th. He will have fear of drowning. Taurus ascendant: There may be two marrlages to the native. (This is sure if Venus is simultaneously in Virgo). Gains will accrue through unfair means. Gemini ascendant: The person will not be in good terms with employers and will have checkered career. If Mercury aspects Mars, favourable results will follow. Mars in 10th will make a Gemini native a source of trouble to others and pitiless. Cancer ascendant: The subject will lack paternal bliss. He will earn through watery products, gold, electric- Ity, teaching, banking etc. Leo ascendant: One will inherit money through father only to spend away a major portion. Maternal happiness will be a rarity. Educational aspect will be strained. Much duress will be experienced during boyhood. Many diseases will occur throughout llfe. Virgo ascendant: This is not a happy position to have a longliving wlfe; During Mars dasa the spouse is prone to accute danger. The native himself will be frequented by miseries and misfortunes. Libra ascendant: Diseases like dropsy, indigestion, burning sensation of digestive organs etc., will be the order of one’s life. Penury will be markedly experienced during Mars dasa. If Mercury is exalted simultancously in the 12th a superior Rajayoga is constituted promoting fortunes and fame. Scorpio ascendant: The native may acquire children but will lose. Occasionally unhappiness will be felt. Sagittarius ascendant: One will have mother of questionable character unless Jupiter is auspiciously posited. The position of Mars in Pisces also indicates a shortliving mother. Capricorn ascendant: The subject will

be brave, will encounter fraternal loss/wrath and will be ordinarily happy. His mother will be attached more towards his co-born than to him. Aquarius ascendant: One may lose either wife or children. Though Mars is a Rajayogakaraka, the 2nd house position does not promote frugality. Pisces ascendant: If Mars dasa occurs in childhood, it may inflict death. If the dasa coincides with later part of life the person will enjoy great Rajayoga. There will be however lack of physical comforts.

धनबारपुत्रवन्तं नृपसचिवं दण्डनायकं स्यातम् । नृपतिमुवारं कुस्ते दिनेव्वरनिरीक्षितः कुजः स्वक्षे ॥२५॥ मातुरहितं क्षताङ्ग स्वजनदेयं च मित्रहितं च । स्वरयुहेऽसुक् वाशिङ्व्टः सेव्य कन्याप्रजं कुर्ते ।२६। परधनहरणे निपुणं चानृतकं कामदेवभक्तं च । कुर्ते स्वमे ज्ञदष्टे देवर्षं वेद्यापति भौमः ।२७। प्राज्ञं मधुरं सुभगं मातृपितृवल्लभं धनसमृद्धम् । ग्रनुपममीदवरमाद्यं त्रिव्यागुरुनिरी क्षितोऽवनेः पुत्रः ।२८।। स्वगुहेऽसूक् सितहृष्टः स्त्रोहेतोर्बन्धभागिनं कुस्ते । श्रसकृत् सङ्टच्व विभवं स्त्रीहेतोराजितं चापि ।२६१। चोरविधाते शुरं निर्वोर्य स्वजनपरिहीनम् ।

श्वन्यस्त्रीभर्तारं जनयति सौरेक्षितः स्वमे भौमः ।।३०।।

25-30. MARS IN OWN RASI AND IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: Should Mars at birth be in Aries/Scorpio and be aspected by the Sun, the person will possess wealth, wife and children, be a king’s minister, be a justice, be famous and be a charitable king. If the aspect is by the Moon, the native will be bereft of mother, will have a wounded body, will hate his own people, will not have friends, be jealous and will have female children. If Mercury aspects the said Mars, the subject will be an expert in stealing others' money, be a llar, be devoted to Manmatha (i.e. highly libidinous), be hostile and will frequently visit prostitutes. If Jupiter lends his aspect to Mars as above, the native will

be learned, sweet-spoken, fortunate, dear to parents, be very affluent, and will be a king par excellence. Venus aspecting Mars as above denotes that the native will be imprisoned due to females, and will be deprived of his money on account of females, more than once. If Saturn aspects Mars as mentioned above, the native will be capable of hindering thieves in spite of his being not valorous, will be devoid of his men and will maintain another woman.

Notes: When Mars occupies his own sign in aspect to friendly Sun, the person will enjoy all kinds of wealth. (For Aries, Leo, Cancer and Scorpio, Mars related to the Moon, the Sun or Jupiter is generally beneficial ). Mercury aspecting Mars in Aries/Scorpio will give him all bad qualities and make him go after other women. When Venus aspects Mars in Aries/Scorpio, the person is prone to risk through females. Saturn aspecting is congenial for becoming a police officer and the like while it contributes to illegal relationship with females.

बनपर्वतेषु रमते । रामाद्विष्टो भवेद्रबहुविपक्षः । सितमे रविखा दृष्टे प्रचण्डवेन्नः कुजे धोरः ।३१।। मातुरपथ्यो विषमो बहुयुवतीनां पतिः प्रियस्तासाम् । धुक्गृहे वाधिवृष्टे रखभोदर्जावते भौमे ॥३२।। कलह्प्रियी बहुवचा मदुकायो मन्दपुत्रधनः । सितमे भवति च भौमे बुच्च्टें शास्त्रवित्युरुषः १३३। वादितगीतविधिज्ञः सौभाग्ययुतः स्वबन्धुद्वितइच । घुष्मवने क्षितिसुते हव्टे गुरुख्या भवेत् स्फीतः ।३४। नृपमन्त्री नृपदयितः सेनानाथः प्रसिद्धनामा ज। घुष्युहे भवति कुजे शुक्रेख निरीक्षिते सुखितः ।३५॥ सुखभाक् व्यातो घनवान् मित्रस्वजनर्युतः कुजे बिद्वान् । श्रेरिपुरप्रामार्तामषिपः सितमे ख शनिवृष्टे ॥३६। 31-36. MARS IN A HOUSE OF VENUS AND ASPECTED BY OTHERS: If Mars is in Taurus/Libra, with the aspect of

the Sun, the person will seek to move in forests and hills, will hate women, will have many enemies, will have fierce appearance and be courageous. If Mars as above is aspected by the Moon, the native will not honour his mother, be wicked, will lord many women and be dear to them and will fear war. If Mercury aspects the said Mars, the native will promote quarrels, will speak much, will have a soft body, will possess (indifferent or) few sons and little wealth and will be learned in Sastras. If Jupiter aspects Mars as above, the subject will be skilful in music and in play of musical instruments, be fortunate, be dear to his relatives, and will be pure. If Venus himself aspects Mars in the above condition, the person will be a king’s minister, be liked by the king, be an army chief, will have famous name (i.e. titles etc.) and will be happy. Saturn aspecting Mars as above indicates that the native will be happy, famous, wealthy, be endowed with friends and own men, be learned, and will be head of a group of villages/towns, or group of men.

Notes: The aspect of the Sun on Mars in Libra/Taurus makes the native living in lonely places or remaining aloof from others. He may not have good relationship with his spousc and other women in general and is pronc to litigations. If the Moon be the planet aspecting Mars as above, the person will have relationship with many women, will contract diseases of the blood—private parts etc. Mercury’s aspect on Mars thus posited will promote learning, and belligerent tendency but the native will have either few sons or will have indifferent ones. If Jupiter aspects Mars as above, the person will be wealthy, will have untarnished image and will be generally skilful. Venus aspecting Mars in the former’s Rasl the native will enjoy high position in lifc, particularly in fields like army, and become widely famous. Saturn’s aspect on Mars so posited is favourable for wealth, position, relatives etc., particularly if the ascending sign belongs to Saturn or Venus.

विद्याधनशौर्ययुतं गिरिवनदुर्गच्रियं

महासत्त्वम् ।

बुषभवने रक्ताङ्गो जनयति हृष्टः सदा रविता ॥ ३७।।

सुजिनं धनिनं कान्तं कन्यापुररक्षकं युवतिसत्वयुतम् । ज्गृहे नृपगृह्पाल जनयति चन्देक्षितो भौमः ।३८।। लिपिगरिपितकाव्यकुशलं बहुभाविरामनृतमधुरवाव्यं च । ब्रूतं बहुदुःखसहं जनयति बको बुषेक्षितो शक्षें ।३१।। राजपुरुषं प्रकाशं दौत्येन विदेशगं नरं कुर्ते। सर्वक्रियासु कुशलं बुधराशौ नायकं च गुरुहृष्टः ॥४०।। धुक्रेखा दृव्यमानः स्त्रीकृत्यकरं समृद्धसुभगं च । बुषभवने रक्ताङ्गः कुस्ते वस्त्रान्नभोक्तारम् ॥४१। श्राकर गिरिदुर्गरतं कर्षक्तिदुःखभागिनं कुदते । . श्रतिशूरमति च मलिनं यमेक्षितो बुघगुहे विभवहीनम् ॥४२।।

37-42. MARS IN MERCURY’S RASI AND IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If at birth Mars be in Gemini/Virgo and in aspect to the Sun, the native will be blessed with learning, wealth and courage, be fond of hills, forests and fortresses, and be highly strong. If the Moon lends aspect to Mars as above, the person will be happy, wealthy, splendorous, will guard women’s apartments, will be endowed with women and will manage the king’s residence. If Mercury aspects Mars as above, one will be skilful in the art of writing, in mathematics and in poetry, be garrulous, be llar, be a sweet speaker, be a messenger (or an ambassador), and will endure lot of misery. Should Jupiter aspect Mars in Taurus/Libra, the native will be a king’s representative, be bright, will go to foreign countries as an ambassador, be skilful in all doings and be a leader. If Venus aspects Mars in above position, the person will do the jobs of females, be very fortunate and will enjoy food and robes. If Saturn aspects Mars as above, the person will be interested in wandering in mines (i.e. places beneath surface), hills and forests, will have husbandry as his livelihood, be highly miserable, be very valorous, dirty and be devoid of wealth.

Notes: If Mars is in a Rasi of Mercury, i.e. either in Gemini or in Virgo, and aspected by the Sun, the native will

enjoy a high degree of education, particularly in the field of law, chemistry etc. The aspect of the Moon on such Mars is favourable for positions like security officers, house governors etc. If the planets involved are with strength the positions will be superior and if weak the state relates to ordinary watchmen and the like. The aspect of Mercury can give positions like ambassador and the person can prove himself a famous writer, poet or mathematician. Pursuing astrology is also worth with this position. Jupiter aspecting Mars thus posited is again favourable to be an ambassador or to hold senior positions in Embassies. Should Venus aspect Mars in Gemini/Virgo, one will carry out the orders of femalcs. If the planets involved are auspiclous the person will have a smooth married life. Saturn's aspect on Mars may denote miners and the like and agriculturists. This combination adds to miseries and misfortunes.

पित्तर्गदितवेहस्तेजस्वी दण्डनायको स धीरः । चन्द्रगृहस्थे मौमे दिनकरदृष्टे भवेत्पुरुषः ॥४३।। क्षहुभिर्ग्याधिभिरार्तों मीवाचारो विरुपदेहर्च । क्षक्षिराक्षौ भूतनये वाविनावृष्टे सत्योकव्च ॥४४।। क्लिनः पापाचारः' शुद्कुटुम्बो बहिष्कतः स्वजनः । कार्षटके बुधवृष्टे क्षितितनये भवति निर्लज्जः ॥४५॥ विस्यातो नृपमन्त्री विर्धास्त्यायान्वितो भवेद्धन्यः । गुरदुष्टे वाशिभवने भोगद्च विर्वाजतो बक्ने ॥४६। स्त्रीसञ्चादुद्धिग्नः परिभूतः स्त्रोक्तस्तथा बोर्षः । कर्षाटके कितिपुत्रे सितवृुष्टे स्याद्विपन्नधनः ॥४७।। जलसंयानाप्तषनः कल्षितिपालसमः पुमान् ललितचेष्टः । शशिगुहसंस्थे भौमेयमेक्षिते स्यात् सदा कान्तः ॥४८।। 43-48. MARS IN CANCER IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Mars be in Cancer at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be subjected to billous diseases, be splendorous, be a justice in position and be valorous. If the Moon aspects

Mars in Cancer, the person will be troubled by various diseases, will have mean conduct, will possess an unsightly body and be miserable. Mercury aspects Mars in Cancer, the person will be dirty, sinful, will possess a mean family, will be rejected by his own men and be shameless. If Mars in Cancer is aspected by Jupiter, the native will be famous, be king's minister, be learned, charitable, wealthy and be bereft of carnal pleasures. If Venus aspects Mars in Cancer, the subject will be grieved on account of women’s company, be insulted by women and will lose wealth on account of women. Should Saturn aspect Mars in Cancer, the native will acquire money through journey in water, be equal to a king, be sportive in his acts and be always bright.

Notes: If Mars is in Cancer and is in aspect to Jupiter, Moon or the Sun it constitutes Neechabhanga, which will consequently rise to acquiring position and money. The aspect of Moon or Sun or Mars in Cancer will produce many diseases while that of Jupiter makes one averse to sexual pleasures or may give less opportunities in this direction. Should Venus aspect Mars in Cancer, it is not congenial for a happy married life and will be a cause of misunderstandings with females. The aspect of Mercury on Mars in Cancer causes stormy weather in family llfe while that of Saturn indicates position of a navigator, driver, ship captain etc., and gives wealth.

प्रसातानां हितकारी मिर्रः स्वजनव्च संयुतव्वण्डः । गोकुलवनाद्रिचारी सिहे भौमे तररिदृष्टे ।४E।। मातुर्न बुभा मतिमान् कठिनशारीरो विपुलकोर्तिः । केसरिभवने भौमे शाशिना वृष्टेऽङ्गनांत्रार्थः ॥५०।। बहुशिल्पश्यो सुब्बः काव्यकलालम्पटो विषमवीलः । पञ्न्वमभबने भौमे बुधेन वृष्टेऽतिविपुराश्च ॥५१। 'भूपतिसमीपवर्तों विद्याचार्यों विद्युदबुद्धिश्च । स्वविसुते सिङ्गस्ये चुदता वृष्टे चमूनायः ॥५२।

विवियस्त्रीभोगयुतः स्त्रोसुभगो नित्ययौवनो हृष्टः। लेयगुहे रक्ताङ्गे सितेन दृष्टे भवेज्जातः ॥ ५३॥। वृद्धाकारो निःस्वः परवेद्मप्रमखाशोलवान् दुःखो। दिनकररावौ रुधिरे दिनकरतनयेन संवृष्टे ॥ ४॥।

49-54. MARS IN LEO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Mars is in Lco at the time of one's birth, and aspected by the Sun, the native will be humble, helpful to friends, be endowed with own men, and be fond of wandering in cow-houses, forests and hills. If the Moon aspects Mars in such a position, the native will be ominous for his mother, be intelligent, will possess a hard body, be widely famous and will obtain money through women. If Mercury aspects Mars in Leo, the native will be clcver in many arts, be a miser, be skilful in poetry and fine arts and be wicked. If Jupiter aspects Mars in Leo, the person will be close to the king, be highly learned, be of pure mentality and be an army chief. Should Venus aspect Mars in Leo, the person will have union with many women, be fortunate in respect of his wife and will be ever juvenile. Saturn aspecting Mars in Leo denotes that the person will look like an old man, be poor, will wander in others’ houses/be miserable.

Notes: Mars in the Rasi of the Sun if aspected by the Sun himself will confer abundant wealth. But this combination will show some affliction to heart. If the Moon aspects Mars in Leo, he will pose danger to mother particularly for Taurus and Aquarius ascendants. When Mercury aspects Mars so placed, one will become proficient in writing. Jupiter's aspect on Mars in Leo is a favourable augury in the matter of finance and position, but the native will be fiery in temperament. The diseases that may occur to him may be easy to diagnose while cure may not be fast. The aspect of Venus on Mars in Leo will help the person maintain his youth intact while he may go after many women. Saturn aspecting Mars in Leo is generally very bad but if the Lagna be one of Aquarius, Capricorn or Libra, this relationship becomes very favourable and the native will have abundant wealth and be happy. If it is mutual aspect, even

Aries and Scorpio natives will stand to benefit.

लोकनमस्यं सुभगं वनगिरिदुर्गेवु लब्धगुह्वासम् । सुरगुरुभवने भौमः करोति रविणेक्षितः क्रूरम् ॥५५।। विकलं कलहप्रायं प्राज्ञं रुधिरः करोति शश्चिदृष्टः । विद्धांसं गुरुभवने नृपतिविशद्ध सवा पुरुषस् ॥५६।। बेधाविनं सुनिपुणं शिल्पांचार्य बुधेन संहृष्टः । मुद्भवने क्षितितनयः कद्दोति विद्रांसमत्यक्तम् ।।५७।। श्रकलत्रं सुखरहितं रिपुभिरधृच्यं च वित्तवन्तं च । गुरुभवने गुरुदृष्टो व्यायामपरं कुजः कुर्ते ।। ५८ ॥ युवतोनामतिदयितं चित्रालड्कारभाविनमुदारम् । विषयपरमति च सुभगं गुरुमे काव्येक्षितः कुजः कुरुते ॥ ५६ ॥ गुरुमेऽ्स्क् शनिदृष्टः कुद्वारीरमुदारमाहवे पापम् । श्रटनं सुखलवरहितं परघर्मरतं कुजः कुर्ते । ६० ॥ 55-60. MARS IN JUPITER'S HOUSE IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Mars occupies Sagittarius or Pisces at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the subject will be worldhonoured, be fortunate, will live in forests, hills and fortresses, and be cruel. If the Moon aspects Mars as above, the person will be deformed, belligerent, learned and be always inimical to the king. If Mercury should aspect Mars in above position, the person will be a scholar, be skilful, learned in fine arts and be highly learned in general. If Jupiter aspects Mars as above, the person will be devoid of wife and happiness, will be beyond the reach of enemies, be wealthy and be fond of exercises. Should Venus aspect Mars as above, the person will be very much dear to women, be interested in cosmetics, makeup etc., be charitable, be libidinous and be fortunate. If Saturn aspects Mars as above, the person will have defective body, be sinful, wandcring-natured, devoid of happiness, and interested in others' religion.

Notes: Should Mars be in Pisces or Sagittarius and be

in aspect to the Sun, it brings honour from the world. Recognitions like Nobel Prize winning etc., may follow. The position to be held by the native will have a world-wide bearing even if favourable planets like the Sun, Jupiter or Moon be in a Kendra position to Mars in Pisces or Sagittarius and will make one achieve world-level distinction. (This equally applies to Mars in Leo angular Jupiter, Moon or the Sun). Kalyana Varma attributes evil results to the Moon's aspect on Mars in a Jupiterian Rasi except that the native will be learned. Though the Moon and Mars are friendly and the position of Mars is in a friendly Rasi, the author's observation deserves significant note. This relationship causes some deformity of limb. If the aspects are potential, it may be right from birth and if be meagre there are chances of proneness to deformity in some form or other at a later stage in life. Saturn’s aspect on Mars in Sagittarius or Pisces also indicates some physical deformity, lack of happiness and attachment to other religions than his own. The aspect of Venus is fortunate.

सूर्ये क्षितोऽतितीदरां सौरयहे मूविजः ब्ुश्ते । ९५ ॥ चपलमहितं जनर्या यमभेऽलडकारभागिनमुद्दार्म् । भ्रस्थिरसौह्दमाट्य जनयति चन्द्रेक्षितो बक्रः ॥ ६२ ॥ श्रतिमघुरगमनमधनं रवितनयग्हे न विव् तमसत्वम् । कापटिकमधर्मपरं जनयति बुधवोक्षितो भौमः । ६३ । श्रतिरूपं मन्दगहे न्पतिगुरासमग्वितं स्थरारम्भम् । बीर्घायुषं क्षमातो गुरुसंहृष्टः करोति बन्ध्वाप्तम् ॥ ६४ ॥ विविधोपभोगमाढ्यं शनिमे स्त्रीपोषरानुरतमेव । शुक्रेख हृद्यमानो जनयति कलहप्रियं वक्रः ।। ६५ । नृपतिमतिवित्तवन्तं युवतिद्वेष्यं बहुप्रणं प्राज्ञम् । सुखर हितं ररात्रोण्ड करोति शनिमे बानीकलितो भौमः ॥ ६६ ॥

61-66. MARS IN SATURN'S HOUSE AND ASPECTED BY OTHERS: Should Mars be in Capricorn or Aquarius and

be aspected by the Sun, the subject will have very dark body, be couragcous, will have many wives, sons and abundant wealth and be very sharp. If the Moon aspects Mars as above, one will be fickleminded, be not well disposed towards his mother, be fond of beautification, be charitable, be not firm in friendship, and be rich. If Mercury aspects Mars in Capricorn or Aquarius, the subject will walk very slowly, be not rich, will not have any profession, be not strong, be not outspoken, and be not virtuous. If Jupiter lends his aspect to Mars as above, the native will be very beautiful, will possess kingly qualities, will fulfil his undertakings, be long-lived and be endowed with relatives. If Venus aspects Mars as above one will be endowed with various kinds of (carnal) pleasures, be interested in fostering women and be belligerent. Should Saturn aspect Mars, the latter being in the former’s house, the person will be a king, be very affluent, will hate women, will possess many children, be learned, be devold, of happiness and be timid in war.

Notes: If Mars is in Aquarius or Capricorn and is aspected by the Sun, there will be plurality of wives, many male issues and great wealth. If Mars is exalted in the ascendant while the Sun is in Cancer, more than one marriage is inevitable but when Aquarius rises with Mars, the Sun being in Leo as the 7th lord, it will only end with one marriage. Mars in a Rasi of Saturn in aspect to the Moon will have an adverse bearing on mother’s longevity. Mercury's aspect is unfavourable for money and profession. Should Mars in Capricorn or Aquarius be aspected by Venus, the native will enjoy pleasures of bed with many a woman and will be devold of moral conduct. Saturn's aspect on Mars thus placed is auspicious for money and position while the native will not be happy in many fronts in life. He cannot maintain good relationship on family level.

इतिगंकल्यारावर्मविरचितायां सा रावल्यां भौमचारो नाम पञ्न्वविशोऽ्ध्यायः ।२५।

Thus ends the 25th chapter entitled "Effects of Mars in Varlous Rasis” in Saravali of Kalyana Verma.

## Chapter 26

## ड्रथ वुधचाराध्याय ।। २६ ॥

## Effects of Mercury in Various Rasis

प्रियविप्रहस्तु वेत्ताचार्यो विटघूर्तचेष्टक्षगाब्रः । सङ्गोतन्त्तमिरतो न सत्यवचनो रतिप्रियो लिपिवित् ॥ १ ॥ कूटकरो \_\_ बद्धाशो व बहुत्रमोत्पन्ननष्टघनः । बह्वराबर्धनभागी खलस्थरः स्यात् क्रिये बुधे कितबः ॥ २ ॥

1-2. MERCURY IN ARIES: If Mercury is in Aries at the time of one’s birth, the native will be fond of battles (be uncomprising), be very learned, wicked, emaciated, interested in music and dance, be untruthful, be attached to sexual pleasures, be a writer, will produce fictitious things, will eat much, will lose hard-earned money, will incur debts and imprisonment frequently and will be sometimes fickleminded and yet sometimes firm in disposition.

Notes: The placement of Mercury in Aries will give a tendency of stubbornness and uncomprising nature. Even at the slightest thought of sexual pleasure, the native will get deeply merged in it and will be after women. He will face frequent loss in transactions and accumulate debts, will be a good eater and a good writer as well. He will be both firm and infirm in disposition. If there are other combinations warranting imprisonment, Mercury's position in Aries will strengthen those. The native will be more a loser with Mercury in Aries. (His occupation of Scorpio will also give rise to similar unfavourable results, vide sloka 15-16 infra).

Now a glance at the results of Mercury’'s position in Aries while different Rasis become ascendant: Aries ascendant: The native will lose his mother or father early or may live away from parents. Childhood will see frequent trouble,

while the later period may be prosperous if other favourable positions emanate in the radix. The native may be piously disposed. Taurus ascendant: He will not have many children, will not gain but be interested in religious activities. Gemini asccndant: He will earn abundant money but is susceptible to serious losses. He will be the eldest issue of the family which is more certain if aspected by Jupiter or Mars. Cancer ascendant: The dasa of Mercury will prove fully beneficial. Leo ascendant: There will be yogabhanga (obstruction of prosperity of the native) in Mercury dasa and loss of paternal and self-made wealth in the hands of enemies and in litigations. Virgo ascendant: He will be short-lived, will face obstacles in all his undertakings and will exhaust all ancestral properties. Libra ascendant: The spouse will not be loyal to the native. The native will himself be a profligate. Scorpio ascendant: The native will prove a proficient writer, will have a long lease of life, will enjoy Rajayoga in Mercury dasa, and will earn land, wealth and others’ properties. Sagittarlus ascendant: He will enjoy maternal happiness, conveyances lands and learning. Father’s death may be early if the Sun is not strong enough. Aquarius ascendant: He will be exceptionally timid, will fear speaking before public, will not maintain good relationship with his elders and will have many sisters born after him. Pisces ascendant: He will improve his monetary position after marriage. If however Jupiter is in Libra, all earnings will be lost and he will become abruptly bankrupt.

बलः प्रगल्भवाता व्यातो विज्ञातवेवशास्त्रार्यः । व्यायामाम्बरमुवर्णमाल्याभिरतः स्यरप्रकृतिः ।। ३ ॥ स्फीतघनश्रीसहितः प्रियवल्गुक्यो गृहीतवाष्यञ्च ।

गाम्यर्वहास्यक्षीलो रतिलोलो व बुधे बवमे । ४ ।

3-4. MERCURY IN TAURUS: Should Mercury be in Taurus, the native will be skilful, eminently liberal, be famous, will have knowledge of Vedas and sastras, be fond of exercises, robes, ornaments and garlands, be firm in disposition, will have sincerely earned wealth, will possess

a chaste wife, will be a soft and sweet speaker, and be after sexual satisfaction.

Notes: In Taurus, this planet proves very favourable in many respects, in general while adversities will depend on the ascendant and the planets aspecting. Generally speaking, the native will be skilful in the matter of association with females for carnal pleasures. The various ascendants with Mercury in Taurus are able to produce the following general effects:

Aries ascendant: One will suffer from some eye disease or the other. (With Saturn and Mercury in Taurus it may promote dental deficiencies). He will gain through cousins and brothers. Savings may not be much; will earn more and spend more. Taurus ascendant: He will be beautiful, healthy, spiritual, learned and modest. His progenic ability will not be at stake though Mercury is impotent by nature. On the other hand, he will beget many issues. Gemint ascendant: This position of Mercury in Taurus does not confer beneficial results in its dasa but the native will be liberal and will do only good acts. Cancer ascendant: He will earn in fair ways but will sometimes incur losses. Leo ascendant: Spiritual teachers, astrologers, vedic exponents, secretarial assistants etc., will do well to have Mercury in the 10th in Taurus. The native will earn abundant fame, money etc. Virgo ascendant: He will be a source of luck to the family. His father will particularly prosper. Libra ascendant: This position denotes an ill-placed or ill-disposed father. The native will enjoy premarital sexual relationship with many women. Hls marriage may consummate only with great difficulty. His elder brothers may be a source of disappointment. Sagittarius ascendant: He will not enjoy a position with powers. His wife will be sickly. Capricorn ascendant: He will have loss of children or those will be subject to frequent onslaught of diseases. Aquarius ascendant: His mother will be of questionable character unless Mercury is well-aspected. His learning will be hindered. Pisces ascendant; He will not be happy with brothers and sisters. He may have a step-mother.

चुभवेवः प्रियभाषी प्रह्यातघनो विकत्यनो मानी । प्रोजिक्तसुखकोऽल्परतिद्वस्त्रीयुक्तो विवावरतः ।। ५ । श्रुतिकल्पकलाभिज्ञः कविः स्वतन्त्रः प्रियः प्रदानरतः । कर्मठबहुसुतमित्रो नरमिथुनस्थे बुधे भवति ।। ६ ।।

5-6. MERCURY IN GEMINI: If Mercury occupies Gemini at birth, the subject will have an auspicious appearance, will speak swectly, be very affluent, be an able speaker, be honourable, will give up his happiness, will cohabit less, will have two wives, be fond of arguments, will be learned in Vedas, Sastras etc., be a poet, be independent, dear, very munificient, proficient in work and will have many sons and friends.

Notes: Mercury in Gemini gives a beautiful appearance. (This is more true if the sign in question ascends.). He will have more than one marriage. He may sometimes lack happiness but be highly intelligent. The following are the general effects when Mercury is in Gemini in relation to the 12 ascending signs:

Aries ascendant: The native will have more female coborns than male. He will be highly intelligent and will have a sickly father. Taurus ascendant; He will earn plentily and save. If Jupiter from Sagittarius, is opposed to Mercury there is a risk of one’s becoming poor. If malefics join Mercury in Gemini, it may be fatal. Gemini ascendant: If Mercury is alone in the ascendant, he proves very auspiclous. The native will have healthy body, wealthy family, be highly intelligent, obstinate and pleasant in disposition. (According to Bhrigu Sutras, Mercury in Gemini or in Virgo ascendant gives happiness from brothers and attainment of heavens after death.) Cancer ascendant: The native will have nothing left out of his earnings, and will be free from discases. Leo ascendant: He will be ever happy and will earn by fatr means. He will have more daughters. His children will become very famous. Virgo ascendant: One will have significant position in life, be modest and be of

good conduct. For such a native association with predictive sciences can lead to prosperity. He will pursue earnings in many a way. Libra ascendant; One will have an unfortunate father who will flourish later on. Scorpio ascendant: He will enjoy a long lease of life. If Saturn joins, it will be greater. Mercury alone in the 8th in Gemini may cause obstacles in educational pursuits, open to betterment in years to come. Sagittarius ascendant: One will obtain a chaste wife but a few children. His Mercury period will confer significant prosperity. Professionally he may be happy. Capricorn ascendant: This placement of Mercury has a mixture of both good and adverse fortunes. Sometimes it produces irreligious persons without code of conduct. Aquarius ascendant: He will have intelligent progeny and more female offspring. If Mercury receives unfavourable aspects from the nodes, Mars or Jupiter, lunacy may result. For this yoga, the strength and disposition of the Moon must be reckoned. Pisces ascendant: The 4th house indications will generally prosper. If Saturn aspects Mercury in Gemini, the native's wife may not get on well with the father of the native.

प्राज्ञो विदेशनिरतः - स्त्रोरतिगेयार्विसक्तचित्सद्व । चपलो बहुप्रलापी स्वबन्धुविह्वेषवादरतः ।। ७ । स्त्रीवेराम्नष्टधनः कुत्सितश्षोलो बहुक्रियाभिरतः । सुकविः कर्कटसंस्थे स्ववंशकीत्या प्रसिद्धञ्च ।। ८ ।

7-8. MERCURY IN CANCER: One born with Mercury in Cancer will be learned, will be fond of living in other countries, be interested in cohabiting with women and also in music, be fickleminded, prattling, inimical to his own relatives, will be fond of arguments, will lose wealth on account of enmity with women, be of bad disposition, be interested in many jobs, be a good poet and will be popular on account of the fame of his ancestors.

Notes: When Mercury is in Cancer, the person's livelihood may be in places other than his birth place, The native will be fond of entertainments. He will not be in good

terms with his relatives. He will take pleasure in liaising with courtezans and will spend heavily on this vice. (If Cancer happens to be the 6th hosue with Mercury therein, 1.e. Aquarius ascending, he will be a first-rate drunkard.) He takes birth in a family which is already popular and famous. The native's personal disposition depends greatly on the Moon in particular, and on other related planets in general. What the 12 different ascendants are capable of giving with Mercury fixed in Cancer, can be estimated from the following account.

Aries ascendant: The child causes great pains to its mother at the time of delivery and possible sickness or debility to her prenatally. Normally he resides in a place adjacent to water. He will have abundant fertile lands with some litigations on account of them. Such natives are more unexceptional to have cardiac afflictions. Should the Sun join Mercury in Cancer, this calamity is more sure to follow. Taurus ascendant; Mercury in his dasa may sustain the prosperity given by Saturn dasa. The native's co-born may gain from him whereas he will stand to lose on their account. Cancer ascendant: An undesirable position indeed for good health, for it can cause mental instability and nervous breakdown. Even dreaded diseases like paralysis will take birth in the life of the native, if Saturn is related to such Mercury. Leo ascendant: If Mercury is not alone but with two favourable planets in the 12th, the person will amass wealth. Otherwise, there will be expenses in astronomical proportions. Virgo ascendant: He will gain through water and water-born products. He will either be the eldest issue of the family or will be residing with his elder brother or sister for a long period. Libra ascendant: If Mars joins Mercury in Cancer in the 10th for Libra ascendant, the native's father will have fear or danger from water. Mercury alone in Cancer will denote serious trouble for mother’s longevity. Scorpio ascendant: The native will not receive any help from his father. Alternatively the father may be short-lived. Sagittarius ascendant: There is a lot in want in married life and public relations. Frequent changes in livelihood are denoted by this position. Capricorn ascendant:

The married life is a little better with Mercury in Cancer for Capricorn ascendant as compared to Sagittarius counterpart. There will be unfruitful wanderings in Mercury's major period. Aquarius ascendant: Frequent expenditure on health of children and misfortunes to the subject are denoted. Pisces ascendant: He will get beneficial resuits in Mercury dasa. Generally, one will worship a female deity and obtain her blessings.

जानकलापरिहीनो लोकस्यातो नतत्यवाष्यश्च । श्रल्पस्मृतिश्च घनवान् सत्त्विहोन: सहजहन्ता ।। ६ ॥ स्त्रीदुर्भंगः स्वतन्त्रो जघन्यकर्मा बुधे भवति पुरुवः । प्रेष्योऽप्रजस्तु सिहे स्वकुलविरुद्धो जनाभिरामञ्च ॥ १० ।।

9-10. MERCURY IN LEO: Should the planet Mercury occupy Leo at onc's birth, the native will not posscss even bit of wisdom (I is a bit and hence i}T is interpreted thus), be famous in the world, be not truthful, will possess a weak memory, be wealthy, be not strong, will indulge in fratricide, be unfortunate in respect of wife, be independ. ent, mean in conduct, be a servant, be devold of children, be against his own race but be favourable to others.

Notes: Gnana kala pariheeno has been interpreted by some commentators that the native will lack wisdom and will be devoid of association with arts. However, according to kalyana Varma, the native with Mercury in Leo may lack wisdom. Even Varaha Mihira in his Brihat Jataka (ch. 18, sloka 10) uses the word \equiv denoting absence of wisdom. Gunakara of Horamakaranda (1496 A.D.) too uses the same word in this context.

This position of Mercury, however, should be favourable for world-wide fame. The native will be unhelpful to his co-born, whether he literally kills them or not. Children and marriage may not give him happiness. Sometimes, there is a chance of the native not obtaining progeny at all with Mercury in Leo. His memory cannot also be of much help to him in times of requirement.

In individual cases of different ascendants, Mercury in Leo produces the undermentioned effects:

Aries ascendant: This is an ascendant which lacks absolute wisdom with Mercury in thc 5th in Leo. The subject will not have happy progeny. Taurus ascendant: This position may prove a maraka for the mother. More so in Mercury's major period. Without meaning any disrespect to the original text, we may say that the person will be intelligent and wise, for the statement originally made and rendered is only a generalisation. Gemini ascendant: Dryness of throat, its burning sensation, gruff/shivering voice, trembling hands etc., are some results to be considered on adverse side. There are advantages with this position of Mercury to—a fortunate parent, resources through forests etc., and lands, though uncultivable. Virgo ascendant: There will be a tendency to seek sexual gratifications in unnatural ways, joblessncss for a greater part in life, emaciated body and poverty. If Mars joins Mercury in the 12th, blood transfusions may be occasionally required if helped by the Moon, the need docs not arise as above. Libra ascendant; Mercury in the I 1th in a dry sign as lord of the 12th and 9th may make money some what scarce. The fortunes of the native are also susceptible to break downs. Should Venus join with strength, then the position gets quick relief. Scorpio ascendant: Though one may call it 'Amala Yoga' with Mercury in the 10th it does not prove useful to the native; on the other hand it generates some worry or the other. This baneful planet for the ascendant in question posited in Leo forebodes definite early evil to mother. She may not have any more living children following the native under consideration. Sagittarius ascendant: This position suggests tastelessness in married life and absence of progenic ability in the wife. If however she has strong disposition of 5th lord/Jupiter and the like, this defect is greatly modified. Capricorn ascendant: The subject's eye sight will develop defects at a very young age. Misfortunes in his life will not be uncommon. Aquarius ascendant: Though the wife may be of good disposition, she is bound to be barren or sickly. His children will, if ob-

tained, be not very intelligent. Pisces ascendant; Married life may be long-lasting, with public relations unfruitful and strained. Inflow of money and its outflow seem to have equilibrium.

धर्मप्रियोऽतिवाय्मो चतुरः स्याल्लेष्यकाव्यश्ः । विज्ञानक्षिल्पनिरतो मघुरः स्त्रोव्वल्पवीर्यश्च ॥ ११ ॥ ज्येष्ठः पुष्यः सुट्टुदां मानी विनयोपचार्वादरतः । क्यातो गुर्णर्दारः कन्यायां सोमले बलवान् ॥ १२ ॥

11-12. MERCURY IN VIRGO: If Mcrcury occuples his exaltation sign (Virgo) at the time of one's birth, the native will be quite virtuous, eloquent, skilful, will have knowledge of writing (i.e. be an exponent) and poetry, be learned in fine/mechanical arts, be swect in disposition, be liked by women, be not much virile, be the eldest son of family, be honoured by virtuous, be disposed to serve others, modest, argumentative, famous, charitable and strong.

Notes: Mercury's exaltation detracts the virility of the person, according to the original text. Obviously because this planet rules impotence in a person and its strength gives rise to more impotence. The 5th lord’s position and Jupiter also stand for consideration. Remaining in Virgo, Mercury will produce following results for the 12 different ascendants:

Aries ascendant: While an authority says that Mercury in the 6th in Virgo promotes debts, enemies and diseases fully, it seems this position combats these adversities. (We cannot say that these defects will be completely offset. To some extent one has to suffer these debacles with Mercury in Virgo in the 6th ). Taurus ascendant: Should Mercury be in the 5th in Virgo, the subject enjoys excellent Rajayoga with wealth, conveyances and high position. His children may reach good status in their lives. Gemini asbendant: Even if the Sun severely combusts Mercury in the 4th in Virgo, according to Jataka Parijata the person recelves abundant education. He may professionally prosper. Can-

cer ascendant: His co-born will be instrumental for his good fortunes. Mercury dasa will give only mixed results. Leo ascendant: When Mercury occupies the 2nd in exaltation, the person will be an expert spcaker, will have appealing face, will gain huge sums and will live long. He will also obtain hidden wealth and be successful in competitions etc. Virgo ascendant: The native will earn abundant fame, learning and wealth. He will attain heights in life in Mercury dasa. Professionally he will remain well-placed throughout. Libra ascendant: He will spend his money on charity, religlous acts etc. Mercury does not make much contribution to the native's prosperity when occupying the 12th in exaltation. Scorpio ascendant: The nativc will enjoy a long span of life and will earn wealth uninteruptedly. However, he is not free from troubles of general nature. Sagittarilus ascendant: He will obtain a spouse of a supreme class possessing all virtues, and will enjoy great position in life. It is possible that there will be frequent debacles in earnings. His spouse can look forward to excellent understanding with her mother-in-law. Capricorn ascendant: The native will be a synonym of fortune. Particularly his Mercury dasa will take him to enviable heights. Aquarius ascendant: Mercury will only give an ordinary yoga. Generally, such natives acquire more female children. Lives to them will be more miserable than happy with Mercury in exaltation in Virgo in the 8th. The life is of course for a long span. Plsces ascendant; Marriage will prove a beneficial contract. The latter part of Mercury dasa may inflict death.

शिल्पविवादाभिरतो वाश्चतुरोऽर्यार्चमीप्सितव्ययकृत् ।

नामा विक्पष्यरति-वप्रातिथिवेवगुरुभक्तः

" 3 "

हतकोपचारकुक्षलः सुसम्मतो देदाभश्तदच ।

सप्तममबने साविखे सठव्चलक्षिप्रकोपपरितोषः । १४ ।।

13-14. MERCURY IN LIBRA: If Mercury falls in Libra at the time of one's birth the native will possess knowledge of arts, be intent upon arguments, be an able speaker, will spend money laviahly, will have business in various direc-

tions, will honour wisemen, guests, gods and preceptors (elders), be skilful in pretending to serve others, be amiable, devoted to gods, fraudulent, fickle-minded and sometimes short-tempered and yet sometimes (notably) peaceful.

Notes: Remaining in Libra, Mercury will produce following different effects according to different Lagnas.

Aries ascendant: Mercury in the 7th in Libra may give -a brilliant, educated and beautiful spouse, who will also be cunning, unadjustable, sickly and lavish. Sometimes in the post-marital life, the native himself may be subjected to diseases. There is also a possibllity of unhappy marriage if the Sun is simultaneously in deep fall, joining Mercury. It has been observed in our experience that the Sun/Mercury yoga in the 7th gives an Aries native marriage out of his religion and even country. Taurus ascendant: Mercury in Libra gives worries on account of monetary problems and of children. The person may develop spocch problems in the initial part of life but may become all right latter on if Jupiter is favourably placed in angle/trine. If Jupiter is not so posited while Ketu/Rahu join Mercury in the 6th, the defect is perennial. If the Ashtaka Varga bindus are not favourable in the 2nd/6th, this is all the more certain. Gemini ascendant: There may be numerous female children. The native will beget all comforts in llfe. Cancer ascendant: The native will receive high education, inspite of the fact that Mercury rules the 3rd and 12th. Leo ascendant: The native will enjoy fortunes through his sisters. His financial status will gradually improve throughout. During Mercury dasa there will be abundant prosperity. Virgo ascendant: There will be danger to llfe during boyhood, particularly for one starting with Mercury dasa. Monetarily, there will be overall satisfaction. The native proves an able speaker and earns money through his speeches etc. Libra ascendant: Birth may not be in good circumstances of the family. It is however profitable and fortunate in the middle of life. High expenditure, misfortunes to father through the native or vice versa and death in own village will result. Scorpio ascendant: This confers a long span of life while

boyhood will be full of hurdles. Life will not be a bed of roses but will have a thorny path ahead with Mercury in 12th in Libra. Sagittarius ascendant: Plenty of money through ancestors and through marriage will result most of which will however be at stake. Many obstacles will have to be faced in profession and money matters. Death or trouble to an elder brother/sister may follow the native's birth. Capricorn ascendant: Very fortunate circumstances will prevail in professional sphere. They are more successful businessmen than any others. If Saturn joins Mercury effectively, one may become notable among medical men. Aquarius ascendant: Mercury in Libra will give mixed results about one’s fortune and general among prosperity. The native's birth may portend evil to father. Pisces ascendant: The subject may seek physical relationship with many womèn. Married life may not be satisfactory, only tending to separation. Intelligent co-born, problems relating to throat/ lungs and sadistic disposition will result. One may be harsh in speech.
